# MY Ba'ba'

English rendering of Divyanubhu'ti

From the Hindi original by A'c. Parmeshvarananda Avadhu'ta

### Preface

Shrii A'nandamu'rtijii (Ba'ba') delivered a series of discourses in in Valencia (Spain) during His visit in the year 1979. Through these discourses He articulated the already pronounced fundamental tenets of A'nanda Ma'rga. His thesis clearly affirmed the unity of humanity as one & indivisible whole. The basic tenets of A'nanda Ma'rga have been laid on the foundation of the cardinal human values with a universal outlook.

As the present world scenario is surveyed one finds that humanity is fraught with innumerable problems. Despite its advent on to this earth more than a million years ago, human beings have not learned to build a solid foundation for a healthy universal Human Society. This is precisely the reason why A'nanda Ma'rga realizing the basic needs of the humanity has offered a rational solution to the socio-economic problems of the world. Shrii A'nanda Murtijii has repeatedly affirmed that A'nanda ma'rga is the path of the human race. The greatest excellence of this ma'rga, according to Him is that it has been propounded according to the physical and psychological requirements of human beings. The enunciation of his Progressive Utilization Theory (PROUT) offers the panacea for all of humanities problems. PROUT believes in rebuilding the structure of Human Society on the foundation of mutual love and morality. It is the responsibility of the society to ensure that nobody is forced to resort to immoral means for their survival. All of humanities essential requirements viz. food, clothing, dwelling place, medical care & education must be guaranteed by the society. Every person should get complete freedom in the intellectual sphere. All human beings must be free to express themselves in their own mother toungue & practice their system of worship. All must have freedom to march on the path leading to fullest expression of their consciousness through self realization.

It is A'nanda Ma'rga's considered view that a universal human society on our planet can be built only on the foundation of a non-dogmatic cosmic ideology and a sound socio-economic philosophy. It is common knowledge that a hungry person cannot contemplate on a subtle idea what to say of the highest purpose of its existence. It is a well researched fact that people are forced to resort to immoral means only when they are unable to secure their basic needs necessary for their survival. That is why

A'nandamu'rtiji understanding the predicament of the humanity has given a comprehensive ideology. He has gone further and worked towards creating a well thought out strategy for its practical realization.

A'c. Parmeshvara'nanda Avadhu'ta the author of the book under review has unambiguously articulated the need of implementing a holistic ideal in the A'nanda Ma'rga organization as also the society. Somehow in today's perspective implementation of Prout has taken a back seat. The writer has given a fine example of the reality affecting the society quoting from the discourse of A'nandamu'rtijii and drawn our attention in the last chapters of his book. It says that capitalism makes a person a beggar while communism turns it into a brute.

The many references and contexts in the book clearly reveal the urgent need to establish cardinal human values based on Neo-humanism both within and outside A'nanda Ma'rga. Neo-humanism is such a practical philosophy enunciated by A'nandamu'rtijii which encompasses the entire creation. Neo-humanism includes the whole of the eco-system e.g. living world viz. animal kingdom, the plant life & humanity and the non-living world viz. mountains, deserts, rivers, lakes, earth & soil and the planetary environment that surrounds us and protects us from the lethal rays of the sun & the bombardment of the meteors & asteroids. Neo-humanism is such a blessing of Ba'ba' A'nandamu'rtijii that paves the way to the realization of the mission of A'nanda Ma'rga and Prout. The writer has variously focused on the gardening programme and preservation of the plant gene pool by A'nandamu'rtijii giving its very vivid description emphasizing the importance accorded to the subject by Ba'ba'.

Realizing the goal of cosmic brotherhood will necessarily call for the formation of a world government and a universal human society (a common hearth & a common kitchen and one human society). It appears quite naturally a utopian ideal, especially when we constantly encounter divisive tendencies among people at large and distrust among the nations. But the manner in which the writer has so very succinctly introduced the bright side of the divinity of A'nandamurtijii, it appears to be absolutely possible. Through so many demonstrations performed by Him, the writer has clearly elucidated that A'nandamu'rtijii possessed all of the vibhutis (extraordinary spiritual powers). He utilized these spiritual powers for the good of humanity. The book is littered with examples through which He utilized these powers to rejuvenate new life in the writer, saved him from rare deadly diseases and thus making the impossible possible. He convinced the writer of His divinity. This is reflected in the psychology of the writer's mind and is evident in both the writer's struggle in life and his constant self surrender at the lotus feet of his spiritual master.

"Those of you who want to be real human beings, must continue your sádhaná [spiritual practice] thus strengthening your inner life and strive ceaselessly for God-realization,

and simultaneously you must ensure that no irrational, undesirable or inhuman theory unduly influences the collective psychology of the human race. You must be ever-vigilant in this regard. That is why I told you to be vocal against all sorts of injustice otherwise your mission will remain unfulfilled.” (ref. Ba’ba’s speech on ‘Exploitation & pseudo-culture)

A’nanda Ma’rga is a revolutionary way of life. It imbibes in itself the aim of crushing all forms of narrow ideas and divisive tendencies. Those goody-goody persons, whether they are family persons or renunciates, who like to take the path of evolutionary progress will not be successful in bring about revolutionary changes for the allround benevolence of society.

The writer has raised the issue of who is superior- the family person or the monk, in the first chapter itself and concluded that the true measure of superiority is the sincerity with which one lives up to the ideals of their respective lives.this is worthy of praise espcially when he himself is a sanyasin. Examples of powdering down false pride and arrogance can be found at many places in the book. It is true that the whole time workers received practical training in all walks of life from Ba’ba’ more than the family persons perhaps, because the family persons could not devote so much time. Even then there is ample provision for the family persons to sub-serve the organization as tattvikas, a’ca’ryas, & purodhas as also through PKB (Pu’rn’aka’lika Bandhu), SKB (Svalpaka’lika Bandhu) and as members of ACB (Advisory Committee & Boards), BPs/Ups, LFTs. Devotion to the Guru and the missionary ideology enables them to work together in unison like the two wheels of the chariot and thus fulfill their individual & collective mission. The examples of sincerity on the part of the writer given in the book inspire a new direction to our organization.

There is no denying the fact that A’nanda Ma’rga is a flawless philosophy & a spiritual way of life for the entire human race.

A’c. Pratapaditya, Betiah Hata,

Gorakhpur, Year 2010.

### First Chapter

#### My Early days after initiation

Kabir das – the noted Indian mystic-poet says:

> If pilgrimage to holy places yields one fruit of knowledge;

> Then the meeting with saints yields two;
>
> Fortunate is the one who has found the Sadguru;
>
> That is the surest way to realize the Cosmic Consciousness.

This is the English rendering of one out of the many couplets (originally composed in a blended language of Avadhi & Bhojapuri) expressing saint Kabir Das's deep spiritual realizations. His surmise is quite clear that the supreme goal can be attained only with the grace of the Sadguru.

Kabir Das illustrates the manner by which the Sadguru moulds the disciples:

Guru is like a potter and the disciple like a pot. The potter keeps tinkering the pot in the process of moulding it until the pot develops the desired form. The potter ensures that the pot is well supported inside while the outer body is hammered to turn it into the desired shape & strength. After this the pot, once formed, is burnt in the fire to harden it. Once hardened the pot is now ready for sale. The potter then displays the attributes of the pot to the customers striking it with his fingers to produce the right beats characteristic of a strong pot.

Likewise the Sadguru picks up a disciple and transforms its personality in the fire of Sa'dhana' (spiritual practice). The sa'dhaka (spiritual aspirant) attains magnanimity of mind by the grace of the sadguru in the hearth of sacrifice and non-attachment. Eventually one attains freedom from the cycle of birth and death after merging into the eternally blissful form of the Sadguru.

I was fortunate to have been picked up by the Sadguru almost unconsciously. It must been His inscrutable grace. I had no noble deeds to my credit to be a recipient of His grace. Yet, He gave me a place at the altar of His lotus feet by His causeless compassion.

Diks'a' (initiation) is the pre-requisite for progress in spirituality. In & through this process the Sadguru opens the door to Absolute Knowledge. That leads to oneness with Cosmic Consciousness. The process of initiation opens up the path & simultaneously illuminates it.

From my childhood I had the desire to attain God realization. This inspired me to read good books. I made sure not to miss any opportunity that came my way for satsaunga (company of elevated beings). I used to remain unwell and had many issues related to my health since my early childhood. All this did not deter me from pursuing my goal. My craving to meet the Lord of my life was unstoppable. Many a times overwhelmed by sentiments, I would cry for Him. It was under these circumstances while studying in high

school that I came in contact with my a’ca’rya. My a’ca’ryajii was a teacher in the same school. He had a charming disposition and a hypnotic voice and an attractive personality. In the regular classes which I attended with him, he would occasionally speak about spirituality and Yoga sa’dhana’. Inspired by his talks, one day I sought appointment with him and got initiated.

During the process of my initiation, while being seated in his presence, as he meditated and after a while opened his eyes to say that I was fortunate to have got Parama Purus’a himself as my Guru in the name & form of the A’nandamu’rtijii. He further added that A’nandamu’rtijii combines in himself the attributes of Lord Shiva and Lord Krs’na. It was to the Godhood of Shiva & Krs’na that I had natural attraction from my childhood. His utterances immediately caught my attention and created a powerful impact on my mind. I felt instantly elated on hearing his words. I thought for myself that I was fortunate that the Lord himself has accepted me as His disciple.

Following my initiation as I continued my regular practice of sa’dhana’, I started experiencing bliss and having many spiritual experiences. Then suddenly one fine morning to my surprise, I found all my health problems, with which I was suffering since early childhood, vanished, as if, in thin air. Simultaneously I gained self-confidence and became courageous enough to face whatever situation that may come my way. These qualities I had lacked as a young boy. Continuing my practice of sa’dhana’ I started experiencing that I was floating in the ocean of bliss. In this state I would behold the hypnotic spell of Bal-Krs’na (krs’na as a child) and would often lose consciousness mentally listening to the sweet melody of Krs’na’s flute. Occasionally I would visualize Krs’na in everything, every leaf of trees around me. The bliss experienced on hearing Krs’na’s flute was inexplicable.

Slowly I started devoting more & more time for sa’dhana’. I would sit meditating for long hours. I used have occasional spiritual experience. I also started involving in intensive praca’ra of A’nanda Ma’rga. Simultaneously I would never miss any opportunity to be in the company of my a’ca’rya.

Now onwards it became a normal routine for me to convince my class mates about the benefits of Sa’dhana’ and A’nanda Ma’rga way of life. In this process, every person known to me got initiated and started practicing sa’dhana’. Seeing my spiritual progress my a’ca’ryajii also developed a liking for me. He would always inspire me to practice more sa’dhana’. On many occasions I used to meditate with him.

Slowly the number of new sa’dhaka’s started swelling and regular Dharma Cakra was started in a Dharmasha’la’ close to A’ca’ryajii’s residence. The attendance in the Dharma Cakra used to be around 50 persons. It was the most sought after occasion for me, since it would be very inspiring & devotional.

My A’ca’ryajii was also a Ka’pa’lika and many times I would accompany him to cremation grounds during his night Sa’dhana’. It used to be a very blissful experience for me. Whenever I would visit my village, we would go to the cremation ground, usually accompanied by a few friends, we used to practice sa’dhana’ in the night. Fear of ghosts & spirits started diminishing in my mind. As I progressed in Sa’dhana’ my love for His mission increased.

As days passed my personality underwent a noticeable change for better. My friends, my family members and village folks were amazed seeing rapid changes in my conduct. My food habit became vegetarian without onion & garlic. Seeing this my family members also resolved to change the food habits and now all of them started eating vegetarian food without onion & garlic. In my studies also there was a marked improvement. As a result of regular sa’dhana’ my concentration & memory improved. I would now spend less time for studies and my result in the examination used to be much better. I would qualify in first or the second rank in my school.

### My first DMC

Days were passing happily and then one fine morning my A’ca’rya Narendra Dada disclosed the good news that Ba’ba’ was visiting Raipur for the DMC. All margiis were jubilant hearing the good news. This inspired us further and many more of my school mates got initiated. On the appointed day I reached Raipur from my village along with my margii friends. It was the month of December 1960, DMC was held in a Dharmasha’la’. Ba’ba’ arrived by train from Tatanagar. A’c. Nityananda (who later became the first Avadhu’ta of A’nanda Ma’rga by the name Satya’nanda), A’c. Kshitij & Ma’nas da’da’ (Ba’ba’s younger brother accompanied Him). A tumultuous reception awaited Ba’ba’ at the railway station of Raipur. He was received amidst deafening slogans of A’nanda Marga Amar Hai. Manava Manava Ek Hain, Vishva bandhutva ka’yam ho, so on & so forth. The high pitch of devotional fervor of Ma’rgiis was to be seen to be believed.

I had some doubts on seeing Ba’ba’ dressed like a gentleman in white Kurta – Dhoti. I could not imagine that somebody, looking so human, be God? I mentally questioned whether He can be the personification of Lord Shiva & Krs’na. A’caryaji must be lying that Gurudeva was God. He had not told me that Gurudeva was a family person and that He was used to putting such an ordinary attire. I had never seen His photograph before actually seeing Him in person. I had thought that my Guru must be a fierce looking long bearded Hima’layan Yogi. With this doubt in my mind I had my first darshan of Baba’ with my old village friends. I liked His talk, but could not accept Him as the embodiment of Shiva & Krs’na. At that point of time I was still an immature person.

Next day in the evening of DMC (Dharma Maha’ Cakra) I had a strange experience. We were waiting for Ba’ba’s arrival with about 250 margiis in the hall. Most of them were lost

in the devotion while they sang bhajan in chorus. The mood in the hall was mystical. The sentiments have been aroused. The devotees were in tears. Occasionally one could hear loud outburst of hunka’r which was frightening and caused my body to shiver. As I entered the hall I felt a strange sensation overtaking me. After a while when I got seated I visualed white effulgence pervading the environment of the hall. I found myself engulfed by effulgent light from all over. I shut my eyes effortlessly. When I opened my eyes lo! I was beholding Ba’ba’ who had taken seat on the dais. From dais where Ba’ba’ was seated I saw Ba’la-krs’na emerging out of the white effulgence from Him. This form of Ba’la-krs’na was atonce incredible and I started feeling dizziness as if I was intoxicated. Visualizing the magnificient form of Krs’na at the same time listening to hypnotizing & sweet melody His divine flute I lost sense. About the same time I also started beholding the form of shiva which emerged from the same effulgent light.

The veil of my doubts and confusion about the divinity of Ba’ba’ was atonce lifted. By His grace I was able to visualize the divine forms of Krs’na and Shiva. I had an instant realization that Ba’ba’ was the combined form of both. Visualising His majestic form I felt blessed.

Slowly devotion to the lotus feet of Ba’ba’ dawned on my mind. As a result there was a noticeable progress in my Sa’dhana’. My love and craving for Ba’ba’ increased to great extent. Simultaneously my yearning for his His darshan and personal meeting also increased.

During the DMC the vision of Baba transforming Himself into the childhood form of krs’na had a mesmerizing effect on my mind. Later whenever or wherever there used to be any Tattva Sabha (a small form of ASD Camp) or assembly of margiis I would make sure I participated in those events. Each of these programmes left an indelible mark on my mind. They inspired me to dedicate myself for intense sa’dhana’. I attended five such camps. It was here that I came in contact with Dada Vandanananda, Dada Acinta’nanda, dada Sha’nta’nanda & Dada Ashesha’nanda. Slowly we came closer to each other.
Later I also attended DMCs at Raipur in 1962 & again in 1964. DMC in Jabalpur was in the year 1963. Ba’ba’s Pravacana (addresses) and the vibrations emanating from His varabhaya in those DMCs had a profound effect on me. As a result my devotion for Him and my dedication at His lotus feet started soaring high. I always craved to be in His company & to do His work. To the extent my attraction for Him increased in the same proportion my attachment for the world reduced. After returning from Jabalpur DMC my desire to renounce my life for His mission became firm.

I along with 25 of my school mates went to attend the Jabalpur DMC in the awe inspiring personality of my A’ca’rya and in the affectionate care of A’c. Ramlal Dani. It was in this DMC that A’c. Keshava became Sha’nta’nanda Avadhu’ta, and 16 of my friends decided to dedicate their lives for His mission. I also wanted to dedicate but I

was refused since I was still not 18 years old. In this DMC Ac. Arun who later became A’c. Svarupa’nanda Avadhuta was Ba’ba’s attending secretary.

I returned back home with other sa’dhakas in a unhappy and disappointed disposition. But my craving for Ba’ba’ was now burning and the thought of those sweet moments spent in His presence never left my mind. Once again in the year 1964 I got another opportunity to have an audience with Ba’ba’ during Raipur DMC. I was totally perplexed & did not know how to prepare myself at the prospect of a personal contact with Him.

After returning from the DMC function I would ceaselessly think of Him & wished to dedicate my life for His mission. The intensity of my detachment from the world kept on increasing. There was a constant mental clash due to the attraction for my family members and simultaneous detachment to take to the life of a monk. In the end my desire to be a monk always used to win. I had almost totally relinquished my attachment to my family.

Finally in the beginning of 1965, suddenly attracted by His divine inspiration I left my home to meet my a’ca’rya Narendra Dada and two other a’ca’ryas of Raipur viz. Shrii Ramlal Dani & Shrii Kailash Bhalla. I enjoyed their unconditional love & inspiration. I stayed in their company for a few days. From there I went to Patna to attend the DMC. On meeting Ba’ba’ there I offered myself for His mission. That DMC was unique in the sense that a group of 36 margiis decided to be monks of A’nanda Ma’rga and proceeded for training to Ranchi. It was this group which produced many a’caryas viz. sarvashrii Jagdishvarananda, Vitmoha’nanda, Paresha’nanda & Shanta’nanda. Until such time there was no formal training centre. After spending a few days in Ranchi we came to Patna and from there proceeded to Sigra training centre in Va’ra’nasi. From there after a month without qualifying as an a’ca’rya I was posted as the secretary of PFI (Progressive Federation of India) in Patna.

### Patna DMC – May 23, 1965, Venue: Senate Hall of Patna University

I came to Patna to attend A’nanda Pu’rnima’ DMC held on 23rd May 1965. That was to be the biggest DMC of my life. Before that I had attended the DMCs organized locally. A’nanda Pu’rnima’ DMC in Patna was attended by more than ten thousand margiis. The environment there was intoxicating. It was here that I witnessed a unique confluence of Bhajan–Kiirtan & awesome devotional vibrations. Scores of devotees were dancing there inside the hall to the tune of Bhajans. Once Ba’ba’ arrived at the dais the environment turned into an uncontrollable frenzy of mystic love. The devotees' cry was unstoppable & many were relaying Humka’ra. Many devotees were dancing unmindful of the surrounding and crying out His name lost in divine ecstasy. I was deeply moved by the feelings of love for Him. It was here that I saw many spiritually elevated sa’dhakas. In the spell of this otherworldly spiritual environment I got to visualizing the most enchanting form of Ba’ba’. Right there I made a firm resolve to renounce my life for His mission. This DMC proved to be both historical and the most significant event of my life. I took the vow to be a monk of the order of A’nanda ma’rga.

### Training to propagate margii ideals in the field – Saharsa, Purnia & Araria in the year 1965

In those early days of A’nanda Ma’rga Praca’raka Samgha it was a rule that every whole time worker must undertake field training as an apprentice of either a senior worker or a departmental supervisor or some senior family a’ca’rya. Since it was my first posting I was assigned to Shrii Balramajii who was a Prout volunteer for pracar training for a period of 25 days. He took me to Saharsa, Murliganj, Navagachiya’, Pu’rnia, Araria & to their farthest Sikat’i block and many villages which are situated on the border of Nepal. I was absolutely new and unaware of the places I visited. I started learning the art of delivering lecture among the village people. I used to see that Balramjii was very adept at using the couplets from Kabir, Surdas, Nanak, Tulsidas giving references from the stories of Ramayana and Mahabharata interspersed with the shlokas of Giita’ to convince all sections of people. He would propagate the margii philosophy & way of life and Prout’s socio-economic ideas to the common people in his characteristic smart way.

In this process I came in contact with many senior ma’rgiis. I was beset by their simplicity and implicit devotion for Ba’ba’. They told me many stories of their experiences with Ba’ba’. Speaking to them and working with them increasingly boosted my confidence to deal with the new environment.

I travelled to the villages of the Sikat’a block of Araria district with Balramjii. There were margiis in each and every village and they were all very much devoted to Ba’ba’. It was a rainy season. There was no means of travel in those villages. We would usually start for the villages early morning after breakfast. We had to walk through the paddy fields through the raised boundaries of the plots. We kept traveling throughout the day for 8-10 hours. I used to get tired since I was not accustomed to walking long distances. Typically the villagers on enquiry about the distance of any village will usually respond by saying that the place was close by. But usually it would take much more than that and we would keep walking for hours to reach there. In this way we used to traverse a long distance on foot walking for more than 10 hours a day.

In those days Margii units were active in more than 15 units of Sikat’I block. There used to be Dharma Cakra once every week in each of those units. There were hundreds of margiis in that block and it was easier for us to propagate A’nanda Ma’rga and Prout. It was a pleasant experience. In this way we visited the village of A’c. Vishveshvarjii & learnt many techniques of pracar from him. There I met many margii enthusiasts. This place was on the border of Nepal. The headman of a village was a A’nanda Ma’rgii. He took me to a town of Nepal. This was my first visit to Nepal. After the field training I returned back to Patna. In this way some 25 days were spent as an apprentice.

### A’ca’rya Training in Jamalpur

After working for a few months in the PFI office in Patna I was asked to undertake a’ca’rya training in Jamalpur. In those days Abheda’nanda Avadhu’ta was the personal secretary of Ba’ba’. He was soft spoken and a gentle person. He was also an elevated sa’dhaka, There were only 6 trainees including me. A’cara Dashratha was our trainer. We were fortunate to enjoy Ba’ba’s darshan every day both in the morning & evening. It was indeed a great opportunity that Ba’ba’ granted me to undergo training in His presence there.

I was fortunate that Ba’ba’ used to bless me with personal contact almost every day before going to His Railway office in the morning. It was here that he gave me many hints about Sa’dhana’ and service. This contact gave me ample opportunity to massage His body. Occasionally I would receive His special love & affection and get to sit on on His lap. Ba’ba’s love was unconditional, divine, spontaneous and blissful. Seeing this other trainees at time used to be jealous of me. Everybody had a natural craving for His close company. But it was out of my control. A’ca’rya Abhedananda avadhu’ta also at times used to tease me for the special treatment being meted out to me.

On the last day of my training in the noon A’ca’rya Dasharath was supposed to conduct our examination. That day Ba’ba’ came to the Ja’grti and asked Abheda’nandajii to call me in His room. I entered His room as directed by Abhedanandajii and prostrated before Ba’ba’. I looked at the charming form of Ba’ba’. He was seated on the cot and the room was filled with divine effulgence. I was beholding that most spectacular sight and found myself seized by a blissful feeling. While I was stuck their beholding His divine form, my attention got diverted on hearing his sweet voice. He addressed me by calling me a’ca’rya Rsikesh and asked for my welfare. Responding to his query I said that I have yet to qualify as an a’ca’rya as my examination was scheduled this noon. Ba’ba’ parried lovingly to my childlike response and said that you have qualified as an a’ca’rya. I thought that when He has called me so then I have most certainly become an a’ca’rya.

After a while Ba’ba’ said that today He was very much pleased with me. Then He said that all of your wishes of name, fame and of occult powers could be yours by mere asking. Hearing these sweet & pleasing words I was over taken by a blissful feeling and suddenly jumped up to be seated on His lap. Sheltered on His lap I thus spoke to him that I have no other wish left to be fulfilled when I am blessed to be seated on your lap. But He repeatedly sought to bless me by fulfilling any of my wishes. But I was drifting in bliss and my mind was without any desire and I did not know what to ask from him. All of a sudden a thought crossed my mind. I looked forward to his approval of my wish. He said He was duty bound to grant my wish. Then I asked him that I wish to be devoted to his lotus feet for all time to come and that I would like to accompany Him to whichever planet or star He might visit according to His divine plan. I wanted to be always

dedicated to his mission in the service of His creation. Revered Ba’ba’ blessed me and said Tatha’stu (let your wish be fulfilled). Hearing His assurance I was lost consciousness and drifted in the ocean of bliss. Soon after Ba’ba’ left the room and proceeded towards his office.

Following this incidence I remained abnormal for many days. I used to remain lost in His thought and dhya’na and used to float in the sea of bliss. This condition lasted for quite a few months.

At times I used to be tired of His Dhya’na but it was overpowering. This was, ofcourse, happening by His grace. I did enjoy this state of Dhya’na. I now understand how lucky I was that the difficult to attain Dhya’na had become so easy for me. Some thing which even the great yogis in the days of yore could achieve with much difficulty. Such grace made an ordinary mortal like me to effortlessly do dhya’na. I continued to enjoy this experience for many months. I realized that anything was possible by His grace. The devote can bind Him with the rope of his unreserved love and He, in turn, allows himself to be caught of His own accord.

Training in the implementation of Prout

Venue: Delhi, December, 1965.

My first posting was in PROUT department. According to the then prevailing organizational system, workers posted in a particular department were subjected to intensive training in philosophy and extensive training in the working of that department. Accordingly I was deputed to Delhi to get trained under the supervision of Dada Rangana’tha’nanda. Dadajii was the Chief Training Secretary in PROUT department. He used to conduct regular classes in the philosophy and practice of PROUT for the available trainees. The training was quite exhaustive. It considerably improved my self confidence. I was equipped with necessary tools to propagate PROUT in public. This was a very satisfying experience. I also met Dada Dhiira’nanda. He was the editor of English PROUT. He was a very gentle person. His worked very peacefully. He also, occasionally, took our classes.

After the training there I was deputed with another trainee WT to a nearby town for gaining practical experience. We started our praca’ra work in a village of Mahrauli with the help of a margii brother of the area. Our first contact was with a school Head Master. He helped us to get an opportunity to address a meeting in the high school. From there we proceeded to a remote village in Gurgaon. There we met the village Head Person and addressed meetings to the village folk. Through these meetings we introduced PROUT and A’nanda Ma’rga to the village community. Villagers regarded us as Brahmaca’ris and offered milk and sweet curd water and fed us good food.

Thus continuing with praca’ra from village to village we came to the district headquarters of Gurgaon. It was the festival of Diipavali on that day. We got accommodated in a Dharmasha’la’ but we had no money to buy our food. There was nobody known to us in the town and we had not developed the habit of asking alms. The day was thus spent without food. I and my colleague Priyabrata Brahmca’ri talked of the strange coincidence of our remaining without food on the festive occasion of Diipavali. Diipa’vali in India is the most important and happy festival celebrated with lots of fun, food and fireworks. Children love this festival the most. The thought Ba’ba’ was the primal thought in our mind. We thought He was playing with our minds. With this thought we were strolling on the road. On the way some person lovingly bid namaskar to both of us. It did not end there. He further requested us to accompany him to his village. We were surprised at the turn of event. In his village we had an excellent meeting with villagers. We were able to nicely introduce A’nanda Ma’rga to them.

After the meeting the family of our host surrounded us and lovingly served us all kinds of delicacies. We were more than satisfied as He had silently arranged all this taking care of our needs. When we were to return from that village the host gave us sufficient money which was enough to take care of our needs on the way till we reach our office. We wept at his grace. Following this I returned back to Patna.

Ba’ba’ releases waves of bliss during New Year DMC at Muzaffarpur,

Venue: T’unaki Sa’o Dharmasha’la’, December- January, 1965/66

It was here in the hallowed land of Muzaffarpur that I was blessed to receive His contact. It happened on two occasions during 1966. My first contact took place in March ’65 during the Vasantotsava (Holi festival) DMC. I entered His room with one whole timer. At that moment I was harbouring an intense desire to be a worker of His mission. Ba’ba’ lovingly enquired from both of us about our desire? Both of us silently looked at Him and did not speak a word.

In the New year DMC I was inspired watching Ba’ba’ distributing His love freely to ma’rgiis on the occasion of Holi festival. The margiis enjoyed playing A’biira-Gula’la amongst themselves. I also remember to have tasted the native Pua’ – a popular sweet dish of Bihar prepared on this occasion.

My second contact with Ba’ba’ took place in Tunki Sa’o Dharmasha’la’. To me it was a very important & a historic personal contact. I was then a new worker posted in north Bihar. I was absolutely inexperienced in Dharma Pracar. Many senior and devoted ma’rgiis lived in this region. While conversing with them I would often develop a sense of inferiority complex. Rev. Ba’ba’ himself introduced me to A’c.Sakaldeva & A’c.Gangasharanjii in His room. He told them that I was a young worker and that He

was leaving me in their care. That they should help me as needed. Since then I continued to receive their loving affection and support. I realized this was how He took care of his workers and never left any one uncared.

After a while Ba’ba’ again called me in His room. Those moments were really rare. He lifted the veil of Ma’ya’ and filled my heart with His divine love. By His grace from that moment onwards I started experiencing bliss during sa’dhana. The intensity of my attraction for Him went on increasing as days passed. That is why my meeting with Him proved to be both important & historical for my spiritual awakening.

Much later once Ba’ba’ asked me where did you had spiritual realization for the first time. I had replied it was in T’unaki Sao Dharmasha’la’, in Muzaffarpur. Ba’ba’ then observed that He still remembered that moment and the place. As He was recollecting that incidence I lost myself in ecstasy.

Meeting Ka’lika’nandajii

Place: Jamalpur, Year 1965

I was first posted in Patna as office secretary of PFI. This was a blessing in disguise. I would quite often find an opportunity on one pretext or the other to visit Jamalpur and enjoy darshan of Ba’ba’. On one such visit to Jamalpur, I got an opportunity to go on field walk with Him with two other ma’rgiis.

We enjoyed walking with Ba’ba’. On the way He kept talking and gave us many blissful instructions until we reached the tiger’s grave. At the tiger’s grave Ba’ba’ took His seat and three of us started massaging His feet. When we were thus engaged in His service, I noticed that some figure was materializing near the huge Tamarind tree which stood close by. Soon after Ba’ba’ told us that ka’lika’nanda is here to seek my directions on some important matter. He, then, proceeded towards the Tamarind tree. I was, both, a little afraid at the same time inquisitive about kalika’nandajii. I had an inner desire to meet him in person. After a while Ba’ba’ returned and inquired, if we would want to meet Ka’lika’nanda. We did not say anything, but Ba’ba’ asked us to come along with Him. We followed Him as instructed. When we all reached near the tree Ba’ba’ pointed towards Ka’lika’nandajii and told us that he is your elder brother and that you should pay your respects to him. We did pran’a’m to him. He was looking very ferocious. We were in awe seeing him so close. No sooner we had his audience he vanished in thin air. This is how Ba’ba’ graced us with the opportunity to see Ka’lika’nandajii in his physical form.

Powdering down the pride of a wrestler

Place: Jamalpur, 1965

I was a new worker and had come to Jamalpur for Ba’ba’s darshan. As I reached the town I developed a blissful feeling. The same day a wrestler from somewhere in the vicinity of Jamalpur had also come there for Guru darshana after his initiation. He had heard from his ma’rgii friends that during personal contact Ba’ba’ would usually punish His disciples for their wrong past actions. This fear of punishment was constantly lurking in his mind. It is generally believed that wrestlers are simpletons but once they accept something they remain devoted to that ideal or person. He got the opportunity of personal contact with Ba’ba’. He thought for a while that if Ba’ba’ were to try to physically punish him he would snatch the stick from His hand since he considered himself a stronger person than Him.

He entered Ba’ba’s room and prostrated before Him as advised by his a’ca’rya. As he got up he found Ba’ba’ in a Raodra ru’pa (ferocious form). His body looked like a mound of hill and his hands & feet also appeared to him as huge filling the entire room. Seeing Him like this he was overcome by fear and started trembling. Internally he repented for having thought the way he did and apologized. He prayed and beseeched Him to assume His normal form (kalyansundaram ru’pa).

As he thought so He became, sweet and divine. In a very natural manner Ba’ba’ scolded him for his past misdeeds. Then He took oath from him to be an ideal person and took him in His bosom. He was atonce taken aback in the way Ba’ba’ lifted his huge body in His lap. He thought for a while that Ba’ba’ must be experiencing discomfort by letting him sit on His lap. All of a sudden he was overcome by the unexpected surge of emotions and started crying like a child. Now a new man was now born out of him.

### Why does the mind fail to concentrate in meditation?

Place: Jamalpur Jagrti, 1965

Ba’ba’ was surrounded by His devotees in the Jagrti at Jamalpur. Those present were enjoying the nectar of His sweet words and His divinely exquisite & charming persona. An environment of heavenly charm was existed in the A’shrama. When Ba’ba’ was finished with His discourse. A devotee stood up and asked Him. Why is that sometimes we enjoy our meditation while at other time we our unable to concentrate and our mind gets disturbed and we do not like to meditate.

Ba’ba’ responded & said that it is quite natural to be unable to concentrate the mind during sa’dhana’. Human life is the result of evolution through many past lives. The reactions of the unserved actions performed in the past lives remain accumulated in the subconscious mind of the individual. These reactions in potentiality influence our mind and disquieten our mind. This is very natural. Spiritual aspirant need not get disturbed by this situation. You must be regular in sa’dhana’. Do not be unduly concerned about

whether or not your mind gets concentrated or not. When you perform sa’dhana’ keeping your goal fixed on Param Purusa you will surely move forward. Slowly your mind will get concentrated and you will experience bliss in sa’dhana’.

Sa’dhana’ is the result of a struggle both internal and external. A sa’dhaka is verily a soldier; pricks of the thorn on the difficult path of sa’dhana’ signify progress. The result of sa’dhana’ is to be offered to Parama Purus’a. Sincerity in sa’dhana’ is the surest means of attracting His grace which alone leads a sa’dhaka to god realization.

### Who is great - a person who has renounced his worldly life - a monk/a nun v/s a family person?

In the early days of A’nanda ma’rga the organization was quite small. The general secretary of the ma’rga used to carry the central office in his backpack. Ba’ba’ was also easily available to the devotees. He would maintain a sweet personal relationship with all His devotees. He was not only their is’t’a (Supreme goal of life) but also their Guru & guardian of the family. Devotees used to get their personal and family difficulties resolved directly from Him.

Once during the general darshana in Jamalpur Ja’grti a sa’dhaka raised a question. Who is great—a family person or a renunciate? Ba’ba’ posed the same question to the devotees present. They remained undecided. Somebody talked in favour of family persons and others in favour of renunciate monks & nuns.

Finally Ba’ba’ started narrating a story.

Once there lived a moralist and a brave king. He was besieged by one knotty problem which he could not resolve. The question was—who is superior, the renunciate or the family man? He made an announcement in his kingdom that anyone who will give a logical answer to this question will get half of his kingdom and his daughter as his wife. If he failed to convince, then he will be jailed and his nose will be severed. Many persons unsuccessfully tried but were jailed and their nose severed. This trend continued for some time. After some time a monk came forward to give a plausible answer to the king’s question. He had no desire for kingdom. To find an answer he suggested to the king that we will have to move like a monk to find a logical answer to his question. The king was very keen, hence he conceded to the monk’s suggestion and after handing over his kingdom to his son and the ministers he proceeded with the monk to resolve his mental problem.

They moved around to different places. Finally they arrived into a new kingdom. The king there had organized the svayamvara (marriage) of his daughter and to commemorate the occasion invited all the eligible bachelor kings & princes known to him. The assembly not only consisted of the prospective princes but other commoners

and well wishers including monks who were present there to bless the couple. The princess moved around to select his bride groom. She was, however, not impressed with the assembled kings and princes. In desperation she moved away towards others who were also present there and was attracted towards a young monk whose face reflected the charm of his spiritual attainment. No sooner the princess after being impressed of the monk moved with the bridal garland towards him. Seeing her movements and reflecting her intentions the monk realized that he may be drawn into a relationship which he did not want. Therefore he decided to leave the noble assembly. The princess who was charmed by the monk followed him. The monk finding no other alternative ran away into the woods. The princess also followed him there. Meanwhile the other monk and the king in the garb of a Sadhu followed them. The young monk ran faster and disappeared. The princess was unable to locate him also lost her way in the forest. She got tired and after the dusk sat down under a tree. It was cold winter night. The place was desolate and did not know what to do. She started crying. The other monks who had followed her arrived there and tried to console her while assuring her that it would be safer to rest here and next day morning they would escort her to her father’s kingdom. Finding no other alternative she conceded to their request to take rest under the tree with the two monks.

The tree had a nest of a bird in which a male and a female bird with their young used to live. These birds observed the condition of the three guest who had taken shelter under the tree. They were without adequate clothes to cover them for the cold night and were not having any food to eat. They thought of helping them from their precarious condition. Sitting on a branch of the tree they located a place where fire was burning in the forest. They brought the fire from there and dropped it before the guest who immediately arranged some dry woods and made a fire place to protect them from the cold. Next the birds thought of arranging their food. The male bird decided to offer himself as food. He thought of falling into the fire. The female bird thought likewise. She thought that the male bird would be insufficient as their food she also decided to fall into the fire. Eventually the young bird also thought likewise and decided to offer itself. Thus one after the other the three birds fell into the fire & offered themselves as food for the guests.

The monk pointing to the king in the garb of a sadhu asked if he had understood who was really great – a monk or a family man. The monk later explained that neither is superior to the other. It is only the one who sincerely attends to one’s duty is superior. The family of the three birds proved themselves to be superior by observing their duty and the sanyasin who ran away not getting lured by the worldly possessions of the kingdom and the princess was also equally great. It is the sincere performance of one’s duty which makes one superior. It is not important whether one is a sanyasi or a family

person. The king was amply satisfied and returned back to his kingdom happily and released all his prisoners.

Strange ways of a devotee – Shrii Biharilaljii of Patna, year 1965

I was then posted as a office secretary in the PFI Department. I was a new worker. We were informed that Ba'ba' was going to visit Patna. Biharilaljii was the district secretary (now Bhukti Pradha'na) of Patna. He was taking care of Ba'ba's personal needs e.g. ba'ba's lodging, food, & other personal requirements. He was a devotee of ba'ba' and he used to enjoy serving delicacies to his guests. Now that Ba'ba' himself was his guest he was all bliss. He along with his family members took good care of Ba'ba'. This was my first opportunity to see for myself Ba'ba' enjoying the hospitality of His devotee. For the devotees He was creating a flood of bliss drowning all those who desired to enjoy His physical presence.

After Ba'ba' had finished his lunch Biharilaljii gave a mango (a special sweet variety of Bihar) in Ba'ba's hands for Him to take. Biharilaljii was out of mind. After giving the mango he went on explaining the manner in which the mango was to be taken. Ba'ba' listened in rapt attention to his instructions. I was present there and witnessed the pleasure Biharilaljii's was deriving. Ba'ba' on His part was all praise for his demeanor. He continued to give him due importance for such a simple matter.

My avadhu'ta diiks'a'

Place: Residence of a'c. Rambilas Rai-1966

During my a'ca'rya training in Jamalpur I enjoyed Ba'ba's contact and experienced his grace and compassion almost every day. He constantly pulled my mind I felt His strong attraction. About the same time I developed a strong craving to become a avadhu'ta. I wanted to surrender everything of mine to Him. I understood that a avadhuta was directly committed to the Guru.

In 1966 March on the occasion of Holi I got an opportunity to attend the DMC in Muzaffarpur. I was then posted as office secretary in PFI office of Patna. During the DMC I entered Baba's room along with a worker with a strong desire to become a avadhu'ta. The omniscient Ba'ba' inquired, what do you want from me. What has brought you here? I could not speak anything to Him. He lovingly looked at me and smiled. I was overwhelmed by His grace and came out of His room.

After a few days Ba'ba' came to Patna for DMC and stayed with a'ca'rya Rambilasji who was posted their as a deputy Police superintendent. Ba'ba' called the then GTS a'ca'rya Praka'sha'nanda to give him the list of names of the prospective candidates for avadhu'ta diiks'a'. Da'da' Praka'sha'nanda was not inclined to include my name in the

list. He did not carry a good impression of me. When the list was brought to Ba'ba', Ba'ba' asked him to include my name. I learned this fact from a worker who was a witness in Ba'ba's room when Praka'sha'nanda gave the list of prospective candidates to Ba'ba' for avadhu'ta diiks'a'. I was once again besieged by his affection.

### My posting as a relief organizer

I was then working in north Bihar spreading the message of self-realization and service of created world among the people of Mithila'. This region abounds in many senior margiis and family a'ca'ryas. I was fortunate to listen to many of their mystic and blissful experiences. I enjoyed the company of family a'ca'ryas viz. Sarvashrii Chandranathjii, Nagiinajii, Sakaldevjii & Gangasharanjii. There I heard the painful news of the gruesome murder of our five Dadhicii's on 5th March 1967. On this day five of our dear Da'da's had been brutally killed by Marxists goons.

After a few days of this incidence I was called to report back to Ranchi. Ba'ba' had shifted from A'nanda Nagar to Ranchi following the 5th March incidence. I reached there with the desire to be blessed with His darshan. There I was given a new posting as the Relief Organizer incharge for whole India. I was unprepared for such a onerous responsibility. The whole country was facing a severe drought and hundreds of people, young & old had died of hunger. I wondered how I will organize massive relief needed in such a calamity. Whole of Bihar & eastern Madhya Pradesh and West Bengal were severely affected.

Ba'ba' constituted the Draught Relief Committee with its headquarters at Exhibition Road in the house of Shrii Shashi Rainjanjii. Margii volunteers devoted themselves providing relief in their respective areas. Ananda Ma'rga Drought Relief Committee organized hundreds of free & cheap food stalls, and water supply tents. I was responsible for inspection, control and starting new centres. I was a novice, however Ba'ba' chose an inexperienced person for this important work. To educate me and to build my confidence Ba'ba' would lovingly explain me how I was to organize the relief centers thus always encouraging me to accomplish the task ahead with accelerated speed.

I was supposed to work with Dr. Ramesh who was the Relief Secretary. Ba'ba' would himself prepare my tour program and guide me about the work that I was to carry out in different places. Margiis were inspired and in no time over 500 free and cheap kitchens were started. Thousands of volunteers were involved in this task. One day Ba'ba' instructed me the standard to be followed in selecting the volunteers to take responsibility of the relief operations. The standard was:

1. Beware of someone who is talkative and sentimental

2. One who is of serious disposition, speaks after thinking and is practical can be relied

3. One who is emotional & gets easily assailed in pleasure and pain should not be relied much. On the contrary someone who maintains a balanced disposition in all circumstances should be involved in all important tasks. He/she should be allowed to handle more and more responsibility

4. Volunteers who are honest and devoted should be chosen to take additional responsibility.

I followed the guidelines given by Ba'ba' and fortunately there were more than the required dedicated volunteers who were engaged in relief work and brought laurels to our service work. Everywhere our work was receiving appreciation.

Late Shrii Jaiprakash Narayan was himself involved with the relief work being done by the government. He too, was impressed with the dedication and selfless service of A'nanda Ma'rga volunteers. He appreciated us. For any help he always relied on the sincerity and honesty of our volunteers. We were also helping the government in grain distribution. Impressed by our dedication and honesty the then prime minister Shriimati Indira Gandhi arranged hundreds of tons of grains for distribution amongst the Drought affected population through A'nanda Ma'rga Relief Committee. On our own we took up the task of reaching out to the remote inaccessible regions viz. Sarguja & Bastar areas of erstwhile Madhya Pradesh which were badly affected by the Drought & where none of the NGOs seemed to be interested in going for the service of drought affected people.

To further expand the scope of our relief activities Ba'ba' directed me to nominate a sub-organiser for every diocess and a relief branch organizer for a district. Being new to the job I was baffled with the enormity of the work. Reading my thoughts Ba'ba' himself gave me the names of sincere, active & honest Margiis and A'ca'ryas to be involved in this huge task. I informed all those persons and called them to reach Ranchi for an audience of Ba'ba' further instructions. All of them reached Ranchi on the scheduled date. I was the youngest among them and most inexperienced and I was asked to instruct them. A'ca'rya Narendra Deva who was my own a'ca'rya was made the sub-organiser of Raipur diocess and A'carya Ramlal Dani and A'ca'rya Kailash Bhalla were made the branch organizers of Sarguja and Bastar respectively. I was always hesitated to pass any instructions to these senior a'ca'ryas.

Baba' called all these a'ca'ryas to His room and told them that though this boy pointing to me is much younger and inexperienced than all of you, yet he is my representative and all of you would take his instructions as my own. In no time all my problems were solved. My own inhibitions were overcome. I acted as His medium and He made use of

me to discharge such a gigantic responsibility. I was beset by His style of putting all persons at ease and making them blissfully accept full responsibility and accountability.

I continued my work with these three a'ca'ryas for many months to come. They were inspired persons and carried out the assigned tasks beyond my own expectations. Our relief was being rendered in far off villages. Though these a'ca'ryas were family persons but their dedication to the task and their devotion to Ba'ba' was matchless. In fact they were the sources of inspiration to all of us whole timers involved in the relief work. I enjoyed working with them and had many exciting experiences.

In August 1967 Baba' came to Raipur for DMC and stayed in A'nanda Ma'rga school in Bairan Bazar. I also came to attend the DMC with A'ca'rya A'nada Kishore. I brought a proposal to organize relief in the villages of Sarguja district. These were some of the worst affected villages due to draught. Ba'ba' took the task upon Himself. He resolved the problems involved in the plan. He called the three a'ca'ryas already involved with the relief to take this additional task of organizing relief in Sarguja district.

A'nanda Nagar was also equally affected and our Children's home was also facing acute shortage of food. I was able to organize and deliver 20 quintals of wheat for which Ba'ba' appreciated my action. I enjoyed the posting as the relief organizer, since I got to learn how to organize many volunteers for clearly defined service activity and to keep them inspired to continue the good work for a long time. I was also blessed in the process to receive His grace and enjoy His company.

Following the completion of the massive relief operation I was transferred to A'nanda nagar as office secretary No.2.

### Chapter -2 - A'nanda Nagar

#### Posting as a central office secretary no.2 in A'nanda Nagar

#### Ba'ba' as Sama'ja Guru

This incidence perhaps relates to the year 1968. I was then posted as the office secretary no.2 of General Secretary in A'nanda Nagar. Dada Pran'ava'nandajii was the central office secretary and was stationed at Ranchi where Ba'ba' was staying. The assignment required me to collect the work done report of all workers posted in A'nanda Nagar and then to report back to Ba'ba' in Ranchi every Sunday.

After the incidence of 5^th^ March A'nanda nagar was in shambles. It was almost totally destroyed by the Marxist goons. That is why Ba'ba' was devoting much time and energy in rebuilding A'nanda Nagar. I was also under sever pressure. He did not accept any delays in the progress of A'nanda Nagar.

During one such Sunday reporting Ba’ba’ gave a new target for constructing buildings for Poultry, Piggery, Fishery, Duckery, Dairy Farm and Seri-culture. I found it very difficult to prepare my mind to proceed with the construction of Poultry and Piggery. I thought it was contradictory to the ideal of a sanya’sii. Hence I did not initiate work related to these two activities. In the next reporting with Ba’ba’ He inquired about the progress of those specific tasks. I stood in front of Ba’ba’ like a dumb. Ba’ba’ understood my inner conflict. He clarified and told me that He was not only a Dharma Guru but also a Sama’ja Guru. Being a Samaja Guru He has to support these vocations. They are a part of building up village economy. These are part of essential plans for developing a self sustainable economy. It was then that I understood His concern for the welfare of all.

In His discourses Ba’ba’ has defined the role that is played by the Sama’ja Guru. Sama’ja Guru has to utilize its integral knowledge, intellectual prowess, personal charm and organizing capacity to lead the humanity along the path of all round progress in the three spheres of human existence. Ba’ba’ as Ta’raka Brahma combined in Himself the qualities necessary to guide the planetary society towards its cherished goal of realization of Cosmic Consciousness. It is for achieving the holistic growth of humanity that He has propounded PROUT. PROUT is the new socio-economico-political idea which aims at establishing the leadership of sadvipras (spirituo-moralist leader), economic democracy (bottom-up block level planning) and believes in guaranteeing basic human requirements to all.

#### Punishment for a mistake in allowing an imposter scholar in A’nanda Nagar

As office secretary no.2, I was responsible for the entire Delhi sector. The organization had not become truly global then. But my responsibility included progress in the field. It was my job to collect the progress of all the field workers viz. RS & DS and report back to Ba’ba’ once every month. I was accountable for the progress in the field. In case of workers posted in A’nanda Nagar I was to collect daily report and then present the progress in A’nanda Nagar once every Sunday in Ranchi. It was an important work. Ba’ba’ was very particular and strict in the matters of progress of assigned tasks. He kept a close watch on their movements and activities. For me it was like walking on razor’s edge. Those days I would get His love and severe punishment both at the same time.

Before being appointed in a’nanda nagar I had worked with Dr. Ramesh who was the Relief Secretary. He was a great devotee of Ba’ba’. I was impressed by his dedication and his honest working style.

Once he send a cultured gentleman with his personal letter to A’nanda Nagar. He had written in his letter that this person was working on his Ph. D. on A’nanda Ma’rga. therefore I should provide him as much detailed information as possible. I read his letter before the then GS Da’da’ Sarveshvarananda, dada madhavananda and Dada Yogananda. They suggested that he should be treated properly. Accordingly I started revealing maximum information about A’nanda Ma’rga. At times Da’da’ Yogananda and da’da’ Madhavananda also gave him many additional information.

Next Sunday I went to Ranchi for reporting the daily outturn of the workers of A’nanda Nagar. When I was reading the report of da’da’ Yogananda and da’da’ Madhava’nanda in which I had mentioned that these workers spent some time with such and such person and shared some information about A’nanda Ma’rga with him. Ba’ba’ inquired about that person. I informed Him that Dr. Ramesh had sent him to a’nanda nagar. Hearing this he burst out angrily. He told us that the person was from intelligence department. I was dumbfounded. I realized my mistake. He asked me why did you give him shelter in the a’shram? I was stunned. I again repeated that he had come with a letter from Dr. Ramesh. I had also consulted the GS and other central workers before giving him shelter in the A’shram. He gave me very severe punishment. I could not accept that and was very seriously hurt. This incidence created a fear psychosis in my mind and avoided getting close to Him. There was some spiritual cause behind this happening that I have explained elsewhere in the book. Later Ba’ba’ punished all the three workers involved.

#### The satya’graha in A’nanda nagar comes to an end

After the incidence of 5th March a satygraha was launched by the margiis. It was lead by a’c. Narendra Dev. Ba’ba’ gave him this responsibility. The Marxist government of West Bengal had taken possession of the place where the five Dadhiciis were brutally murdered.

According to the programme daily five margiis used to go to that place and perform sa’dhana’ there and would remain there. The police used to arrest them on some false charges and they were being sent to Purulia Jail. This Satyagraha dharna’ went on for a few months.

All of a sudden on day this satygraha stopped. During the weekly reporting He inquired why the satyagraha was not taking place? I could not give a satisfactory reply. Ba’ba’ sternly asked me to get the information within 24hours and report back to Him. I went to bokaro where A’c. Naredra was staying for the purpose and learned about the reason for discontinuing the satyagrah. I informed Him immediately. Satyagraha resumed in next two days. This was a strange coincidence. He was always very strict and kept watch on each evert programme.

### The game of Chess

In those days da'da' Prabha'sa'nanda and Da'da' Prashant'nanda (former GS and PA) were posted in Ananda nagar. Ba'ba' was usually dissatisfied with their daily report. Once he asked me what are these two workers doing there. I casually replied, I see them playing chess. Ba'ba' became angry and playing chess is a waste of time. I informed them about Ba'ba's displeasure. He was always very particular about workers discipline and utilization of their time.

### False cases and injustice against workers

It was almost a regular routine of the police. They would find one or the other false pretext and get the workers of a'nanda nagar arrested. Once Dada Amita'nanda, Dada Cidghana'nanda and dada Svarupa'nanda and me were arrested by police on the false charges of stealing a pen and for mis-conduct with local aboriginal girls. This was repeated three times. We had to get bail for getting out of jail. In the court all these cases were proved false.

Once again on similar false pretext the police brought a few girls from the village to the court to give false evidence against us. But when the judge inquired the girls said we have never seen these monks. We were immediately released.

### Weekly repoting

It was a occasion planned by Ba'ba' for giving me His contact. I thoroughly enjoyed every reporting. It was my great fortune to have got His close personal touch. I remain ever indebted to Him for His grace.

### ERAWS central Store

The publication of the books for the AM schools was the responsibility of AMBE. This was a good source of income of ERAWS department. Ba'ba' was very particular about this. He used take detailed report of the accounts. I could give satisfactory reply and was punished severely. I could nort reconcial to this punishment. I was badly hurt mentally and returned back to A'nanda Nagar with aheavy heart. I cried internally for having been punished so severely. Suddenly he sent a worker the very next day and called me near Him and fiiled my heart with indescribable love. It was the otherworldly bliss. I remained in the spell of that powerful feeling. It was like a spontaneously overflow of supreme beatitude.

### Chapter 3

### On Spiritual Demonstrations

#### The Divine Form of Shrii A'nandamu'rtijii: venue Ranchi Ja'grti, 4th January 1969

This incidence relates to the golden period of my own life.

I developed an urge to reveal my own realization of Ba'ba's mysterious yet Divine form. Although I know it is devotees personal and guarded treasure. My own capacity to verbalize the realization is limited. I also know that the Infinite & the unfathomable entity cannot be described in words. Even then I have this overpowering urge to describe my own experiences. I am inclined to think that maybe my own experiences may help other devotees to relate these experiences with their own realizations. This may enable them to communicate with the Divine according to their own mental need. I am not sure how it would be useful to them even then I proceed to express my desire.

Spiritual scriptures describe the sadguru as an eternal being. He has no form & is not bound by bodily limitations. His love & compassion for the seekers is non-causal. Whenever a seeker develops a craving for Him, he manifests himself in different times for the benediction of all.

It was Ba'ba's grace that He pulled me near Him. I was intensely attracted to His charming persona. I used to meditate on His physical form whenever I would get close to Him and experience bliss. Ba'ba' would guide me to concentrate on sahasra'r cakra & go in deep meditation. Following His directions I would effortlessly visualize His Divine form and dive in the ocean of bliss.

After sometime, not to my liking I started fearing His physical form. Simultaneously there would also be a strong undercurrent of love and attraction for Him. This used to be quite confusing. I used to wonder and at times cry to realize His real form. This was a very painful and torturous to my mind. Suddenly one fine morning by His grace all my doubts were cleared and I was then beholding His eternal form.

It was the year 1969—Ba'ba' called it as Sa'dhana' Vars'a and gave umpteen number of spiritual demonstrations. This was a time of revelation in my life. It was perhaps 4th January, 1969 and evening Darshan of Ba'ba' was in progress in Ranchi Jagrti. There was a mysterious & blissful charm floating in Ba'ba's room. He asked me to sit close to him. Then He touched my A'jina' cakra (between my eyebrows) by His stick and commanded me to concentrate my mind on sahasra'ra cakra (top of the head). Of its own my mind got concentrated. I first saw white effulgence on the sahasra'ra cakra with Ba'ba' positioned in Varabhaya Mudra and smiling. He commanded further to penetrate deeply, following this I realized that my physical body of flesh & blood was disappearing. In the beginning I was a bit afraid & scared of this sudden dissolution of my body. But then I was reconciled after watching Ba'ba' in Varabhaya Mudra. The body Ba'ba' was

not made of flesh & blood but instead it was formed out of white effulgence. Seeing Him made up out of vibrations and effulgence my mind entered in a state of ecstasy. I lost myself in divine bliss.

As I described the scene Ba'ba' explained that I was beholding the real form of A'nandamu'rtijii - the lord of sahasra'ra cakra. He said that A'nandamurtijii can be realized in deep meditation only by his grace. He is the embodiment of bliss. He is blessedness personified (Kalyana Sundaram). He is not bound by any fetters. There is neither good nor bad for Him. None is a sinner or virtuous for Him. All are His children. Everybody has the right to sit on His lap. He cannot hate anybody since all exist within His cosmic mind. By his supreme grace after witnessing His divine splendor I entered into Nirvikalpa samadhi (the trance of non-qualified bliss) and stayed in that state throughout night. When I regained consciousness I found that some dada was feeding warm milk to me. Slowly I became conscious. But I continued to enjoy that supernal bliss for few months.

I realized the real form of the Supreme Guru. That guru has unparalled love for His own creation. He reflects the feelings, ideas and aspirations of human beings. He alone assumes human form to liberate the devotees from all kinds of bondages. In Skandha Puran'a Shiva replies to Parvati's query thus:

Listen O Pa'rvati & know it for sure the fact which I can state three times without any doubt that Guru alone is the Lord of all, Guru is Dharma personified and an uncompromising faith in the sanctity of Guru alone is supreme service & sacrifice.

Describing the greatness of Guru Saint Kabir also says that of all the great virtues the grace of SadGuru is incomparable, infinite and causeless. There are three things which are considered causeless: they are – Brahma, His creation and His grace

### Experiencing the Cosmic form of Guru

The materialist's concept of this universe has been now challenged by the material scientists themselves. That there is no consciousness in the universe has long been discarded as too naïve an idea even by the rationalists.

Consciousness is a reality. This idea must be accepted. The time is not very far away when there would be ample evidence through a large body of individual spiritual experience which would bring consciousness within the range of human experience. The scientific advancement with the passage of time would bring much of the supernatural phenomenon within the ambit of common human knowledge. It would convincingly prove to the socalled atheists that knowledge is not static. With the progress of material science there would be increasing interest in the spiritual values of

life. Humans would realize that genuine happiness is not material it is a mental attribute. Happy person has to be necessarily a contented and detached from material wants.

The spiritual thought of the east, specially, the ideas enshrined in Upanishada and the wealth of practical experience accumulated from ages related to the practices of yoga and Tantra reveal an inner working in which all of the universe appears to be interrelated.

Shrii Krsna in His Giita has revealed that the Lord must take the human form to redeem Dharma and establish it in its rightful place. And ensure victory of good over the evil.

In the history of A’nanda ma’rga is dynamic. Every year nay every month A’nandamurtijii propounded new ideas supported by beauty of rationality & reason. These new concepts would enthrall the A’nanda Ma’rgiis. In a similar vein Ba’ba’ named the the year 1969 as the sa’dhana’ Year. In this phase Ba’ba’ gave practical demonstration of the many mystical concepts and spiritual secrets. He would often use a monk or a family person as His medium for carrying out His demonstration..

A similar demonstration which was performed on the writer in the evening of 29th April 1969 is described below. That eventful day Ba’ba’ arrived in the Ranchi Ja’grti at 6pm. He was surrounded by His devotees in His room. All of them were enjoying His darshana. His room was filled with mystical aura.

In the midst of His talk He revealed that today He was going to conduct an incredible demonstration. He continued that about 3500 years ago Lord Krsna had revealed His Divine form to Arjuna (Vishvarupa darshana). Today also He would reveal the selfsame Vishvarupa. Having said so He called the writer to come close to Him and sit in Baddha Padma’sana keeping the spine straight. As He commanded so I followed. Then He touched my Trikuti (Ajina’ cakra) and asked me to concentrate my mind on Sahasrara cakra. As he spoke my mind instant got concentrated in Sahasrara cakra.

Then He directed me to see His physical form made of flesh and bones from toe to top. He then inquired from me to speak out what I was seeing. As He said these words, His physical form disappeared and I started visualizing Ba’ba’ in Vara’bhaya Mudr. But this Ba’ba’ that I was beholding was not the one who was seated there in flesh & blood. No, He was not His characteristic physical body, instead He was made of light waves and white effulgence. He appeared to me in a majestic form, divine and incredible. He further commanded me to visualize my own blood and say what I see there. In my blood particles also I saw Him present in Varabhaya Mudra. He asked me to see the same way in my bones, nerves and bone marrow. He was present there in His incredible ethereal form. Then likewise He asked me to see the pores of my own body. There also I visualized the same effulgent form of Ba’ba’ in Vara’bhaya.

Thereafter he commanded me to go beyond my body and speak out. What I saw then was most awesome. Words fail here for me to describe the beauty I was beholding. Now the periphery of my mind suddenly expanded on its own. Billions of stars and milky ways all came within my own mind. Then He commanded me to go beyond the solid factor of the universe into its liquid factor. I found the liquid factor was more pervasive than the solid. Then I entered into the luminous and through the aerial to the ethereal. In each stage the extent of subtlety went on increasing. The luminous was subtler than liquid and aerial was subtler than luminous likewise ethereal being subtler than the aerial. Now my mind had expanded in the ethereal. In each stage I visualized His magnificient form in Vara'bhaya Mudra. I was drowning in the ocean of bliss. It was an incredible experience, something unearthly and beyond imagination. Then He asked me to expand my mind into the minutest particles of the universe. As He asked me so it happened. Expanding beyond all the frontiers I merged into Him and I lost consciousness. I don’t know how long I remained in that state.

In the meantime He left for His residence. He left instructions that I must come back in senses within 12 hours. If I am delayed then I may attain Moks'a. Then it would be difficult to maintain this body. As soon as I come back into consciousness, I should be fed warm milk. Then I will regain normal consciousness. I became conscious at 5am in the morning. As I woke up I was given warm milk. And slowly I regained consciousness. I had lost any appetite for food. I wanted to remain in the same state of bliss for ever.

Following this demonstration, I continued to enjoy off and on that state of blissful staticity for many months. I wanted to be in that state only. Even now when I think of that experience I lose my senses & start to enjoy bliss.

Only the one to whom He gives this realization knows Him, Knowing Him means being Him

### Nrsingh rupa darshana

This incidence took place in Ranchi Ja'grti in continuation of the that momentous sa'dhana' year of 1969. As Ba'ba' entered the Jagrti there was a commotion among the ma'rgiis. The margii gathering was filled with devotional exuberance. Ba'ba' settled in the comfort of His room. The setting there was mystical. The devotees were seated in His room drinking the nectar of His divine charm. The environment smelled the sweet fragrance in the air surrounding Him. Nothing in the surrounding could remain unaffected by the unspeakable ectasy.

He called me to sit before Him in Padma'sana with my spine erect. Then He touched my Trikuti with His magical cane stick. He asked me to concentrate my mind on

Sahasra'ra cakra. I followed as directed. All of a sudden without my doing my mind got focused on Sahasra'ra cakra. It was very unusual but a blissful experience. There on the sahasra'ra cakra I was now beholding Him in Vara'bhaya Mudra over a floating splendor. His form was made of effulgent radiance and not His normal body of flesh. Ba'ba' told me that His real form was made up of effulgent light. That alone is the object of Dhya'na for a sa'dhaka. His resplendent form is beyond all bondages. All have equal right to sit on His bosom. Being a blissful entity as the controller of the Sahasra'r, for proper governance of the world He comes as Maha'sambhu'ti with a mission.

> Yada' yada' hi dharmasya gla'ni bhavati bha'rata,
> Abhyuttha'nam' adharmasya tada'tmanm' srja'myaham
> Paritra'na'ya sadhu'na'ma' vina'sha'ya ca dushkrta'm'
> Dharmasanstha'pana'rtha'ya sambhava'mi yuge yuge

That is why in the form of Kalki or Nrsingh He controls the universe through Ota and Prota yoga seated on the Mula'dha'ra Cakra of all.

Then He commanded that I should pierce my mind through all cakras below Sahasra'ra and focus on Mula'dha'ra and describe your experience. Effortlessly my mind went deeper and deeper. At the Mula'dha'ra Lo! I was beholding a golden lion. The pores of that Lion were effulgent with golden light. I started losing my consciousness due to the surging waves of bliss as I was beholding that magnificent splendor. After a while Ba'ba' asked me to concentrate my mind on the Golden Lion and look at what do you see. As I focused I started beholding a floating effulgence and over that radiance Ba'ba' was couched in Vara'bhaya. Ba'ba' told us that was His Nrsingh Rupa. Ba'ba' said Nrsingh means supreme human form. It is wrongly depicted as half lion and half human. A'nandamurtijii has assumed a samkalpa deha (Body created for a purpose). His physical form is the object of ideation (Dhya'na) for sa'dhakas. This is the way to supreme realization.

### Unity in diversity

This incidence took place on the occasion of A'nanda Purnima' DMC at Ranchi in 1969. Thousands of ma'rgiis had assembled there. The environment was full of devotion. The last day of DMC used to be as always a moment of painful separation from the beloved.

Ba'ba' was speaking about the role of Ma'ya'diisha & His Ma'ya' during the departing darshana. He said that ma'ya was functioning according to a predetermined law. It is subordinate to Him. Those who take shelter unto Ma'ya'diisha go beyond the influence of ma'ya'. Hence your future is safe and bright.

Then He called Dada Pra’nakrsna’nanda who was seated on the dais. He was asked to stand facing the margii audience. Ba’ba’ asked him what was he seeing. He said there was a crowd of margiis in the front. Then Ba’ba’ pointed His cane stick towards him and asked him the same question again. He said that now I am beholding only You. Saying so, he fell lifeless and entered into Nirvikalpa Sama’dhi.

Before departing Ba’ba’ gave some instructions about the dada who had entered Nirvikalpa sama’dhii. Finally looking at the margiis He once again repeated that your future was safe and bright.

### Darshana of Ba’lakrsna

From my early childhood I had a strong craving for Ba’lakrsna. His child like form was my main attraction. I used behold His resplendent charming form and would often look Him in the world around. I used imagine Him sitting on the branch of tree and playing His flute. At times I would listen to the sweet melody of His flute and lose consciousness.

After I became a sanyasi this desire to behold Him in Balakrsna form remained unfulfilled. Then the Sa’dhana’ Varsha came and one fine morning He called me near Him for conducting a demonstration. He told me to sit in Baddha Padma’sana with erect spine. He touched my Trikuti with his magical cane stick and commanded to concentrate on A’jina’ Cakra. As He said so it became. Then He commanded to take my mind in the past 3500 years. As he said these words my mind dived deeper. I was looking at my own past samskara like a moving panorama. Finally my mind rested at An’a’hata cakra. Then he told me to focus my attention and describe what you see. All of a sudden I saw a beautiful forest with tall green and flowering trees. In the midst of that was a flowing river. In the bushes and the woods Ba’lakrsna was playing with His friends in Vrnda’vana. His form was very attractive, charming and hypnotic. He was carrying a flute of bamboo which tied to His waist band. Beholding His incredible Ba’laru’pa I lost my consciousness and don’t know when the margiis and Ba’ba’ left the room.
