I am really fortunate for having been chosen as His medium and for getting my childhood desire fulfilled.

### Out of body journey of my mind to a real distant land not physically visited by me

This demonstration was conducted by Ba’ba’ during Sa’dhana’ Vars’a. It was the same old setting in the evening darshana at Ranchi Ja’grti in that year of 1969. The room of Ba’ba’ was illuminated with the shine of milkwhite glow. A crowd of devotees mostly from Delhi sector were present. Among them were three margiis from Philippines (Maharlika-

the Samskrta name of the Country). They were Dr. Perelim (Priyadarshi) a senior Physician, Shrii Wilson (Kamalanayan) a very senior administrative officer of the government of Philippines (equal in rank to a Governor), Shrii Pestoral (Gun'a'nanda) a leading businessman.

Ba'ba' was speaking about the science of journey to distant places by mind leaving the body at rest at some place. To demonstrate He asked me to come close to Him & sit with the spine erect in Siddha'sana. I followed His instructions. Then He pressed my Trikuti with His right hand thumb. After that He commanded to concentrate in A'jina' Cakra. My mind got effortlessly concentrated. Then He directed my mind to move out towards the east. He told me that I should describe whatever I see on the way. I suddenly realized that my mind has on its own has moved out proceeded as told by Him eastwards. After a while I started watching the lights coming from the buildings of a large town which was illuminated in the evening. Ba'ba' asked me to go near some shop & read out the name of the town from the displayed sign boards. I read out loudly and said it was Calcutta. Ba'ba' affirmed and said that at this point you are passing through Calcutta. He asked me to keep moving farther east. After some time I told Him that I was seeing water everywhere. Ba'ba' nodded and said you are passing over the Bay of Bengal. As I kept moving further east, I watched lights of another big town which was radiant with bright lights. Like before He asked me to read out the name of the town. I read the sign board and was astonished to see it was Bangkok. Then I crossed the Pacific Ocean and came close to another large town. There also I read the sign board and found it was Metro Manila. Ba'ba' said at this moment you are in the centre of the Metro Manila town. Then He directed me to proceed towards the west. Going that way I reached a crossing and read out its name, Bro. Pastoral (Gun'a'nanda) jumped from his seat involuntarily. He was emotionally charged after hearing the name of the crossing. He then confirmed that it was the crossing next to his house. Following this Ba'ba' guided me to take the right turn and look at the huge building standing there. When I started to describe the view of the building before my eyes, Bro. Pastoral (Gun'a'nanda) once again lost control and could not help but jump again in amazement. He then yelled it was his own house. Then Ba'ba' directed me to enter the building. As I entered there inside the drawing room I saw a young, chubby & fair boy seated on chair, resting his chin on the palm of his hands. His elbow rested on the drawing table. I described the features of the boy, Gun'a'nanda was flabbergasted and cried out loudly that it was his son there.

Ba'ba' then further commanded me to enter his (boy) mind and speak out what was the boy thinking at that moment. I entered his mind and and read his thoughts. I started narrating that the boy was thinking that his father had left in a rush for the darshan of his Guru in India. He did not consider for a moment my own requirements. I have my university examination after next two days. If he was here I would have felt comfortable.

As I finished this sentence Ba’ba’ called off the demonstration abruptly and started to leave. Soon after this event Gun’a’nanda hurried to the nearby GPO and spoke to his son over phone. He inquired about his wellbeing. Then he asked him what was he thinking at such and such time. He got the confirmation of what Guna’nanda had heard from me.

### Stealing Bro. Wilson’s (Kamalnayan) Memory & Intelligence

This demonstration was performed by Ba’ba’ on the following Sunday in the same Sa’dhana’ Vars’a. There was a large assembly of ma’rgiis from many places in the Jagrti. Ba’ba’ spoke that day in English. After His pravacana, He pointed to Kamalnayan and asked him to explain his discourse. Kamalnayan spoke very well and covered each point that Ba’ba’ has dealt with in His discourse. It was an awesome performance. Margiis were impressed by his memory.

As soon as Kamalnayan finished his talk, Ba’ba’ remarked that when he was delivering his commentary he was entertaining the propensity of pride (ahamka’ra) & thought of his excellent memory. Ahamka’ra is the greatest enemy on the path of spirituality. Human capability is very limited. Human beings have a small cranium. How much knowledge can they assimilate in their small brain. And it is unbecoming of good people to consider their knowledge as a matter of any great achievement.

Then He asked Kamalnayan to come close to Him. Kamalnayan immediately complied. Then He called me to sit facing Kamalnayan. Then Ba’ba’ asked me to concentrate my mind on A’jina’ Cakra. I did as told by Him. My mind was instantly concentrated on the A’jina’ cakra. Then He asked me to enter the mind of Bro. Kamalnayan and tell Him what I was seeing. As He said these words I was watching a blue effulgence (jyoti). Ba’ba’ then remarked that blue colour is indicative of intelligence. Kamalnayan is intelligent and has good memory. But he is also proud of his qualities. Now Ba’ba’ continued, I am going to withdraw his intelligence (buddhi). Then Ba’ba’ spoke some mantra. Later pointing to Kamalnayan, Ba’ba’ inquired his name. Kamalnayan looked at Him and tried in vain but could not speak. Ba’ba’ repeated His question. Kamalnayan was confused & failed to reply. He seemed in a pitiable condition.

Then He commanded me to enter his mind now. I entered Kamalnayan’s mind and watched, as He had commanded. I said that his (Kamalnayan’s) mental colour has changed to black. Ba’ba’ remarked that black colour is indicative of stupidity, devoid of any intelligence. Kamalnayan in this condition neither knows his name, nor can he recall it. He then again repeated some mantra to restore Kamalnayan’s memory and intelligence. Then He commanded me once again and asked him to enter kamalnayan’s mind now. No sooner Ba’ba’ finished saying, I looked at kamalnayan’s mind and now

saw the same old blue effulgence indicative of restoration of his faculties of intelligence and memory. Kamalnayan now returned back to his usual self as before.

Once Ba’ba’ commented that the planet earth is like a mustard seed in the great universe. In that tiny planet earth how small is a human being almost invisible. Human beings are very weak and pitiable. So called human knowledge and intelligence is so little but their pride is like a mountain which is why they are endangering their existence.

### Demonstration of my past life 1100 years ago

As usual Ba’ba’ came to Ranchi Jagrti for evening darshana. The mood of the devotees present there turned blissful. It was the same momentous year called sa’dhana’ vars’a. As He entered His room the crowd of devotees rushed in to drink the nector of His supra natural charm. During His discourse He called me to come close to Him. Then He touched my Trikuti with His can stick and commanded me to concentrate on my A’jina’ cakra. I did as told. He then asked me to go back to the past 1100 years ago. As he commanded my mind effortlessly followed and went back in the past I felt as if my own mental reel was unwinding backwards. It stopped at some point on its own.

Then Ba’ba’ called my name & asked me to describe whatever I was seeing. At that moment I saw a dense forest and a hillock inside the forest. I also saw a yogi meditating on the hillock. He was sitting tight in an erect pose. That yogi had long body hair. His beard and the hair were very long falling behind his back. Ba’ba’ said that it is your past life as a sa’dhaka. You do not know but I have known you since so many lives that you have lived.

Ba’ba’ had remarked on several occasions during His discourses that margiis have not come to Him all of a sudden. Instead they have been with Him from many lives in the past. They may know but He knows about them.

He has also said that the natural law according to which humans forget their past lives is benevolent in nature. Because if human beings remembered all their past lives then they will lose mental balance and go mad.

### Demonstration about Netaji Subhas’candra Bose

I had heard earlier that Ba’ba’ had conducted demonstrations where He had shown that Netaji was alive & that he was devoted to a life of Sa’dhana’ in a cave in the Himalayas.

In Ranch Ja’grti it was the occasion of morning darshana. Ba’ba’ had already arrived earlier and had finished His routine activities of personal contact. And now He was reclining on the bed in His room. Ma’rgiis have taken seats & were feeling blessed in

His presence. It was quite natural for the devotees to be blissfully unaware of the world around when He was there. Those were the most significant times for A’nanda Ma’rgiis. Ba’ba’ was easily accessible to common ma’rgiis and they depended on Him in all situations. No task was difficult. Nothing was impossible by His grace. It was one such gathering in Ranchi. He was delivering His morning discourse. In His characteristic manner every discourse was normally accompanied by a practical demonstration to prove the philosophical idea that He was discussing during the Darshana. Continuing in the same strain He called a ma’rgii sadhaka to sit near Him. He touched the A’jina’ cakra of that sa’dhaka and then He commanded that sa’dhaka to speak what he was seeing in his mind. The margii after a while started describing. He spoke of seeing mountains around. Ba’ba’ directed him to look carefully if there was any other thing in the mountain. He said he was seeing a cave in the mountain. Ba’ba’ then commanded him to enter the cave and see. As he entered the cave & saw some human figure. Ba’ba’ asked him to go near that person in view and then speak. He said there was a Yogi sitting in Padma’sana with his eyes closed in Dhya’na mudra. Ba’ba’ asked him if you can recognize that Yogi. The sa’dhaka waited for while and then said, the yogi looks like Netaji Subhas’ Chandra Bose. After that Ba’ba commanded him to enter the mind of the yogi ask him whether he wishes to come back to world? After a while the sa’dhaka replied that the Yogi is refusing to come back to the world. With this Ba’ba’ asked that sa’dhaka not to disturb Him any more in His sa’dhana’.

During the Sa’dhana’ Vars’a in the year 1969, Ba’ba’ conducted the above demonstration. At that time Netaji was living in a Himalayan cave near Tibbet leading a life of a recluse devoted to Yoga Sa’dhana’.

Demonstration of similarity of samskaras between two persons

This demonstration was to reaffirm the ageold saying that there is unity in diversity. The universe is apparently diverse, but fundamentally it is one. Vedas repeatedly affirm that One Supreme Consciousness has expressed into this universe of diversity. (Ekoham’ Bahusya’m’). Whether seen or unseen, crude or subtle the origin of everything is that Singular entity.

The differences among the created beings, between the plants and the animals then between animals and the human beings are cause under the influence of Ma’ya, which has created variety of samskara to create differentiation among the created beings.

In Ranchi Ja’grati during His Darshan, Ba’ba’ explained that the variety created in the perceptible world is due to differences in samskaras. Essentially there is only one singular entity. That singular entity alone has metamorphosed itself into this created world of variety by virtue of Sadrshyaparina’ma (Homoform or curvilinear evolution) – which causes Kala’ Prabha’h (curvatures) thus creating differentiation among the

species created due to their samskara. He further explained that if by any process the samskara of two apparently different things become identical then they cannot maintain their separate identity.

After that he called two margii brothers to sit near him. Then He asked them to sit erect in Padma’sana and to fix their mind on A’jina’ cakra. Both of them immediately complied. Then Ba’ba’ took away their individual samskara and infused in both of them a common samskar of love for God. Slowly both the margiis started coming close to each other and after a while caught hold of each other tightly and lost consciousness. They had lost any differentiation what-so-ever. They continued in that state.

Ba’ba’ then explained that they are now in two bodies but they have identical samskara. If they are left like this they will not be able to live as two different persons. They will leave this body and will unite as one mind and will be reborn as another person, since they can not have separate individuality.

Later Ba’ba’ infused krodha vrtti (propensity of anger) in one and Lajja’ vrtti in another person. No sooner Ba’ba’ infused two diverse samskaras in the two persons they loosened the grip and shied away from each other. After a while they became normal as before.

Ba’ba’ continued to enlighten. There are different kinds of individuals as there are different animals and plants terrestrial or acquatic. All of them have differences on account of their samaskaras imborn or imposed. Amongst human beings some have predominance of fear or shyness in some infatuation and in others hatred and in yet another anger so on and so forth. If Parama Purus’a so desires, then He can infuse the same samskara in all the created beings. In that situation all the created beings will unite to become one. Then the universe of variety will cease to exist. But Parama Purus’a has created this universe of variety. This is Its expressed desire. Hence universe will never come to an end. It will exist for eternity.

### Demonstration of Kaunka’lama’linii Sama’dhi

It was one of those blissful evenings in Ranchi. The Ja’grti was full of devotionally charged ma’rgiis and WTs waiting for His arrival. Ba’ba’ came and took His seat in His room. Margiis joined Him and sat surrounding Him. Ba’ba’ started His discourse. Today he was speaking about the many varied states of experiences which the advanced sa’dhakas realize as they progress spiritually. He called me near His cot. They He touched my Trikuti by His right hand thumb. He commanded me to focus on A’jina’ cakra. My mind got instantaneously focused their. Then He directed me to take my mind on the lower cakras. It happened effortlessly and my mind got fixed at an’a’hata cakra.

He also touched my An’a’hata. Then he commanded me to speak whatever I experience hereafter.

As He touched my an’a’hat I started drowning into a flood of divine ecstasy. I also started experiencing a white effulgence encircling my An’a’hata cakra. Simultaneously I was beholding a strange feminine figure looking alike to Kali wearing a garland of Naramund’a (human skull). It was not any fearsome form, but very sweet and divine. As I saw her waves of indescribable bliss flooded my mind and I lost consciousness and entered into Samadhi.

When I came back from the sama’dhi I experienced excruciating pain in my body. I could not gather enough courage to sit down. The residual waves of bliss were still carrying my mind to the other world.

Ba’ba explained to the sa’dhakas that I was experiencing Kaunka’lama’linii sama’dhi. He further added that this was a very special blissful experience which Lord Shiva had imparted to His consort Kali.

### Sama’dhi of madhura Bha’va

It was a usual day. We all waited for Ba’ba’ to arrive in the Ranchi Ja’grti. There was a sudden commotion as we heard the sound horn of Ba’ba’s car announcing His arrival. He straight way proceeded to His room and after the Personal Contacts of new ma’rgiis were over, there He sat on His bed waiting for the ma’rgiis to come in. Margiis rushed to enter His room. He talked on some enlightening devotional topic. This was followed by a demonstration.

He called me and commanded me to sit in siddha’sana. I happily complied. He brought His cane stick out and touched my Trikuti with its end. Then He directed me to focus on A’jina’ cakra. As He spoke, unaware of what was happening I found my mind has got concentrated on its own on An’a’hata cakra. After a little while I started having a glimpse of the Divine effulgence encircling the An’a’hata Cakra. As I watched the effulgence a blissful experience permeated through my mind. I also experienced as if everything of the world has become beautiful and sweet. I felt that from everything nectar is secreting. A sweet feeling and taste also pervaded my mind. After this demonstration was over I realized how immaculate and flawless & sweet is the spiritual life and its experiences.

### Ba’ba’ imparts the divine experience of hearing internally AUM-ka’ra Dhvani

Ba’ba’ has been infinitely kind and graceful, that is why I have been getting drenched by the incessant rain of His grace on a humble and simple child of His.

Baba very caringly utilized my body and graced many divine experiences like AUM-Ka'ra on a number of occasions to me and also at times in small groups. I have an irredeemable debt to Ranchi Ja'grti. It was here that I was chosen by Him to be His medium to receive many of those spiritual experiences. This sacred place humming with His devotees stayed as a silent witness of those golden moments of my life.

The occasion this time was one of those fine noon time darshana of Ba'ba'. His discourse that day was centring round Pranava Dhvani (AUM-ka'ra). He explained that Pranava is an internal sound. It comes within the range of auditory range internally of those devotees who are able pull their Kul-kund'alinii beyond A'jina' cakra by the grace of the Guru. On hearing the divine sound mind loses itself into the Macrocosm (Universal Mind). The sa'dhaka then loses itself into savikalpa sama'dhi. As He completed His discourse, Ba'ba' called me to come closer. I pushed my way near Him. He commanded me to sit erect in siddha'sana. I readily complied. Then he touched my A'jina' Cakra by His cane stick. By the sheer magic touch of His stick my mind was at once focused there. In a short while I started listening AUM dhvani (sound). The sound was coming in waves. As the sound of AUM started simultaneously I was getting inundated in a flood of unspeakable bliss. I don't remember how long I heard the AUM sound since following this experience I entered into sama'dhi. This feeling of bliss persisted for many days.

### An intuitive experience of Divine effulgence (Brahma Jyoti Darshan)

That evening Ba'ba's discourse was on Brahma Jyoti (Supreme effulgence). He said that the path of spiritual sa'dhana' is the path of enlightenment, of knowledge & liberation from all sorts of bondages. Human beings acquire resources to move on that path and also receive the requisite courage and strength only from Him. The Prakrta sa'dhaka surrenders to the Supreme Being. The sa'dhaka implores Him to pull one from darkness to light.

(asado ma sad gamaya,

tamso ma jyotirgamaya,

mrtyor ma amrtam' gamay)

Who is that effulgent entity? It is Nis'kala Brahma. Its effulgence is white. The light of all glittering entities is derived from that Supreme effulgent entity. The sa'dhaka by the grace of parama Purus'a is able to behold that effulgence. That effulgence is called Brahma Jyoti.

Na tatra suryo bha'ti, na candrata'rakah

(the sun and the moon lose their luster before that supreme resplendent entity)

Nema' vidyuto bha'nti, kutohyamagni

(Lightening meets with the same fate what to speak of the fire)

Tameva bhantamanubhati sarvam' (all derive their brightness from His resplendence)

Tasya bha'sa' sarvamidam' vibha'ti (His resplendence alone makes all entities bright)

Like the experience of AUM dhvani, Ba'ba' imparted this awesome realization of Brahma Jyoti darshan on many occasions to me. Once Ba'ba' bestowed the same experience upon me by in Ranchi Ja'grti. He explained that Brahma is the embodiment of Jyoti (Jyoti svaru'pa). Jyoti darshana is tantamount to Brahma Darshana. Some times the Jyoti darshana is also associated with the experience of Divine smell. It simply means that at that point the Lord has shifted His nucleus to that place thus imparting to the elevated sa'dhakas the experience of Divine smell.

During one such darshana in the blissful Sa'dhana' vars'a, Ba'ba' asked me to sit in Padma'sana and contemplate on the A'jina' cakra. I followed as directed and soon found that I was beholding bright white effulgence (Brhma Jyoti) at my Trikuti without any effort from my end. Simultaneously I started experiencing a kind of exaltation. Ba'ba' continued to explain that experiencing Brahma Jyoti is possible only by Divine grace.

I have had the experience that soon after the spiritual demonstration my mind would invariable lose consciousness and I would enter into sama'dhi. I also found that after the demonstration my body used to react and an excruciating pain followed, this would be followed by a lingering euphoria transporting my mind to a mystical land of overwhelming delight. This transcendent state used to pervade my mind making it very soft, peaceful, happy and thus I would lose the awareness of pain.

There were many who enjoyed these demonstrations being performed on me. Some used to sympathize and looked upon me with a sense of awe. There were also a few who would envy. During one of those momentous times, Ba'ba' knowing what was going on in the mind of those persons, once said during His discourse that when some body's mind and body is not prepared, utilizing them for demonstration may be harmful.

### Demonstration on my me transforming my mind into a fish (Matsya Bhava)

This was a usual day of His evening darshana in Ranchi Ja'grti. For the devotees it was the most pleasurable experience to witness His supernal charm. Ba'ba' was explaining that the created world is made up of vibration, form and colour. If the wavelength of any object is changed then that object will undergo transformation accordingly.

He then called me to come near Him. Then He touched my Trikuti and commanded ‘be a fish’ (Matsya bhava). As He spoke these words to my utter surprise I developed the feeling that my body was getting transformed into a fish. I also developed a craving to get into water. I was seized with a strong feeling that I may not survive without water. It was quite a strange feeling. Now my body was twisting involuntarily and I had severe pain all over my body.

Ba’ba’ addressed the ma’rgiis that He has changed the vibrations of the inferential waves (tanma’tra) of five sensory organs (viz. smell, taste, form, touch and sound) of Parmeshvara’nanda. This made me think like a fish.

Ba’ba’ introduces a new rule in the spiritual world – for Salvation (Moks’a) Service of the created beings is an essential prerequisite like Sa’dhana’

Ba’ba’ used to create a mystical environment of unspeakable bliss in Ranchi Jagrti in that blissful Sa’dhana’ vars’a. His coming to the Ja’grti used to fill the margiis with a new inspiration. They would instantly get enlightenment. There mental darkness would get dispelled vanishing doubts and skepticism from their minds (PS no. 1528 - Pra’nera A’lo dhara’ya dha’lo andha tamasari nashii).

The same feelings are expressed in the following Prabhat samgiita

> “A’ji Aja’na’ pathika eseche,
>
> mukulita latika’ya surbhita malika’ya phuler para’ge bheseche”

(Today the Unknown traveler has come,

His coming has enlivened the slowly blooming creepers and shrubs,

The garden of flowers is spreading heavenly smell & the pollen is flooding).

One such enchanting evening Ba’ba’ started speaking about the requirements for attaining salvation. He commented that Jina’na, Karma & Bhakti (knowledge, action & devotion) all three are essential prerequisites for attaining salvation. The devotees were drinking the nectar of His fascinating charm and bewitching smile with their almost enraptured minds.

He called out the name of one of the da’da’s sitting in the audiance to come near Him. The Da’da’ quickely responded. He came forward & sat near Him keeping his spine erect in siddha’sana. Then Ba’ba’ called two other sa’dhakas to come & sit near him. After that Ba’ba’ commanded the da’da’ to look into the past life of one of the two sa’dhakas. He enquired from them to speak who they were in their past lives & what did they do? As He spoke these words the Da’da’ started having the vision of the sa’dhaka’s

past life. He saw that ma’rgii was a great sa’dhaka in his past life. He was devoted to intense and austere sa’dhana. His hard and dedicated sa’dhana’ got him spiritually elevated. As he grew old, he had a sudden realisation that he has spent all his life in austerities and sa’dhana’ but he has not done anything for the service of the created world. He had realized that the world was the metamorphosed form of Na’ra’yana. This shortcoming became the cause of his rebirth. Now he has got immense opportunity to be of service to the world.

Next Ba’ba’ directed the Da’da’ to look into mind of the other sa’dhaka and to speak about his past life. Da’da’ started having a similar vision & got to see the past life of the sa’dhaka. He saw that the ma’rgii sa’dhaka was Karma yogi in the previous life. He spent all his time in the service of the world around. He was empathetic to the suffering of those around him. He devoted himself to selfless service of the needy and the distressed. He served them with complete detachment, yet later he also realized that though he did serve the Narayana in the form of the world, but he did not equally devote himself to His sa’dhana’. This has become the cause of his rebirth as a devotee.

Thus for achieving salvation a balance in Karma Yoga and Bhakti Yoga is necessary. A devotee must remain inspired in the service of the suffering beings. Serving the distressed cleans the mind and makes it humble and pure. S’adhana’ is possible only by the humble and pure.

### He is always with us

His mere touch floods all mind spontaneously with the supreme beatitude. The Guru is the most compassionate of the beings. He keeps a watch on His disciples to ensure that they move on the destined path & that they do not fall in the trap of ma’ya’.

The incidence described here relates to seminar year 1970. I had just been called back from my Bhopal posting where I worked as a DS and later posted as central social security secretary and relief incharge. We had been given the responsibility of writing down RS guide book. I was assigned the task with two other workers viz. Dada Omkara’nanda and Dada Jyotishvara’nanda. Both of them were very relaxed & carefree persons. But they were in a state of confusion and were losing faith in the Guru.

We were busy writing the guide book in Saccidananda Bhavan which was situated a little away from Ranchi central office. I used to come back to central office at ten in the morning for the darshana of Ba’ba’. These two workers would usually go away the same time for taking lunch in the Curu hotel of their choice. We would assemble in the afternoon after the morning darshan of Ba’ba’ was over. At that point they used to tempt me by taking the names of the delicacies taken by them in the hotel and pass acrid remarks. They would reproach me & would blame me for not joining them for lunch. This

continued for ten days. Once they almost forcibly took me to the Curu Hotel for lunch. On our way back we found that dada Vijayananda had been sent by Ba’ba’ in search of all of us. He came up to Mahaviira Chowk near the Curu Hotel. After locating us he got visibly annoyed & told us that Ba’ba’ was waiting for us in the Ja’grti. We were totally nonplussed. I felt guilty for having submitted to my colleagues guillibles.

It was 2pm in the noon. Ba’ba’ was waiting in His room in the Ja’grti. We all three entered simultaneously in His room and sat down after sa’sta’unga prana’ma. Ba’ba’ asked one after the other from two of them and both of them spoke blatant lies as replies to His simple querries. Later He told them to leave his room since He had some work with me. When they left the room Ba’ba’ asked me why I did not complete seminar in all the dits of Bhopal Diocess. I immediately conceded omission on my part and I received punishment for my fault. I was relieved of my guilt after getting punished. Soon I found that my mind had got flooded with indescribable bliss. His most enchanting affection washed away all my mental filth and I found myself purified. The other two workers did not continue for long & later left the organization within next few days.

Ba’ba’ witnesses of our inner most thoughts - Senior A’ca’rya training in Ranchi

It was the month of September of 1969 in Ranchi. The venue was our central office in Piska Morh. I had just returned back to Ranchi after a long journey the same day. I was then posted there as the central social security secretary and relief incharge. Those were the days when Ba’ba’ was imparting advanced training to a select group of senior a’ca’ryas. To name a few those who were attending the classes were A’c. Chandranath, A’c. Raghunath, A’c. Dasarath, Dada Shraddha’nanda, Dada Pranava’nanda, Dada Vijaya’nanda. The classes were being conducted from 9am to 12noon and again from 3pm to 6pm.

The then central office secretary da’da’ Pranava’nanda was in a hurry to rush for the classes. He advised me to look after the office and left. I was tired of a long journey and was sitting on the verandah in Lungi-ganji. That day I was the only person present in the central office. All the senior workers had left for the classes. Suddenly around 3pm I was thrilled hearing the blowing of horn of His car. It was an unscheduled visit. I had no time to get ready in proper dress and just rushed to the main gate to allow His car to enter the office premises. His car came inside the campus. I ran to open the door of His car and welcomed Him. Ba’ba’ went to His office and sat their. I was overjoyed as I was beholding His magnetic charm and waited for his instructions.

Ba’ba’ was in a pleasant mood. He enquired from me about the senior a’carya classes? I replied that I did not know since I was not participanting. Ba’ba’ persisted and kept asking what do the a’ca’ryas speak after they come out of the classes. I said that today I heard them speaking about the most awesome class of the morning. They remarked

that Ba’ba’ blessed us as a’ca’ryas but the real training is taking place now. Ba’ba’ continued saying that I will enrich A’nanda ma’rga philosophy to such an extent that it will inspire generations of humanity for ages to come. I will also create a strong organization which will be the nucleus of dharma and service of the created world.

His sweet words vibrated my mind and body. I was speechless and looked at His bewitching face. Then Ba’ba’ took leave from me saying I must go now the trainee a’ca’ryas are waiting for Him.

Thus Ba’ba’ gently took care of the pain in my heart for not being able to attend the classes.

### Opportunity to assist Personal contact

I got the opportunity once when Dada Pranava’nandajii was away from office on some urgent work. Being central office secretary no. 2 it was my responsibility to arrange personal contact with Ba’ba’. I read out the list with the addresses of the margiis standing in queue for PC to Ba’ba’. The first name in the list was of a rich middle aged person. Second was a gold medalist student and the third was a smart student from Betiah with leadership qualities. He suggested that the matured rich person may wait for the time being, the gold medalist would devote himself to build his career hence the first choice be given to that student leader from Betiah

As advised by Him I sent that student first. He was a brave young energetic person. He remained for about 40 minutes with Ba’ba’. Ba’ba’ told him of his past and punished him for his mistakes Then He took a pledge from him to be an ideal person. He also received lots of love of Ba’ba’ and was duly inspired. He came out crying. As days passed a radical change was came in the personality of that student. He proved to be a great asset of the mission. He popularized the margii ideals in the schools and colleges. And UPSF got strengthened in that region.

### Bhopal posting and DMC

Education, Relief and Welfare section (ERAWS) of A’nanda Ma’rga is the vehicle for spreading Neo-Humanist education and to provide relief to the needy in the event of any natural or manmade disaster. I was posted as the Principal of A’nanda ma’rga Primary School and DS Bhopal in 1969. Before leaving for Bhopal, I went to meet Ba’ba’ to seek His blessings. On entered His room I did sa’sta’unga pran’a’m & the sat on the floor facing him. He lovingly told me that He was posting me as DS Bhopal. He asked me to start a school there. He said that I was being sent there in advance. Soon He will follow me there. I left him and started for Bhopal.

In Bhopal ma’rgiis welcomed my posting. There was a new but strong team of enthusiastic ma’rgiis. The core team of margiis came from the Government of India Electrical Installation called BHEL. They were actively pursuing for starting the school. A’carya Chitle was also posted there as Suprintending Engineer, Govt of Madhya Pradesh. With there collective efforts, finally we got a plot of land allotted by the BHEL administration. The school was immediately started and soon many children were admitted to our Kindergarten. A’c. Chitle and his wife helped me in every possible way to establish the school.

Soon after I had barely completed three months that I received a call from DPS Central informing me of Ba’ba’s coming to Bhopal for DMC. I was thrilled so were the Margiis. They were looking for such an golden opportunity. They could not, however, imagine that the Lord will satisfy their heart’s desire so soon. They prepared a detailed plan for making the DMC a grand success and initiated actions to execute the plan. A’c. Chitle’s house was very large and it was equipped with all the facility, hence it was decided that Ba’ba’ will stay with Chitlejii’s family. For the DMC programme we were permitted to use the campus of Regional engineering college. It was an excellent arrangement. The campus was well maintained and it was huge. It was the most appropriate location for the DMC function.

Ba’ba’ soon arrived in Bhopal. He was received the warm welcome of His devotees who were yearning for the darshana of their most beloved Guru. Ba’ba’ was driven to A’c. Chitle’s residence. No sooner He arrived there, Ba’ba’ became furious and wanted to be shifted to some other place. We could not understand His drama. We didn’t know what to do. Chitle family was also taken aback. A’c. Chitle went to Him and requested Him to stay, but He was unrelenting.

We had no clue where we can take Him. He came forward with the suggestion that He would like to stay where the ma’rgiis were staying. We immediately started looking for some avenue and soon learned of a room with attached toilet and kitchen close to the DMC hall. This place was isolated and complete in itself. He was soon shifted there. He returned to His normal enchanting self. The DMC programme was very inspiring and enjoyable. Margiis received His grace and Vara’bhaya.

On the last day before he was to leave, he called me and said that He had kept his promise of following me to Bhopal.

### Sa’dhana’ Piitha Training (SPT)

Ba’ba’ was never short of programmes to keep us inspired and busy. Though the a’ca’rya & whole timer training was ongoing, we were missing some salient point necessary in the life of a dedicated sanyasin. He wanted us to be prepared for the most

untoward incidences. He wanted us to get rid ourself of prestige and arrogance. He wanted us to get rid of shyness and hatred. He wanted us to be prepared to be able to survive in the most difficult circumstances, to be ready for any eventuality.

He gave the new programme called SPT to be followed by all family a’ca’ryas and WTs. The programme briefly required the following.

1. It would last seven days
2. It would be compulsory
3. Before you begin you must leave your personal belongings aside
4. Keep only one langota and a dhoti and a begging bowl.
5. Go out and beg your food (no cooked food to be accepted)
6. Say only Hari ‘AUM tat sat’ when you visit any body’s place for begging. No talks. Maintain mauna vrata
7. If for some reason you have to speak something to somebody, donot accept anything from him/her
8. Cook your food from whatever you get only once
9. No utensils are to be used except an earthenware pot
10. Spend all your time in Kiirtan/sa’dhana
11. Use mud for cleaning your body and datuna (Neem or babula) for cleaning your teeth. No soap or toothpaste. No oil. No shoes. No comb. Sleep on the floor.
12. Under no condition you are to retaliate, even verbally, if abused or scolded on your begging tour. Keep your calm. Hari Aum tat sat is your only weapon
13. In the evening go out for Dharma pracara

I proceeded to Va’ranasi for my SPT from the Ranchi central office where I was posted. I was accompanied by Dada Ra’ma’nanda, Parashiva’nanda. Dada Dhiira’nanda and Prajina’nanda joined me in Varanasi training center. We followed the system as prescribed by Ba’ba’. We were given the prescribed uniform for SPT. Everyday in the morning we would leave at 7am and go the nearby villages for alms. We would return back and cook our meals on the fire to be prepared by each one of us. We did not know how soon the time passed. We used to go to the bank of Ganges and enjoy watching the waves and doing sa’dhana’ there on the Dasha’samegha Gha’ta.

Once while on the alms tour I had to face a person who scolded me without even looking at my face. I immediately left that place. It only made me more humble and devoted to Him. The evening time doing Dharma Pracar was very stimulating. We used to meet many good people. A few came forward to get initiated. SPT is great boon for developing devotion, humility and surrender.

Seminar

After my eight months stay in Bhopal as DS, I went to Ranchi to attend RDS. It was there that I was transferred to center as Social Security Secretary. Those days Ba’ba’ had initiated seminar programme. All the workers and margiis throughout India got themselves busy in making the programme a success. Seminar was declared as compulsory duty for every margii. Occasionally Ba’ba’ also visited the venues of seminar in the vicinity of Ranchi. The popular seminar slogan was – seminar seminar more and more seminar, still more seminar, village level seminar. Later Ba’ba’ included seminar as a part of sixteen points to be followed by margiis.

I was new as a central worker. There Ba’ba’ gave me the responsibility to organize the seminar in Maharashtra state with Dada Jagdihsvara’nandajii as the trainer. Before departing for their respective seminar destinations all of the central workers were summoned to Ba’ba’s room. Ba’ba’ told us that in the provincial seminar the minimum strength should be 500, otherwise defaulters will be punished. Those days there used to be one seminar for the entire state. This used to be followed down to the village level. The state level seminar in Maharashtra was scheduled to take place in Kolhapur situated in the western Maharashtra. Organizing 500 margiis for the seminar in Maharashtra was quite a task. I visited each district headquarter with widely distributed pockets of margiis in the vast territory of the state. I met the margiis individually. I tried to convince them and inspired them expressing the urgency assigned by Ba’ba’ to attend the seminar. With the constant pursuation & hard work of 2 months we could barely manage 350 margiis to attend the seminar there.

I returned back to Ranchi after the seminar was over. The organizers and the trainers of each state level seminar stood there before Ba’ba’ and read out their reports. Those of us who could not manage 500 margiis to attend the seminar were reprimanded. The punishment to the defaulters was to do 1000 sit-ups in 24 hours. We got perplexed. We had no option except to follow His command. Most of the workers left after finishing in between 50-100 situps. The condition of dada Shraddha’nanda was indeed deplorable due to his advanced age. I felt pity for him. I took Ba’ba’s instruction like Mantramu’lam. I continued even after coming out of His room. Those days I would go to the near by cremation grounds for Kapalika sadhana’ almost every midnight. Before my Ka’palika sa’dhana’ I completed my share of 300 situps. As soon as I completed the situps I heard a loud voice of Pranava’nanda jii calling me that Ba’ba’ has condoned my punishment

and that I should not do situps anymore. But by that time I had already completed 700 situps. For frail and weak body of mine what I had already doen was too much. My nerves got stiffened and I started experiencing excruciating pain. I cried out due to the unbearable suffering. I could neither stand nor sit. Even morning duty became a punishment. I felt vexed at the unkindness of my Guru. The same day Ba’ba’ called me and affectionately consoled me by saying that you should not have done so many situps. I was further confused hearing those words. But my physical condition became deplorable day by day. I used to cry in pain and curse Him like a child in rage for causing me so much suffering.

Once again I was given the responsibility of organizing a state level seminar in Ajmer in the state of Rajasthan. In the same condition I proceeded to Jaipur. But my physical condition did not show any signs of improvement. I had difficulty walking and could not sleep. A’c. Manohar laljii’s family served me well. But to meet the ma’rgii I went to Tonk. After I arrived there my condition further deteriorated. I was treated in a hospital by a renouned doctor who operated the the hard tumour in my thigh. In a matter of 25 days I was cured of the trouble. But those 25 days were spent like hell.

From there I returned back to Ajmer and got busy with the preparations of seminar. I started begging Ba’ba’ to relieve me from the central posting. I was finding it difficult to discharge my responsibility as a central worker. As I was thinking, about the same time Dada Sada’nanda arrived there from Ranchi to relieve me. He was sent by Ba’ba’ and advised me to immediately proceed to Ranchi. I handed him the charge of seminar and left for Ranchi.

After my arrival in Ranchi, Ba’ba’ called me in His room and filled my heart with unspeakable love and bliss. He told me that when you used to call me crying out miserably in pain, I would feel your pain. Saying so, Ba’ba’ embraced me. This made me cry uncontrollably like a child. He said that each person must subserve its own samskara. Then He told me that He was now posting me in a cool city in south. I was delighted at everything that was happening.

His concern and seriousness about seminar was evident from the fact that He made it a condition that whosoever wants to attend His darshana or accompany Him in field walk must have necessarily attended seminar.

### Initiation of Ba’ba’ Na’m Sam’kiirtana in Amajharia, Netrahat near Ranchi

It was the year of 1971. Continuing the tradition of designating each year by a new programme, the year 1971 was called the the year of UKK (Utilisation, Katha, Kiirtan). It was on similar lines like the year 1970, which was designated by Seminar and the year 1969 as Sa’dhana’ Vars’a - the year of spiritual bliss. Ba’ba’ gave practical

demonstration of the theoretical concepts of A’nanda ma’rga philosophy. Through these demonstrations He provided the much needed scientific foundation to the spiritual sa’dhana’ (practices). He demystified the esoteric elements of Yoga and Tantra Sa’dhana’. His demonstrations freely gave experiences of many different kinds of Sama’dhis – a number of them being quite unknown even in the sa’dhaka sama’ja.

Until October 1970 there was no system of Kiirtana in A’nanda ma’rga. Although Ba’ba’ did speak of the importance of Kiirtan. He used to quite often quote some of the few Samskrta shlokas widely known as Shiks’a’st’aka (eight shlokas composed by Maha’prabhu) of Caitanya. Spiritual life of those days demanded strict discipline and austere and rigourous life at the command of the Guru. As an aberration there were many dogmatic practices in vogue among the disciples. There used to be absence of a rationale behind sa’dhana’ practices. Ba’ba’ inherited this social spiritual environment. Accordingly He gave revolutionary new concepts to liberate human mind from the untoward influences of superstition, blind faith and dogma. But this was not enough He intended to suffuse the human mind with sweetness of devotion. It was precisely this background in which the philosophy of A’nanda Ma’rga was born.

It was the month of September of the year 1970. I was then posted as a central worker in Ranchi. Ba’ba’ desired to proceed to an isolated place with His family for a break from His daily hectic schedule. GS dada Sarveshvarananda with the help of other margiis and workers located a place in the forest having natural scenic beauty and lovely weather. Ba’ba’ immediately agreed and the government guest house of the forest department in that location called Amajharia was reserved for Him. He had desired that His visit to the picturesque surroundings where He was going to stay be kept secret to avoid crowding. Then Ba’ba’ proceeded with his family to the designated location. His PA and a few of the security guards also accompanied Him. Howsoever, Dada’s might try the place did not remain hidden to the margiis.

Some of them reached there and stayed there to enjoy Ba’ba’s morning and evening walks in that beatific and adorable natural surroundings. Ba’ba’ continued to stay there and enoyed the picturesque natural setting.

It was one fine evening of 7th October. Ba’ba’ was sitting on a raised floor with a few devotees outside the guest house. It was a peaceful mystic evening. An unparalleled natural beauty surrounded the place. All of a sudden Ba’ba’ broke the silence. He pointed to distant sky and said that it appears as if a sweet melody is being carried by the air. Try to listen carefully. After a while all the sa’dhakas present there in one voice spoke atonce in amazement. They told Ba’ba’ that they were hearing the melody of ‘Ba’ba’ Na’ma kevalam’ samkiirtana. To this He asked whether they knew from where the melody was coming and who was singing? No singer was however visible. Ba’ba’ continued and told them that the melody of Ba’ba’ na’ma kevalam’ kiirtan was emerging

from the space above. The kiirtana is being sung by the Gandharvas and Kinnaras. Some time elapsed while all enjoyed the music of Kiirtan. Ba’ba’ broke the silence and asked how would it be if a 24 hour Akhanda Kiirtana was arranged tomorrow. All those present instantly agreed.

A’c. Brahmadevaji – a local family a’ca’rya was one among the many sa’dhakas present. He took up the task of informing margiis living in the towns and villages near Amajharia. Next day there was a flood of ma’rgiis arriving there. As directed by Ba’ba’ a Pandal was erected and the place was decorated by the forest flowers and mango plant leaves. Kiirtana was started with full devotional fervor. Ba’ba’s presence added to charm of the sweet na’ma kiirtana. Margiis sang the kiirtan in their local tunes and devotional flow was created affecting all. About same time as the kiirtana was coming to an end, the kiirtan at that moment had reached its crescendo, suddenly to everyone’s surprise Ba’ba’s car started to move towards the Kiirtan Pandal. Ba’ba’s son Gaotama who was playing outside saw the car moving. He shouted and called out Ba’ba’ name & His PA rushed in. Ba’ba’ looked at the car and all of sudden the car came to a halt. Later He told the ma’rgiis that impelled by the attraction of the Na’ma Samkiirtana even the crude objects, plants, animals, birds are vibrated. None can remain unaffected.

Ba’ba’ returned back after the akhanda kiirtan to Ranchi. It was the evening darshana on the occasion of Diipavali, Ba’ba’ said now the Kiirtan has been empowered for the spiritual benefit of humanity. It is your duty now to propagate Kiirtan throughout the world.

### Bangalore posting

I could not bear the pressure of work in the centre. Especially the punishment meted out for faulting His dictum turned out to be too much for my frail body. I started mentally requesting Him to relieve me from the central posting to some peaceful location where I could work for His mission. Being the Lord of mind He got me relieved from the seminar duty when I was in Ajmer, Rajasthan. Dada Sada’nanda was rushed as my reliever to Ajmer. Dadajii carried a message for me to immediately return back to Ranchi. I reached there. At Ranchi I was fortunate to have received His affection & then I was transferred to Bangalore as DS.

Yatishvaranandaji was my RS Bangalore. He got me introduced to the local margiis and briefed me with the activities of the diocese. After arriving there I realized as Ba’ba’ had said that I was being transferred to a city with airconditioned climate. Bangalore was exactly like that. The temperature there did not vary much throughout the year. It was a beautiful town. Each locality has its garden.

At that time a A’nanda ma’rga school with 60 children was being run in a rented place in Rajaji nagar. There was also a children’s home with 10 children. There was a pressure to pay for the monthly rent of the school building. For the maintenance of the children’s home I contacted local bodies and thus I was able to arrange for sufficient food, vegetables and fruits for the children’s home.

After some time dada Keshva’nanda jii was posted there as RS replacing Yatishvaranandaji. Dada Tadbhava’nanda, Karuna’nanda, Nigma’nanda and Maha’viira’nanda were already posted there. We all enjoyed being together. Our relationship with the margiis was also very cordial. Lalji and his family, Sarvashrii Ramayyajii, Dr. Chari, Venkateshmurti, Kamleshjii and Ramujii were very devoted margiis. They were always ready to help. Every month we used to feed 250 poor people. There was good margii activity. Many new people came in contact.

However, there was monthly burden of paying the monthly rent of the school building. A ma’rgii had donated a piece of land near the HMT campus. I decided to build Ja’grti on that plot got into the construction. By His grace and my firm determination the Jagrti was built in record time. Ba’ba’ was very happy to learn about it. But my hard work had its toll on my health. I developed severe stomach trouble. I got Ba’ba’ advice for my illness and used the herbal medicine which cured me of my problem. Once again after a year or so I fell ill. I had excruciating pain all over my body. I had difficulty even in getting up from the bed. RS dada & REI dada took me to Patna for treatment. I got cured but it took two months of long drawn treatment. After I became healthy Ba’ba’ transferred me to Bhagalpur as DS. I joined my duty and relieved Dada Tyagiishvara’nandajii. I continued to enjoy the bliss of His compassionate grace.

### Bhagalpur posting

I came to Bhagalpur as DS in 1972. Bhagalpur has been a leading town in the history of A’nanda ma’rga. It was the home of many senior family a’ca’ryas viz. Pashupatijii, Anantajii, Krsna’nandajii, Gaoriishankarjii and Deviicandajii. All these A’ca’ryas worked hard for the propagation of the ma’rga. I was lucky to get to meet them and to know many of their experiences of Jamalpur days. Being the biggest town of Aunga Desha near Jamalpur, Bhagalpur has been Ba’ba’s Karmabhu’mi. In Bhagalpur Diocess there were 60 active units of the ma’rga. I would travel the year round in these units to meet ma’rgiis and to convey the new programmes given by Ba’ba’. At that time Ba’ba’ was in Bankipur central Jail, Patna. Moving in the units was quite a hard work. Anandapur High school was also located in this diocess. Ba’ba’ had personally contributed to initiate the school project. This place was the attraction of margiis. They used to get their children admitted in this school hostel for studies.

Our school was located on the banks of Ganges near Sundarvati Mahila College. It was good location for sa’dhana’. Being an old margii center it had many opponents. It was considered to be a problem diocess in the organisation. I learnt from Keshavanandajii that I was deputed by Ba’ba’ to speed up the margii activities here. It was a very comforting news for me since Ba’ba’ considered me capable of improving the situation here.

In Bankipur jail Ba’ba’ was poisoned by the CBI. In protest Ba’ba’ went on long fast. Margiis and workers were worried about the ill effect of His fast on His weak body. Some of the Sanyasins commited self immolation in protest for His release. Those who took recourse to such acts were great devotees of Ba’ba’. They were Dada Divya’nanda, Dada Dineshvara’nanda, dada Atula’nanda and many others.

In protest of the constant harassment of Ba’ba’ and other margiis in the Jail the margii society turned aggressive. A stream of protest raillies and meetings took place in Delhi and Patna against the state and central government. I participated with hundreds of ma’rgiis in a rally to gherao Bihar state assembly and chief minister’s residence and in Delhi to protest before the Parliament and at the India gate.

Once after the poisoning of Ba’ba’ in Bankipur Jail, we took out a protest rally and gheraoed the residence of the Chief Minister Abdul Gafoor Khan. He ordered police lathi charge on the unarmed workers and the ma’rgiis. Workers and margiis were badly beaten and more than 80 of them got arrested on false charges and jailed in Fulbari Sharif Jail. Interestingly we symbolically established sadvipra sama’ja in the jail and published a daily news letter. Later we were released on bail. The compaign to get the release of Ba’ba’ was accelerated throughout the world. Many workers immolated outside India. This was a period of terrible turmoil in the organization.

When dada Dineshvara’nandajii immolated himself in Old forte of Delhi, thousands of margiis rallied in New Delhi. Dada Dineshvara’nanda jii was my intimate friend. He was a dedicated worker and was bent upon getting Ba’ba’ released. A day before he presented me his bag saying please keep this as my memorabilia, since this may be our last meeting. I took it lightly but comforted him. Next day when I heard of his self immolation, I regretted for not having been serious. The same thing repeated with Dada Atulananda almost the same way. I had no clue. Dadajii was determined to immolate himself. He wanted to highlight the suffering of Ba’ba’ in incarceration perpetrated by an enmical government. Contrary to the Indian government the governments around the world were moved by the sacrifice of these great devotees of Ba’ba’.

About the same time dada Tya’giishvara’nandajii was arrested in a conspiracy in Bhagalpur and was jailed. At the instance of CBI he was subjected to inhuman torture. Ultimately he died in jail. Ba’ba’ was pained at his death. He did mention that

Tya’giishvara’nanda was brutally tortured. Every part of his body was impaired as a result of the brutalities of the police. I was equally broken on hearing the news of his death. I lost my composure and my own confidence was badly shaken. I haven’t known anyone like him. He was a dedicated but a silent worker. One rarely comes across such a man of extraordinary integrity.

During my posting in Bhagalpur I had desired for a DMC there. But it did not materialize until 1979 until Ba’ba’ was released from Jail. I was sent by Ba’ba’ as the central organizer to Bhagalpur to arrange the DMC programme there. Thus He fulfilled my desire. We received Ba’ba’ with our hearts filled with devotion and love in Madhu Aungika’-Marga Guru Residence.

After Bhagalpur I was posted in Lucknow. Before joining my next posting, I got to meet Him in jail. I was deeply moved seeing the physical condition of Ba’ba’. He caught hold of my hands and raised my spirits. He asked me to keep coming. I wanted see Him released.

In Bhagalpur I had many new experiences. I watched the turmoil in the organization. I continued to receive His grace.

### Arrested in MISA & lodged in Lucknow prison

During my posting in Lucknow as DS, the then Prime Minister Mrs Indira Gandhi promulgated emergency on 26th June 1975. The leaders of opposition parties were arrested and put behind the jail. Organizations like RSS & Jamaite Islam & A’nanda Ma’rga were banned and their volunteers arrested. It was a very critical situation.

It happened to be a morning that fateful day in my school. I was busy admitting new students to my Kinder Garten school. Suddenly I noticed that the school building was surrounded by the police and then the police came inside the campus and arrested me under DIR. I was then sent to Lucknow central jail. There I met many margiis and volunteers of other organizations and leaders of many political parties. Slowly I got reconciled to the idea of living in jail. I developed friendship with other inmates and started helping each other to live comfortably in the Jail.

Then all of a sudden 14 of us alongwith some of the top leaders of the political parties were transferred to Adarsha Jail of Lucknow. All of us were afraid of what the government could do to us. Many had lost hope of future. People got worried about their families. I took upon myself the duty to console them. I used to tell them that we have not committed any mistake. One day the sins of those who are behind these wrong doings will be exposed. Then we will be released. I realized that for want of faith in God,

the leaders of whatever background or social status they might have come suffered from fear of an uncertain future.

I found the opportunity as God sent. Hence I devoted myself in intense sa'dhana', & kiirtan and a'sana. The deputy superintendent of Prison assigned me the responsibility of the library. This gave me an excellent opportunity to study many good books. One day an interesting event took place. I was meditating under the Banyan tree near the site where prisoners used to be hanged. While I was in deep meditation a squirrel fell from the tree on my lap. The sudden fall of the squirrel caused my body to react violently and I jumped of my own accord. One of the security men who was watching me later told other jail staff that swamijii's body was levitating in air during sa'dhana'. This news spread everywhere and the jail staff and prisoners developed respect for me. Generally all the people in the jail were much impressed with my way of life of sa'dhana', sattvika food habits and sweet & smiling behavior. The jail doctor was also impressed with me and recommended special diet for me.

Later after lifting DIR the government enforced MISA on us. Other political leaders were enjoying B class facility excluding we Ma'rgiis. This frustrated me. I thought and prayed Ba'ba' that these leaders have their relatives to support them from outside but for us you are the only one. Without my asking one of the senior political leaders approached the collector for upgrading me to B class. In a matter of three days I also received the same facilities. This is how Ba'ba' took care of his children.

After a few days we were again shifted to the City Jail. There were about 600 political prisoners there. We used to live helping each other. Occasionally we will put pressure on the jail administration to provide us more facilities. Many people got impressed by A'nanda Ma'rga way of life and got initiated. We were eight margiis in the jail and were regular in daily collective meditation and kiirtan. The surplus food saved by us on the fasting days was used by us for Narayan seva of the poor prisoners every month. Workers of socialist party, BLD and an old congress man would join us in nagar kiirtan regularly.

We were now receiving news from outside, including Ba'ba's Vanii. Then one day we received the disturbing & shocking news that Ba'ba' has been awarded life imprisonment. Soon after I had a dream in which Ba'ba' appeared and consoled me and told me not to worry.

After a few months emergency was lifted and Mrs Gandhi announced for parliamentary elections. Soon we were all released from jail. After that we got busy with recovering our properties from the government custody. The leaders who were with us in the jail helped us whole heartedly in this new situation. Our disciplined life style had a overpowering influence on the minds of the party workers and leaders who watched us in the jail.

### Tantrika Parvas

This scary event took place in Lucknow - the capital city of Uttar Pradesh. Even now when I remember that incidence, it sends a shiver down the spine. It involved two of us there, me & another a'carya. It was time for our Ka'pa'lika sa'dhana'. We wanted to go to a remote cremation ground this time.

We came to know of one such location and decided to reach there. We were staying in the A'shram of a famous Ta'ntrika. From his A'shram that place was completely isolated and also quite far away. It was the darkest night of the year being Shravani Purnima'. Tantrikas have a special significance for the day. Obviously most Tantrikas chose to visit the burial ground to perform their rituals for attaining some occult powers.

Although the weather was unfavourable, we proceeded to the destined place on our bikes. It was a windy night. We reached there about half an hour late. It had just started to rain. Just about the same time we sat down for our sa'dhana', Dark clouds had already gathered all over the sky and to make it worse there was heavy lightening. Despite the weather being unfavourable, we were determined to complete our night sa'dhana'.

As we were approaching the cremation ground, the whole place gave a scary look. Eerie sounds filled the area. It made us shiver in fear & we moved while catching hold of each others hand out of that macabre fear. Though we were afraid, we somehow tried to keep our poise. We talked between us about the haunted place & the petrifying experience we were having. We had a premonition that unknowingly we getting caught into some wicked plan. Just when we were talking we once again heard those bizzare voices. That horrendous environment almost paralysed us. We felt helpless and could not decide what to do next. However we took a firm resolve that come what may be we will not return back and would stay together & face the situation. Even if we were to meet with death here as a result of some wicked Tantrika's heinous plot, we would prefer to die rather than quit. Our first reaction out of fear was to return back but then we regained faith in the supreme power of our Guru. We were determined that it would be cowardice to return and would reflect badly on us. Thus we resolved to move on and be ready to face the consequences. We had the internal conviction about the certainty of receiving Baba's help, when needed. Having thus resolved, we kept walking towards and eventually reached the center of the burial ground.

It was here that we found ourselves surrounded by hundreds of bizarre beast like humans of huge size and shapes. All of them carried iron rods. These beastly beings were running here & thither shouting at the peak of their voices calling out to catch us

and kill. This scene was unimaginable & unbelievably scary and made us involuntarily tremble & thus loose our composure in gruesome fear.

We had learned that the location was notorious for the thieves & dacoits. Looting was a common occurrence there. We thought that the villagers must have mistaken us for those dacoits and we must face them with a cool mind. We have to convince them that we are sanyasins having come over here for our spiritual practice. But none of those spooky looking people were coming close to us. They were just running all over and screaming at high pitch. This continued for an hour. Our time for sa’dhana’ was fast passing, but we could not possibly decide to perform sa’dhana’.

Tired of all the sinister scenes we sat on the floor. Those spine chilling screams continued albeit infrequently. After a while lo! we started to behold a group of unusual looking people moving on bikes. These people vanished after crossing the railway line into the nearby village. This bizarre scene continued for half an hour. Still unsettled and undecided we realized it was getting 2am and we were left with little time for sa’dhana’. At last we resolved to first finish the most important task in hand one by one. I took the position as a guard and the other a’ca’ryajii went for his sa’dhana’.

After he had somehow peacefully finished his sa’dhana’, I proceeded to perform mine. No sooner I started my sa’dhana I found myself engulfed in a cloud of wild fire. Now I was seated in the midst of fire. But to my surprise there was no burning sensation. I was afraid of fire but slowly regained confidence as I kept repeating his name. I soon realized that it was all a play of some avidya’ ta’ntrika who intended to scare me away. There was no way fire could be produced in such a desolate place. As I was thinking, the fire soon moved and vanished into the village nearby in a matter of ten minutes.

As I was overcoming from the shock of the fire vanishing, I heard the sound of ripples of water, as if, somebody was swimming in the nearby pond. It appeared to me that someone in white kurta pyzama was swimming in the pond. Slowly that that wicked person, perhaps the ta’ntrika started to move towards me. He stopped at a distance of about 30 feet and in then lo! he has turned himself into a ghastly buffalo of huge shape & size charging towards me. It pointed its angry eyes towards me and started digging the earth by his foot & screamed at me but did not move. My whole body trembled involuntarily out of deadly fear. I thought for a while that if the buffalo were to really charge on me it would surely kill me. But then all of a sudden, I do not know how, I regained composure and confidence.

Having failed to scare me away this way, the ta’ntrika started mesmerizing me by applying the force of his wicked incantations. He changed many poses & postures & tried to cast his spell to intimidate me. This caused burning sensation all over my body. However in the midst of all this trepidation I continued to receive His grace and went

ahead to complete my Sa’dhana’. But as I was done with my sa’dhana’ I found myself virtually paralysed with fear unable to move out of that place. Then I thought of singing kiirtana. Hearing my kiirtana the other brother Dada, who was waiting for me at some distance, came near me. I enquired from him if he did notice any abnormal thing. He quickly replied that he had seen me engulfed in fire.

As we travelled on our way back from that desolate place towards the town, on the way we met a group of people on the roadside. Pointing towards us they were talking amongst themselves that these monks possessed occult powers far beyond all of us. They have must be the disciples of a powerful guru. They kept speaking that they failed to scare us despite using all their tricks. On our part all through our stay in that eerie place, we were terribly afraid and anxious, kept trembling in fear.

Soon after I let the incidence known to Dada Shraddhanandajii and requested him to mention it to Baba, when Ba’ba’ was informed He simply laughed at the whole thing. After a few days I got the chance to see Him in person. As I got up after sa’staunga prana’ma, He inquired about the incidence. Then He told me that the Avidya Ta’ntrika that night had used all his force and even applied his macabre Ma’ran’a mantra to kill me, But he could not cause any harm since you all are vidya’ sa’dhaka.

### Cremation grounds of Bhopal

In Bhopal there occurred another incidence of similar nature. Our organization was developing fast there. Many young and enthusiastic margiis were actively working for the organization. They had developed interest in learning more and more lessons of sa’dhana’ and other practices. They also knew my schedule of visiting the cremation grounds every new moon day. We had our school in the newly constructed area of Hindustan Electricals Limited. The cremation ground was located 10 kilometer away from our school on the other end of the town. We had to cross the town to reach there. Margiis used to regularly accompany me for night pu’ja’.

This was an absolutely desolate cremation grounds in the woods. It was a dark drizzling night. In all we were holding on to a bizarre & scary sight in the cremation grounds. As I started my sa’dhana’ after encircling the margiis who had accompanied me to the cremation grounds that night, I heard a very tormenting scream from them. It was, as if, someone was being offered at the altar by slitting the neck. Margiis were imploring me to come to help & save them.

I got very disturbed and could not quite understand what exactly was happening to them. But without losing time I rushed to the spot where I had left the margiis. No sooner I reached there margiis were in a strange & pitiable condition. All three of them

were inside the circle holding each other tightly. They were terror struck and their body was involuntarily trembling. They were damn scared. I assured them not to worry since I had come. They were unable to speak even when I repeatedly asked them about the happening.

After a while having regained mental balance by my assurance and inspiration they, somehow, became normal. One of them then narrated the full account of the event. As soon as I had departed from them for my sa’dhana’, they sat down facing each other inside the circle. All of a sudden a large piece of flesh fell between them from the sky above. It got divided into many pieces on its own and each one started jumping and dancing producing eerie sound. This scared them and they got virtually petrified and started crying and screaming. They trembled in ghastly fear & lost sense.

As we were entering the cremation ground we did experience heaviness and a strange vibration. We saw a bull bellowing and making muuhhhrrr sound in the far corner of the cremation ground. But we ignored that incidence. Perhaps some avidya’ ta’ntrika was performing his ta’ntrika rituals there. He might have felt uneasy in our presence wanted to scare us. He must have used his avidya’ powers to create the eerie & scary incidence. He wanted to some how drive us away from the cremation grounds.

Incidence in the office of the Bhopal City Police Superintendent

I am impelled to write some of these occult experiences. I feel inspired as I write down these real happenings to encourage sa’dhakas on the path of spirituality. We are the fortunate recipients of an advanced esoteric system of Tantra sa’dhana – which aims at the expansion of human mind and liberating it from the all sorts of weaknesses & bondages. Who-so-ever practices this system of sa’dhana’ diligently is sure to experience bliss. There is no doubt that one acquires tremendous mental strength and strong determination as a result of regular practice. This not only makes us physically & mentally strong but also protects us from the evil forces.

My early life was strewn with many physical and consequential mental ailments. But my spiritual urge brought me in close company of a family a’ca’rya of A’nanda Ma’rga. He inspired me on the spiritual path.

In just about a month’s time there occurred certain noticeable healthy changes in my persona. This change was at once remarkable. To my surprise not only that I got rid of my physical ailments but my mind also became strong. This encouraged me to sincerely continue with my sa’dhana’ with renewed strength. I also started experiencing spiritual bliss. My mind became sharp and relaxed. I used to spend all my free time for propagating dharma sa’dhana’.

Now as a worker I was appointed as the diocese secretary of Bhopal. This was my first posting in ERAWS department. I was asked to start a school there. By His grace I started a new school in HEL city area. There I was able to organize an active & strong team of margiis in Bhopal. Then I learned from the central office about Ba’ba’ arrest in Coochbehar by the CPM government of West Bengal. Margiis were shocked to learn on learning the unexpected news. We decided to organize a peaceful resistance and a public meeting to decree the undemocratic action of dictatorial Marxist’s government. About the same time due to some other incidence there arose a tension between the Hindu and Muslim communities of the town. Hence we were not permitted to organize any public meeting.

After a few days I accompanied a local margii to visit the office of the police superintendent to apprise him of the need to organize a rally and sought his permission. We assured him that it would be a peaceful event and will not create any law and order situation.

The police officer was an experienced and impressive person. He was also the president of the Mesmerism Society for Western India established by the famous magician Shrii PC Sarkar. Seeing me in orange clothes he started praising his own knowledge of Tantra. He said that he can control anybody by the strength of his black magic.

In the beginning of my talk with him I was disinterested and thought it fit not to get involved in his bashful utterances. My purpose of visit was to get his permission for the rally. But he continued to push his own ideas of Tantra, Hypnotism and magic. I continued to listen to him in rapt attention. In the end I expressed my opinion and said that I do not believe in avidya tantra since it is a waste of time and does not lead to higher spiritual attainments. Suddenly I noticed that his face has turned red and he became serious because he considered my statement as a challenge to his belief.

I was seated in front of him and a margii brother was by my side. He started his process to hypnotize me. His body began trembling and he started to sweat profusely & his face turned yellow. It appeared as if he was totally exhausted and had exhausted his powers in vain. I felt pity on him and wanted to help him regain his lost poise but he felt dizziness. I got alarmed at the bizarre incidence. After some effort he regained his composure and sought forgiveness for his intrusive act. I was quite surprised at the incidence.

Later he called his assistant and dictated an order to allow us to hold a rally. He also invited me to come and visit him at his residence. After two days a deputy superintendent of police who was initiated by me happened to meet the police superintendent. He told my shiks’a’ bhra’ta’ that he had tried in vain to hypnotize me but

suffered the reaction out of his uncalled for act. He also mentioned that A’nanda ma’rga sa’dhana’ is very powerful. I felt immensely satisfied and thought how lucky I was to have received such a divine gift from Baba without any special achievement to my credit. Also that He is always there to covertly or overtly help us, whenever we need him to fight over our internal or external weaknesses.

### Meeting a avidya tantrika during my student life.

I was initiated as early as 1961 in the beginning of my student life in high school. My a’carya was a family man. He was a brilliant person and a multifaceted personality. His Guru bhakti was adorable nay, unparallel. At the time of my initiation he had told me that I was fortunate to have become the disciple of Taraka Brahma. My acarya was himself a Ka’pa’lika and and also encouraged me to accompany him to the cremation grounds in the night. After coming in his contact the fear of ghosts and spirits vanished from my mind.

I used to the cremation grounds with my friends to perform sa’dhana’. This way I was gaining self confidence. One day a known avidya tantrika challenged me that I will not be able to burn the stove today as he would not allow that to happen by his powers. But contrary to his claims I was able to burn my stove and prepare my breakfast which quite surprised him. Later he got initiated and lost all his powers achieved through avidya tantra.

### Strange experiences in Hazipur cremation grounds

This relates to an incidence of 1966. I had become an avadhu’ta. Being new to Kapalika sa’dhana’ i had some an extra zeal for the practice. I enjoyed doing kapalika sa’dhana in a isolated cremation grounds whenever I found an opportunity. I was then posted in north Bihar. A senior family aca’rya & kapa’lika Ksitija jii was then posted in Hazipur.

On a new moon day, I accompanied A’c. Ksitij for kapalika sa’dhana’ to one Kanaora Ghata cremation grounds. In that place another avidya tantrika was already busy performing kapalika sa’dhana’. As we arrived there he started using his powers to drive us away. We then got seated on the banks of Narayani Gand’aka River & started performing our sa’dhana’. He tried to apply his powers by splitting the mud on the bank of the river before us. This disturbance, however, did not scare us and we managed to finish our sa’dhana’. As we were continuing our sa’dhana’ in our usual manner, we noticed that he felt frustrated as his occult powers could not harm us anyway. Finally he left the place. This was another occassionwhen we found that the Avidya’ ta’ntrika could not harm us.

On our way back to our surprise we noticed a mad elephant, has broken its chains, was running trumpeting in and around the dense forest. It was indeed a scary sight.

### Meeting Roti Ba'ba'

I remained confined in Lucknow Jail for 18 months during the period of emergency. I was released in April 1977 soon after the emergency was lifted. There after I had the previledge of meeting two elevated & well known sa'dhakas.

One of them was Roti Ba'ba'. He used to reside in a remote village of Raibareilly. He was known among the people of that region as Subhash Chandra Bose in disguise. A new ma'rgii told me about him. Since then I wanted to meet him. Later I went with that margii brother and A'c. Apurva'nanda Avt. We proceeded to his place to meet him. It was in the early morning when we arrived to his ashram. We introduced ourselves as the sanyasins of A'nanda ma'rga. He welcomed us and took us in his personal room. We also got ourselves seated in front of him after offering our prana'ma to him. He started profusely praising Ba'ba' and said that you are very fortunate to have got A'nandamurtijii as your Guru. He will be very soon released from jail. None can confine Him there. You should remain sincerely devoted to Him. Your life will be successful.

He offered us fruits and sweets for the breakfast. He was very tall man (six feet plus) and handsome person. He was famous among the village people as a great Yogi. He used to stay bare body. The people of that area used to respect him and thought him to be Netaji Subhash babu.

### Meeting the famous Avidya Tantrika Bhu'tana'tha

Bhu'tana'tha then used to stay in his huge ashram near Chinhat a little away from Lucknow town. He was one among the famous Tantrka Gurus of his time. Shrimati Indira Gandhi also used to visit him occasionally. Swami Bhu'tana'th had given a gardand of Rudraksha which used adore her neck. Many known political leaders like her and also some senior government officers were regular visitors to his ashram.
