I reached his ashram alongwith Apurva'nandajii and sandiipa'nandajii. On being informed of our arrival he took us to his personal room and was very courteous and respectful towards us. He enquired about our welfare and also about Ba'ba'. We also paid our respects to him and thanked him for meeting with us. He said that your Guru will be released from the jail soon.

Looking at the two avadhutas accompanying me he said these persons will not continue for long with the Guru and will desert Him. I found his talks quite strange. Addressing me he said that you will stay with him till the end and will receive His grace. He had attained some occult power (karna Pishacinii Siddhi) that is why he could know the future and also read others mind. After some time both the avadhu’tas left the organization. What he had said turned out to be true.

### Saving life of a devotee bitten by a cobra

This incidence took place in the year 1978. It highlights His grace & compassion. After emergency I was released from jail. Soon I posted as RS Allahabad region and another brother Dada Laliteshvaranandajii as DS A’jamgarh. Dr. Ramchandra of Maow was a new margii but in a very short time had become a good sa’dhaka & devotee. He was a good margii praca’raka. He tried hard to establish a unit in Maow. Dharma Cakra used to take place in his house. His wife also took initiation soon after and she also developed as a good sa’dhika’. I started visiting their place regularly.

When days were just passing happily, suddenly one day a strange incidence took place. His wife was bitten by a cobra when she went to take the wheat flour out of the flour box for preparing rotis. She ran out of the kitchen and cried that she has been bitten by a snake. Her husband rushed to find out what had happened he saw a poisonous snake inside the box. Every member of the house was disturbed by the incidence and they called out for Ba’ba’ help. Slowly the body of the margii sister turned blue and she became unconscious. Members of her family continued to serve and treat her while continuing to call for Ba’ba’s grace. When the family members were busy taking care of her the margii sister in an unconscious state saw a dream in which ba’ba’ appeared and consoled her. He assured her that she would soon recover.

The following day she regained consciousness and her condition started signs of improvement. I happened to visit her house after a few days, I learnt of the incidence later from the same margii sister herself. She told me about how Ba’ba’ appeared to her in the dream while she was unconscious and saved her life.

### Leasing second life to Bindeshvarijii - Place: Patna Ja’grti, September 1978

On the momentous day of 2nd August 1978 Ba’ba’ was released from Patna Jail after the charges against Him were finally quashed by the Patna High court. Margiis from all over the world had assembled there at the jail gate to welcome Him. He was driven in a car to His residence in Patna. All through the route He was followed by jubilant margiis raising slogans of His victory in a procession. Margiis were enthused seeing Him. Ba’ba’ was, however, still reeling in poor health due to his nearly six year long fasting inside the Jail for His alleged poisoning by Jail doctor.

For many whole timers it was their first occasion to meet Him. Soon after He called for RDS. This was to take place after nearly eight long years of His absence. We all were excited and anxiously waited for that moment to have His audience. On learning about the proposed RDS I also reached Patna in time to attend the programme as RS Allahabad. Revered Ba’ba’ was busy taking report of workers inside the hall and outside the His room Bhaktapravar Bindeshvarijii was inspiring margiis by his contagious devotion. He would busy awakening kundalinii of those sitting near him. He would occasionally roar or burst into laughter taking Ba’ba’s name loudly. It was a mystic environment created there.

While RDS was in progress Ba’ba’ all of a sudden said that Bindeshvarijii is going to pass away after a short time although he is unaware of this. There was sudden wave of commotion and caused anguish and pain among the workers present there. Soon after that Ba’ba’ left the hall & proceeded to his personal room. We informed A’c. Ramtanukjii about what Ba’ba’ had mentioned. He was also taken aback and was equally pained to hear these words.

He immediately went to Ba’ba’s room and beseeched him to save Bindeshavrijii’s life as his worldly duties were still unfinished and there was none other in his family to take care of his young children. We also joined Ramtanukjii and pleaded to get Ba’ba’s favour to alter the destiny and to save Bindeshvarii dada. Baba’,however, responded in a natural manner saying birth and death were natural phenomenon and what he could do in such matters. While we continued to pray to Ba’ba’ to save dada’s life. In the mean time Ba’ba’ proceeded to the hall for his routine general darshan to margiis there. While general darshan was in progress, In the verandah Bindeshvarijii as foretold by ba’ba’ passed away. There was a commotion among the margiis. Everyone was affected and was in tears. A few ma’rgiis came inside the hall and informed ba’ba’ about the death of Bindeshvarijii. Pleading of distraught ma’rgiis to save him continued to mount on Ba’ba’. After a while Ba’ba’ said that if you can bring red flowers of Java’kusum then I can think of something to do for him. Then He instructed that Bindeshvarijii body should be brought inside the hall and kept near Him. In the meantime Ba’ba’s security guard Vishnudevajii brought the red Javakusum flowers from the compound of the Ja’grti and gave them to Ba’ba’.

For a while Ba’ba’ turned serious and started crushing the flowers between His palm and murmured some inaudible mantras and dropped a few drops of juice of the flowers (Javakusuma). As soon as the drops of flower juice fell on bindeshvarijii’s lips suddenly there was some movement in his body and there were signs of revival of his life force. Ba’ba’ then asked margiis to hold Bindevarijii’s feet and legs. Bindeshvarijii’s body started trembling. His process of inhalation and exhalation resumed and he tried to get up as if he had just awakened from sound sleep. This incidence brought smile on the

face of all those who were witnessing. Ba’ba’ had earlier told margiis not to reveal anything connected to Bindeshvarii’s death to him.

Ba’ba’ said He had extended Bindeshvarijii’s life by a few years. Those who witnessed the event felt blessed. Even today whenever I remember this incidence my body starts shivering and tears flow naturally gripping my mind by a overwhelming vibration of His non-causal grace and compassion. He is truly the Lord of death (Mrtyurmrtyu)

My first RDS after Ba’ba’s release from the Bankipur Jail

Venue: Patna jagrti and Ba’ba’s residence, September 1978

Ba’ba’ remained confined in Jail from December 29th 1971 until the noon of 2nd August 1978 on false charges labeled by CBI and govt. of India. He suffered in many ways and was also poisoned by the jail doctor at the instance of the political high command. Eventually He was honourably released after all the cases against Him were quashed by Patna High court. This was the most auspicious and thrilling moment in the lives of His disciples. His presence once again revived our devotion and freed all of us from the mortal fear.

To me personally, it was a moment of great rejoice. I felt tremendous joy when I was called for RDS in September 1978. I was urnestly looking forward to have His personal audience and to receive His love. During RDS Ba’ba’ was very angry with the low organizational outturn and admonished us for that. I felt child like annoyance & pain on His face due to our neglect. I kept thinking why can’t He, being omnipotent, command us lovingly and get His work done. His mere wish can fructify all that He wishes. Was it necessary to reprimand His workers soon after coming out from the jail? I was disturbed as a child and while there one afternoon having finished my lunch I was resting while contemplating on this subject. About the same time I was called to rush to His residence immediately. I complied and entered His room and did sa’sta’unga pran’a’ma. No sooner I had got up after prostration Ba’ba’, He querried “why are you angry with me”? Being your Guru I have accepted very serious responsibility about my children. I have to take the role of a hard task master much against my own liking. He lovingly pulled my arm closer to Him and said do I like to punish all of you? I do it for your welfare. I never enjoy this. My love for you forces me to punish you for your welfare so that you are able to rectify your mistakes. I found that saying this Ba’ba’ was very much moved. I considered myself fortunate to have witnessed the compassionate form of my beloved Guru. I instantly decided and took a vow not to react in future over His reprimands.

I am deputed to inspect Joshimath School

As Regional secretary Allahabad Region, I learnt about the problem relating to a LFT who was posted then posted in Joshimath School. He called me and requested me to

visit his school. Considering the cold climatic condition of the place I did not go there, instead advised some other worker to visit him and attend to the problem. Although I had tremendous attraction for Himalayas, but I was afraid of the cold season there.

Once during RDS in the month of March (Periodic reporting session with Ba’ba’ named Review Defect & Solution) Baba called me in His room and enquired about Joshimath school. Ba’ba’ said that it is a difficult place and you must take special care of that school. After a while He asked me if I had personally visited the place. He was unhappy to learn that I have never been there. He instructed me to immediately proceed there and take stock of the situation and resolve the issues. Even during March it used to be quite cold in Joshimath, I thought I will not be able to stand the cold weather conditions there. Ba’ba’ being omniscient was able to read my mind and hence commanded me to proceed straight to Joshimath from here itself. As commanded by Him I immediately started for Joshimath via Rsikesha, Devapraya’ga, Rudrapraya’ga, Karn’apraya’ga and Nandapraya’ga. On the way for the first time in my life I was privileged to behold the heavenly natural beauty of Hima’layan region. Then proceeding along the incessant stream of the Ganges I went up to Joshimath from Haridva’ra. On my arrival there the dada posted there became jubilant seeing me. I took the opportunity and called for a parent teacher meeting and sought their help and support to resolve the problems and impediments in the smooth functioning of the school. The parents expressed their readiness and agreed to help. Thus the problems were solved once for all. The dada took me to the cave of Shamkara’ca’rya. There I also met, the then, Shamkara’ca’rya of Joshimath Swa’mi Sha’nta’nanda. He was fully acquainted with our school and the Dada incharge and carried a good impression of our educational activities.

I stayed there and enjoyed the natural scenic beauty of the place. From there I also went to Badrina’tha which was just ten kilometers away and then went up to Vishnupraya’ga. The beauty of the ice clad mountainous region there was hypnotic. I stayed there for four days and enjoyed doing Sa’dhana’. I had heard that Himalaya had been the land of sa’dhakas and realized masters. The place was still vibrant with spiritual fervor. Although it was a biting cold climate there still I survived without much difficulty and in the process I was able to overcome the fear of Himalayan cold weather. The volunteers posted in Joshimath were also inspired by my visit since there problems were also resolved. This small incidence inspired by Ba’ba’ taught me the lessons in dutifulness and discharging of responsibilities. He created such an environmental pressure to remove unwanted fear from my mind.

In the following RDS Ba’ba’ became very happy after learning about the improved situation of the Joshimath school and the support given by the parents.

### Resolving the Land dispute of Jarva’ (UP)

Government of UP leased out a piece of land to A’nanda ma’rga for starting a kindergarten school and a children home for destitute children in Jarva’. However later the government reviewed its decision & wanted to the property to be returned. Thus a problem arose. I informed the then GS about the reversal of the government decision. During the RDS Ba’ba’ called me and advised me to meet a particular officer in the government and told me to explain him my problem. He told me that that officer will be able help me in finding a solution. On my return I met the concerned officer who suggested me the way out and the problem was resolved.

### Land dispute in Allahabad

Like Jarva the Municipality of Allahabad had also leased a piece of land and a building for us to start a school of A’nanda Ma’rga. When emergency was promulgated in India in the year 1975, A’nanda ma’rga was banned and all its workers and margiis were arrested and put behind bar, the Additional District Magistrate captured the same plot.

After emergency was lifted in April 1977 the issue was taken up with government by us. But that officer was unrelenting. Dada Girijanandajii was the RS of Allahabad region at that time. He kept pestering the officer but he was not in a mood to oblige. Shri Satyaprakash Malviya was the minister in charge of the concerned department. We met him and explained him about the problem. He was fully aware of the matter and agreed to help us. He called the Additional District magistrate and advised him to return the leased land to A’nanda Marga. But that guy did not oblige. Finally the minister had to take a strong action of suspending that official and eventually got the land and school building back to us. Ba’ba’ was following this matter and was guiding Girijanandajii about the line of action to be followed. Later when I became the RS of Allahabad, He was instructed me to remain vigilant about margii properties so that they are maintained properly.

### Land and building of A’nanda ma’rga School in Azamgarh

This was our own property. However during emergency the state government allowed the Armed Police force to occupy the school building. They were not willing to hand over the property back to us. Girijanandajii informed Ba’ba’ and under His guidance he took help from the ministers concerned who were known to Girijanandajii and with their intervention the land was recovered.

# Chapter five

## Dharma Maha Cakra

I was fortunate to have got the opportunity to attend Dharma Maha Cakra (DMC) functions at many places. Of special importance were the two DMCs, one was held in Veterinary College Patna and the other in Allahabad. Simultaneously many more DMCs took place in Aurangabad, Daltonganj, Bhabhua & Ranchi which I could attend.

The DMCs most significant to me were those which took place after 1978. By that time I had a bit matured and was able to better appreciate His non-causal grace. Its climax occurred when He included me in Madhura Gos'thi. Thereafter I did not want to miss any opportunity to be near Him.

The speed of progress of Ma'rga got a tremendous boost after 1978. He toured whole of India and also the world in the year 1978-79 and released an unprecedented wave of devotional inspiration to all margiis through these DMCs. The devotional vibrations He sent out through His Vara'bhaya Mudra' pushed ahead the service projects launched by A'nanda Ma'rga throughout the Globe.

From 2nd August 1978 He stayed in Patna until 25th of October and then moved to Kolkata. Since then until the New Year DMC, he toured India and conducted four DMCs one each in Kolkata, New Delhi, Mumbai & Madras. Then after the new year DMC in Kolkata he toured all the important places throughout India and conducted 20 DMCs. This was a historical tour of ba'ba'. I had the privilege to attend 14 of these 24 DMCs and became the witness of his hypnotic charm & recipient of His unfathomable grace. I witnessed many of His demonstrations and was witness to some of His divine sport.

### Delhi DMC, November 1978

Thinking in retrospect when Ba'ba' was arrested on framed up charges on the 29th of December 1971, a CBI official had arrogantly remarked to Him that now you are arrested, your organization will crumble to pieces. Baba' in His characteristic style responded by saying that when I will be released from the Jail you will see that my organization has become 16 times more powerful.

On His release from Bankipur prison in Patna, the enthusiasm and festive mood of the Margiis was to be seen to be believed. Since then the progress of the ma'rga was unstoppable. Soon after He addressed four DMCs in Kolkata, Delhi, Mumbai & Chennai. I was then posted as the RS of Allahabad region. I received instructions to rush to Delhi on the occasion of DMC with all the Ma'rgiis of the region on the scheduled day. This message was welcomed by the Margiis who were eagerly looking forward to having Ba'ba's audience and personal contact since eight long years. All the Margiis and their families prepared and booked their tickets to be there in Delhi to attend DMC.

As I reached Delhi, I was called by Him to report good news. It was a cherished occasion of my life to be near Him and to serve Him. I enjoyed the blissful moments in His company. Ba’ba’ told me that He loved me a lot and since He loved me, He would utilize me as His medium. Some of His WTs have slackened in terms of work performance. This He said has happened due to lax administration due to the long years of His physical absence. Using me as His medium He will punish these erring WTs. Won’t you get disturbed by this? I replied in affirmative and then He asked me to go.

Soon after PA Dada called me and sent me in Baba’s bed room. Immediately then Ba’ba’ started admonishing me for the poor performance of the Diocess secretaries of Meerut, Bareilly & Jhansi for the schools there were sub-standard. The DS Meerut who was then standing outside His room was asked to come inside his room. Then Ba’ba’ began punishing the DS for his poor outturn and also me for not having discharged my responsibility being his immediate supervisor. This news of punishment created a stir among the WTs and this made them think and plan to improve their output.

Same evening and the following day he called all the WTs for RDS and pushed me for the faults of my subordinate WTs. He kept punishing me hard while at the same told the WTs present that He is forced to punish their RS because of their slackness & poor outturn. On hearing this all the DSs present there appealed Ba’ba’ not to punish the RS, since it wasn’t his mistake. They all promised Him that they will now be active and cover up the lost time.

Hearing this He relieved me but started punishing WTs one after the other. This had the desired effect and the WTs took vow to accelerate the speed of missionary work. During DMC Ba’ba’ released waves of devotional vibrations which affected all margiis who attended the DMC with tremendous spiritual inspiration and missionary zeal. I was lucky to have been used by Ba’ba’ as His medium for accomplishing His task of inspiring and reenergizing WTs.

### Bhopal DMC; February 1979

This was my second DMC since I had worked earlier as DS Bhopal where I had discharged the primary responsibility for organizing a DMC in 1969. In this DMC being a Sectorial worker of SDM I was supposed to prepare a group margiis for Kaoshikii & Ta’nd’ava dance performance before Ba’ba’. A’c. Narendra da’da’ attended this DMC with hundreds of margiis of Chattisgarh Sama’ja. Here I was given the task of arranging group meetings of Sama’ja workers and ma’rgiis with Ba’ba’ and thus received His love & grace in His close company.

### Kanpur DMC; March 1979

This DMC had a special significance for me because I was appointed as the central coordinator for the forthcoming DMC here. I had earlier worked in this region as RS and I had an intimate relation with local margiis. Kanpur had a good team consisting of active Ma’rgiis viz. Sarvashrii Arun Gupta, Upendra Jha, Jagdish Bhalla, Prof Kevaldhari, GL Sharma & Tomarjii.

For conducting the DMC there were certain conditions to be fulfilled

1. To have our own Ja’grti,
2. Own School,
3. MG Residence &
4. Own Printing Press.

Ma’rgiis were determined to make the programme successful. A printing Press was arranged immediately and work to fulfill other conditions was initiated.

Finally Ba’ba’ arrived at the Kanpur Airport as scheduled & was welcomed by the Margiis. For reasons of non compliance of certain conditions put up by Ba’ba’, He did not accept the food cooked by the Ma’rgiis of Kanpur. Later on when the Ma’rgiis gave written assurance to execute the promised tasks within a stipulated period acceptable to Ba’ba’, He happily took the food. Arrangement of food for Ba’ba’ was the responsibility of Arunjii’s family. All of them enjoyed serving Him the local delicacies.

The DMC programme went on very well. Margiis enjoyed Baba’s discourses which touched the inner core of the margiis.

Later He gave a special responsibility to me to contact the workers of the region who had left the organization and to inspire them to bring close to Ba’ba’. By His grace in this effort three workers came back to the fold of the Ma’rga. DMC was a grand success.

### Jammu DMC; July 1979

I realized that Ba’ba’ took special care to inspire and bless the margiis and He Himself worked hard to accelerate the progress of the Ma’rga despite being weak. He would call meetings with WTs and Ma’rgiis to speed up Ma’rgii activities and always involved them spontaneously & made them commit to accomplish the seemingly impossible targets.

In Jammu Ba’ba’ stayed at the residence of Dr. Sehgal. He was a devoted active margii. This was my first visit to Jammu. The DMC was very inspiring to me. Ba’ba’ showered His love and blessing on me on many occasions during His stay. I felt transported to the other world.

Ernakulum DMC, July 1979

From Jammu up in the north to the other end of south to Ernakulum, it was a very long journey by train. The system required every departmental or sectorial head/ WT to attend every DMC. Here I was responsible to conduct collective performances of Kaoshikii and Ta’nd’ava dances before Ba’ba’. Dada Ananya’nanda was my Area level WT.

We took up our specific tasks and started organizing our own departmental programmes soon after arriving there in Ernakulum, so that all preparation are completed for His grand welcome before He reached there. I was blessed to be called for His massage. While being there with Him I also gave some good news regarding ma’rgii activities in the region. Arrangement for ba’ba’s stay was done at the residence of Shrii Gopalkrishanjii. He was then serving as the District Sessions Judge in Ernakulum. His wife was also a great devotee of Ba’ba’. She was very happy to have got this special previledge to prepare His food with the help of other ma’rgiis.

A three hour Akhand’a Kiirtana was organized and I led the kiirtana with full devotional fervor with other ma’rgiis. I was very inspired since this was the first year of Madhur Gos’thi. I used to help ma’rgiis develop devotional sentiment for Him. Perhaps this is what we all always liked to do.

I was also responsible here to organize personal contact with Ba’ba’. A scholarly professor in the university was also in the queue. He had an inkling to test Ba’ba’. after the contact when He came out he appeared to be more confused. On the contrary a illiterate village folk native of Kerala was overwhelmed by His grace since Ba’ba’ had spoken to him in the local dialect of Malaylam language. He is the lord of devotees. He is unattainable by so called knowledge or erudition. Only devotional surrender can command His grace.

Ba’ba’s DMC speech was full of devotion. Devotees were delighted by the pious touch of their Lord. In workers meetings He gave an inspiring demonstrations on the convertibility of Tanma’tras viz. form, flow and smell.

From Ernakulum Ba’ba’ proceeded to Visakhapatnam via Madras. For the night He stayed with His retinue at the residence of Shrii Shiva Bhagwan Goenka.

Visakhapatnam DMC, July 18, 1979

I was then posted as the Delhi Sectorial Secretary PMSA. I was also moving with Ba’ba’ on His DMC tour. We came back to Madras from Ernakulum and had our night hault with Ba’ba’ at Shrii Shiva Bhagwan Goenka’s residence. We were to travel by train in the morning to Visakhapatnam and Ba’ba’ was to take His flight at about 9am.

I was a little unhappy since I would be reaching Visakhapatnam after His arrival and will not be present there to welcome Him. But strange though it may appear, Ba’ba’ missed His flight. Later we learnt that He got up a bit late after His morning Sa’dhana’ and when they reached the airport the destined flight had already left.

Ba’ba’ regretted for missing the flight and asked for immediate arrangements to leave for Visakhapatnam since His devotees were anxiously waiting there for Him. He repeated His anxiety on way to Visakhapatnam.

It is true that devotees desire to meet the Lord is immeasurable and so is the intensity of desire of the Lord to meet His devotees. Genuine love is reciprocal. That is why without caring for His own comfort of travelling in the car for such a long distance of 1000 kilometers He proceeded without losing time. On the way the car ran out of petrol. Since there was no Petrol Pump on the highway for next 60 kilometers, He asked His driver to proceed and the car ran without petrol until they reached the next Petrol Pump.

On the way at Tadipalligudam arrangement for His rest and snacks was done by His devotees, however he did not stop saying I must reach Vizag without any delay.

I reached there in the evening before Him and joined in the efforts of the margiis preparing for His welcome. Due to some misunderstanding the Bhukti Pradha’na and the accompanying volunteers missed Ba’ba’s car as He entered the Vizag town, However, He fulfilled my desire to welcome Him at the place chosen for His stay. He reached Vizag after 2am. Collective performance of kaoshikii & Tand’ava dances were presented before Him with lots of devotion & warmth.

That night Ba’ba’ called me and enquired about everything. I was overjoyed by His grace and continued to enjoy the bliss in Dhya’na until daybreak. That morning Ba’ba’ was ready in the morning after His breakfast for His morning walk. General darshana that morning was simply blissful. Devotees thronged the hall and waited anxiously for His darshan. Ba’ba’ began very softly and said that He had come from a far off place to see them. I have come here only for you. Everyone present there was anxiously waiting for that moment. Margiis were experienced bliss beyond their expectations. Ba’ba’s love engulfed everyone. Margiis virtually gulped every word uttered by Him like nectar.

The DMC programme was superb and he delivered His discourse on Trio in Spiritual progress. He concluded His discourse by blessing the devotees in these words “By the grace of the Supreme Lord you will easily surmount this dangerous Máyá. So even those ordinary human beings who take shelter in Parama Puruśa’s all-pervading compassion, are sure to attain the supreme excellence of spiritual grandeur; nobody has any reason to be disheartened. The Supreme Entity always call anybody and everybody, “oh human beings, oh painstaking human beings, don’t be afraid, don’t be

disheartened, I am for you, oh human beings, please come near me, you please come and sit on my lap, I am for you.”

A large contingent of Chattisgarh Samaja vahini came from Raipur led by A’c. Narendra Deva. This DMC was very special for me. The entire environment of DMC was densely suffused with the waves of devotion.

### Nagpur DMC, October 1979

After getting released from Bankipur Prison Ba’ba’ increased the speed of Ma’rgii activities manifold. He gave a new programme of opening Bio, Trio & Qudro offices. The concept of these new offices was make sure all workers other than those posted in ERAWS to stay together in these offices and not with Margii families anymore. This was His condition for conducting DMC that the places approved must have Bio, Trio offices.

On one hand He desired DMC to be conducted in Nagpur, but at the same time He put the compulsory condition of Bio-Trio offices in all Blocks. Central workers were asking the sectorial workers to give in writing that these offices have been already opened in all the Blocks. This was desired for Marga Guru to hold the DMC. When all sectorial WTs gave in writing through GS Ba’ba’ gave His permission to organize DMC in Nagpur. Soon after that Margiis and WTs devoted themselves for organizing the programme of DMC.

On the scheduled date He arrived in Nagpur. He was welcomed with Ta’nd’ava & Kaoshikii dances. After that Ba’ ba’ called a meeting with all the WTs and asked them whether Bio-Trio offices have been opened in all blocks. All of us responded in unison saying yes to His querry. He said if that is so why there are only a few ma’rgiis in Nagpur who have come here for DMC. We could not give a satisfactory reply. Then He got annoyed and became very serious. He said that you have invited me for DMC here telling me lies. I am going to call all margiis now and tell them that all of you sanyasins are a bunch of liars and I cannot hold DMC here under the situation. All of us were at our wits end.

He repeated that sanyasins should never speak lies. They should live life depending on Satya. Whole environment turned very gloomy and serious. All were shocked. He asked us to take vow toching the heart that we will never ever resort to falsehood in our lives. This was a matter of great education to all of us.

Thereafter He allowed DMC to be conducted in a very blissful environment. He released waves of devotion engulfing all those who attended the DMC. We WTs then left for Raipur and ma’rgiis returned to their respective destinations with renewed vigour to expand His mission.

Raipur DMC, 18th October 1979

The vibration of Raipur DMC was unique. Ba'ba' was welcomed with revolutionary fervor by more than few hundred volunteers of Chattisgarh Vahini at Mana Airport. Those were the hay days of days Chattisagrhi sama'ja. A'c. Naredra a devotee par excellence and a great organizer was its chairperson. Margiis were equally delighted and blessed by Ba'ba's presence.

Soon after His arrival Ba'ba' called a meeting of WTs and told them to complete the task of opening Bio-Trio offices in every Block and Panchayat. WTs had not forgotten the incidence of Nagpur, hence they started cracking and engaged themselves to accomplish and achieve their departmental targets.

I was blessed to get an opportunity to serve Him. He asked me to complete the process of Bhukti Pradha'na/Upbhukti Pradha'na elections in the entire region. I also started my work as directed.

Ba'ba' appeared to be extremely happy throughout the DMC in Raipur. A'c. Naredra, A'c. Ramlal Dani, A'c. Kailashchandra Bhalla organized the program considering everybody's needs. Wts also enjoyed the DMC & the arrangements.

The DMC was full of bliss everywhere. Ba'ba' inspired all by his loving words when He said "I want that the present distressed condition of Chattisgarh should come to an end, and it should have a very bright future. Let the people from other parts of the world come here and learn how far the people here have achieved progress, and in which ways they are going to bring about progress in other spheres of life. Let the light of Chattisgarh be ever effulgent, like a luminous star in human society, so that all may learn and be benefited by it." Departing Darshan made margiis and workers cry. The devotional flow was uncontrollable.

Varanasi DMC. October 30th 1979

DMC at Varanasi was historical in many respects. It was here that Ba'ba' introduced A'vartta Kiirtana as an essential part of Madhur Sa'dhana' and allowed it to be performed by all margiis. Since then A'vartta Kiirtana has become an essential part of collective meditation. It was here that Ba'ba' permited Avartta Kiirtan to be performed after His darshana. Collective performance of A'vartta Kiirtana creates such a wonderful environment involving all the participants to enjoy the blissful waves of devotion.

I was appointed as the central organizer for Varanasi DMC. I was trying to achieve the goals set by Ba'ba' for conducting the DMC here. Finally Ba'ba' arrived there at the appointed date and was welcomed by Ma'rgiis. I was blessed by His grace and got the opportunity to massage His feet.

Soon after His arrival Ba’ba’ called for RDS of WTs. Ba’ba’ got annoyed as the work done was unsatisfactory. He refused to accept the evening snack. Being the organizer I considered it my responsibility to please Him so that He will take something.

I entered His room and while massaging His feet I told Him about the arrangement of DMC and that the Ma’rgiis of Varanashi enjoyed the DMC function. Then I told him that today we have prepared Golgappa for the snack. He enquired if spicy water and potato with chat of peas was prepared as accompaniment. On my affirmation He allowed us to serve Him Golgappa. The arrangement was already made and Ravindraji’s of Gazipur with his family and Shashiprabha Didi were ready with all items at the door of Ba’ba’. Once He agreed I indicated to them to enter His room to serve Him. Ba’ba’ enjoyed the Golgappa served by Ravindraji with its accompaniments which being added by Shashiprabah Didi and Madhuri Didi. Baba’ relished golgappa and those serving Him felt immensely satisfied for having got this rare opportunity. I had once heard Him say that when He was a student He would occasionally take Golgappa. This impelled me to try and serve Him this delicacy.

On the 29th night I learned about a strange happening. Shrii Ashokjii, the Bhukti Pradha’na of Allahabad, was posted as security guard outside Ba’ba’s room. Late night I was woken up by him. He told me that he saw many strange people conversing with Ba’ba’ in His room. Strange though it was, yet I wondered about the veracity of the incidence. I questioned him and enquired how that was possible? He confirmed and told me that he was dead sure of what he had seen. I thought may be these strange people could be some luminous beings (deva yonis). They might have come there to meet Him and to seek some guidance. Usually Ba’ba’ would call His PA dada in the night asking him to arrange for some of His personal need. That night, however, He did not call His PA also.

Next day in the morning Ba’ba’ was ready quite early and came out of His room at 9am and asked the security incharge to call all the WTs immediately. Soon everyone complied and rushed to His room. As we entered His room we found Ba’ba’ was seated gracefully on His bed. His face was shining and the sweet smile was playing on his pink lips. After a while He explained the purpose of calling them. And then He described the process of A’vartta Kiirtana. This was followed by collective performance of A’vatta Kiirtana before Him. All of us enjoyed the new style of Kiirtana.

Later speaking about Madhur sadhana He said that from many corners he had been getting the report in which WTs & margiis were wanting to know why is Madhura Sa’dhana being confined to only a selected few. From today I allow all to perform A’vartta Kiirtan which has been hitherto an essential part of Madhur sa’dhana’. That is how the DMC in Varanasi was historical and unique in many respect.

Gwalior DMC

Gwalior DMC was next in line of DMC programmes after Varanasi. Ba’ba’ was received with tumultuous ovation by the ma’rgiis who came from the hinterland of Braja and Bundelakhanda sama’ja. Like everywhere Ba’ba’ took stock of the work done in the region. The WTs responsible for poor progress against their individual targets were punished. This was followed by a demonstration on a worker using form and smell tanmatras. WTs felt greatly inspired.

Shri Gulabjii and his family were responsible for serving food to Ba’ba’. Margiis received inspirational guidance following the BP meeting. I got the opportunity to massage Baba’s feet here as well by His grace.

Ahmedabad DMC

Directly under the supervision of BP Ahmedabad and the collective efforts of Ma’rgiis of Gujarat DMC was very nicely organized. There was good arrangement for everyone’s lodging and boarding.

On His arrival from Gwalior Ba’ba’ was ceremoniously welcomed in native Gujarati tradition. Like everywhere Ba’ba’ conducted RDS with WTs and a demonstration was performed on Tanmatrika Samadhi using form and smell tanmatras.

There was an unprecedented wave of bliss let loose by Him and there was none who remained unaffected by the flow of its vibration.

From Ahamedabad I was to proceed to Coimbator in down south a place which was to take 40 hours of train journey from Ahmedabad. Coimbatore was the next DMC destination, I left in the night after DMC by train after seeking His permission.

Coimbator DMC, 9th November 1979

Ba’ba’ stay in Coimbator was arranged at the residence of Shrii Rajgopaljii – a devotee and a sincere sa’dhaka. DMC was well planned. Like everywhere Ba’ba’ was received with devotional fervour in south Indian traditional style.

During the RDS Ba’ba’ conducted a demonstration on me as His medium. He touched my Ajina’ Cakra and later asked me to concentrate my mind on Vishuddha Cakra. No sooner I had fixed my mind on the Vishuddha Cakra I started seeing white effulgence (shubhra jyoti) which was encircling the Vishuddha Cakra. Thereafter, I lost physical sense due to intense flow of bliss. The inner sight of that effulgence was blissful but beyond description. Ba’ba’ said he is visualizing Brahma Jyoti. Eventually I entered into

Bhava Sama’dhi. Ba’ba’ said this realization is attainable as a result of long and arduous sa’dhana’ spanning many lives. Even now whenever I try to visualize that Shubhra Jyoti I tend to experience bliss

This DMC was very inspiring for me.

### Hyderabad DMC, 10th November 1979

From Coimbator our next stop was Hyderabad. Ba’ba’ stayed with Shrii Ram Chandra Reddy. He was a great devotee, a staunch moralist, a strong disciplinarian and a sincere ma’rgii. He retired as Chief Engineer from Andhra Pradesh Government. Ba’ba’ was in a very happy mood here. Like before Baba conducted RDS and inspired the WTS to accelerate the pace of work – strengthen your structure and devote yourselves into the service of humanity was His command.

Here also I was blessed by Him and got to serve Him by massaging His feet.

Margiis were greatly inspired by Baba’s DMC speech. Departing Darshan was very devotional and margiis gave a befitting farewell to their Guru.

### Etah DMC

For me Etah DMC was both important and awe-inspiring. I was the central organizer for this DMC. I did try to make the DMC a great success with the support and co-operation of Ma’rgiis. Ba’ba’ played a strange sport of testing the ma’rgii hard. I also got disturbed but simultaneously enjoyed His Liila’. Ma’rgiis did try to organize the Hall for the DMC, lodging and boarding arrangement for Ba’ba’ and Ma’rgiis and made other arrangements as well. However, by the time of the scheduled day of Ba’ba’s arrival barely 50 ma’rgiis had arrived there. Ba’ba’ told His PA since only a very few ma’rgii have arrived there in Etah, He would not go there. His planned flight from Delhi to Agra did not materialize and PA dada informed that Ba’ba’ is not going to Agra since according to Him arrangements in Etah were not up to the expectations of Ba’ba’. The welcoming team with the escort to bring Him to Etah from Agra was already arranged. This news demoralised the ma’rgiis and they did not know what to do. Margiis decided that me alongwith Dada Shankarananda and Dada Dhyaneshananda led by Jayprakash Bhaiji should go to Delhi and request Him to bless Etah by the touch of His holy feet. Soon after we left Etah at 4pm and reached Delhi at 10pm. There I met PA dada and requested him to persuade Ba’ba’ to conduct DMC in Etah. A day was already lost and ma’rgiis are crying and feeling dejected. PA dada went in His room and apprised Him of the situation of Etah and also informed him that Jayprakash Bhai has come here with other sectorial workers.

Thereafter Jayprakash Bhai was allowed in Ba’ba’s room. He requested Him in all humility to bless Etah by His Holy presence. Instead He started ridiculing Him and asked Him why should He come there. Nothing has been done. No organizational targets have been fulfilled. He promised that we will complete all unfinished tasks. However Baba did not relent. He came out of His room. Then Shankaranandaji went inside and he also could not convince Him. He was followed by Dhyaneshanandaji who also could not succeed.

Then I went inside His room at 12 midnight and started doing His massage and requested Him to oblige. But He was in no mood to accept since still the margiis have not arrived there. He inquired whether Arun and the BP of Kanpur have arrived. I said they are on the way and will be there in the morning. After that I became silent. Then after a while He himself enquired why He should go there. I thought for a while and said that Ba’ba’ it is your old affectionate Braja Bhumii. As the last Taraka Brahma you have played many Liila there. They are your devotees and they all are anxiously waiting for your darshan. Hearing this reply He was pleased and said that your logic is unbeatable. He immediately called His PA and asked Him to make preparations to fly to Agra. I am also getting ready. I was felt blessed watching His drama.

About 2am Jayprakash Bhai and other sectorial workers left for Etah. I felt weak and sick after coming out of His room and stayed back. Soon Baba proceeded to the airport.

I reached Etah next day evening. There I was informed by Shanti didi and the Ma’rgiis that Ba’ba’ has not taken His lunch. We are all very pained and don’t know what have we done because of which He is not accepting the food.

I went in His room and apprised Him of mental state of the ma’rgiis because of your not taking the lunch. Ba’ba’ assured me that He will take lunch in the night and will also make all those ma’rgiis happy. I came out of His room and informed them about what had transpired between me and Him and inspired them not to worry since He has come to His own Braja bhu’mii and you should feed Him sweet butter (Ma’khana Mishri).

The third day before General Darshan Ba’ba’ called a meeting of WTs. He was in His mystic form. The environment in the room was flooded with spiritual vibrations. Devotees were focused on His Divine form. Ba’ba’ graced the devotees and conducted another demonstration of smell and form Tanmatras on two workers. When this was in progress sister Sha’nti overwhelmed by devotion for the lord entered the room inadvertently with a plate full of Ma’khana – Mishrii (sweet butter-a dainty sweet liked by Shri Krsna). Ba’ba’ who was in the midst of the demonstration feigned a bit disturbed. Seeing Sa’ba’ she could control not herself and the plate fell down from her hands, and she left the room atonce in a state of mental distress. Ba’ba’ finished His demonstration and then all of us collectively enjoyed A’vartta Kiirtana.

When I met her after the meeting sister Shanti started crying regretting to have disturbed Ba’ba’. I told her that you are blessed to have seen Him in the midst of a mystic demonstration. That day Ba’ba’ enjoyed the food cooked by her. Then He called all the sincere & active margiis for a group meeting in His room and blessed them. All were overjoyed having received His love and blessings.

Forming Madhura Gos’thii-a historical event: Venue: Madhu Ma’lainca Lake garden, Kolkata, 19th June 1979

After a long gap of eight years in Jail, when He was released He put special emphasis on DMCs in as many places as possible. In the same strain A’nanda Purnima’ DMC 1979 was held in Park circus Kolkata. This was attended by a large following of margiis and WTs. All of sudden a worker came to me while I was busy with some organizational activity and informed me that Ba’ba’ had called 6 workers in His room immediately. All of us collected and left for Lake Garden and straight way went to His room and sat in His presence after Sa’s’taunga Pran’a’ma. Ba’ba’ was waiting for us. As we entered the room was filled with an air of strange spiritual vibrations. A heavenly smile played on His lips. All of us present in His room were bewitched by the intoxicating aura encircling Him. Blessed as we all felt beholding His charming supra natural form. Very softly He posed a question to all of us. He asked “why has He come to this planet?” it was the most unexpected question to us, unable to reply we all looked towards Him for an answer. After a while He said that I am here to create a flood of devotion. We all felt blessed hearing His illuminating words through which He was revealing the true purpose of His advent.

He continued saying that He is going to leave such an amazing vibrations in the dusty earth of this planet where all its inhabitants will soon realize to be the fortunate one’s to have been born here. Devotees attain spiritual realization through many states of ideation viz. Da’sya Bha’va (engaging oneself as a servant of Prabhu), sa’khya Bha’va (feeling of friendship with Him), va’tsalya bha’va (imagining oneself as a child of Param Purus’a), madhura Bha’va or Ra’dha’ bha’va (a feeling of inseparable love and sweetness for Him). In the first three stages a feeling of duality prevails, however in Madhura Bha’va one feels total oneness with Him. This is the highest stage of ideation. To be established in madhura bha’va is the goal of Bhakti. He further elaborated that these stages of ideation are realized by Guru Krpa’. However, through intense sa’dhana’ and kiirtana a sa’dhaka can awaken the highest stage of Bha’va. Then He explained the importance of Kiirtana. His sweet voice and illuminating words of wisdom were like the drenching showers of nectar filled with His sweet love.

Since he had chosen me to witness such a historical incidence of great spiritual significance I wrote all that I remembered. But as ill luck would have it I lost that diary

and those words could not be retrieved until I got some hints in the Bhakta & Bhagvana – the book written by Shri Vashis’tha.

Human being is the most evolved creature. Its extent of relationships is both pervasive and extensive. Normal humans exist at physical and mental level. Hence their relationships remain confined to mother-father, brother-sister, friend-foe, husband-wife, master-servant & so on. But there is another realm of existence beyond the physical and mental. That is the spiritual realm. In this subtler realm of existence human beings goaded by their samska’ra (reactive momenta) take to any of the following four stages of ideation and relate to their Goal (Is’t’a) or Supreme Destination. For instance:

1. Da’sya Bha’va – Here the devotee considers himself as a servant of the Parama Purus’a, like the story of Hanuma’na and Lord Ra’ma. Although this is a higher stage of devotion, but duality remains.
2. Sa’khya Bha’va – In this stage the devotee conceives his Is’t’a to be his friend. Though he may consider himself as inseparable with two bodies but one pra’n’a(life force). Here also feeling of duality remains. For instance the story of Krsna-suda’ma’ or Krsna-Arjuna in Mahabharata.
3. Va’tsalya Bha’va – in this the devotee assumes himself as the father/mother or foster father/mother. Here also there is duality in the relationship of mother-son. For instance the relationship of Vasudeva/Devakii
4. Madhura Bha’va – the relationship of Radha-Krsna or Miira-Krsna.

The devotee of Madhura Bha’va always yearns to please the Is’t’a. This is considered the highest category of Sa’dhakas. This is described as Ra’ga’tmika’ Bhakti. When the devotee craves to realize Parama Purus’a with single minded devotion the Is’t’a is attained. This strong attraction of the devotee like-wise compels the Is’t’a to pull the devotee by His intense love enabling him to merge with the Is’t’a. The ideation to realize the Is’t’a as the Supreme Love of his life by total self surrender is called Maha’ Bha’va or Ra’dha’ Bha’va. In this stage duality ceases to exist. Ra’dha’ & Krsna identify into one. In this stage there remains only the intense desire to merge with the Is’t’a, anxiety of separation, infinite attraction and single minded love. The devotee of Ra’dha’ Bha’va perceives this created world full of sweetness (Rasamaya), Blissful and embodiment of Mohana.

Ba’ba’ explained the history of Kiirtana which according to Him was closely associated with spirituality and devotion. During the time of Sadashiva humanity was leading a natural life which was largely based on the pursuit of satya. In the times of maha’bha’rata the social environment had turned both crooked & complex. Krsna put maximum emphasis on prapatti. Kiirtana had not evolved then.

Kiirtan started in the pauran’ika age. The oft-repeated shloka

Na’ham tis’tha’mi vaekunthe yogina’m hrdaya na ca

Mad bhakta’ yatra ga’yanti tatra tis’tha’mi na’radah

Is taken from Vishnu Puran’a. According to mythological story Kiirtan was popularized amongst common people by Na’rada who was its exponent as well.

Finally He said He is creating an institution named Madhura Gos’thii for spreading devotional sentiment. Thus Madhura Gos’thi was constituted. We were announced as the founding members of Madhura Gos’thii. We all were the direct recipients of His grace to witness such a historical event. Now it is your responsibility to spread devotion among the masses and to increase the numbers of madhura gos’thii. He then touched each one of us and gave His blessings. The experience that day was so intense that I continued to enjoy that blissful feeling for many days. I continued to spread His message of love among the margiis and workers. He would many times enquire from me about the progress of madhura gos’thii.

I used to get articles about Madhura Gos’thii published in Shankhana’da. All the members were actively doing pracara of kiirtana mahima and devotion. I was fortunate to get Ba’ba’s physical contact and love. Ba’ba’ used to conduct regular meetings of members of Madhura Gos’thii and inspire them.

Many ma’rgiis and WT workers used to approach me and other members of Madhura Gos’thii to propose their names for inclusion in madhura gos’thii. I used to convey their desire to Ba’ba. Sometime Ba’ba’ would Himself ask me or Dada Keshava’nanda to include the name of a particular worker. At times He would call some worker and include him in Madhura gos’thii.

Once I went to Him and recommended the names of Dada Ragunath & Tadbhava’nandajii for inclusion in madhura gosthi, but Ba’ba’ told me that they were the sa’dhakas of different bha’va. It would be better for them to continue sa’dhana’ according to their own bha’va.

This went on. Many margiis and workers used to envy me and would pass comment for the favour that I was receiving from Him. Whenever I found any opportunity to be close to Him, I would convey to Him the feelings of margiis & workers. Once Baba told me that He was aware of the feelings of margiis and at the opportune moment they would get to taste the sweetness of madhura sa’dhana’.

On 30th October 1979 in Va’ra’nasi DMC He introduced A’vartta Kiirtana to all the margiis and thus madhura sa’dhana’ was made available to all.

Significance of A’vartta Kiirtan

For the first time A’vartta Kiirtana was invented by Him in Va’ranasi DMC. A’vartta kiirtana is an essential part of madhura sa’dhana’.

In A’nanda Ma’rga there are two special categories of Kiirtana. One of them is akhanda kiirtana and the other a’vartta kiirtana. In Akhanda kiirtana the movement is in anticlockwise direction. The reason for this is that in human body Kula Kund’alinii is seated in the lowest vertebra i.e. mu’la’dha’ra cakra in clockwise three and half serpentine folds. That is why to awaken the kund’alinii and to goad it towards the Sahasra’ra cakra it is impacted by the repetition of siddha mantra of kiirtana. Whenever we have to win over any obstructing idea we must take recourse to opposite stance – “samgra’me vaepariityam” – to fight a crude idea take the help of the force of consciousness. This is the rule to be followed.

In A’vartta kiirtana the mind is to be fixed in sahasrara cakra. It is done while dancing in lalita ma’rmika singing kiirtana. One remains in one place and moves in four directions clockwise singing kiirtana in different tunes. Tunes are changed this is followed by change of direction. Now when you return back to the original position you continue dancing and doing kiirtana in the fifth tune looking upwards and finally in the sixth tune while looking downwards. Kiirtana in each tune should be done for about the same time. During each change of direction from start to finish there is a definite ideation to be practiced as follows.

1. While facing the front in the beginning dancing Lalita Ma’rmika and singing Na’ma kiirtana one ideates – I have an innate love for Parama Purus’a
2. changing direction with new tune clockwise one ideates – I am a sincere person
3. changing direction with new kiirtana tune again – I purify myself in the fire of sa’dhana’
4. changing direction with new tune again – I am ready to face all kinds of difficulties whether internal or external
5. returning to the original position but looking upwards with hands raised singing kiirtana and dancing continued– I surrender unto Parama Purus’a
6. continuing in the same position but looking downwards singing and dancing – I take shelter unto Parama purus’a

A’vartta kiirtana aims at dissolving our individual self into Shiva.

### Visiting Kanyakumari

My visit to Kanyakumari was inspired by the biography of Swami Vivekananda who had been to Kanyakumari when he had visited the southernmost state of Tamilnadu. Kanyakumari is situated at the confluence of Bay of Bengal and Arabian Sea. This is one such beautiful place on the planet earth where one can watch the beauty of both the Sun rise and the Sun set. The story goes like this. Swami Vivekananda when he

went near the tip of the shore from where the rock of Kanyakumari is visible. He was so much moved by the beauty and vastness of the ocean there that a strange attraction overwhelmed his mind and he instantly decided to swim across the sea to land there. Reaching there he sat on the rock and meditated. He had a blissful vision of the great future of his motherland which he loved so dearly. This incidence of his life inspired me when I was a young boy. After reading this passage I decided in my mind that someday I will also visit that place and meditate on that rock. Now the rock has been converted into a temple in Swamijii’s memory. It has now become a travel destination of thousands of travelers around the world. Indeed the beauty of the place is hypnotic. Visualizing the vast sea one loses physical consciousness and the mind experiences the subtleties of the other world.

Setubandha Ra’meshvaram

I had a childhood memory of the bridge built by Lord Ra’ma (Setu Bandha Ra’meshvarama) in the mythological story. Since then I cherished a desire to see that mythological bridge in Ra’meshvaram. Once I went with brother Bhaktvatsalamjii of Selam for AMURT relief work. The view of the expanse of sea was overwhelming. I thoroughly enjoyed my sa’dhana’ there. From there we went to Ra’meshvaram temple which is located about 12 km from Ra’meshvaram. On our return journey we went to see the Miina’ks’ii temple. This is an example of the pride of our cultural heritage.

Kalpataru Ba’ba’: Residence of Ma’rga Guru,

Madhumalainca, Lake Garden, Kolkata, 11^th Dec. 1979

I was then posted as the sectorial worker of SDM. I was responsible for all the activities of the SDM organization. A’c. Vijayananda Dada was the Chief Secretary, but the organizational responsibility was on my shoulders. Baba’ had just returned from His hectic tour of 18 DMCs. I had also attended 14 of those DMCs. There was irresistible flow of devotion. Madhur Gos’thii was at the height of its glory. There was a constant flow of Ba’ba’s grace. I used to consider myself very fortunate.

Ba’ba’ was annoyed with poor progress of Dharma Praca’ra and Education Relief and Welfare activities. There was a fixed day and time for reporting the progress in these two areas. Since I was a worker of SDM, I was also responsible for the poor progress of Dharma Praca’ra. Hence I was also supposed to attend reporting with central workers.

On the 11^th of Dec. 1979, Ba’ba’ was taking report from GTS after lunch. GTS was dada Satyapara’nanda. He was giving details of each trainee undertaking training in Va’ra’nasi. Suddenly Ba’ba’ enquired about a boy who had promised to Him in Tripura DMC to become a whole timer during his personal contact. He asked if that boy has come there or not. GTS replied in affirmative and then added that he did come to the

training centre but could not adjust with the Matha & its strict conduct rules. He has since then returned back to his home. Ba’ba’ told him in a pleasant manner that whether he knew about his past life. He replied that he did. Every time a new entrant comes to the Matha it is a part of Shramana’s responsibility to take notes about his background. The Shramana started describing his name, place of stay, details of his parents, his qualifications and schools attended and his permanent address and so on. Ba’ba’ smiled and enquired whether you know the past history of the boy. Shramana could not speak. It is the responsibility of the GTS to know the past lives of all of the trainees who attend your Matha.

After that Ba’ba’ asked him to hold the thumb of His right foot. Then He started describing the fourteen past births of the boy and seven past lives each of the boy’s maternal and paternal family. He added that the Shramana should, likewise, know the past lives of all their trainees.

Then He changed the discussion and turned towards the Shramana. He told him that Ba’ba’ is illiterate and lacks worldly knowledge, but by dint of your occult power He came to know the history of the many past lives of that trainee. Now tell me, how did you get to know this magic. How could you do this? The GTS was silently enjoying His Liila’. Since the GTS was remaining silent, Ba’ba’ told him to call the workers who were waiting outside for reporting. Perhaps they may have the answer to my query. We were all waiting outside Ba’ba’s room and no sooner we had heard Him call us, all five of us & Ba’ba’s PA entered His room. The environment in the room had a mystic charge. Ba’ba’ turned towards us and said, I have a knotty problem for you to resolve. This GTS knows some magic. A little while ago when he touched the thumb of my foot, I could describe the past lives of one of his trainees. How did it happen? How did he use his occult power on Me?

All of us replied to Him that it was His Liila’. You are all powerful and omniscient. You don’t need any external help to accomplish whatever you want to do. Hearing this He said that He wants to give all of you a boon. Whatever may be your desire it will be fulfilled. We were dumb and could not speak a word, but continued to look at His face. Again He said that I am going to be Kalpataru for five minutes and then He assumed a strange mudra and implored us to ask for any boon. Whatever is your desire, it will be yours for the asking. Then he waited, we did not ask any thing and the time was over. He repeated that you have lost a golden opportunity. I will once again become Kalpataru for five more minutes. Once again we did not ask any boon. Then Ba’ba’ looked at Dada Asiima’nanda and told him that being the Press Secretary you are constantly under pressure of the organization for establishing press in every dioceses. Why don’t you ask for Va’kasiddhi. Any number of presses will be yours by your mere wish. Dada Asiima’nanda in a natural and simple manner replied that he doesn’t need any occult

power. Everything is happening fine by your grace. Then He turned towards the then DPS and told him to ask Anima’ Siddhii. That way you will save much of the postal organizational expenditure, since you will be able to convey to the minds of all the margiis. But he also did not ask any thing. Next in line was ERAWS secretary Dada Rama’nanda. He also declined. Then He asked me that He would grant the power of Antaraya’mitva (knowing everything) which will attract all persons of the world towards you. My reply was also the same like other Dada’s. He continued to pester us but we remained blissfully ignorant and continued to enjoy Him play His Liila.

Then at last He told us that we should not ask anything from Him. Even then if He insists ask Him to grant you Para’ Bhakti. Once you have got para’ bhakti you have got everything. He who posses Para Bhakti attains the Supreme goal.

Universe is being created by the Nucleus Consciousness

Venue: Lake Garden, Residence of Ma’rga Guru, 24^th Dec. 1979

I was advised by the central office to get my passport readied. Once it was ready on 21^st of December 1979, I informed Pranavanandajii, the then Office Secretary, that my passport has arrived. He informed me that I should prepare to go to Berlin sector for my next posting. Hearing him say so made me numb, it was something I was not prepared for. I was not at all inclined to go to Berlin sector after having learned about the cold climate prevalent there and the materialistic way of life there. I worried about my new posting and the fact that I may not be successful in the new circumstances - western life style, food habits, their culture and the glamour of the materialistic life. I thought why has Ba’ba’ become so heartless. Why does He want me to go to such a far away place from Him. I don’t know the margiis of that region. I was not sure if I would be able help develop devotional feelings in them, and I would be able to win over their love and affection. These and many similar thoughts made me worried.

Those were the early days of madhura Sa’dhana’. I was particularly a fortunate recipient of His love and attention, which I did not want to lose. I requested the OS Dada Pranava’nandajii not to press for my early ticket booking. I asked him to wait until the New Year DMC, in which I wanted to meet the ma’rgiis before leaving for my new posting. OS dada got annoyed with me and told me, if you continue to insist like this I am going to complain to Ba’ba’ about your unwillingness to accept new posting. I was disturbed by his reply and mentally blamed Ba’ba’ for my plight.

On the evening of 24^th of December, I was supposed to leave. I was very disturbed & worried. In the afternoon of my day of departure Ba’ba’ called me. With very heavy heart I went to Him and sat down in His presence after sa’sta’unga pran’a’ma. He lovingly talked about my departure to Berlin sector. As soon as He said this I started crying. He

consoled me and asked me what was disturbing you? You are going there as my representative to speed up the work of my mission. You will be successful there. You will have no difficulty in adjusting with the food there. The margiis will regard you and love you like here. They are no different than the margiis of India. I have just returned from Europe after conducting DMC at many places four months ago. In short he answered all the questions that I have been thinking constantly these days. Then He told me that you are thinking that you are going far away to a distant land from Him. But I live in your heart. I am not far away from you. Then he told me, whenever you will call me I will reach there physically near you. Then He blessed me. I realized that my heart was flooded with His love. My whole being started vibrating with His loving grace. I started massaging his feet.

Suddenly I felt, as if I was, now beholding a transparent but pink body of Ba’ba’ before me. His blood was also pink colored and I was seeing the bundle of His nerves. Ba’ba’ asked me to smell His feet, I felt a strong rush of smell of fresh Kevada’ flower from His feet. It was an intoxicating but heavenly feeling. Then He extended the palm of His right hand and asked me to smell that as well. It smelled like fresh rose flowers. Then he asked me to smell his head. It smelled like the scent of Chandan. Finally He asked to smell His naval point which was emitting a strong smell of Lotus flower. This led me into the other worldly experience. This is the same naïve from where the universe is being created and maintained. I don’t remember when I entered into sama’dhi. I realized that a lot of time has elapsed when I regained consciousness and opened my eyes and I found that Ba’ba’ was softly massaging my body. Then I heard OS dada calling that it is time for my flight. I left Baba’s room after sa’s’t’a’unga pran’a’ma. OS dada had already kept my luggage in the taxi. Soon both of us proceeded to Dumdum airport. At the airport I got my luggage checked in and after immigration sat in the plane which took me to Snow Field Airport of East Germany. From there I took the train to cross the Berlin wall and reached Berlin the capital of West Germany.

I don’t know what all means He employs to manage His own affairs, but soon found that every event unfolds itself as He desires. I realized that He took care of me as foretold by Him. He also made sure and I could happily adjust with the new environment.

### Europe Chapter

#### My first overseas journey

After OS Dada saw me off at the Dumdum Airport for my journey to Berlin Sector, I took to the Russian Aeroflot flight and traveled to our Berlin sectorial office in Berlin after crossing over the Great wall dividing East & West Germany. Dada Keshishvarananda came to receive me. As we were traveling, police seeing our strange uniform took us to the police station for enquiry. After about two hours, they contacted our sectorial office

secretary Dada Bhuvanesha’nanda and he had to come there to escort us to the sectorial office. This is how I was greeted in Berlin? I thought for while why this has happened even though Ba’ba’ had assured me that I will be welcomed there.

I stayed there for two days. Then I received instructions to proceed to Oslo the capital of Norway via Copenhagen the capital of Denmark. When I reached Copenhagen on the 29^th of December, it was extremely cold and temperature was – 10 degree. There was snow everywhere and it used to be dark throughout day and night. This was my first experience of facing such cold climate. I got worried thinking I may not be able to face this cold and my body will wither away in this climate. I used to cry and ask Him why He did post me in this inhospitable place. Here everything was so very different from India – the food, living style, clothing, mannerism and climate everything was just the opposite of what I was used to. I could not see day light and sunrise for many months, since it used to snow all day and night and it used to be dark throughout.

Copenhagen was the global headquarters’ of PROUTIST Universal (PU). Dada Sa’dhana’nanda was the OS there. There were many margiis – LFT brothers and sisters who were handling many projects. There used to be Kiirtana everyday both in the morning and evening. Margiis would try to keep me in good humour and encourage me that it would be alright in a matter of few days when you will be able to adjust with the new environment. Dada Karuna’nanda introduced me to one margii sister Jyoti. She used to take care of me like my elder sister. She taught me about the psychology, mannerism and way of life there. She encouraged me to be positive about everything since it is going to be alright very soon. She would inspire me by saying that since Ba’ba’ has sent you here He will ensure that you are successful in spreading His mission. Slowly I started to adjust and my own capacity to withstand cold increased and the environment there started to appear natural to me.

After staying in Copenhagen I proceeded to Oslo. I was asked by dada Karunananda to open my headquarters there. It was colder than Copenhagen. Here I met the Bhukti Pradha’na and told him the purpose of my coming there. He was annoyed with my proposal and said who will take care of you here. Please return back to Germany. I was surprised at his curt reply became dumb. I had never expected such kind of remorse. I had no money. I cried and blamed Him for everything that was happening. I would think was this the welcome He had planned for me here. I failed to understand His Liila’. It was quite unusual behaviour. I was not yet adjusted to the idea of taking my food from the fridge on my own and eat in the kitchen.

On one such days when I was lost in my own thoughts a ma’rgii brother Va’sudeva lovingly approached me and asked me to come along with him. He said he shall arrange everything of my need. You don’t worry. With this Ba’ba’ did what was required for me according to His assurance.

He took me to a ma'rgii family. They were Brother Ravi and sister Shashi. They welcomed me and took care of me. I stayed with them for three days. I apprised them of the plans and programmes of the organization. They were enthused and took me to many places for praca'ra. They had good contacts. I got to see and acquaint with the old and new margiis of the region. I addressed many meetings and initiated many people and started many units and DC. Thus I visited Kongsberg, Christianson, Fredrick tad, Bergin & Trongham and was able to do dharma pracara to my satisfaction. I feel obliged to the ma'rgii family which encouraged me in that place. My own pride of being an old and senior worker was humbled. Only when I surrendered to Him that I received His help. I returned back to Oslo after 20 days. By that time the BP Oslo had heard of the good work I had done. He too was inspired and started helping me in doing my organizational work.

Saint Tulasidas has said "ja'para krpa' Ra'ma ki Hoii, ta'para krpa' kare saba koii (the eligible one who is able to invoke the grace of Parama Purus'a, receives unconditional help of the whole world).

First three months were the testing times for me. I would quite often lose patience and get nervous. At times I used to think of even returning back to India, since I had come with the return ticket. But once my pride got powdered down everything became normal. I also got acclimatized and started moving outside normally in the cold weather.

Ma'rgiis got me a first class Euro-rail ticket valid for three months. I was able to move in the train everywhere without any problem. I started getting invitation from all over Scandinavia for praca'ra. I became famous in that region. I would narrate my own experiences with Ba'ba' and they got inspired to work for His mission. Thus six months were spent happily.

After that there was time for sectorial RDS in Germany. There I met all the workers (Dada & Didi). This was a very happy occasion. I traveled everywhere in Europe attending regional retreats in Germany, Holland, France & Italy. Slowly I got nicely acquainted with the margiis. Organizational work progress also improved. I developed special affinity for Scandinavia. Missionary work also gained momentum and I started experiencing His grace at every step.

I had once gone for praca'ra to Bergin. There I happen to meet sister Niiti. She was from Lapland. The extreme north of Norway, Sweden and Finland together is called Lapland. This area belongs to North pole. It is covered with ice for most part of the year say about 9 months. There you get to see night for 6 months and day for six months. At midnight you can see bright Sun in the sky. When she told me this a desire to see the midnight Sun developed in my mind.

### Courageous journey to Lapland

It is located 3000 kilometer away from Oslo. Sister Niiti invited me to visit Lapland. I accepted her invitation and the date and place of landing were decided in advance and that she would come and receive me on the Ship. I traveled by train to the border of Norway to a place called Norvik. Thereafter I traveled to Harstad by bus. It was the month of May and at 11pm, normally there used to be sunshine, but due to heavy snowing it was dark all over. Many travelers had come there for watching the midnight sun. All hotels were over booked. I could not get any accommodation. I was under the impression that it would not be that cold. But due to heavy snowing it was very cold. I did sa'dhana' in a park and called Him to help me since I was caught unaware and did not know where to go in the night. I thought if I don't get any help I will die in the cold. At 12 midnight a drunkard came near me and looking at my strange dress asked me to about my whereabouts. I told him that I am an Indian and I am here to watch the midnight sun, but I don't know any body here. He said it is a snowy night and you will die of cold if you stay outside. I remained silent. He said he will arrange some place for my stay tonight. I talked to him about A'nanda Marga. In a short while another drunkkard came there. He was more intoxicated and had little sense. The second person told me to come along and he would try to get me some place to stay tonight. When I started to go with the other person the first one cautioned me that the men I am about to go is a murderer. But then I thought it is better to go with this man then to die in cold. He took me to many places viz hospitals, retiring homes and restrooms, but none opened the door. It was a very cold night and there was heavy snowing.

Finally he said that I am sorry I am unable to help you. There is but one alternative. My own place is 25 kilometers away from here, if, you have no objection then you came stay with me. I told him that my only condition is that you have to drop me to the Harstad port at 7am, since I am traveling to Hemerfest port by ship. He agreed and we started for his residence. This was an isolated location. As we entered his house, he closed the door and locked it from inside. We sat down on the dining table facing each other. As I sat there he brought a loaded revolver and pointed it towards me. I was absolutely taken aback and recalled what the other drunkard had told me. I started repeating Guru mantra and Is't'a mantra and called Him in distress. I thought for a while that the man appears to be in a depressed state of mind and should be handled sympathetically. I told him sweetly that I was his guest. It does not behove a good person to treat his guest like this. I got up and went near him and started massaging his back. Then I took hold of the revolver kept it away from him. He was now a changed person. He arranged everything for my comfortable stay. Next morning similarly he arranged for my bathing and I finished my sa'dhana'. Then I went to him and requested him to take me to the Harstad port. He woke up and then quickly got himself readied

and dropped me at the port in time. I thanked him for all the help and learned a lesson of keeping one's word & dutifulness from him.

I left for Hemerfest and reached there in two and a half days by the ship. On the port sister Niiti waited for me to welcome. From there she took me straight away to her residence where she used to live with her parents. At her house seeing her parents were annoyed with her daughter for bringing a heathen. They were old, orthodox and conservative catholic Christians. They thought of me as a Satan. Sister Niiti was disturbed by this unwelcomed situation and felt pained. She was, however, a brave woman of progressive ideas. She had an alternative in her mind and immediately installed a waterproof tent near a lake about a kilometer from her residence. She used to get food for me everyday from her home. I used go with her every day for dharma praca'ra. The persons I used to meet in the town were people having old mode of thinking and were little averse to learning any thing new. I did not quite know what to do in such a situation. I remembered Him and prayed to Him to make use of me for His work. Same night after five days I initiated the first person. Slowly it started to pick up and young persons started coming to meet me in the tent. Most of them were young enthusiastic persons. In this way I initiated 9 persons. This was quite satisfying since in a remote location like this one I was able to plant a seed of yoga and sa'dhana'.

Once in the morning I was busy with my sa'dhana' near the lake on a hillock. Suddenly I heard an unusual sound. When I opened my eyes I noticed about a 100 or so reindeer looking straight at me. They were so cute. I had read about them in books. My first reaction was of fear but as soon as I raised my hands they ran away in all the directions. This was an amazing but endearing sight.

I wanted to visit an Eskimo village and to know about their life style. I was told that they were living about 100 kilometers away. One fine morning we left for that village by bus. On reaching there we sat down in the field outside the village and did sa'dhana'. However we were disturbed by the mosquitoes. These mosquitoes were much bigger in size than I had seen. In the village we met an old man who was tanning the reindeer skin. I enquired from him about the mosquitoes he said they only disturb the outsiders and not the locals. Saying this he laughed. It occurred to me that since these Eskimos keep their body covered with reindeer skin mosquitoes they don't get bitten by them. Secondly these people bath may be once in two months, hence their body emits a typical pungent smell which must be keeping the mosquitoes away.

After that we collected a group of young persons and started talking to them about Yoga and meditation. They were quite curious about our talk. While we were busy discussing with them, suddenly a fat lady appeared there stroking her javelin and started ridiculing us loudly in her local language. The young people we were talking told us that she was the village head woman and we should better leave the place. They told us that she

might call other villagers who come here & may harm us. These Eskimos consider people of Norway as exploiters. On further enquiry the youngsters told us that these people think that you may be trying to convert them. They are orthodox catholic Christians and don’t like people of other religious faith to influence them. Since the village headwoman was influential we were advised to leave the place which we did and returned back. Most people here live in reindeer tents, but now air conditioned house have also come up with all modern facilities. Government is supporting them to develop economically.

Once I had gone to a town for praca’ra, there seeing me in saffron uniform I heard a person commenting about A’nanda Ma’rga. When I asked him how he knows about A’nanda Ma’rga, he said he attended a programme by Dada Karunananda in Oslo University. I wanted to learn meditation but he insisted that I should be vegetarian first. But I could not reconcile to his suggestion and did not learn meditation. But I was surprised to see that your mission is working even in such a remote location. I promptly replied A’nanda ma’rga is omnipresent. He was an educated person and he alongwith his girl friend learned sa’dhana’. He was a local person. I was happy that Ba’ba’ has used me to propagate His mission in such remote places using such curious medium.
