I also went to many islands with sister Niiti and met many different people and after initiating 10 persons I returned back to Oslo having spent 25 days in Lapland.

### A’nanda ma’rga in Iceland

Iceland (http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Europe) is an island country in the North Atlantic Ocean on the Mid-Atlantic Ridge. It has a population of about 320,000 and a total area of 103,000 km². The capital and the largest city is Reykjavík, with the surrounding areas in the southwestern region of the country being home to two-thirds of the national population. Iceland is volcanically and geologically active. The interior mainly consists of a plateau characterised by sand fields, mountains and glaciers, while many glacial rivers flow to the sea through the lowlands. Iceland is warmed by the Gulf Stream and has a temperate climate despite a high latitude just outside the Arctic Circle.

According to *Landnámabók*, the settlement of Iceland began in AD 874 when the Norwegian chieftain Ingólfur Arnarson became the first permanent Norwegian settler on the island. Over the following centuries, people of Norse and Gaelic origin settled in Iceland. From 1262 to 1918 it was part of the Norwegian and later the Danish monarchies. Until the 20th century, the Icelandic population relied largely on fisheries and agriculture. In 1994, the nation became party to an agreement that established the European Economic Area, thus allowing it to diversify from fishing to economic and financial services.

Iceland has a free market economy with relatively low taxes, while maintaining a Nordic welfare system providing universal health care and tertiary education for its citizens (http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iceland#cite_note-13). In recent years,

Iceland has been one of the wealthiest and most developed nations in the world. In 2010, it was ranked as the 17th most developed country in the world by the United Nations' Human Development Index, and the fourth most productive country per capital, 2008, political unrest occurred as the nation's banking system systematically failed.

Icelandic culture is founded upon the nation's Norse heritage. Most Icelanders are descendants of Norse (particularly from Western Norway) and Gaelic settlers. Icelandic, a North Germanic language, is closely related to Faroese and some West Norwegian dialects. The country's cultural heritage includes traditional cuisine, poetry, and the medieval Icelanders' sagas. Currently, Iceland has the smallest population among NATO members and is the only one with no standing army. According to *Freedom of the Press*, Iceland has one of the most free presses in the world.

A geologically young land, Iceland is located on both the Iceland hotspot and the Mid-Atlantic Ridge, which runs right through it. This location means that the island is highly geologically active with many volcanoes, notably Hekla, Eldgjá, Herðubreið and Eldfell. The volcanic eruption of Laki in 1783–1784 caused a famine that killed nearly a quarter of the island's population; the eruption caused dust clouds and haze to appear over most of Europe and parts of Asia and Africa for several months afterward.

Dettifoss, located in northeast Iceland. It is the largest waterfall in Europe in terms of volume discharge, with an average water flow of 200 m³/second.

There are also many geysers in Iceland, including Geysir, from which the English word is derived, as well as the famous Strokkur, which erupts every 5–10 minutes. After a phase of inactivity, Geysir started erupting again after a series of earthquakes in 2000. Geysir has since then grown more quiet and does not erupt often.

With the widespread availability of geothermal power, and the harnessing of many rivers and waterfalls for hydroelectricity, most residents have inexpensive hot water and home heat. The island itself is composed primarily of basalt, a low-silica lava associated with effusive volcanism as has occurred also in Hawaii.

The climate of Iceland's coast is subpolar oceanic. The warm North Atlantic Current ensures generally higher annual temperatures than in most places of similar latitude in the world. Regions in the world with similar climate include the Aleutian Islands, the Alaska Peninsula, and Tierra del Fuego, although these regions are closer to the equator. Despite its proximity to the Arctic, the island's coasts remain ice-free through the winter. Ice incursions are rare, the last having occurred on the north coast in 1969.

Just after I returned back from my brave journey of Lapland in July 1980, I received an invitation from the ma'rgiis of Iceland to come and visit their country for two months. July-August is perhaps the best season in whole of Europe and Scandinavia. Good season in Europe is more like spring season in India. SS dada also wanted me to visit Iceland, hence I decided to go there. Brother Rajesh received me at the Airport. From there we went to AM Jagrti in Reykjavik. Our Jagrti was very beautiful and well maintained. Four Margii/LFTs were already staying there. They were also running a Kindergarten and a Co-operative. Both the institutions were every popular among the

general public. A'nanda ma'rga and Prout were also known among the people there because of its scientific and progressive ideas. Sister Aditi was the Bhukti Pradhana and brothers Rajesh, Jyoti, Mahaviira were dedicated LFTs. I moved in the Capital city of Reykjavik and many islands of Iceland and initiated some 50 persons. The common population here is a happy lot and easy to get along. They are open to new ideas and support the liberal and democratic ideas.

### A'nanda Ma'rga in Greenland

The story of first praca'ra in Greenland is really quite strange. The then SS Dada Karuna'nanda went to attend WRDS (World Review Defect Solution meeting) in Kolkata. Ba'ba' enquired whether A'nanda Ma'rga units have been established in all the countries in Berlin sector? Dada replied in affirmative. Again He asked if there is a Margii Unit in Greenland. When he denied, Ba'ba' asked if Greenland was outside your sector. He nodded but Ba'ba' was visibly annoyed with him and he received punishment for not presenting the correct picture of his sector. Then He gave SS Dada a target of 20 days to establish a unit there. SS returned back to Berlin sector and instructed the RS Oslo region Dada Dharmaveda'nanda to proceed to Greenland for praca'ra. This was a very difficult task. Greenland is the greatest island of the world with an area three times that of India. At that time its population was hardly 40,000. This region is in North Pole. It remains covered with ice sheets almost the year round for 10 months. Its climate is inhospitable. Everywhere there are only hills and ice. A small population lives in the south western shore of Greenland where warm water from Arctic Ocean flows. This maintains the temperature of the south-western shores. During cold climate the temperature here stays at -40 degree. Other parts of Greenland the temperature is normally -80 degree. Survival is very difficult. The inhabitants are Eskimos. They use sledge carts driven by Wild Dogs or Reindeer on ice. There life is adventurous but very hard.

These Eskimos are mostly found on the shore of south and west Arctic Ocean. The remaining Greenland has no population, since the climate in those parts is very harsh and unsuitable for human survival. These people do not live at one place. They are nomads and are migratory by nature. They keep moving from one place to other in search of more hospitable land. Reindeer is their main asset. Reindeer is like a deer living in the ice capped mountainous region. Reindeer is raised mainly for meat, hide and antlers and to a lesser extent for milk and transportation. To protect them from extreme cold nature has endowed them with two layers of fur, a dense wooly undercoat and a longer haired overcoat consisting of hollow air filled hair. Reindeer are ruminants. They mainly eat lichens in winter specially reindeer moss. Reindeer are not considered fully domesticated as they roam free on pastures. Reindeer are ruminants, having a four-chambered stomach. They mainly eat lichens in winter, especially reindeer moss.

However, they also eat the leaves of willows and birches, as well as sedges and grasses. There is some evidence to suggest that on occasion, they will also feed on lemmings, arctic char, and bird eggs. Reindeer herded by the Chukchis have been known to devour mushrooms enthusiastically in late summer.

Reindeer meat is popular in the Scandinavian countries. Reindeer meatballs are sold canned. Sautéed reindeer is the best-known dish in Lapland. In Alaska and Finland, reindeer sausage is sold in supermarkets and grocery stores. Reindeer meat is very tender and lean. It can be prepared fresh, but also dried, salted, hot- and cold-smoked. In addition to meat, almost all internal organs of reindeer can be eaten, some being traditional dishes. Furthermore, *Lapin Poron liha*, fresh Reindeer meat completely produced and packed in Finnish Lapland, is protected in Europe with PDO classification.

Reindeer antler is powdered and sold as an aphrodisiac, nutritional or medicinal supplement to Asian markets.

The meat of reindeer is the main food of Eskimos. They also make their shoes, socks, coat, pants and cap out of the skin of reindeer. In the past Eskimos lived in the houses made of ice or tents made from reindeer skin, but now they are living in air conditioned houses. They generate electricity from hot water and maintain the temperature of the house at comfortable levels. Everything eaten by the population is being imported from Norway and Denmark. Earlier it was ruled by Norway, but now it is an independent nation. Many people from Scandinavia have settled here and live in towns. Because of cold climate the rise in population is very slow. Parents with three or more children are supported by the government and the children get all the facility until they get educated.

Since A’nanda Ma’rga units were already there in Iceland, RS dada informed Ma’rgiis about Ba’ba’s desire to spread A’nanda Ma’rga in Greenland. A flight for Greenland used to leave once a week. A ticket was arranged by the ma’rgiis for RS dada to travel there. He arranged a meeting in a community hall where about 30 persons came to listen to him. One sister among them took initiation. It was she who helped him to further propagate A’nanda ma’rga. RS dada returned back to Iceland after a week. Later on LFT Shatrupa went for further praca’ra to Gotabe (now Nuuk) – the capital of Greenland.

Again in the year 1980 when SS dada went to attend WRDS in Kolkata, Ba’ba’ expressed the desire to conduct DMC at the following four places in Berlin sector.

1. Lisbon – the capital of Portugal
2. Luxemburg a small country between Belgium and Holland
3. Helsinki – the capital of Finland
4. Nuuk – the capital of Greenland

There was no pracara of A’nanda ma’rga in all the above four places. There were only a few ma’rgiis and hence it was a big problem to organize DMC in these places.

I was responsible for Scandinavia. Hence SS dada asked me to organize DMC in Greenland. I was disturbed when I was given this responsibility. Greenland is a huge island and it is covered with ice for 10 months in a year. Travelling to Greenland is very expensive. There are virtually no ma’rgiis there. Only our LFT Shatrupa has some information about the place. I wrote a letter to Dada Dharmaveda’nanda and requested him to come for my help to organize DMC in Greenland. I also collected the address of sister Kadambari of Greenland. It was a month of October. I proceeded to Copenhagen to catch the flight to Nuuk. As soon as I left the counter after checking in my luggage, an air hostess approached me looking quite surprised. She asked me if I was travelling to Greenland. She went further and asked me if I was sure where I was going. I was damn sure. I said yes, I am on my way to Greenland. She said it is indeed a brave decision. In October very heavy ice storms start and continue for many months. People generally return back to Europe from there in this period. After she had given her view I went and sat inside the airplane.

This place took me to an American Airport named Armstrong. From here I was to take another flight which was to take me to the south towards Nuuk the capital of Greenland. When I landed in Armstrong there was heavy ice storm. It was very dark everywhere. In no time I found my body was frozen even in an Airport that was air conditioned. After sometime I reached Nuuk Airport after taking another flight. At the airport I was very happily welcomed by LFT Shatrupa’ and Sister Kadambari. I got a gut feeling that Ba’ba’ is surely going to get some work done through me.

By Ba’ba’s grace and the co-operation of two dedicated sisters. I started my work and was able to conduct meetings in the University, colleges and the clubs. I was also able get access to the local radio station and used give a talk on A’nanda Ma’rga and also give interviews on the TV. Everybody had come to know by then that an Indian Yogi was there teaching Yoga & Meditation in the town. It was a small town of 10,000 people. Hence it was not that difficult to get across to most people. Sister Kadambari got the Union leader initiated. He happily accepted his new samskrta name of Janak.

He was the education minister and he helped in the propagation of our Neo-humanistic education system. In a months time I initiated some 40-50 people & created two LFTs. They promised me to start two NHE schools and also agreed to make all arrangements for Ba’ba’s visit.

Before I left for Oslo I enquired from sister Kadambari what attracted her towards A’nanda ma’rga and why & how she came in A’nanda ma’rga. My question made her cry. Then she narrated the strange incidence relating to her coming to A’nanda ma’rga. She

completed her graduation from Nuuk and after that she returned back to her town in southern Greenland which was 4000 kilometers away from Nuuk. In Greenland a big town means a population of 500-600 people. The population in Greenland is scarce.

Six months period during cold season is very depressing. There is no sunrise. That why this unnatural situation draws people towards consumption of heavy drinking and narcotics. This causes weakening of their nerves and brain and they get mentally sick and suffer from melancholia. Suicidal tendency becomes predominant in their mind. They lose faith in the purpose of life.

Kadambari was also a victim of similar situation. Once she had bought cyanide and slept before taking cyanide. Then she had a dream and in the dream she was beholding a divine being in specks who was in a white attire like Ba’ba’ (she had no clue about Ba’ba’). He came close to her and gently massaged her head. He told me not to get depressed and don’t even think of committing suicide. Suicide was a sin. This life is for living. You must not think of dying. I am sending an a’ca’rya for you to learn sa’dhana’. He will be here in next 20 days from now. You must learn sa’dhana’. This will change your mental condition. Everything will be fine thereafter. You have a spiritual mission in your life. Through your medium spiritual sa’dhana’ will be propagated for the all round benefit of many people. When I woke up after the sleep, the thought of helplessness and suicide disappeared. I was now a changed and a happy person. That divine being also showed me in my dream the face of the monk in orange attire who was supposed to initiate me. When she saw RS dada Dharmaveda’nanda delivering the speech in the community hall she immediately recognized him and took initiation. Initiation changed her life totally. She started thinking all the time of Ba’ba’ and His mission. When she heard of my coming to Nuuk, she also came here to help me organize praca’ra work.

We are all his chosen children. It is he who arranges everything in our lives so that we remain in His shelter. We are all blessed.

After finishing my work in Greenland I returned back to my central office. There I received the message from SS dada to go to India to attend WRDS & New year DMC. When I reached A’nanda nagar to attend WRDS & DMC, Ba’ba’s nephew Paltu approached me and said that Ba’ba’ has asked to meet me and ask for my experiences in Lapland and Greenland. When I heard him say so I was awe-struck. How Ba’ba’ alone manages all the show in absentia and uses us as His medium.

Next day when I got the opportunity to meet Him, He asked me why is Iceland not an Iceland and Greenland is not a green land. I had replied that the climate of Iceland's coast is subpolar oceanic. The warm North Atlantic Current ensures generally higher annual temperatures than in most places of similar latitude in the world. This is caused by the violent volcanic eruptions which keep the air temperature under control. Greenland

is in reality a whiteland. There is ice everywhere. Hearing my reply Ba’ba’ lovingly looked at me, I felt a rush of blissful waves enveloping me and filling my mind with happiness.

When Greenland was first discovered, then Vikings (Decoits of Ocean) settled on the south western shore and to attract the Europeans here named this place as Greenland.

Dharmaguru Ba’ba’: Leo town in south France, 1979

Ba’ba’ returned back after His two month long European tour in July-August 1979. He conducted DMC at many places during His journey. I was posted in Berlin sector in December 1979. Once I happen to visit Paris regional office in Leo. There the Ma’rgiis told me how Ba’ba’ had come over to that Jagrti. Then they told me the event that took place when Ba’ba’ forced the WTs to shift to our own Jagrtii instead of the hotel where He was first taken for stay. They told me that Ba’ba’ had blessed this office during His European tour. It was then a rented Ja’grti and since we had no other place during Ba’ba’s visit arrangement for His stay was made in a good hotel. On His arrival when He was brought to the hotel (where beef and pork was regularly served) He refused to stay there and asked them to be taken to our Jagrti. He was immediately shifted from there to this Jagrti. Although the Jagrtii was not prepared and not equipped with proper facilities He preferred to be there. He was very happy in the Jagrtii and did not mind the small inconveniences. His coming there inspired Margiis and throughout His stay He enjoyed being in the company of margiis and He blessed all to their heart’s content.

### Chapter 7

Manila Sector : I will measure the depth of your devotion – Lake Garden, January 1981

After a year I visited India to attend DMC at A’nanda Nagar and get the darshan of Revered Ba’ba’. Ba’ba’ called me during the World RDS and told me that He had heard about the good work done by me in Berlin sector. Now I want you to shoulder a big new responsibility. I remained silent and continued with Ba’ba’s massage. I continued to speak about the good news from my sector. After that my new posting as Sectorial Secretary Manila was announced. I was disturbed internally and worried about the responsibility of new posting. I remembered how I had learnt to live in an entirely new environment with new people of Berlin sector and somehow managed to advance the missionary cause. Again He wants me to work in a new environment where food habits, way of life and environment and mentality is just the opposite of Berlin Sector.

Omniscient Ba’ba’ knew my mental state and to encourage me He called me near Him and said that He had posted me there after considering all the requirements. He inspired me saying that you will succeed in your work there and will not have any difficulty. You will receive the support of margiis and wts. His consoling sweet words

helped to reduce my anxiety. I made up my mind and started to prepare to work in Manila sector. After DMC all the WTs came back to Kolkata. My air ticket for Bangkok and other places was also reserved. On the day of my departure I went in His room for sas’taunga prana’ma & to receive His blessings. As I sat there I found Him in a serious mood and in a rather stern manner He asked me that He was going to measure my devotion for Him in respect of my output in compliance of following three things.

1. Extent of funds you will be able to raise for Tiljala Jagrti Construction
2. The number of plants you will bring for my garden and
3. The number of items you will add to my Museum

These three points were put forth for judging my output. Hearing these, rather, harsh words I got stressed. A thought crossed my mind, can I ever pass His tests without His grace. He maintained His disciplined stance until finally He asked me to proceed to airport, since it was time for my departure. I kept crying like a child while leaving His room. However I continued with my self-analysis and tried to figure out if I failed anytime in my commitment. Soon I overcame with my remorse and was determined to be successful in His test. On the way to airport I kept crying and this followed throughout my trip when I was constantly calling Him until I reached Bangkok. On my arrival there I received a warm welcome by some margiis and wts. After collective meditation when they asked me to speak about ba’ba’, I could not control myself and wept. This had a positive effect on all & they were inspired and got vibrated after hearing me talk about Ba’ba’. From here I proceeded to Kualalampur – the capital of Malaysia. Like in Bangkok the margiis greeted me there also. After Dharma Cakra I was asked to speak which I could not comply as I kept crying and this had a profound effect on the margiis.

My next margii destination was Singapore, like at two previous places margiis of Singapore greeted me. The day I reached there in the jagrti it was Sunday Dharma cakra. Hence ma’rgiis arrived there in large numbers. They were great devotees of Ba’ba’. After Dharma Cakra when they asked me to speak, like before I could not control myself and cried out loudly. This created an otherworldly environment and many margiis were overwhelmed by the devotional waves and joined me. Margiis returned back home after the Dharma Cakra and returned to the Jagrti next day also and wanted to hear something from me. I remained abnormal and the spiritual waves vibrated their minds as well.

Thus travelling further I reached Manila the capital of Philippines and my sectorial headquarters. Margiis in large number came to the airport to receive me at the airport. My mental condition was the same here as well and this had a very positive effect on the margiis. They were greatly inspired during my stay of two and a half month in their sector. After that I returned back to Kolkata to attend World RDS. On this return journey I was loaded with the rare plants of the South East Asia and carried my sectors contribution for Tiljala.

Before my taxi arrived at Madhu Malainca residence of Ba’ba’ in Lake garden, I found that the attending secretary of Ba’ba’ was waiting for me at the gate. He virtually grabbed me and carried me to Ba’ba’s room. I prostrated before Ba’ba’. He smiled in His exquisite style, the memory of which is still alive in my mind.

I remembered the difference in my mental state in which I had left Him when I was posted to Manila sector & now. After a while Ba’ba’ enquired about my tour of the new sector. I did say that it was great by His grace. But soon broke down and wept. He caught me in His arms and gave me the much wanted bliss by His touch.

Ba’ba’ continued and asked me what do I understand by devotion? He himself replied. To dance according to His tune is real devotion. Since then I always made sure to first accomplish the task given to me before asking for anything.

### Berries Plant with thorns

As desired by Ba’ba’ I used to carry many varieties of plants from many countries of my sector every time I came for the world RDS. Ba’ba’ used to give them a Samskrta name and would get them planted in His gardens at appropriate places. Once I happen to bring a plant of Berries from Philippines. Ba’ba’ named it as Maharlika’ Maha’Badari. But due to some negligence the plant died. Ba’ba’ became very annoyed with person responsible for His garden including Asiima’nandajii. He sent word to me to get a replacement of that plant. I tried may level best but could not secure that plant. I was also getting punished for my failure to get that plant. I became a laughing stock before my co-workers who used make fun of me. In this manner some eight months passed. I was getting punished in every world RDS. I was really disturbed at my failure. Once when I was being punished I asked ba’ba’ the botanical name of the plant to search that in the botanical garden. Ba’ba’ smiled and told me that you would not find that plant in big gardens or the Botanical gardens but this plant is a typical berries plant whose leaves have thons on them. Now you go and search for it and you will get one.

On my returning back to Manila, I went to Davao where Cidanandajii was posted as the shraman in our Training Centre. I explained him everything and then we went in jeep in the forest in search of the plant. There we met a old experienced gardener. When we explained him the characteristics of the plant he said he has that plant in his garden. We ordered 50 of those plants and on my return to Manila I sent those plants through WT to Kolkata. On receiving the plant Ba’ba’ was overjoyed and praised me. I wondered what in heavens has He got in that plant. That is something we humans cannot fathom about the mind of our Lord.

### Ba’ba’ wanted us to get Ornaldi Ruflesia the biggest flower of our planet

Once Ba’ba’ asked me to get Ornaldi Ruflesia plant. The flower of this rare plant is the biggest available on our planet. Its diameter is more than one meter. It is a creeper like plant and grows taking the support of a special tree. Bali, Sumatra & Java of Indonesia is the home of this plant. On being instructed to get that plant I was totally at a loss what to do and how to get it?

On my return back to the sector I initiated the process to procure one such plant. In the sectorial RDS I asked all the workers about Ba’ba’s desire to get the plant. Dada Citkrsnananda took it upon himself to get this plant. He succeeded in getting it though with great difficulty and the plant was sent over to Kolkata. However it did not survive due to poor care. The caretaker of the garden received severe punishment for losing the plant. I was then asked to get another plant of the same variety.

In every WRDS I had to receive His punishment for my failure to arrange the plant. Time passed by and even after 7 months we could not manage a replacement.

This plant was the National Flower of Indonesia. A few plants were available in java Sumatra, but thery were under strict surveillance of the national security. Being a rare species other countries were also on the look out and used to send request to Indonesian government to get this plant. Once the Japanese Prime Minister visited Indonesia, he offered $100,000 for the plant but could not get one. This was next to impossible to procure this plant, but Ba’ba’ was adamant.

Finally Citkrsnanandajii once again got another plant by earning the friendship of an official in Bali. On being told by him over phone that he had collected the plant I was overjoyed. He went by air with the plant to Kolkata and gave it Ba’ba’. He was delighted and blessed Dada Citkrsnananda.

Nothing is impossible by His grace. He tests us of our patience and sincerity. With strong determination everything is possible.

### Golden chicken plant

This is a unique but rare species found only in Indonesia. Its fruits look very much like golden chicken. I was asked by Ba’ba’ to get this rare species of plant. It was difficult but by His grace it took us a few months before we could procure one for His garden. Ba’ba’ got many of the rare plants through us. We also made all the effort demanded and accomplished the task given to us. In this process we were tested of our grit and patience. When we used to fail and called out for His help, He would make the otherwise impossible task easy and give us success. That way we would constantly think of Him and experience His grace. Thus missionary work in Manila sector went on progressing.

### Contribution for Tiljala Jagrti

The construction of Tiljala Jagrti was never ending & so were the demands by the central office. It appeared as if it was a endless journey and all margiis and WTs were tired of constant pressure put by General secretary and the Central Office Secretary to fulfill the allotted quota. I was also equally disturbed by their demands. Finding no alternative I started calling out to Him to relieve us. He was always taking care and I used to get my target met through one or the other unexpected medium. After two years the construction was over and we were relieved. I was fortunate that we were able to meet our targets and our potentialities were utilized by Him for His mission.

### The incidences which occurred at Singapore, Jakarta and Manila

These incidences all remind me of His omniscience, omnipresence and His compassionate grace that is always protecting us in all circumstances. The incidences occurred during 1982. I was then the SS of manila sector and was staying in the Jagrti at Singapore with two WTs. It was noon time. About the same time a gentle lady came to the Jagrti and insisted to cook for us. We conceded to her strong desire. She prepared the food and fed us well. Soon after taking our lunch I found my whole body started burning. I felt like getting unconscious. I prayed for Ba’ba’s help.

Thinking in retrospect I was a little apprehensive that the intentions of the lady may not be all that right. The food did prove to be poisonous to me. The WTs accompanying me were also disturbed and did not know what to do. They were, however, not affected.

Again the same lady came in the night to cook our dinner, I got scared of her. That evening somehow the WTs convinced her that we were not hungry at all. Finally she left for good.

In the same tour I also visited Jakarta and stayed with the Regional Secretary. He took me to a strong Muslim sympathizer of the Ma’rga. He always supported the margii activities. He welcomed us in his house and we were introduced to each other. He discussed about our social service projects and about many of the devotional aspects of Ba’ba’. He wanted us to dine with him and we could not decline his request. We were assured that the food will be strictly vegetarian and sentient. The thought did cross my mind to avoid dining at his place, but I could not refuse him. After we had finished, my body and mind were adversely affected.

Then I came back to Manila. I was thinking that something was wrong with me and that I was being cheated under the spell of avidya’. I did not pay heed to this and came to Kolkata for World RDS.

World RDS started the following day and next day when my turn came, Ba’ba’ asked me about some white item that I have taken in Singapore Jagrti on that specific day. The WTs present all laughed and made fun of me on being ridiculed by Ba’ba’. Ba’ba’ observed that the WTs were making a fun of me. then He told them that I did not take any thing static. When He asked me again, I replied that it was tofu. Everybody laughed.

Ba’ba’ further said that everything was clear in my mind that the food prepared by the lady who cooked for us may prove be harmful, still i did not pay heed to the inner voice. Then he started punishing me, and continued to speak. Next time again in Jakarta despite his inner voice suggesting him to refuse the request for food, he overlooked that and took the food and suffered physically and mentally. Why didn’t you refuse? Was it necessary to please that person? He then continued to take my samskara away using His Dukhaharan stick. I enjoyed His grace and cried in bliss for receiving his constant attention.

Have you been permitted to use the scented oil?

Venue: sectorial office: Pako, Manila, 1982

I would normally carry sufficient coconut oil for my daily use from India. Once it so happened that my oil got exhausted and in Manila I could not get the unscented coconut oil. I was then working as SS Manila. There I consulted my seniors viz. dada Cida’nanda who was the Shramana in Davao WT training Center & Dada Japa’nanda who was the Shramana in GBTC LFT training Center 60km. away from Manila. Dada Cidananda suggested that baby oil has very little scent and may be used, Dada Japa’nanda also agreed to his suggestion. Finding no other option I also started using the baby oil, though I was not personally convinced with their argument.

After few days I had to come back to India for attending World RDS in Lake Gardens, Kolkata. When I presented myself before Him he asked me what you were discussing on a particular day in Manila amongst yourself with other senior workers? I did not reply. Ba’ba’ continued and said when you were not convinced of their argument about using the scented baby oil why did you use it. Since when did you get the permission to use scented oil from Ba’ba’? I accepted my mistake and told Him I shall never ever repeat it in future.

Incidence of taking peanuts

Once I was caught by Ba’ba’ for consuming peanuts. Peanuts are not static (ta’masika) nor they are prohibited for WTs, but for me peanuts are not good. I used to stomach upset after taking peanuts. Ba’ba’ said that knowing well this man has been taking peanuts. Then he punished me with His Dukhharana (that which take away your pain) stick. Other workers present were enjoying my predicament. In fact since we were not

getting any healthy thing to eat outside Tiljala those days, we would take peanuts which were easily available everywhere.

I was overwhelmed by His constant attention. His kindness and the care is our only refuge. He takes care of us all even in small matters related to our day to day life.

### Regarding posting of GBTC Shramana in Bali Island

Ba’ba’ posted a Regional WT as Shramana for starting a Training Center there in Bali. I was then staying in Singapore jagrti. The newly posted WT arrived there and informed me that Ba’ba’ has sent him here for starting the GBTC Center in Bali. I thought for a while and told him that there is no ma’rgii in Bali. He was disappointed hearing my words. I advised him not to worry, we will soon send a few senior workers to Bali and once they are able to create sufficient ma’rgiis then you can go there and start the training center with their help. Until then you can continue to do pracara work in Malaysia and help in the construction of Jagrti there. He got busy with the assigned work, meanwhile I inspired a few senior workers to go to Bali and create margii units there. They went there but could not create margiis in the island. In this process six months passed. By that time the Shramana was moved to Manila. He got himself involved in pracara work there.

Meanwhile according to the scheduled programme I had to rush to Kolkata for WRDS. When I stood before Ba’ba’ for reporting, He enquired, how is your training center in Bali doing. I was speechless and felt lifeless as I had no plausible answer to His querry. He continued probing. I could not say any thing. he then sternly told before all the SS that what would he say? The Shramana did not go to Bali and hence training center was not opened there. Then I was punished and he scolded me & said that this useless fellow has taken my taken my mission back by six months. Then He asked me, in how many hours you can get the training center started. I said Ba’ba’ it will take 200 hours. He continued with severe punishment and ridiculed me for taking so much for such a simple task. When, finally I mentioned to complete the job in 72 hours that he stopped beating.

Soon after that I called the Shraman posted by Ba’ba’, who was in Manila then, and asked him to rush to Denpasar-the administrative headquarters of Bali. I told him what had transpired in Kolkata and that Ba’ba’ was very unhappy with me having delayed the progress of his missionary work in Bali. He immediately complied and reached there without any delay by air & got cracking with the task assigned to him. In less than fifteen days he called up and informed me that he has been able to initiate 60 new persons. After two months he again called up and said that 150 margiis have been intiated and that Dharma Cakra has been started in five places. A house was rented out for starting

the LFT training center. The news delighted me. I was to leave for World RDS to Kolkata. It was a great news to be delivered to Ba’ba’.

After reaching Lake Gardens I straight way went to meet Ba’ba’. I was very anxious to see Him and sent word through His attending secretary. Soon after I entered His room, I sat facing Him after prostration. But Ba’ba’ turned His face the other side to show His displeasure. I was very upset. A little while later He said that you want me to know the good news of startup of the Bali Training Center. This would have happened six months earlier, if you had not stopped my medium from going there. All the persons in Bali were destined to be initiated by that very A’ca’rya. I realized my mistake and also the fact that everything happens at all places by His sweet will alone. He alone has been creating the necessary spiritual vibrations for accomplishing the assigned task.

### All dance at the tune of Rama Gosai

#### Humans are forced to dance like monkeys

We dance like puppets. This is what I have experienced. Whenever He comes to this world, all His devotees also accompany Him. They are naturally drawn towards Him. They are the ones whom He utilizes to accomplish His mission.

### Ba’ba’ Dream: Sectorial Office Manila, 1982

Being the SS of Manila Sector I had many responsibilities of the sector. There was financial pressure from both the Center and the Sector for discharging organizational duties. I would remain anxious on this count. I would often discuss about the means to organizing funds with my co-workers. They used to come up with many ideas for solving the financial problems. Once a group of workers suggested that if I could arrange 25,000 Malaysian Dollars, then they would venture into a business. They would also repay the loaned money within three months.

I used to maintain good relations with the margiis. Thinking in the interest of the missionary work I asked a Malaysian Margii Shrii Jina’neshvarajii for a loan. I assured him that the loaned money would be returned to him in three months. He trusted me and readily agreed to advance the loan as requested. I handed over that amount to Dada Citkrsnananda who was then working as CES and Dada Arghya’nanda who was the PRS of Manila Sector. Somehow their venture did not succeed and they lost the amount. Hence the money could not be returned according to my commitment. I felt let down for not honouring my words. This caused tremendous disappointment and I was stressed out and lost the peace of my mind and suffered from insomnia. The workers concerned were supportive and assured me that they will do their best to return the loaned money. But I did not know what to do and hence I was overcome by strong negativity thought to commit suicide.

On one such sleepless nights I slept after crying out for His help. I had a Ba’ba’ dream. He came close to me & lovingly massaged my head. He asked me to be brave and to relinquish the thought of committing suicide. It is a sin to even think of suicide. He also asked me to speak to the money lender and explain him your real situation. He would appreciate your problem and you will find that the problem will be resolved. Then He disappeared. All of a sudden I got up. I felt very light and I forgot the thought of committing suicide. I followed His instructed and my problem was resolved.

Even now when I try & recollect my mental state I get scared. Following this incidence I decided not to indulge in taking loan.

I fail to accept the proposal to earn money

In the last leg of my posting as SS Manila the missionary progress was tremendously accelerated. In all countries & islands of my sector margii projects were flourishing. Schools, Children’s Homes, co-operatives & Master Units were being managed & new one’s started and so was the demand for more money. Pressure started mounting to develop alternative sources of money. I lacked the necessary mental disposition to undertake such an activity and considered myself incapable of doing business of any kind. I would often call Him mentally to relieve me from this responsibility. Meanwhile Ba’ba’ called a few workers and SS to bless them to start a business venture. He called some of them to A’nanda Nagar and blessed them for success. Seeing my mental condition He did not ask me but punished me for my lack of equanimity. I was eventually relieved from the post of SS and I handed over the charge to Dada Citkrsnananda.

Spreading the mission in Vietnam & Cambodia

Ba’ba’ wished that the ideals of A’nanda ma’rga be spread in all those countries which were currently under the Communist. It used to be quite difficult to work in those places since the secret police would keep a watch on our activities and considered our ideas to be a national threat. But Ba’ba’ kept us under lots of pressure, hence I sent workers a couple of times to these countries without any success. Dharma Pracara & Yoga were banned there. When during one of my next WRDS I told Him about my failure to do Dharma Pracara there, He asked me to serve the the refugees of Vietnam & Cambodia who were staying at the border of Thailand. I followed up with His instructions and sent out workers there. This way we succeeded & created margii units. This happened at the fag end of my tenure as SS Manila.

Ba’ba’ asked me to get a truck load of Plants

Venue: residential quarters of Ba’ba’ in Patliputra Colony, Patna, Bihar

I was attending the World RDS in Patna. All SS & other workers were present there. The SS’s of the nine sectors failed to fulfill the target set for us to deposit the required number of plants of the rare species which we were supposed to deliver for Ba’ba’s gardens. We were asked by Him to collectively get one truck load of different plants from Hazipur near Patna as our punishment. We were tired & also that we had virtually exhausted the money. For this job we needed Rs.45,000/- and we were unable to collect that kind of funds. We could hardly appreciate His passion for plants. On a particular day all the SS’s in a grim mood were sitting in front of Ba’ba’s room. All of sudden Ba’ba’ came out of His room and looked at our dull faces. He was all smile and addressed us by saying “No knot is a knot for my naughty boys”. His sweet comment relieved all of us from the dullness which had seized us. All of us laughed with Him to our heart’s content. Next day the truck load of plants arrived from Hazipur and we were allowed to proceed to our sectors.

Ba’ba’ was vibrating the entire universe sitting in His small room

DMC in Philippines were memorable. The old Margii who had experienced the bliss of being in His presence wanted Him to come again. They used to express their desire while conveying their pran’a’ma whenever I would be going to Kolkata for WRDS.

On one such occasion in Kolkata I got an opportunity to massage Ba’ba’. That day He was in a pleasant mood. I conveyed pran’a’ma of Filipino margiis and also their request for holding DMC there. After accepting margii’s pran’a’ma He quarried, why should He conduct DMC in Philippines? I replied that your devotees want to receive the touch of your lotus feet on Philippines. He responded by saying that He can infuse spiritual realization in the whole universe while being seated here in this room. I was dumbstruck hearing His awesome words. My mind was drowned in the flood of devotional flow that He let loose. Truly He was not bound by time & space. He loves the whole creation within His cosmic mind. He can vibrate the minds of His devotees, nay all His creation, by His sweet will.

Ba’ba’ likes His devotees to develop militant mentality & not to be goody goody boys

The above comment was made by him some time in 1983. The incidence occurred when after the WRDS all the WTs (Didis & Dadas) were waiting in His Car parking space to receive His blessings before leaving for their respective sectors. The system required that before leaving each one of us has to offer a garland to Him and seek His blessings and permission. Ba’ba’ had just left for field walk. We were waiting for His return with garlands.

On His alighting from the car, all SS welcomed Him. He smiled seeing all of assembled there and asked “what do my goody goody boys and girls have to say”? None of us

responded but looked to Him? He continued, goody goody means those who are gentle and good. They want peace and justice, but lack daring capacity to fight injustice. My children should be lion hearted. Do you understand? We all laughed and understood His spirit.

He came to create sadviras to establish sadvipra sama’ja. Sadvipras are ever ready to wage a pactless war against all forms of injustices, all kinds of exploitation, repression and suppression. They are ever vigiland to wage a war against their internal and external enemies. They believe in the dictum “Peace is the result of fight. Therefore the peace lovers of the world never shy away from fight”.

### Dharma Samiiks’a’

Following A’nanda Purniima’ DMC in May 1981, Ba’ba’ started a new and unique programme of Dharma Samiiks’a’. Through this programme he reviewed the physical, mental and spiritual progress of His devotees. He examined the past and present samskaras (unserved reactive momenta) of the sa’dhakas. Then He avised them the way out by giving instructions in the right food, a regimen of a’sanas, self restraint and natural remedies. He would also punish them for any of theirs unsavoury actions thus relieving them of their sam’ska’ra of many past lives.

He explained that the purpose of Dharma Samiiks’a’ was to make a sa’dhaka Dagdha Biija (spent samskara – a clean slate like mind). This can happen only by His causeless grace. This was an unprecedented event of human history of past fifteen thousand years. He ssaid why should you keep carrying the load of your samskaras for eternity.

I was then in Manila sector suffering from an acute form of incurable blood cancer. I was virtually emaciated and lacked any vitality to travel. But after learning His new programme of Dharma Samiiks’a’ I decided to proceed to Kolkata despite doctor’s advise being to the contrary. I had my ticket booked for Kolkata via Bangkok. Just before my flight was to leave I fell unconscious and the plane left. On assuming consciousness I got my booking done for the next day.

When I reached Kolkata and was driven in taxi to Madhu malainca – His residence. I found his PA was waiting at the gate for me. No sooner I had alighted from the taxi he caught hold of my hand and pulled me out and pushed me to His room. Once in His room I prostrated and sat before Him. With fatherly love He enquired about my health. Unconsciously I replied that I was good. He retorted what good? Ba’ba’ examined me from my toe to top through His all knowing gaze and asked me to take rest and that He would call me tomorrow.

Dharma samiiks’a’ had already begun. Ba’ba’ was conducting his sessions in the central office at Jodhpur Park. Hundreds of Margiis from India and overseas wwere present

there. They would stand in queue every morning and evening in the hope of getting their turn.

All the SS were also present there and seeing all of us assembled in His room He remarked that these SSs have come from far off places and that they are our shahi (special) guests. He asked us to stand by His side and watch how He would conduct Dharma samiiks’a’ of the ma’rgiis.

We stood there as directed enjoying the mystical event which was to unfold before us. A pleasant incidence occurred when He was conducting Dharma Samiiks’a’ of a ma’rgii from Bengal. He asked that ma’rgii if he regularly fasted on eka’dashii. He affirmed that he did. He continued and reminded him it was summer time and you were fasting and feeling very thirsty. You went for bathing in the pond and while you were still under water you drank water and thought nobody has seen you here. But I was there in your mind as your witness. Was it Nirjala Eka’dashii (fasting without water)? No it wasn’t. You must then accept punishment. He agreed and like this there were many pleasant unpleasant incidences which occurred when we stood in His presence watching ma’rgiis being reviewed one after the other.

### Curing me of my Blood cancer

It was during His busy schedule in Kolkata that He cured me of blood cancer. It was 22nd July 1981, perhaps the golden day of my life. He permitted me for Dharma Samiiks’a’.

It all started when all the SS and the central workers were standing there before Him for reporting. Hongkong sector was the first as scheduled for reporting. He was unhappy with the slow pace of progress in that sector. This was followed by my turn.

When I stood there He started saying pointing to me that yesterday when I asked him about his health he had told me that he was good. But as a matter of fact he has just 24 hours of his life left. Blood cancer has totally consumed his body and he is, today, standing at the verge of death. My physician in Manila had told me about it six month before and I had conveyed about my condition to the then GS dada Sarveshvarananda.

I was suffering from this incurable disease, but I was not so much worried about my death. I had a busy schedule of work and so much to do as SS that I did not think much about my illness. The work in our sector was in full swing and expanding to cover all countries and islands of the sector. Whenever I would get tired of physical distress and pain, I would call Him. Many times in severe pain I would fall unconscious. It was in this background that I came to Kolkata. So when He spoke about me that I was about die in 24 hours, everyone present there became concerned. Ba’ba’ said that blood cancer has caused total destruction of his blood cells. If somebody had told me earlier about his

serious condition, I would have advised him what to do. But now he is dying of his own accord. Let him die. What can I do in such a situation. He is suffering due to his own samskara.

As He spoke I got the internal feeling that he will not let me die before Him. I was not afraid of death instead I was enjoying His company. Then He caught hold of His dukhharan stick and said when he his blood cancer was detected, why nobody informed me. I replied like a naïve that I had informed GS Dada about my condition. He became angry on GS Dada and fired him for not letting Him know about such an important matter concerning an important worker.

However, Ba’ba’ continued to touch my body with His stick and started narrating the suffering I had undergone from my childhood. He said he suffered from many diseases including typhoid, TB, acute stomach ach and many small and big diseases. There was no chance of his survival. But I wanted to utilize him for my mission. Being my devotee and a dedicated worker of my mission, I will not let him die. Medically it is not possible to save him, but I will do whatever is required to save him from the jaws of death.

He asked me to stand before Him and touched my An’a’hata cakra by His stick for long and with His eyes closed He healed my cancer. At that time I was having a pleasant sensation in my body and feeling as if a new life force was entering my body. I thought to myself that with such a force in me I can crush mountains. He continued to touch my An’a’hata Cakra for more than forty five minutes. His own body was profusely perspiring and He got tired. Finally Ba’ba’ said that now he has been completely cured of the blood cancer. But he has to wait for 21 days for the new cells to be formed, then he can regain normal strength. He should be taken care off well. Then He retired me in the custody of dada Vijayananda. Dada Vijaya’nanda took care of me as I rested in his room like a feed boy. Occasionally Ba’ba’ would call me near Him and would examine my progress. In His presence I would feel rejuvenated with strong waves of life force entering my body.

This way I stayed in Kolkata for 25 days and got completely recovered. I started feeling healthy and then one day expressed my desire to Ba’ba’ through GS dada to return back to my sector, which was immediately endorsed by Him. He advised me to take care of my health, to eat nutritious food & strictly follow conduct rules.

This news of my having been cured of blood cancer by Ba’ba’ spread like wild fire in the margii society across the nine sectors. Margiis of Manila sector were particularly waiting for my return to meet me after being cured of blood cancer. When I reached my sector I received warm welcome and they took good care of me. Ba’ba’s grace is unconditional and non-causal.

> He makes the dumb speak and the limp surmount the mountain
>
> I surrender unto that ever graceful Ma'dhava, the personification of Bliss.
>
> (Ma'dhava means the Lord of the causal matrix of the universe)

### Tiljala Jagrti construction - an interesting incidence

In 1980 Ba'ba' wanted Jagrti and office building construction for both Dadas and Didis separately to be initiated at Tiljala. For the purpose two separate lands were bought in the vicinity. The construction was in full swing in the year 1981 and 1982. It was Ba'ba' who took upon himself the task of inspiring workers to contribute funds so that the work is accelerated and the task is finished as soon as possible.

The then GS Dada Sarveshvarananda and OS dada Pran'avananda were responsible for the construction. They would pressurize the SSs to contribute maximally. The target amount for each sector would increase substantially in every WRDS. All SSs were virtually disenchanted and felt undue pressure. Nobody was willing to do contribute any further. About the same time a court notice was sent to Ba'ba' by a building material supplier. This was regarding the payment of the balance dues to the tune of Rs. 2.5 Lac. The notice said if the dues are not cleared in month's time then legal proceedings will be initiated as result Ba'ba' may have to present himself in the court. All of us were in Tiljala for the WRDS. Ba'ba' became very angry on GS and OS after being told about the court notice. We were also summoned in His room and before us also he ridiculed GS & OS for their failure to create such a situation. We were also affected by their plight. Ba'ba' then said these two persons have troubled you a lot for contributing funds for construction. They have also put me in such a difficulty. It is because of them that the court has sent the notice to me. Eventually Ba'ba' said that this would be your last contribution towards Tiljala construction. He directed that the amount due to each SS should be sent within 20 days. All of us agreed to comply the schedule. Thus the problem was resolved.

### How the future is destined to unfold?

Whole timers from across the nine sectors were present for World RDS in Madhu Malainca, Lake gardens, Kolkata. Ba'ba' had started with SS of Hongkong sector. He took the report of outturn and was seemingly unsatisfied and hence punishment followed. This was His routine, and no SS was an exception. But this time along with reporting He also made interesting comment about the socio-cultural, politico-economic scene of each sector. For instance He remarked, that all the countries of Honkong Sector viz. China, Japan, Taiwan, Korea and others had a rich cultural and spiritual

tradition. Thinkers like Confucius, Tao and many others enriched the minds of its people. Mahatma Buddha created a strong spiritual impact on their minds. Generally the people in this sector have spiritual urge. Japan, Taiwan and Korea continue to progress materially.

When my turn came, He talked of the rich socio-cultural heritage of Thailand, Indonesia, islands of Malaysia, Cambodia, and Vietnam. Singapore is economically developed and Malaysia is steadily advancing. The culture of Philippines has been badly hit by pseudo-American influence. Suva, Berlin & New York sectors are economically developed. Nairobi sector has been incessantly exploited socially, culturally, economically and its natural resources have been denuded by the so called developed countries. Most of the countries of this sector are socially backward and economically exploited.

The otherwise backward countries of today viz. India, China, Brazil, Mexico, Indonesia are rich in mineral resources. After 35 years from now these so called developing nations will become the developed nations of the world and those which are developed today will lag behind. Today the countries of America, Europe and Australia make fun of the economically backward countries. A time is going to come when these countries will lose their economic might and then they will also be the laughing stock of the then developed countries. Nothing is permanent in this world, everything is a passing show, a changing reality and a moving panorama.

How & why will this occur? Those so-called rich countries have utilized & exploited their own resources maximally. Their economy is moving towards saturation. On the other hand the developing countries have huge unexploited and untapped natural resources. With the acquisition of science and superior technology these countries will be able to make better use of their potentialities and will get richer.

He further added that the fortune of India is very bright. In coming 35 years it will be the spiritual leader of the world. Its cultural and spiritual wealth is unparalleled. You will dedicate your might to ensure that it gets the coveted status.

### You will not speak a lie under any circumstances

Ba'ba' initiated a new school of music & set each song to tune at Deoghar, a small town in Bihar. It was that memorable day of 14th of September 1982, when he composed the first song and this process continued until 20th October 1990 - a day before His physical departure from our planet. He called the new school of music - Prabhat samgiita. Prabhat samgiita is a store house of a staggering 5018 songs. The compositions are in eight different languages. Every day was memorable in that devotees would wait to learn about the composition of new songs. Ba'ba' would quite often inspire ma'rgiis to

learn new songs and sing individually and collectively. Programmes were also launched to conduct Prabhat Samgiita concerts across nine sectors.

In Manila sector also a live Prabhat samgiita concert was organized. Leading musicians & singers participated in the programme. On the solemn occasion Gokul Piitha (a sweetmeat delicacy liked by Shrii Krsna) was also prepared. Didi A’nanda Vijaya worked hard to produce Gokul Piitha (the process was taught by Ba’ba’ Himself). It was a likeable sweet and was prepared with fineness it deserved. Obviously everybody wanted to have a larger share.

I had a feeling of satisfaction that a Prabhat samgiita concert could be successfully organized in my sector. I had a strong desire to convey the good news to Ba’ba’. The opportunity came when I visited Kolkata to attend WRDS. Normally Ba’ba’ would call me near Him at least once whenever I came for reporting. I waited for two days but contrary to my expectations there was no call from Him. My anxiety was mounting. I enquired from His PA and learnt that there was a new condition to be complied for seeing Him in person. According to that only that WT who has organized minimum five Prabhat samgiita Concerts will only be allowed to see Him in person. Naturally this was very disturbing information. Mentally then I planned to submit a wrong report of completing five concerts to see Him. I conveyed to dada Rama’nanda that five concerts were held in my sector. He informed Ba’ba’ about this. Ba’ba’ agreed to meet me next day in the morning at five am. I was ready the very next day before 5am and went to his room. As I got up after sa’s’taunga Pran’a’ma, He enquired whether I have completed five concerts of Prabhat Samgiita in my sector. I could not speak a lie and remained silent. Ba’ba’ who was reclining on His bed, suddenly jumped up in a show of anger, as if exhaling fire. He ridiculed me contemptuously. I was absolutely non-plussed & did not know what to do? As I returned back from the initial shock, I thought to run away from his room. It was like a thunder and He asked me why the hell I spoke a lie. I replied that I did that to meet Him in person. His mood softened and He asked me to bolt the door from inside. I followed suit. Again he queried regarding the real purpose of my lying. I repeated what I had already said. By this time His mood had changed and now he was taking to me sweetly. He advised me that I should never ever resort to lying in future, even if it is to meet me. He further advised that the life of a sanya’sii should be based on satya (satya’shrayii) in all conditions. I promised Him that I shall never take shelter of falsehood. Thereafter He regained His usual affectionate self of Kalyansundaram. I was, then, swept away in the flood of bliss that ensued from His Divine form.

### Summing up the progress of A’nanda Ma’rga in South-East Asia during my tenure as Sectorial Secretary (SS)

It was the January of 1981 that Ba’ba’ appointed me to be the SS of Manila sector. I was doubtful and a bit afraid to handle such a huge responsibility. Ba’ba’ knew my predicament and inspired me to take up the new challenge. He also assured me you will be welcomed by the ma’rgii there and that I will be successful in accomplishing the missionary objectives. As days passed by I realized that everything happened as He had ordained, fortunately for me He chose me to be His medium.

My first tour of Manila sector began with Bangkok, Kualalampur, Malakka, Singapore, finally Manila. As if it was preplanned, wherever I went in my sector, I received a very warm and affectionate welcome by both the WTs and margiis alike.

Tears rolled down whenever I thought of Him and experienced the flood of His grace. I do not remember for how many days of my posting in Manila sector I had cried out for Him. I also realized that He was the Lord of life and death, when He cured me of Blood cancer which was then, a incurable disease.

Again He had repeatedly asked me to get the plant of the berries with thorns (Lancania Americana), which could not be procured despite the efforts of so many workers of my sector who tried unsuccessfully in many botanical gardens, finally I pleaded like a child, then He smiled at my plight and told me to go back to sector and search for it and you will find it.

In the two and a half years of my tenure in Manila sector many unusual incidences occurred. Most countries of Manila Sector are very poor. Except Philippines everywhere else Margii establishments were non existent. Communication was difficult and expensive. During my tenure intensive and extensive missionary work progressed in all of the sector. New Jagrtis were constructed, Master units lands were bought, ne Kindergarten schools started, Yoga Centers opened. All this needed huge deployment of funds and effort. He made all this possible through our, otherwise, weak medium.

Manila sector includes – Philippines, Malaysia, Indonesia, Singapore, Brunie, Thailand, Myamnar, Laos, Cambodia and Vietnam. Most countries then were under ruled by dictators. Geographically south-east Asia is surrounded by Pacific sea and is the home of thousands of small and big islands. The climate in the whole sector remains warm and sultry like in any tropical country. There is hardly any need of warm clothings. There are only two seasons-summer and the rainy. It abounds in natural beauty everywhere.

You will find all varieties of plants and trees viz. coconut, banana, rubber, Palm and jackfruit. Huge areas are covered by thick forests. It has many species of land and aquarian animals and birds. The variety of sweet and juicy fruits available in part of the world is unthinkable anywhere else. The most of the population in south-east Asia is

Christian, Bouddha followed by Muslims, while only a small section of people follow Hinduism.

A’nanda ma’rga is a popular movement in Philippines. After India it is here that there are maximum devoted ma’rgiis. A’nanda ma’rga System of sa’dhana’ and AMURT’s service activities are popular and admired by the public. A’nanda ma’rga is registered under the security exchange of the government.

The population of Philippines is 45 million which inhabit its 700 islands. Philippines is divided in three parts – Luzone in North, Veshaya in center and Mindnao in south. Its people are mild, sweet, sentimental and lethargy by nature. They are friendly and straight forward with the foreigner. For long time it has been ruled by Spanish and Americans. Henceunder their influence it has lost its own cultural traditions. Most of the educated people aspire to live in the US. Unlike India and China their family bond is loose. The social order is matrilineal. Drugs and drinking is spoiling the younger generation like a epidemic.

Due to favourable climate and developed agricultural technology and practices, it has been exploited by multinational companies. It is a debtor of US. Since 2009 its economic condition has further deteriorated. It currency has been devalued and inflation is killing the common people.

We are running 22 schools, and many Yoga centers where we conduct Food For All program and regular medical camps. Dharma cakra is regularly conducted in several cities. There are four master Units, seven children homes, and ten Jagrtis. In metro Manila we running two schools, a children’s home, a Printing Press, a vegetarian Restaurant and five big office buildings. Sectorial MG quarter is being constructed. Our sectorial office is in center of manila and is recognized spiritual center of Philippines. In Iloilo and Shibu our Jagrtis are famous beautispots and are famous for regular free food distribution and medical service.

In the southern region in Davao we have three Jagrtis and a Whole timer training center, three schools, one children’s home, a disadvantaged children home and Marga Guru Residence. AMURT recognized institution for service like the Christian Missionaries and we also jointly work with Red Cross. In Mindnao Government had recognized our Neo-Humanist Education system meditation practices. AM a’ca’ryas are called upon to give instructions in our kind of Meditation and Yoga’sana in government schools.

After Philippines, Singapore region including east & west Malayesia, Singapore and northern Sumatra happens to be the most developed A’nanda ma’rga Yoga centers. Penang, Ipoh, Alorstar, Selan Gor, Melatown, Joharbaru, Quantan, Kotakina, Balu & Kiching are some of the Malayesian town where popular AM Schools and Yoga centers

are operating and serving Malaysian people. Likewise in Medan of north Sumatra and Singapore we are running Yoga centers and schools. Ma'rgiis are, generally, good devotees and ideological.

A'nanda Ma'rga's third developed region is Jakarta which includes beautiful islands of Indonesia, South Sumatra, Bali & Kalimantan. This region has 4000 islands and a huge concentration of population of 140 million, of which 70% are Muslims, 25% Baoddha and remaining Hindus and Christians. Its cultural tradition is old. Generally there is a fear of Avidya' Ta'ntrikas. Many peculiar stories from Ra'ma'yan and Maha'bha'rat are popular here. Bali is an Hindu island and are devotees of Shiva. They Shiva Liunga (Phallus Worship) is worshiped here. They are the believers of Tantra and have a conservative disposition. It is a developing country and people are, generally, vocal like Indians. Although its climate is tropical-hot, even then while travelling in Bus or train they will keep the windows shut for fear of wind (Ma'ruti – god of wind). A very large population is addicted to smoking.

A'nanda ma'rga is becoming increasingly popular among Chinese, Christians and Hindus. Muslims are attracted to our non-dogmatic practices. In Indonesia there are six centers and children's homes and schools, especially in Jakarta, Chirebon, Bandung and Sol. There are seven active centers in Bali.

The fourth region is Bangkok. This includes Thailand. It is a Theravadi Buddhist nation and they are proud of their heritage. Generally people here are gentle and peaceful. It is full of Buddhist Matha. Buddhist Sanyasis are respected here. Green revolution has made this country self reliant to a great extent. New ideas and strangers are unwelcome here. Despite tremendous effort, A'nanda ma'rga could not establish itself here. Two of our schools and homes are operating and our services are being recognized. AMURT/AMURTEL have earned a good name after last Sunami hit Thailand. AMURT has built many shelters and schools for the Sunami affected population.

Next region is Rangoon which includes Myamnar. It is poor country ruled by a dictator with virtually very little freedom of expression. The native people are receptive to new ideas and Meditation. Although with some difficulty A'nanda ma'rga is spreading. AMURT has been active here to serve the disaster affected people of the country.

Last two regions are Namphen and Saigon. The countries in these regions viz. Cambodia and Vietnam are under communist rule. People are interested in spirituality, despite iron curtain A'nanda Ma'rga is spreading, albeit, slowly. In both countries strong units have been established. Hundreds of Ma'rgiis are spreading spiritual vibrations through kiirtana and Prabhat Samgiita.

### I am commanded to proceed for world tour

In the later half of 1983 I was posted in Manila as global GBTC Shraman (ASD). During WRDS in Kolkata me and my SG Dada Shubhabuddhananda were punished for not taking adequate action to improve organizational discipline and progress of work. Ba’ba’ inquired, "What have you been doing in Manila? Why have you not undertaken world tour?" I replied that my job was of stationary nature as I am supposed to conduct LFT training there. He was not convinced and asked me to proceed for a tour of Europe and America. Immediately after His instructions I prepared a plan for my tour and took permission of my SG & Ba’ba’ and proceeded for Europe. During the tour I got to know many new people and developed relations with many influential persons. I returned back to India after my tour duly satisfied and went back to my place of posting in Manila sector.

Purpose behind His asking me to take up this tour remained a mystery until the future events unfolded. Only His all-knowing mind knows why He does something, how He plays with our little minds and always prepares us in advance before the real turn of events.

### Wife of Pradyumnajii is cured of Cancer

Pradyumnajii is an old Ma’rgii of Purnia, in Bihar. His entire family is an ardent follower of A’nanda Ma’rga. Ba’ba’ has stayed in their parental home called Arvind Nivas and gave them the golden opportunity to serve Him.

This incidence took place in 1983-84. I met Pradyumnajii in Kolkata during our WRDS. On my enquiry about the welfare of his family I learnt that his wife was suffering from Cancer and was admitted in Patna Medical College Hospital for treatment. I encouraged him and told him my story, how Ba’ba’ cured me of blood cancer and saved me from my terminal condition. I also suggested that he should get her here as soon as possible for the Darshan of Ba’ba’.

I found that in three days he brought his wife to Lake Garden. I met her. She was really sick & appeared very weak. However, mentally she was full of devotion for Him. It was the morning hours that day and Ba’ba’ had come down for His field walk. Before boarding the car He enquired about the welfare of the devotees. When He came near Pradyumnajii He called him and enquired about his family members. Both the husband and wife were overwhelmed by His grace. I was also watching them from a distance while Ba’ba’ was affectionately conversing with them. Later I met them and learned of their satisfaction. They stayed there for three days and his wife started showing signs of improvement and eventually got completely cured.

### Ba’ba’ is the Lord of devotees

Once during demonstration year in 1969, He had revealed that He was the embodiment of devotion. This universe is His mental creation and all have equal right to be seated on His lap. On another such occasion He had asked a group of devotees about the purpose of His coming to this world. He opted Himself to reply. The true mission of His life is to suffuse all in the flood of devotion.

Once during His tour of Manila, He went to an American cemetery for a morning walk. He sat below a Chinese Banyan tree with His entourage. He was His charming self and devotees experienced flow of His bliss. Inspired by the enchanting environment the devotees surrounding Him started singing a devotional song in chorus. It was a pleasant moment, which moved the entire environment. While the trees around in the cemetery were still as no wind was blowing. But the Banyan tree with its leaves, below which sat the Lord with devotees, were swaying and dancing in rhythm with the chorus song. A devotee seated next to Him could not hold and asked for the reason. With His characteristic bewitching smile Ba'ba' demystified the incidence by saying that the tree with its leaves were also responding to the sweet melody of the song. They were enjoying like we do. Devotees elsewhere must have experienced similar incidences whenever kiirtan is done in open places. Even the birds & animals in the vicinity are also affected by the vibrations of bliss.

I used to be confused about the true nature of Bhakti and the way to attain it? Once I had an opportunity when He told me that to dance according to His will with the sole intention of pleasing Him is real devotion.

### Take bath even when you have fever!

It was the occasion of WRDS in Kolkata. I was suffering from high fever and hence did not attend reporting that day. Ba'ba' expressed His displeasure on the then GS Dada. When I was informed about it I went for reporting next day, although my fever had not subsided. As we were standing for reporting in His presence, He inquired who among the workers has come here without bathing. I raised my hand in acknowledgement. He asked me the reason. I told him I did not take bath since I was having high fever. He was not happy and I was punished my act of omission. Then He gave the instruction that bathing was not optional even when you had high fever. Following the punishment my fever and severe body pain vanished in thin air.

### Ego and Pride are His food

We were attending WRDS in Kolkata. This time I had a puffed up ego, for three WTs were created in my sector and many new service projects were initiated. I had expected that Ba'ba' would praise me before all the SS and I will receive the extra share of love from Him.

As we stood there, Ba’ba’ started the reporting session asking for the number of whole timers created in each sector. The SS present attending the reporting were all worried since for the period specified they had failed to create any whole timer. When my turn came, I proudly told Him about the creation of three WTs in my sector. I also did not fail to mention the many new service projects which have been started in my sector. To everyone’s surprise, instead of praising my accomplishments, He not only ridiculed me but punished me more than those who had failed to create any. I was baffled. This, then, prompted me to analyze the incidence to understand why it all happened? It did not take much thinking to realize that it was my own arrogance which has caused it. It was my egocentric attitude which was camofledging my mind, blurring my analytical mind. As aresult I could not appreciate the simple truth that I standing before Him-my all knowing Guru to report. The only thing important there was humility and surrender unto Him & not my ego-trip. I could not understand that in reality we are only mediums & it is He who alone happens to be the real doer. It is His love for His devotees that He devours our arrogance & pride for liberating us from the bondage of avidya’. No sooner this realization dawned on me, I repented for my own folly and prayed for forgiveness.

In the evening the same day, He called me and sweetly taught me the essence of humility and self-surrender. He told me that egoism and arrogance are the greatest enemies on spiritual path.

### Where should my feet face?

I was not sure whether one can sleep turning feet towards the photo of Ba’ba’ or not. Once it so happened that I was given to sleep in aroom where Ba’ba’s photo were hung on all the four sides. I could not go to sleep that night and kept praying Him for forgiveness.

Soon thereafter I was attending the WRDS and when my turn came Ba’ba’ impromptu mentioned to me that one should avoid turning the feet towards the photo of the Guru. But if photos are hung on all sides of a room then you need not worry and sleed without any guilt in mind.

### In German Jail

In compliance of His instructions I left for world tour. My first stay was in Athens the capital of Greece. I stayed there for few days and inspired the ASD worker for extending financial support to the organization. From there I left for West Germany. I stayed in west Berlin with my departmental workers and prepared a plan to expand the our activities in Europe. From their I stayed in Norway, followed by Sweden and Denmark. In thes places I was able to work with local margiis and inspired them to improve their organizational contribution. Then I went to Italy and East Gemany. Then I proceeded to

Munich and stayed with sister Ranjita and similarly expanded organizational activities with local margiis and LFTs.

Then I went to Yugoslavia and Austria by train. Due to some technical problems I was arrested by German police and spent 55 days in German Jail. They kept me in a room with other prisoners. The very first day I was served beef according to their jail Manual. I told the person who had brought the food that I am an Indian Yogi and I take only vegetarian food. He did not pay any heed to what I said and left the plate on my bed. I kept the plate below my cot. This routine continued for three days and despite my explaining them that I will rather die then take any non-vegetarian food. They kept telling me here I have to take food as prescribed in Jail manual or die. It was unthinkable for them that somebody will remain hungry for three days. As three days passed without my taking any food, the jail administration was disturbed. The Jail superintendent called me in his office and enquired. I explained him that being a Yogi I do not take any kind of meat. I am a lacto-vegetarian. I would prefer to die then take meat of any kind. He was quite surprised but understood the seriousness of the situation. After a while he said that jail manual provides only serving Meat everyday except on Sundays when vegetarian food is served. Seeing my adamant position, he was impressed and brought fruits and milk from his own house and gave me.

Next day onwards fruits, milk, bread and butter was arranged for me. Then I requested him to shift me to some other place as I am a yogi and can not stand the smoke in the room as most prisoners were habitual chain smokers.
