He took a day and then shifted me to another room. This was a clean, airconditioned room on the second floor. It was perhaps October and it was quite cold there. From my window the ice-capped mountain tops of Alps were visible. It was a very beautiful sight to behold.

There was a shopping center inside the Jail. If your account has sufficient balance then you could purchase anything of your liking every Thursday and Sunday. The cost used to be cut from your account. Fortunately my account had sufficient balance. There was arrangement of bathing with hot water, two days a week. I used to take bath twice a week with soap and for the remaining days I used to take bath taking water from the wash basin with some difficulty. Prisoners were allowed to go for stroll everyday in the morning between 6 to 7 under supervision of a policeman.

The jail stay turned out to be a great opportunity for sa'dhana'. Everyday I used to get up at 3/4am and complete my kiirtan, sadhana and a'sana. There was ample scope to read good books. The enjoyment during kiirtana here in the jail was unparalleled. This kind of bliss I never ever experienced. It was here that I got the experience of Bha'va Sama'dhi during Kiirtana. I also got to write my experiences and got them published in our magazines.

After the technical problem responsible for my arrest was resolved I was deported to Delhi. I used to get unusually disturbing ideas in my mind during those days and would pray for His help. After I returned back, during a reporting session, He reminded me of those strange thoughts, when I did not reply, He also left at that.

### My posting in Jamaica

It was sometime in 1983. I was then posted in Manila as the Shramana of GBTC. The Central office informed me that I am being transferred as Global Office Secretary, Proutist Universal to Copenhagen. I was advised to immediately pack up and report there. When I was preparing to proceed to Copenhagen, I was called up again and told not to leave for Copenhagen, instead reach Kolkata to see Ba'ba'. I was happy at the prospect of getting the opportunity to meet Ba'ba'. Accordingly I prepared and reached Kolkata and reported to OS & the GS. The same afternoon Ba'ba' called me. He asked me, how it would be if instead of Copenhagen, I am transferred to Jamaica. I replied "Ba'ba', as you may please". He further told me that you may have some difficulty in the beginning, but don't be alarmed. Everything will be fine after sometime. He assured me saying, "I am always with you". I felt comforted by His affectionate words of blessings. Then He instructed me to prepare myself for the new posting and leave for my destination in four days.

After leaving His room I met Dada Sarvananda who had worked in Jamaica and learnt about the situation there. He told me the field is difficult as of now. Margiis are not as supportive as they were ten years ago when Ba'ba' had visited Jamaica and conducted DMC. That was the best period of the Ma'rga. He said he has spent ten years in that island. In early days Ma'rgiis came forward and helped in all programmes. They hired a beautiful house and we started the LFT training center and a school there. Those days Shanti Devi was our Bhukti Pradha'na. She was a very spirited devotee and very wealthy too. She used to pay the rent and the personal expenses of WTs. But suddenly she suffered from breast cancer. She got scared by this. Her close family members also started taunting her. They would ask, why her Ba'ba' was not taking her care? This adversely affected her dedication to Ma'rga and she became inactive. With her other margiis also left. She stopped paying the rent. My survival also became difficult. Eventually I returned back to India. Then Ba'ba' posted another senior worker there. He was too scared to go there. He prayed for an alternative posting anywhere else. Ba'ba' has conceded his request.

I was posted in the backdrop of such a situation in Jamaica. The SS New York was arranging my ticket, but it was taking time, may be 20-25 days. Ba'ba' told me to leave in 3-4 days. Hence I did not want to wait for the ticket from New York sector. I had enough money to buy the ticket to Jamaica. Hence I decided to go ahead and bought my ticket to Kingston via London. Although it was a little expensive, still Guru's

command must be followed. Thus I left for my new field after receiving His permission and blessings. At the Kingston airport I was received by Janakjii-a local ma'rgii, who accompanied me to the Jagrti. It was a huge place with a large compound and a majestic building. There was a mango orchard inside the compound and a huge tamarind tree in the front of the building. But for want of upkeep since last ten months, it was looking like ghost affected Bungalow. Its rent was also not paid since that time. The property belonged to the Chief Justice of Jamaica. It was located in the most prestigious locality of the capital city. Prime Minister's bungalow was in the same locality as also the houses of other ministers and wealthy people of the capital. The poor upkeep of the property had attracted the ire of its owner, who had not been paid the rent for such a long time.

I sent word to call the bhukti pradha'na, he came next day morning. He had no enthusiasm and was totally indifferent. He told me that they have lost faith in Ba'ba' after the last Bhukti Pradha'na Shanti Devi suffered from breast cancer. Many Dadas have visited here but have failed to create any impact. School is not running as it should. We have not paid the rent since ten months. We have decided now to return the property to its owner. You may please return back to India. There is no need of your services here. I was also disturbed by his dejected expression and seeing the gloomy picture of the situation as described by him. I had only $100. And it was an expensive place. Without the help of local people it was difficult to survive what to say of paying the rent. I became very scared and prayed for help. I recollected his parting words. He had told me not to be worried, even though it will be difficult in the beginning. I remembered His smiling face and I was immensely reassured.

After a while I humbly submitted to the bhukti pradhana, that I have been sent by Ba'ba' to take the mission forward in Jamaica and I do not intend to return back to India. I will serve the society here come what may. Your help will be amply appreciated. I will definitely demonstrate the strength of my mission through my good work. He was, however, not convinced. Before leaving in pinching way he told me to stay here, if you like, but don't expect any thing from us. I would still suggest you better return back. I firmly told him that I have not come here to return. It is my Guru who has sent me and I know He will help me when needed. He looked quite surprised and left after sometime. I was taken aback at the strange welcome in this unknown land.

This was the testing period of my life. I was in Jamaica – an island thousands of miles away from India. It used to be a British colony, now it is an independent nation. About 150 years ago British government brought African and Indian labour to work in the fields. Three fourth of the population was of African origin and 20% Indian, remainder were European and American. Most of the people here were Christian. American influence was visible in all walks of life in this, otherwise, it was a very poor country.

My daily routine was to clean the compound. Initially it was taking the whole day to clean the place. When I used to get tired of hard physical work I used to cry in His name. Most of the time I would keep thinking of how to propagate the mission. The situation here was really difficult. After I had spent five days in Jamaica, one evening I was sitting and thinking. All of a sudden a well kept Impala car entered the compound, and from the car emerged a very tall, aristocratic, white man. Looking towards me he spoke loudly – I am so-and-so the owner of this house, I have not been paid my rent for ten months. You guys have spoiled my house. It is badly kept. No painting has been done since ten years. The garden is in such an appalling condition. You must pay my full rent today, else I will get you evicted out of my house.

I remembered Him and gently told him, that I have heard about you that you have very kindly rented your property to us. I have just arrived here four days ago. Since you have already waited for ten months without being paid, I request you to give me another two months during which I will pay all your dues. I will also make sure to improve the upkeep of the house and the garden. If I fail to do so I will myself leave the place and go. My firmness and assurance convinced him. He instructed me that he would now visit me after two months. If you keep your word, well & good otherwise I will drive you out of this place. I agreed and shortly after that he left. I continued to think how I am going to manage so much funds. If I fail then A'nanda marga will be finished from this island. I must accept this challenge in the interest of the mission and the name of my Guru.

It was my first experience with Africans. They used to forcibly enter into the jagrti compound and would take bath under the shower all naked. On refusing to allow they would turn violent. It took quite some time for me to adjust with the environment, way of life and mannerism. To organize help I wrote to all my sympathizers apprising them of the situation in Jamaica and earnestly seeking their support. All these margiis obliged me within the stipulated time and I was able to collect the exact amount of funds needed to pay the rent dues. Thus I cleared the dues of the landlord. On receiving the amount he expressed his happiness and assured me of his unstinted support being the Chief Justice of Jamaica. I told him that I will get the necessary repairs of the building done and also get it painted. He said that he was very pleased with me because I kept my word. From now on you are my friend. You can always bank on my help and support. Hearing these soothing words I was overwhelmed by Ba'ba's grace. Those who came forward and responded to my request for help were the devoted family of Ravi and Shashi of Oslo, Norway. They were the blessed children of Ba'ba' in whose house Ba'ba' stayed during His European tour. Then there were Bro. Shankar and Vasudeva of Oslo again. And also Bros. Prabhakar and Avaniisha from Sweden, sister Jyoti from Finland, brother Devanshu from Munich, Dr Chitra from Singapore, Rene Pushlan President AMPS, Kumar Guru from Malaysia, Mantreshvara from Los Angeles, Shrinivasan from Kualampur and now settled in England. The name of Dada Devashraddhananda must

be included here, but for his moral and financial support it would have been just impossible to survive in those hostile circumstances. Dada Shankarananda was the other sanyasin who looked after my needs without asking.

It was an opportunity to experience His direct grace. My testing time continued for three months, until I was able to completely surrender to Him. Then everything started happening according to His will. The school staff used to be very critical of me. They would not lose any opportunity to make fun of me. But once they saw that I was able to pay the ten months rent dues and got the building repainted, then, they regained faith in the mission. In the meantime I was able to establish good contacts in the Universities, Colleges and clubs. Many influential people of the capital came in my contact and started visiting our A'shram. The school also became healthy and the teachers started getting paid regularly. The school staff started respecting me. Ma'rgiis who saw for themselves the changed situation felt sorry for their non-cooperative behavior. I always treated them well and always inspired them towards Sa'dhana' and the missionary work. Slowly they started joining in the weekly Dharma Cakra. I inspired five LFTs and started training them. These LFTs proved to be a great asset of the mission.

In the meantime I was offered buildings for starting new schools free of cost. These LFT workers came handy. I posted these young men to run those schools. Slowly young educated persons were attracted to the Ma'rga and they came forward to help many of our social service projects in the poor residential areas. Thus I was able to generate interest among the doctors, engineers and artists and musicians as also public leaders, & journalists. These new contacts came forward to help me in many ways to further advance the mission. Thus A'nanda ma'rga regained its lost image and became a popular movement throughout Jamaica.

Days passed. One fine morning the old Bhukti Pradhana sister Shanti Devi came to the Jagrti to see me. She had heard about the good work taking place. She was duly impressed after seeing the improved condition of the A'shram, the school and the training center. She told me of her story when Ba'ba' had visited Jamaica. She was the Bhukti Pradhana. She took care of Ba'ba's kitchen. She received His love and affection. Then suddenly due to her breast cancer and the pressure of her family she lost faith in Him. But now seeing the wonderful work done by me she feels really very happy. She expressed her best wishes for success in my endeavour. I told her that once I was also suffering from blood cancer. My case was one of terminal illness. But I never lost faith in Him. He loves His devotees. To everyone's surprise He cured me. You should restart sa'dhana' with full faith in Him. Everything will be fine. My discussion had a positive effect on her. She invited me to her family and introduced me to her family members. I narrated many personal experiences of Ba'ba'. They were impressed.

Once a wealthy margii came to me and wished he could be of some help. I asked him to help me in getting the present property of Ananda Marga Jagrti to be bought out. This way a permanent asset of the mission will be created in Jamaica. He agreed to extend his full support financially.

Meanwhile the Indian embassy conspired against A’nanda Ma’rga and influenced the Jamaican government against me. They gave the stories that A’nanda ma’rga was a terrorist outfit and described our good work as something anti government. The Indian embassy falsely implicated me. Under their influence the Jamaican Immigration department advised me that since my work permit is getting expired, I should go out of Jamaica and reapply for visa. I was told that my application for work permit will be immediately approved. I was carried away by their advice and returned back to India and from there applied for work permit. But the Indian government played its part and my visa was refused.

Back in Kolkata Ba’ba’ called me to His room. I told Him the good news of Jamaica. He appreciated my effort and praised me saying you did the right thing by organizing the Jamaican youth. I felt blessed by His sweet words. I realized how He made use of me as His medium for advancing His mission in Jamaica.

### Chapter -8

I experience His grace during my tenure in A’nanda shila’ Master Unit (ASMU)

I was posted in A’nanda Shila’ Master Unit in July 1986. Before that Ba’ba’ gave me the opportunity to tour the whole world and visit the A’nanda Ma’rga units. After returning back to India under pressure of the immigration department of government of Jamaica, I was posted as the Rector of A’nanda Shila’ MU. It was here that I went through many significant experiences in my organizational life. It was here that Ba’ba’ got many new projects implemented for the development of ASMU. I also got lots of opportunity to come in His physical contact.

When I was posted to ASMU Dada Nigmananda accompanied me there. Compared to the hustle bustle of my overseas assignments, posting in ASMU was like being in the abode of peace. ASMU was located in tribal belt of Jharkhand and the land area stretched to 104 acres. It was covered with a forest of Sal trees, a small rivulet flowing through the site with a Tantra Piitha called Brgu Piitha at its bank. Apart from a Training Center there was an agricultural farm. There was a school for the tribal children, a childrens home, Abha’ Seva sadana and a dairy farm with five desi cows.

The master unit land was under dispute. The locals and the forest department kept a watch on our activities and looked for an opportunity to capture our land. Because of whole timer training center the MU had gained importance. The police department was

also active in the area because of the training center. It was a challenging situation for us. After coming here I immersed myself in the work to develop the farm. The principal trainer dada Shambhuyananda had left on a long tour leaving me alone in ASMU. This increased pressure on my limited available time.

### Erecting fence of Barbed wire

After being posted in ASMU, I had a chance to go to Kolkata for reporting and His darshan. There He told me to erect barbed wire fence around the ASMU land. Fencing the 104 acres land required expenditure of Rs. 1 lac. It was a huge sum of money those days. It was not so easy to raise such a fund. I accepted His command whole heartedly and plunged into organizing the fund and the activity. I knew in my heart of heart that when He has allotted this task to me He alone will create the necessary resources. I returned from Kolkata and reached Ranchi on my way to A’nanda Shila’. At Ranchi I called a meeting of ma’rgiis and told them about Ba’ba’s instructions to erect barbed wire fence around the MU. The Ma’rgiis agreed to help me for implementing the job. The master unit was infested with thick bushes everywhere. For erecting the fence first these bushes had to be removed. I employed 30 labours for simultaneously attending fencing and bush cutting job. It took me six months to complete the fencing. I used to supervise work from morning eight till four in the evening.

After the fencing was completed, I again went to Kolkata and informed Him about the completion of barbed wire fence. He said, barbed wire alone will not do, you have to immediately takeup building a pucca brick boundary. I looked at His face and did not know what to say. It was a huge task and would call for an expenditure of not less than 15 Lac. I looked towards the face of GS Dada who was standing by my side. I assumed he must be thinking the same way as I did. Ba’ba’, however, insisted that the task must be completed in a month’s time.

### Building Brick boundary

After some inter squables I accepted the task with all my heart. As I stepped out of Ba’ba’s room bro. Prabhakar from Germany was waiting outside His room. He immediately offered me a help of Rs. 1 Lac. Another ma’rgii sister Dr. Chitra followed him and gave me Rs 40k. I sought the support of a few workers for the task. After collecting a part of the funds needed I left for ASMU that very evening. I engaged a brick Kiln and ordered 100000 bricks, 400 bags of cement and the required amount of sand and hired a gang of 40 construction labour and a group of masons to begin the work on war footing. With the boundary construction started the opposition from local village land mafia. Since we were prepared to handle the situation well in time, the construction did not get disrupted. Ba’ba’ had asked me to construct the boundary from the western side of the site. In 15 days we covered a mile long boundary. The locals did not expect the

speed of construction to be so fast. They had planned that after cutting open the barbed wire fence they will enter & occupy the west side land. But their scheme did not materialize since by the time they could prepare to execute their plan the tall brick boundary wall already stood there. I, then, understood why He wanted me to start the construction immediately first covering the western side of the site.

Everytime I went for reporting He would assign a new apparently difficult task for me to execute. This became a norm. with this ASMU started humming with activity.

### Dairy Farm

On another occasion He enquired about the number of cows and the milk produced in A’nanda Shila’. I replied that we had six cows but the milk produced was only three litres. He wasn’t satisfied. I said we had only desi cows. He asked me to arrange to establish a healthy dairy farm with Jersy Cows. By His grace I was able materialize His desire. We bought a number of healthy cows and a proper dairy farm was started.

### Ba’ba’ demands a gift

Once I visited Kolkata without any programme. Ba’ba’ called me in the afternoon that day and asked me that, though, He saved my life twice, I did not give Him anygift. He spoke all this very naturally. But He was damn serious. I did not know what to say. I thought for the moment, can I fulfill His demand. He took a promise from me. Then He asked me to build two bio-gas plants and one Oil extraction Mill in ASMU. I was aghast. I had no clue to arrange that kind of investment. I came out of His room in that perplexed state of mind. This time as if it was preordained, I met with an old Margii bro Mantreshvara from Norway and Manohar from Germany. When I told them about the project given to me, both happily, offered to help me. With this I started to receive help from many other margiis viz. Avanish from Sweden, Vasudev and sisters Sulocana and Jyoti from Finland. Later more funds came from Dr Chitra of Singapore, bro. Kartika & Devanshu from Germany. Bro Kumarguru from Malayesia and Rene Punshelan President AMPS, Malayesia

My stay in ASMU brought so many such memories of accomplishing the tasks assigned to me, once I accepted them whole heartedly. Help overflowed to accomplish the task from around the world. To name a few those who helped me were bro. Prabhakar from Sweden & Shankar from Oslo, family of Shashi/Ravi from Oslo, sister Ranjiita from Munich and bro. Kamaleshvara from Italy, Vishvabandhu from Guatemala.

After returning back I plunged into materializing the project within a time schedule given to me. I went to see the SDO incharge, in Chanho block. He was happy to know about the Bio-gas project and informed me that government was giving 60% cost of the bio-gas project as a grant. But to avail the grant takes time. I told him that I wanted the task

to be over in one month. Understanding the urgency he agreed to depute a engineer and a technician for installing the equipment. I agreed to reimburse the deputation charges. Thus two Bio-gas plants were installed there as per schedule. For the Oil mill I sent Jiteshjii to place order for the oil extraction mill from Gaziabad. The entire project was commissioned within one month. Ba’ba’ blessed me after I informed Him of the completion of His desire.

There is nothing superior in spiritual life to live upto Guru’s command. To materialize Guru’s will is called Guru puja’ and to translate His desire into physical reality after taking Guru’s permission is called Guru-gata.

### Jeep and constructing new buildings

We bought a new Jeep for the Master Unit. Simultaneously we felt the need for new buildings for projects underway. Their construction also started simultaneously. A new building was needed for the training center. This was followed by buiding security posts for guards at the four corners. Agricultural farm was developed along the river. Vegetable production started. Abha seva sadana, Veternary hospital and Seri-culture center were also started.

### Desire to serve the vegetable needs of Ba’ba’s kitchen from Ananda Shila’ MU

Ba’ba’ fulfills desires of His devotees who work to please Him. I used to send vegetables and fruits grown in ASMU to Ba’ba’s kichten. He used to relish those items. Once I thought that I should organize vegetable production in ASMU such that the vegetable requirements of Ba’ba’s kitchen are exclusively met from here.

Once Ba’ba’ commented that like Parmeshvarananda those who are Rectors of other Master Units should also think the same way. When personal secretary of Ba’ba’ told me this I was immensely overwhelmed and my mind transported to the other world.

### Overcoming Water scarcity in A’nanda Shila’

Every year with the onset of summer starting with March through July there used to be acute water scarcity in ASMU. This used to severely restrict drinking water availability and water needed for irrigation & other miscellaneous requirements. Once I inquired from Ba’ba’ where can I find water in ASMU. He retorted that why I am asking Him. He was not an engineer to predict where to find water? After a while He told His PA to convey to Parmeshvarananda to look for water in the north. River was in the north. He wanted to build a dam on the river to solve the water scarcity to some extent. But I could not quite understand the hint. I vainfully spent lot of money on bordrilling bore-well near the river without any success. This amply frustrated me.

Later as I understood, & prepared a plan to build a dam on the river and digging a well in the river course. I worked with the then CTS Dada Sadhaneshvarananda and GS Dada and materialized the project successfully.

### Seri-culture and Gardening

Once Ba’ba’ inquired about the land and water situation in ASMU. He wanted to know what could be done to make the Master Unit self-reliant. I told Him that the land of ASMU is not fertile. It is infested with termite and does not retain water. Ba’ba’ told me that you should instead grow more fruits and develop a fruit orchard. For self reliance you should immediately start seri-culture.

Following His direction I planted Shahatu’ta trees and also built a house for seri-culture. But for want of adequate knowledge of seri-culture the plan did not succeed. But I did plant lots of fruit bearing trees viz. Mango, Jackfruit, Banana, Guava, Papaya, and Lemon. Thus to please Him, I tried my best to develop ASMU as an ideal MU.

### Preparing ASMU for His visit

Once Ba’ba’ was visiting Ranchi. We in AS were very much encouraged by this news and prepared to bring Him to the MU. All the trainees worked hard to improve the house-keeping of the MU. All items of His personal use were bought and arrangement for His comfortable stay. Being busy with these arrangements, I could not present myself at the appropriate time at Ranchi to invite Him. Later as I reached there, Ba’ba’ told me that He knew about all the preparations made for His visit in ASMU. But due to my busy schedule, I will not be able to visit now. However, I am pleased with your effort.

### Building a home for WWD Didis in Ranchi

Once WWD workers were meeting outside the room of Ba’ba’ in Ranchi Jagrti. Ashram of DSL Ranchi was in the outskirts and was unsuitable for living then. When I reached there, Ba’ba’ said that Parmeshvarananda will be able to help WWD in this task. I took it as His blessings and immediately arranged funds for the purpose with the help of ma’rgiis and a land for Didi’was bought in Kadru locality. Construction for the Jagrti on the plot also started immediately in right urnest.

### Adding two rooms to Ma’rga Guru Residence

Once Da’da’ Shraddhananda and A’c. Kinshuk and his family visited ASMU. At that time we faced shortage of living space for Kinshukjii. After their visit construction of two rooms on the first floor was taken up. A meeting hall had already been built there.

### Untimely demise of Dada Sa’dhaneshvarananda

It is well known that birth and death is predestined and out of human control. However death of Dada Sadhaneshvarananda at a young age was a great loss to me personally. He was the CTS and stood in support of me for the development of ASMU. He was a simple, mild mannered worker with a pure heart. Dada Prasha'nta'nanda was posted in his place. In his memory we erected a memorial as our offering of love.

### My last construction work – A student hostel

After lots of thought and to make the MU self-reliant, it was decided to build a hostel for students. Once it was ready it attracted students from whole of Bihar and Jharkhand. Admissions also went up as more joined the school. This work was continued after my transfer by dada Yuktesha'nanda. Even now this school and the hostel is the soul of our MU. Ba'ba' took a promise from me to maintain the sanctity of MG Quarter. Looking at the gross neglect of the MG quarter now I am greatly pained. I accept responsibility for the dilapidated condition of MG quarter & not able to do anything.

### Controlling the severerity of my disease from an alien planet

In the A'nanda katha of Dada Naginajii, I once read the chapter on sa'dhana' & Samska'ra. There he wrote “when a bad samska'ra is redeemed then it disturbs the sa'dhaka. But if the sa'dhaka tightly holds on to the lotus feet of Sadguru, then the sa'dhaka attracts His non-causal grace. As a result the sa'dhaka is able to subserve its samska'ra, rather, naturally”.

In my life also a similarly strange but awesome incidence took place in 1988 in A'nanda nagar. The A'nanda Purnima' DMC had just finished. It was the 4th of June. In retrospect the incidence had its initiation two days before. It was the usual practice that the members of Madhura Gos'thi would get to meet Ba'ba' once after DMC. The responsibility rested on me and Keshava'nandajii. We had fixed the date and time of meeting with Ba'ba'. The meeing was scheduled at 5 pm. The venue was Madhu Koraka - the residence of Ba'ba' in A'nanda nagar. Ba'ba' was informed that 55 members will attend the meeting of Madhur Gos'thi. We waited for Him. His visit was, however, a bit delayed. In the meantime one member expressed to go to rest room. About the same time all of a sudden Ba'ba' came in. He took His seat and the door of the room was closed. Hence that member was deprived of his participation.

The meeting of Madhur Gos'thi went on as expected. Ba'ba' let the flow of devotion drown all the devotees in the room unabated. Later He gave some instructions regarding sa'dhana' and the missionary work. The meeting ended after an hour. Finally He blessed each one of us individually. After the meeting Ba'ba' called me again and asked me the reason of giving wrong number of the attending members. He said you asked me that 55 members will be attending but there were only 54 members. Being a

sanyasi why did you lie? I explained Him the reason, but I was punished for this so called act of falsehood. Same evening again while walking on the roof top he called me and harshly treated me before everybody. I was upset following the incidence.

In the night before He retired dada keshvananda told Him that Parmeshvara’nandajii was upset. Ba’ba’ said that He knew about it and would do the needful. Then He asked Keshava’nandajii to call me in the morning at 6am before His departure for Kolkata. Accordingly I was informed. I became very happy and in the morning presented myself in His room. Ba’ba’ asked me to call in the brahmaca’rii waiting outside His room. The brahmacarii was then posted as DS in A’ssam. Ba’ba’ enquired from the Brahmaca’rii regarding his lapses in following conduct rules. Then He turned to me and asked me why you did not supervise this boy properly. I replied Ba’ba’ he is posted in A’ssam and I am the rector of ASMU. He is not my departmental worker. To this Ba’ba’ said that you are a senior member of Madhur Gos’thi and he is its junior member. Hence you are responsible for his supervision. I accepted my omission.

After that Ba’ba’ beat us by His walking stick made from the stems of Candan tree. In the process the stick was broken. I was enjoying but was concerned about the young worker. Then He called Keshava’nandajii and told him that these boys have broken my walking stick. I was enjoying His play and immediately said “Ba’ba’ I will get you two of these walking sticks made from Candan. He asked Keshav’nandajii to take note of my promise. He then told Keshava’nandajii to ask for commitment from me as to the number of days I will take to replace them? I agreed to replace them within ten days. Thereafter He started getting ready for His departure to Kolkata. I felt totally relaxed and returned back to AS. I continued to enjoy His grace and His peculiar manner of giving me bliss.

Back in Kolkata Keshava’nandajii informed me over phone that one of my shiks’a’ bhra’ta’ (initiate) has delivered the two sticks much before ten days. Ba’ba’ was very happy on receiving the sticks as if He received some heavenly gift. He praised me and told Keshavanandajii that although I punished him, still he has kept his word. It was a delight to hear these words.

After two months I suffered from an horrible disease. It caused me excruciating pain all over the body. This made me cry out loudly. While crying I would appeal to Him not to take away my samska’ra on His body. Knowing my pain I thought this would cause unbearable suffering to His body. During those days I was going through a book entitled ‘Living with Himalyan Masters’ written by Swami Ram. It had a reference of a similar incidence where the Guru had asked for a stick from his disciple and then punished him to redeem his samska’ra. I cried after reading this passage. After a few days Ba’ba’ called me and told me in front many other workers, that Parmeshvarananda has been appealing to Me not to take his samskar on my on body. He thinks it will be very painful

for me. Can he on his own suffer the excruciating pain caused by this appalling disease? I am duty bound to take care of him and steal his samska'ra without asking.

I recalled, once He had commented that the created beings undergo the suffering/ enjoyment of their own doings. I have none of mine, hence I serve the samska'ra of the created beings of my own sweet will, to relieve them from the bondage of cycle of life and death.

### Initiation into Microvita sa'dhana (MVS) and curing my fatal illness

It was the morning of $15^{\text{th}}$ July of 1988. Ba'ba' called his PA & GS and told them that He was initiating a new program called Microvita Sa'dhana'. He gave out the names of a few ma'rgiis and WTs. They were to be enlisted and in a systematic manner called on a specific date and time. He would initiate them in Microvita sa'dhana'.

I was then posted in ASMU. GS dada asked me to inform five margiis from Ranchi viz. A'c. Harishankar, A'c. Amulyaratan Sa'rangi, Dr. Ramesh, Sudhanshujii & Rangla'l datta asking them to proceed to Kolkata to get initiated into Microvita Sa'dhana'. The news about Microvita sa'dhana' spread among ma'rgii community throughout the world like wild fire. Pressure was mounting on GS & PA dadas to get their names included. Ba'ba' on His part has chosen the names of some 155 margiis and WTs to the purpose. They alone were supposed to be called. It was a very blissful moment in the lives of WTs and Margiis who were selected by Ba'ba' for MVS.

Ba'ba' propounded the theory of Microvitum for the welfare of humanity. Microvitum according to Him are the causal matrix of creation. Microvitum is the origin and carrier of life throughout this universe. His new ideas challenged all the existing scientific theories about the creation of universe. Ba'ba' has very specifically claimed that it is Microvitum and not the carbon molecules, which is the cause of life.

Of the three types of microvitum, Prakrti (creative force) utilizes negative microvita on the created beings. Positive microvita, on the other hand, are utilized by Parama Purus'a (Supreme Consciousness). Positive microvita can at the best influence upto Ajina' Cakra. To rise beyond to Guru Cakra His grace is necessary.

Initiation into MVS was an unparalleled event. Through this process He infused positive microvita into the mind of the sa'dhaka. I was initiated into MVS on the $26^{\text{th}}$ of September 1988.

It was a very trying time in my personal life. I was suffering from an incurable and acute illness supposedly having its origin on some alien planet. I was under severe influence of -ve microvita. The pain caused by the disease was unbearable. Ba'ba' told me in the presence of central workers that it will not be before 1000 years hence, that the

planet earth will be affected by this disease. Describing the symptoms of the disease he had said that anyone affected by this disease will go mad instantly and lose once memory. He will die in a matter of 15 days suffering from excruciating pain throughout this period.

I was then posted in ASMU when suddenly I suffered from this unknown disease and lost my memory in an instant. I felt unbearable pain both in my body and mind. I was helpless, not knowing what to do. I felt total hopelessness in my life. I thought why of all I am suffering even when I became a sanyasi. I did not do anything wrong knowingly. What sins I must have done in my previous lives? All through day and night I would pray Ba'ba' to grant me death as I was unable to bear the suffering. I was unable to sleep and lost my appetite. I must have passed 15 days suffering the pain, when a worker was directed by Ba'ba' to come to meet me and to ask me to immediately come to Kolkata as I was suffering.

I left for Kolkata the same day. Next day I reached Lake Garden at 6am of 26th September. There Keshava'nandajii was waiting for me and instructed me to meet Him after taking bath. I finishing my morning duty and entered His room did sa's'aunga prana'ma and sat down facing Him. In that strange state of suffering He called me to come closer to Him. Slowerly to my dismay my memory returned. This was the first most pelasing time for me after 15 days of suffering. Then He asked me to be seated in padma'sana and taught me Micro Vitum Sa'dhana'. It was a lengthy but blissful process and took more than 2hours. During the process I visualized the Jyotirupa of the Parama Purus'a. it was an unique and inexplicable experience of my life. Later He asked me to leave. Many others were waiting for Sa'dhana'. When he asked me to leave Him, I could not hold and cried out, thinking when I leave Him I will be again suffering the same way. Knowing my mental state, He told me that He knew that I was suffering from a agonizing pain. He further said that it was the result of my samska'ra of many lives. It is better to undergo suffering before me. If you insist I can relieve you from this unbearable suffering. But he said He wanted me to serve this samska'ra now. You know well that I love you. A mad person can not cry taking my name holding on to my feet? Is it not my grace? I know you must be feeling that you are dying every moment. But be rest assured that I will not let you die. He infused me with so much strength by His love that I cried loudly. He then told me that I will be alright soon. Now you go and don't tell any one. When I was leaving His room, He looked towards me directing His infinite love to permeate my being. Then He told me to win an iota of His grace. After that He asked me, do you know how to get an iota of His grace? Cry for Him, holding His lotus feet and be ready to suffer for Him and beg for His grace. He will be forced to bestow His grace on you. This is the secret. His grace is snatched and not given.

MVS was a unique experience full of bliss. Another strange thing that happened before He actually initiated in MVS was that in ASMU, whenever I used to cry, then He would appear in my dream and teach me MVS. My dream would soon bring in a new ray of hope amidst untold physical and mental suffering.

This reminded me of the experiences of the great devotees like Mira' and Kabir.

Mira sang like this:

> ‘O Hari! I am madly in love with you,
>
> The people of the world fail to appreciate my pain of separation’

Kabir would sing the same idea a bit differently:

> ‘Friends stop laughing and start to love crying for Him
>
> That lover and true friend is unattainable without crying’

As I came out of His room I found Chandranarayanjii, Dada Paritoshananda & Dada Divyalokeshananda waiting for initiation in MVS. As instructed by Him I straightway proceeded to ASMU without seeing anyone else. Ba'ba' had told me that I will be cured soon, but as days passed it got aggravated. Although the pain was somewhat lessened, still it it remained unbearable. Almost everyday I would retire in the forest near the river flowing within ASMU campus and would cry for long hours. I used to have spiritual experiences and would feel His closeness, but the pain would stay. I kept losing weight and got reduced to a mere skeleton. Despite His solace, due to excruciating pain and state of melancholy, I was living a miserable life. Finally I decided to seek medical help. The doctors, however, were not sure of the symptoms and did not help. Thus three months passed without any respite.

Then I spoke to then GS Dada Sarvatmananda about my strange condition caused by my peculiar disease. I also told him that I was not sure how long I will survive. I suggested to him to depute some worker to the ASMU who can take care of this project well. Then GS dada informed Ba'ba' about my serious illness. Ba'ba' said He knew about my condition. A change occurred from that day. Ba'ba' started taking regular report about my condition on daily basis to begin with and later a few times a day. Then realizing the urgency GS dada asked me to reach Kolkata. Accordingly I reached there and Ba'ba' called me near Him and inquired about my condition. He repeatedly asked about my condition the same day in the evening & after General darshan. Thus I spent a few days under His direct surveillance. GS dada often spoke to Him about me. After five days He called me to His room accompanied by dada Vijayananda. When I reached His room His PA, GS Dada, dada Tapeshvarananda DPS central were already there.

In their presence Ba’ba’ described about the symptoms of my disease. They were exactly like I had been suffering. He asked Dada Vijaya’nanda to take notes of what He was talking about. He further said that none on this planet has ever survived the virus of this peculiar disease. Whoever suffers from this virus, that person loses memory and immediately turns mad. That person dies a very painful death in about 15 days. Parmeshvara’nanda has also lost his memory and gone mad. Since he is a dedicated worker I must do something for him. Saying thus He touched my whole body by His Dukhaharan stick.

Meanwhile Dada Vijaya’nanda kept noting down the details as told by Ba’ba’ during the intervening period of His application of the spiritual power curing me. Ba’ba’ said that it might take a 1000 years hence for this disease to visit our planet. Even then you keep a record of this disease. One who suffers from this disease wants to stay alone. He is scared of the other persons. The diseased person should be allowed to remain alone in open natural surroundings. The person affected by the virus becomes very weak. Even then he/she must perform the prescribed asanas four times a day. He then dictated the asanas to be performed in the morning, at noon, in the evening and at night. He also spoke about the natural herbal remedies and the food to be taken by the diseased person. He said that the person should take boiled rice instead of raw rice. If one likes then one can take the supernatant water of the boiled pulses. One will strictly eat only freshly cooked food. I have cured him of his illness, but he will take time to get recouped, since he has been badly affected by the virus which has affected his vital parts.

Then He turned to me and spoke to me directly. He assured me not to be worried at all. I am always with you. You will not go anywhere without asking me. Eventually He blessed me and asked me to remember Him crying out wholeheartedly holding His lotus feet. How-so-ever painful it may be, He instructed me that you will not leave ASMU. Keep discharging your responsibilities. You will receive all help from Me. ASMU development will take place unabated. Having received his blessings, I continued to stay in ASMU. Ma’rgiis and others helped me a lot as required for the development of MU. He continued to utilize my frail body for His mission. During the three years hence ASMU developed beyond everybody’s expectations. The then CTS Dada Sa’dhaneshvara’nand was very co-operative and continuously supported me. He took special care of my physical needs. It took three years for me to recoupe completely, but I always felt His grace. During my confinement in ASMU, I used to cry for Him, remaining lost in His thought. I had a strong feeling that the kind of spiritual experiences I was having as my disease ripened, were perhaps not possible even in many lives of intense sa’dhana without His grace. He remained the constant companion of my thought process during these painful years of suffering, He once commented to

His PA that Parmeshvarananda is the purest person in the whole universe today. In the final analysis, my disease proved to be a blessing in disguise.

I am appointed to the center as ERAWS secretary

It is my personal realization that Ba’ba’ used the mechanism of transfer & posting of WTs and who knows margiis as well (please refer ‘A’nanda Katha’ by A’c. Nagiina’) to help them redeem their samska’ras. In this process he would powder down their arrogance. I had been working for three years in ASMU. Then one fine morning I suddenly started thinking that I should, now, be working in the center in the closer proximity of Ba’ba’. No sooner I started having these thoughts, all of a sudden I was posted as global HPMG secretary at the Kolkata world headquarters and then in another 25 days as Central ERAWS Secretary.

Those days He was busy with the construction of central office at A’nanda Nagar. All the departmental secretaries were responsible for construction of their respective buildings according to His plan at the pre-determined places. He used to take daily morning and evening report from the central secretaries. Those days ERAWS was supposed to raise 104 buildings for many diverse projects and to initiate some of the commercial ventures. For completing these projects He used to put extreme pressure. Everyday He would ask for the progress report of each item, which was very hard. Funds were equally difficult to manage, almost impossible with no known means or resources. Ba’ba’ was not accepting any excuses. Punishment was almost a daily affair. Under these conditions of working under severe pressure I began praying for my transfer. I & the EI-1 both of us were anxious to complete the targets and show satisfactory daily progress. Often He would comment that the ERAWS secretary is a simpleton but is getting spoiled in the company of EI-1, and would punish both of us. This was a daily routine. One day He became very angry and punished us severely and withdrew our avadhutaship. We had to put up with white dress. I was transferred to ASMU, which was a welcome change for me and I dedicated myself with renewed vigour to further build-up ASMU.

During my tenure as Eraws Secretary an interesting incidence took place. Dada Pranava’nanda was posted as DS Akola. He went to Mumbai in connection with some work. When my EI-1 came to know of his movement, he put pressure on me to punish him for this unauthorized act. I told him that I have spoken to Dadajii and you should not be unnecessarily concerned about this matter. But he was not ready to be reconciled. When he insisted I told him that Dada Pranava’nandajii has a direct reporting relationship with him, hence he can as well speak to Pranavanandajii himself. After that he called dada Pranavananda and dealt with him unjustly. Pranava’nandajii heard him patiently and did not retort.

Just after an hour of this incidence, I was relieved from the post of ERAWS Secretary by dada Pranava’nanda. This incidence affected the EI-1 and he came almost running to me anxiously and asked me help him get out of this awkward situation. I smiled and reflected about His liila’. Ba’ba’ is the master redeemer. It was a great lesson for all of us in behavioral psychology. He always taught us that you should earn respect through your actions & not by your post. Human beings become great by their sa’dhana’, service and self-sacrifice.

Bhagalpur DMC

Bhagalpur Bhukti occupies a significant place in the early A’nanda Ma’rga history. It is located just 60Km. away from Jamalpur. It is one of those places which was frequented by Ba’ba’ during His stay in Jamalpur. It carries the hallowed memory of being one of His many Liila’ Bhu’mis. Even now those surviving devotees have fond memories of the sweet and blissful moments spent in the company of Ba’ba’. The number of ma’rgiis who witnessed those blessed days is fast depleting. Those surviving remember the role Ba’ba’ played as the guardian their families. The first A’nanda ma’rga Jagrti was built here in 1957 and it was in Trimuha’n a place 20km. away from Bhagalpur where RU (Renaissance Universal) and RAWA (Renaissance Artist’s & Writer’s association) institution were founded by Him. He delivered His first RU speech entitled ‘Problems of the day’. It was the same day that He guided the performance of a cultural evening under the patronage of RAWA. Curious are His ways. He has been satisfying the desires of his disciples in myriad ways at times in their knowledge and most often without their being aware of that. I was then posted as DS Bhagalpur about 35 years ago. The ma’rgiis there had a strong desire for organizing a DMC. They worked hard for its materialization. But instead only DMS could take place. They could not avail the enjoyment of His physical presence. Then I was also transferred from there, but the seeds of an unfulfilled desire for a DMC in Bhagalpur remained lodged in mind. Later Ma’rgiis wrote letters to the center requesting for conducting DMC there. But it did not materialize because the certain organizational targets could not be met. Later to meet those targets the margiis collectively bought a large piece of land in Bhagalpur and planned to construct A’nanda Colony on that land. About sixty margiis bought plots in that land and collectively built a Jagrti and MG quarter on the same place. In 1989 Ba’ba toured Tatanagar, Ranchi, A’nanda nagar, Deoghar, Bhagalpur and Burdman. Margiis from all these places worked hard to make His programme a grand success. Ba’ba’s visit inspired margiis and organization activities also got a boost.

I attended DMCs in Tatanagar and Ranchi and I was allowed to attend DMC in A’nanda nagar, since all three places were near A’nanda Shila’ my place of posting. I had a strong desire to attend Bhagalpur DMC, but I could not gather enough courage to seek His permission. All of a sudden in the morning of A’nanda nagar DMC, I was instructed

by GS Dada & PA Dada to proceed to Bhagalpur as central representative to organize the DMC properly before Ba’ba’s arrival. This was something I had been internally waiting for and Ba’ba’ rewarded me by quenching my long standing desire.

I reached Bhagalpur next day morning. I found that the preparations were far from complete. The place supposed to be fixed for His stay was unsuitable. Many unfinished works were yet to be completed. Flooring of Ba’ba’s room was unfinished. The toilet was not ready. Airconditioner was not arranged. Although the margiis were inspired and working with dedication, but the time at their disposal was short. Efforts to get the air conditioner from Patna on rent did not materialize. I called a meeting of the margiis and a plan for completion of all pending tasks on emergency basis was prepared and responsibilities distributed. Air conditioner was becoming the bottleneck. All attempts were futile. Finally one margii could get it, albeit with difficulty, from one of his friend’s house. By the dedication of some professionals & margiis and masons & technicians this almost difficult task was completed by midnight of the day before Ba’ba’ was to arrive. His room was decorated thereafter and articles of His personal use were arranged. Four grand gates were constructed & decorated on the way that Ba’ba’ was to take to enter Bhagalpur town. The first gate was built about 40km. away from Bhagalpur town. A caravan of cars, jeeps and motorcycles was ready to lead Him to Ba’ba’s quarter. The DMC pandal was being prepared on war footing. Margiis in huge number were waiting outside His residence and collectively singing Kiirtan anxiously waiting for His arrival. Sevadal volunteers and their supervisors were also busy giving final touches to the arrangement. With their single minded devotion, enthusiasm and personal sacrifice margiis of Bhagalpur created the mystic environment to welcome their Lord.

The Bhagalpur DMC was unique in many respect. Margiis had built MG quarter in a picturesque surrounding enveloped amidst the cool shadow of a huge Mango orchard. More than 10,000 margiis from Aunga, Mithila, Magadha and Bhojpur samajas attended the DMC. DMC pandal was beautiful and the security arrangement were impeccable. Still a few anti-social elements did try to disturb the programme unsuccessfully. A large contingents of journalists representing all the news media and TV channels came to attend the press conference. In short the whole area was humming with margii activities. Many WTs and LFTs were created. Hundreds of initiations were given. Ba’ba was also immensely satisfied with the outturn and progress of the margii activities. Quite often Ba’ba’ would speak to the local margiis in their sweet mother tongue – Aungika’. He blessed the margiis with His divine vibrations. The vibration during DMC was superbly charged with devotion. So was the environment when He was leaving. It was divinely exhilarating. The pangs of separation of devotees from their beloved Lord and viceversa were something to be seen to be believed. The whole environment was reminiscent of the distraught mental state of the Gopis when Lord Shrii Krsna was leaving Vrinda’vana to go to Mathura’. After the departing darshan as Ba’ba’ started to

leave the dais; the devotees could not bear the twinge of separation and burst into loud cry all at once. Some were lost in ecstasy and the others ran after His car.

### Ba'ba' inspired me to write about developing a Master Unit

Venue: Madhu Korak, Tiljala, Kolkata, 1990

We were attending RDS in Tiljala. RDS was encouraging. I was the Rector of ASMU and with me was another worker who was the Rector of Thano. After reporting Ba'ba' called both of us to His room. Both of us sat near His lotus feet after prostrating (sa's't'a'uunga pran'a'ma). He asked us to write an article describing the outline of Master Unit. He gave the guiding principles and based on those ideas we prepared the article. That became the authoritative white paper on master units.

### Prognosis of the future organizational problems

This incidence occurred two months prior to his physical departure. It was my last personal meeting with Him. As I entered His room He spoke—you are going to face serious problems in your organisational life, but you should not be unnecessarily worried. I felt very awkward at this disclosure of future things to come. He repeated the same thing. I could not quite understand its significance and I felt like crying. He further said that the organization may face critical problems. Do not get alarmed. Relentlessly fight against injustice. Everything will be fine. In that situation you will play a leading role. The future of the organization is resplendent like the Sun. When you grow old and weak devote yourself wholeheartedly only for sa'dhana'. Take refuge unto my lotus feet and remember me constantly and you will attain the goal of your life.

I became a sanyasi when I was quite young. I had a strong desire to realize Him and to experience Divine bliss. I was a naive and did not appreciate the enormity of His mission and that I will be required to swim through troubled waters. It was only later that the understanding dawned on me of what He had said in my last meeting with Him. Later I also learnt from Dada Pranava'tmaka'nanda that Ba'ba' had told Dada Mantreshvara'nanda that He has come with the mission of creating a maha'vishva (great Universe), like Krsna who created the maha'bha'rata. Like in Maha'bha'rata all the events & characters in maha'vishva are already chosen. The script of the Maha'vishva has been written down. It has already begun and He added even if I were to change it it may not be possible.

Another senior worker Dada Samanvaya'nanda was equally seized with idea of the differences among workers even during Ba'ba's life time. He wondered why it was happening like this. Then ba'ba' had told him that in the beginning A'nanda Ma'rga was like pure water of Ganges. But with the passage of time it will be polluted. That also will happen according to His planning. It is then that you will be required to learn how to

swim in troubled waters. The righteous people will learn to deal with the changed situation.

The present crisis in the organization is not outside His planning. We have learnt through Prapatti that nothing happens without His sweet will. He is constantly testing us. We have to be Is't'anis'tha and Adarshanis'tha. We have to be ever-vigilant about our conduct, recognizing the universal character of our mission. We have to fight for justice at all costs. We must establish the rule of Dharma (rationality and righteousness) in all our dealings. Organization must restore its order and appeal. It is only then that we can establish our glorious mission. Precepts of Carya'carya must be restored in both our personal and organizational life.

A couple of years ago I had read an article. It talked about a collective decision arrived at by the heads of a few States about the best practices followed in the countries of our world. Singapore topped the list of the surveyed nations.

There were three reasons for determining the best practices among the nations. They were:

1. Every citizen of Singapore is literate and conscious of their responsibility

2. All children in Singapore receive compulsory education of their National Law & Order. Even a son/daughter will object if, he/she finds his/her parents not following the Law of the land.

3. The administration is very strict and its citizens are generally law abiding.

Ma'rgiis (WTs & family persons) have to accept this challenge by demonstrating their sincerity through their good conduct in their personal lives and to adhere to a spirit of co-ordinated co-operation in collective life in all matter of Dharma sa'dhana and service of the created beings. The organization must evolve in the image of caryacarya. Eventually A'nanda Ma'rga mission will become the life of the universal society.

### A million dollar question is raised by Ba'ba'. Whether the stick in His hand is matter or spirit?

**World RDS in Tiljala Jagrti, Kolkata, 1988**

RDS used to be the occasion of not merely organizational review but it was more for inspiring spiritual awakening in the WTs. Ba'ba' would normally do this through His

demonstrations. The intent in cases was one. It was to awaken the latent spirituality and to goad all human longings towards a singular goal of life.

By the reckoning of the RDS system, the SS took turn in a given sequence and presented their sector-wise progress report. There would invariably be one or the other failure in meeting the sectorial targets. This will naturally invite punishment. Usually punishment was given to the individuals most often for their personal shortcomings in regard to their conduct and sometimes to protect them from a trouble as a prognosis of events to come. It used to be a mystic reporting session. When the turn of the Berlin sector came and the SS started reading out his reporting, Ba'ba' asked him pointing to the stick in His hand, whether it was matter or consciousness. The SS thought for a while & then replied that the stick was matter. On hearing his reply Ba'ba' indicated the SS Berlin to come closer. Then touching the SS with His stick and awakening his third eye, he asked the SS to look again at the stick. Whether it was matter or spirit? Now the SS started seeing the movement of electrons within the millions of atoms of which the stick was made of. He saw that those electrons were not only moving in certain path around the nucleus but they were also producing a peculiar sound. Obviously the stick was not inanimate matter but it was vibrant with life. It was a conscious entity.

In Upanishad the Rsi proclaims: The Supreme Consciousness is greater then the greatest and subtler then the subtlest. It can not be conceived by our sensory orgons.

He can be realized only by converting our crude mind stuff (Citta) into Aham and then aham tattva into Mahat and finally merging the Mahat into Consciousness. It is through this process of subjectivisation that all the objectivities merge into their witnessing entity-the A'tman (Consciousness). This is the only way to realize the subtlest of the entity. This is possible by adhering to a proper system of sa'dhana'.

### Ba'ba' takes the pleasure of showing the gifts brought by the sectorial workers

I was then posted in ASMU and was visiting Kolkata for reporting. This incidence took place about a month before He left this world. One fine morning Ba'ba' instructed His PA & GS that all such WTs who did not come in His contact in the last four years or so should be asked to come here to meet Him within eight days. He expressed His keen desire to meet all of them. GS Dada prepared the list of such workers. They were forty in number. He sent out communication to each one of them asking them to immediately report to Kolkata to meet Ba'ba'. This message brought

cheers to all such workers and they were just delighted and greatly inspired. They started to prepare considering this as an emergency to reach Kolkata in time.

That fateful day five of those workers from the list of GS dada were also present. Each one of them had brought a few gifts for Ba'ba'. They prepared a list of their gift items brought from their respective countries and gave them to PA dada.

It must have been 10pm a worker went round Tiljala informing WTs in all rooms to assemble in the meeting hall of Ba'ba's quarter. Accordingly I also reached there in the hall. As I entered Ba'ba' was already seated there. He was taking out each item gifted to Him and showing them to the WTs present in the hall. He showed his wrist watch made of gold saying it has been brought by such and such worker of such and such sector. He was very pleased and asked the workers present was it not beautiful? It was a proud father showing off His happiness before His grown up children. It was an enjoyable moment all of present there.

Soon after He took out one wall clock and said it was brought by my son from Africa. Then He asked His PA to ensure the wall clock hangs in His room. From now He would watch the time from this watch. The WTs who had brought these items were also standing there and were feeling blessed with Ba'ba's acknowledgement of affection. Finally in His characteristic smile He said that to Him the cost of the thing does not matter, what matters is the feelings behind them. When they went to purchase the items at two different places they thought of me in their mind. They thought if Ba'ba' accepts our gifts then that would be the fulfillment of their dream.

### Premonition of Ba'ba's physical departure

This incidence relates to the period of post A'nanda Purnima' DMC. I was anaemic and experienced extreme difficulty in walking, talking and even thinking. I was also finding it difficult to manage A'nanda Shila'. There was a terrible pressure of work. Several ongoing projects viz. WT training center, school, Children home, Abha' Seva' sadan, Agriculture farm & so on demanded constant attention for their maintenance & development. Financial pressure was always there. Because of the whole timer trainees police would keep coming for enquiry.

As a routine I visited Kolkata for having the pleasure of Ba'ba's Darshan and to collect some funds for ASMU. Since I was unusually sick, GS dada and other central WTs wanted me to stay in Kolkata for treatment. However, Nigmananda dada proposed that instead of Kolkata I should go to Varanasi and stay there in the training center for treatment. The potable water in Kolkata was hard to digest. Therefore I took permission of Ba'ba' and GS Dada and proceeded to Varanasi. In

the meantime an alternative supervisor was appointed in ASMU, thus I got relieved of the burden of managing ASMU and instead concentrated on my treatment. Nigmananda dada took good care of me. I was given a separate room with two attendants. He made suitable arrangement for my treatment by Dr. KP Singh of Banaras Hindu University Ayurvedic Hospital. My condition improved as I started taking prescribed medicines, under the care of Nigmananda dada and the affection of Vandananda dada who was posted there as CTS. As I regained my lost health, I started enjoying sa'dhana' more and more. But don't know why whenever I used to do Dhyana lesson, I used to feel the pain of Ba'ba's physical departure. I would often dream Him leaving His body. It was a very painful feeling. It would disturb my mind and often times made me feel deranged. On many a nights I could not sleep. I used to cry in pain of His separation. It was both painful and confusing.

Such disturbing dreams were unthinkable and painful. These despicable thoughts that He is leaving His body were making me mad. I did not have any clue of what to do. This mental condition continued for many days and weeks. Finally I left Varanasi to go to ASMU. There was marked improvement in my health. After being there for a while, I proceeded for Kolkata for His darshan.

As soon as I reached Kolkata, Ba'ba' called me in His room. What transpired there was unbearably disturbing. I will describe the details later. This was my last personal meeting with Him. Ba'ba' was not keeping well. Four margii doctors viz. Dr Ramesh, Dr. PD Singh, Dr. Goswami and Dr. Karuna were attending Him. Dr Sen of Kolkata was regularly visiting for His examination. After staying there for few days I returned back to ASMU. In those days Ba'ba' was not allowing WTs to leave Kolkata. He was happily giving maximum contact to them. But none knew what was going to happen.

In the meantime Ba'ba' was admitted to Woodland Nursing Home for treatment. In August again I went to Kolkata for RDS and returned back after two weeks. During these months I went to Kolkata on three more occasions. Suddenly the unthinkable happened. The news that He has left His body on the 21st of October was given to us. The news of His physical departure spread throughout the nine sectors like wildfire. Though unbelievable, it was a reality. Margiis were dumb and so were all the WTs. The new LFTs and WT trainees were all unconsolable. I stayed in ASMU and sent them to Kolkata for His Darshan. Later on 25th I also reached Kolkata. The scene there was indescribable. All were equally affected. None was there to console anyone. Margiis from the whole world had arrived. Tiljala Ashram in Kolkata had turned into a virtual assembly of shocked and disturbed margiis. The scene there was heart-rending. The agony of margiis filled the environment with despair. None could imagine that Ba'ba' would depart so suddenly. They were unable to contain their feelings. Many of them kept crying inconsolably. Most of the margiis expressed

their pain through the stream of unstoppable tears. None knew how to overcome their shock and pain.

My own mental condition was somewhat different. He had prepared me to face the unthinkable event to some extent. My mind was already prepared. I controlled myself and consoled others who needed it most.

“All are forced to dance to tune of the Lord” – this saying of Sant Tulsidas was the most appropriate for that moment. Ba’ba’s visit to this planet and stay here for a short time was preordained in His cosmic drama. His leaving us so suddenly, though unexpected, was unbearable. He left us and proceeded to His next destination somewhere in the universe most mysteriously indeed. This time, of course, He did not disclose His tour programme.

### Ba’ba’ inspired me to create whole timers

It was in the beginning of 1990. We were attending RDS in Tiljala. There was extreme pressure to create WTs. After the reporting I got to see Him in person. He asked me, whether it was an impossible task that He was forcing us to accomplish. I have not asked you to pluck the stars from the heaven. I did not ask you to reverse the flow of Ganges. I am only asking you to inspire margiis to work dedicatedly for the mission. I was touched by His expression. I could visualize His pain. I made a firm resolve to get cracking with all my might to fulfil His desire. I did get some success. He created a strong mission, but left so suddenly. However, He continues to inspire us. He has made our task easier. Dedicated effort bears instant fruit. Only through complete surrender unto Him we can surmount the Himalyan difficulties facing us and those which may arise on our path of spiritual realization.

## Chapter – 9

### Epilogue

After the physical departure of Ba’ba’, Sadhakas everywhere wanted that a part of the physical remains of Ba’ba’ be allowed to be taken in As’thi kalasha for storing that in each of the Marga Guru Quarters & Jagrtis. Accordingly a decision was taken by the center that Marga Guru’s remains in Ashthi Kalash would be kept in all MG Quarters. PA Dada of Purodha Pramukha and GS dada were responsible for dispatching the As’thi Kalasha to the designated places i.e. the Marga Guru quarters for maintaining its sanctity.

When margiis of Ranchi learnt about this programme, they also lodged an appeal to procure the As’thi Kalasha for MG Quarter in Ranchi. The MG Quarter of Ranchi is associated with many nostalgic memories of Ba’ba’s stay there. This house was built in

a record time of one month at the instance of Ba’ba’. to keep His promise, Ba’ba’ visited Ranchi and stayed in that house, despite a few difficulties. It was here that Ba’ba’ got himself operated for cataract. There are many interesting incidences related to this. Margiis of Ranchi have been blessed to have received Him in Ranchi and to serve Him. Margiis here have had many opportunities of intimate contact with Him, since it was one of Ba’ba’ favourite places. Ba’ba’ lived in Ranchi for a long time since March 1967 till June 1970 and many more times thereafter. Devotees here always attracted Him, and He was impelled to respond to their devotional urges.

In a meeting held in MG Quarter the following was decided:

1. Five margiis from Ranchi viz. Ramlakhanji, Sudhanshujii and others will go to Kolkata and carry the As’thi kalasha by train. It would be received at Ranchi Railway Station by all margiis and brought to MG Quarter.

2. After receiving the as’thi kalasha three hour Akhanda Kiirtan will follow.

3. Thereafter it will be installed in Ba’ba’s room.

On the scheduled day margiis reached the Ranchi Station and brought the As’thi Kalasha in a procession to MG quarter. Bust instead of Akhand Kiirtana for one prahar (3 hours) margiis started to depart after 1hour kiirtana. That day I reached MG quarter a little late because of some urgent work in ASMU. I reached there about the same time when margiis were just planning to depart after one hour kiirtana. A’c. Amulya Ratan Sarangi also reached there about the same time. Both of us understood that some thing was not right. We spoke to the margiis and inspired them that it was improper on our part to dishonor our collective decision and would tantamount to demeaning the As’thi Kalasha. Margiis conceded to our suggestion and reassembled in the hall.

After that the whole environment got electrified and high poignant vibrations charged devotional surge which pervaded all over as 400 margiis sang Prabahta samgiita in chorus and started Kiirtana. Margiis experienced Ba’ba’s physical presence. There was a very strong spiritual vibration that day. Most margiis almost lost consciousness and were enjoying bliss. After the kiirtan margiis sat down for sa’dhana’. The emotional surge was unstoppable. All margiis were crying. I was also crying with my eyes open. I was watching the As’thi Kalash and Lo! I was suddenly beholding Ba’ba’ in His most compassionately form. He was looking at me. I was wondering how did He come here today. He was in His usual attire, looking bewitchingly attractive. I was crying non-stop and while my eyes were beholding His form.

He kept looking at me and tauntingly said could you behave like this in my physical presence? Am I not present here in the form of my As’thi kalasha? I was overwhelmed by His gesture. I noticed most of the margiis then were lost in bliss.

After Guru Pu’ja’ I spoke about my experience and told the margiis assembly what had transpired there. I also told them about the taunting I heard because of dishonouring our own pledge to perform 3 hour Akhanda Kiirtana. Ma’rgiis there were speechless. The then Bhukti Pradha’na asked for pardon. It was a rare scene. We were all blessed and charged as if He was physically present.

Once Ba’ba’ asked Keshava’nandajii, how would you feel when I am gone? Keshava’nandajii had replied that we will experience your presence more intensely.

Saint Tulsidas, perhaps had something similar in his mind when he composed the following couplet.

> “Only that persons realizes Him to whom He bestows His grace. And the knower can not remain separate from Him after knowing Him. Instantly one dentifies with Him”

### Badrinath and Valley of flowers

Hima’laya has attracted me from my early childhood. Although I was equally afraid of the cold there. Once Ba’ba’ asked me to proceed for inspection of our school in Joshimath. After that visit I was freed of the fear of Hamalayan cold. After Joshimath, I had intended to visit Badrinath. But the gates of Badrinath would usually remain closed until then, due to cold wheather. But my desire remained. After the physical departure of Ba’ba’, I planned to revisit Himalayas with Cidghana’nandajii and Kalya’neshvara’nandajii. Accordingly we took the permission of the then GS Sarva’tma’nandajii and went ahead as planned.

From A’nanda Nagar we first arrived at Haridvar and then proceeded to Thano Master unit by bus. In that serene and the most enticing natural environment we stayed back and enjoyed doing sa’dhana’. It was a very enjoyable time as I remained blissfully unaware and lost in His thought. From there we went on to Rishikesh to Joshimath and finally after a night’s rest we reached Badrinath. It was the month of August. The journey to badrinath was tedious. We passed through deep valleys. It was frightening to cross those valleys. But once there, it was a heavenly site. Badrinath is located on a plain mountain top. Badrinath is twelve thousand feet high above the sea level. The natural beauty is superb. Badrinath is surrounded by ice capped mountains. These mountain tops shone in white effulgence at Sun rise. In that attractive natural environment the mind was soaring high. The grand Badrinath temple was built on the bank of Alaknanda river. There was ahot spring below the mountain. Our next destination was Man’a’ Gaon. Maharsi Vyas had composed Brahmasu’tra in this picturesque mountain. Walking along the Alaknanda we found a very appealing place for sa’dhana’. We had the most blissful sa’dhna’ there. There is no doubt Himalayas is Tapobhu’mi. Bhu’tasuddhi and

a’sanasuddhi happened effortlessly. The mind would automatically lose its bondages and flow in bliss. Ma’n’a’ gaon is located on the border of India and China hence there was restriction to visit the cave of Maharsi Vyas. However we thoroughly enjoyed the scenery and natural beauty of the most alluring of places.

On our way back from Badrinath, we took to Pandukeshar—the sa’dhana’ piitha of Pandu and from there via Govindagha’ta we went to Hemkund—the place where Guru Govinda Singh had performed sa’dhana’. To watch the most beatific of the site of valley of flowers we took the ride of the Tattu—the mountain horse. It was a 16 kilometer long straight rise up in the mountain with very deep valleys on the side. Tattu is well trained for the task. But for us it was the most difficult, at times frightening part of journey. Riding on the tattu we had our hips bruised. It was a very brave journey. But once there the sight of the valley was exquisite. It was filled with heavenly flowers of umpteen varieties. The whole scene was other worldly. After five six hours of travel we reached village Ghagharia. It has a beautiful Gurudvara’. We freshened up there and took rest after taking our lunch. Then we went with our guide to watch the valley of flowers. This was a tough ride of five kilometer further up in the mountain. Once over there we lost sense of difficulties faced. Lo! We were beholding the most adorable sight of heavenly beauty, of flowers so exquisite and enticing in all imaginable colours spread over miles and miles of natural mountaneous terrain. It was the most beautiful thing we had ever looked at. It was bewitching and superbly exquisite. It transcended all human limitations. I was something most perfect, out of my mind’s imagination. In this serene and divine environment all shuddhis happen almost spontaneously. And mind quickly soars high experiencing bliss.

After this our journey back to Rishikesh started. From Ghagharria we returned back riding on the Tattu to Gurudva’ra’ before the sunset. From there next day morning again on Tattu we reached Govindghata and then by bus to Rishikesh. There we stayed in Dharmashala and enjoyed sa’dhana on the bank of Ganges and Lakshaman Jhu’la’. All of us enjoyed the satsaunga. I realized that Dada Cidghana’nanda was a simple soul, almost child-like, with no nonsense attitude, pure in mind devoid of any air of arrogance and a great sa’dhaka.

On our way back in Haridvar we desired to stay with some a’shram owned by the Mathadhiisha of a sect. Somehow those persons declined and did not allow us to stay in their ashram. Perhaps they were aware of the non-dogmatic stance taken by A’nanda ma’rgii sanya’sins.

Thus our journey came to end. Then we returned back to A’nanda nagar.

My visit to Poland – need of Prout

Ba’ba’ has stated very clearly that both the economic systems viz. capitalism and Communism were defective ideologies. Whereas capitalism makes person a beggar, communism makes a beggar a brute. During my tour of the then east Europe I had some realization of the veracity of His remark.

In the year 1999, as a central worker I visited Europe to inspect the organizational activities and the service projects there in Berlin Sector. In the course of my inspection, I wanted to visit our Master Unit in Poland. We started from Berlin to go to Poland in our car. The then SS dada Artprema’nanda and Dada Arghya’nanda also accompanied me. We stopped at a petrol station for filling gas and use the Rest Room. The two Dadas accompanying me went to rest room, I waited in the car taking care of our baggage. It was noon time. After a while three persons who were selling wollen clothings came near the car and forcibly wanted me to buy something. On my refusal they started snatching the baggage from the car. I came out and shouted at them. But they were used to stealing and did not relent. I sought help from the passers by and others watching the misdemeanor of these persons. Instead of helping me they started mocking me and did not pay heed to the trouble I was facing. I was confused and shouted for help. All of a sudden the two workers came in and pushed these thieves aside. Seeing our strength those guys went away.
