This is a living example of the destruction that negative and materialistic communist ideology does to the human sensibilities. Communism has been responsible for thriving on materialistic selfish values. It deprives the human mind to develop refined behaviour, human values and morality.

Later that day we reached our master Unit. The people in the master unit were shocked to hear our story. In fact our master unit in Poland is popular because of its service activities and eco-friendly project. It is supported by the governments of Poland and other European nations. A’c. Vandita’nanda Avadhu’ta is its recor master. He is very popular among the local population.

### Incidence of Rumania (year 1999)

My next inspection trip was to inspect our service projects in Romania. I wanted to visit our street children homes for boys and for girls. Both these children homes were popular among the people. Our service projects have received acclaim from among locals. In this country again the exploitation has reached its nadir point. At one time this country was the archetype of communist governance. The infamous dictator Chechesco was ruling the roast here. He was a cruel exploiter. He must have got millions of his voiceless citizens brutally killed. Not to mention he had to face the same cruel exit from this world. He and his wife were killed in daylight on the main square of Bucharest, north of Danube River.

This is another poor country which was once under communist dictatorship for long time. The common people were subjected to extreme exploitation. The cruel dictator had built a huge majestic palatial house for himself. The common people lived a depraved life of poverty. In the countryside there are countless barren farms. Nobody wants to work there. They have been desolate since ages now. None has the inspiration to work. Commune system has failed them. It has proved to be impractical and unpsychological.

Just to mention a small incidence........ I went with the RS Bucharest for shopping in the capital town. In one place I found there was a huge heap of watermelon. RS informed me that the Romanian watermelons are famous for their taste and sweetness all over Europe. He expressed his desire to serve me watermelon. I readily agreed.

He went to one of the shops and stood there in queue to purchase watermelon. I also went with him and stood by his side. It was one of the famous shops. The shop owner had a pile of watermelon and he was busy selling it out. As I was watching to my dismay a small and old lady shopkeeper next to this shop was stealing watermelon from the heap of watermelon belonging to the big shop in broad daylight in full view of public. She was filling her bags without any remorse. Surprisingly she was all smile while continuing to steal and so were those watching her. When I drew the attention the RS, he told me this was very normal here. Communism has debased human mind. People have lost sense of morality.

Attached to our children home was a plot of five acres of land. It was a lush green plot full of green vegetables, fruits & flowers. In the evening I saw the garden and enjoyed the sight of it. Next day morning there was nothing left. To my surprise I was told that the thieves have stolen everything. Nothing is safe here. This is the result of communist rule.

### My own experience at the Moscova Airport

After my inspection tour I was returning back home. My plane was waiting for six hours in transit at the Moscova airport. After a while a Sikh gentleman came near me and sat down by my side after exchanging namaskar. We started talking. He had come to Russia to meet his younger brother who was working as an engineer there. He himself was a leading advocate from Patiala. As he was talking, he was overcome by emotions. He said the people here have lost all sense of decency and good values. They are no better than animals. There is no rule of law. On my query he ridiculed the people of the place and narrated an incidence which had occurred a few days ago.

He was staying here since last two weeks. He went around to many places. On one such tour his bag and baggage was stolen. He became furious, overcome by emotions &

started speaking at the top of his voice ridiculing the Russians. I tried in vain to calm down but he was inconsolable. He kept repeating that all thieves here. Only thugs rule here. There is no law and order in this country. Nobody will come for help here. I will never come back here. Communism has turned them into demons.

## Chapter 10

### Devotional experiences

#### Why I don’t hear your devotional call?

This incidence occurred during one of the RDS in Kolkata. A senior worker was reporting his outturn. Ba’ba’ was dissatisfied with the progress of his work. Then he said that this boy is very regular in sa’dhana’. He spends lots of time in sa’dhana’. But why his devotional call does not reach me. The WTs present there were quite surprised.

After him the next worker came forward for his reporting. Ba’ba’ was unhappy with his work also. But Ba’ba’ said, he is able to get his devotional call across to me. The WT was relatively young and new. Ba’ba’ further added: In sa’dhana’ he cries out with all his mind and heart. He yearns to be one with me. His intense desire to unite with me gives me pleasure.

#### Three a’caryas propagate AM ideals and way of life in Gaya

Before A’nanda ma’rga was institutionalized Ba’ba’ used to initiate sa’dhakas. Later as Ba’ba’ started a system of training A’ca’ryas. These a’ca’ryas started initiating interested persons on the path of sa’dhana’. These a’ca’ryas (men & women) were drawn from among the family persons. In just 5 years from 1955 to 1960 He created 125 a’ca’ryas. These a’caryas went around Bihar, UP and Bengal for praca’ra.

One fine morning he called a group of three a’ca’ryas to go to Gaya for praca’ra. Since at that time there was no published literature of the ma’rga, Ba’ba’ used to give them notes on different subjects of common interest. He would also tell them the kind of questions people in general are likely to ask them. These a’caryas would note down the main points in a diary. The idea was to ensure the information was handy. The select group of three a’caryas were supposed to address a meeting to the theosophical society of Gaya. These a’ca’ryas were afraid of public censure. The audience consisted of intellectuals of the town. They were not so confident. To help build up their self confidence Ba’ba’ specified the subjects of their speech and gave them enough hints to ensure they are able to present the ideals of the marga convincingly.

One of them was a little more anxious. He inquired if he would be able to address the audience. Ba’ba’ replied that they will be able do it successfully. They were advised to do so after Guru Dhya’na.

As foretold their speech was applauded by public and many interested persons learned sa’dhana’. Thus these a’ca’ryas went around in many towns of Gaya Bhukti and delivered public address successfully. Many people were initiated. After 15 days of praca’ra these a’ca’ryas returned back to Jamalpur to report their accomplishment. In Jamalpur the then GS Pranaya dada told them that Ba’ba’ was having high fever and that they should go and report to Him in His house. Ba’ba’ received them in His house and expressed his pleasure after getting their report. They also got inspired.

After that Ba’ba’ went to the toilet and took bath and again took rest on the bed. The a’ca’ryas found ba’ba’s taking bath rather unusual considering He had high fever. He may catch cold and may suffer from pneumonia.

Ba’ba’ dispelling their doubts told them that nothing would happen to Him, now that they have come back after successful praca’ra in Gaya. It so happened that all three of them started having fever while they were in Gaya. If they had suffered from high fever then their praca’ra programme would have been disturbed. Hence Ba’ba’ took their samskar on himself and suffered for three days in their place.

Only clever devotees understand the secret of their success. He alone has been doing everything throughout this planet and giving the credit to His devotees.

Upanishada has something like this to offer.

Sah eko jalavana iisat iishanibhih

Sarvan lokan iishat iishanibhih

Sah eva eko udabhave sambhave ca

Etad vidurah amrta’ste bhavanti

Unique DMC in Sukeshvara Prasad’s house in Patna

This incidence occurred in the early days of A’nanda ma’rga. Ba’ba’ had created a few grahii a’ca’ryas. They initiated pracar in many places to which they belonged. Many people were attracted to A’nanda ma’rga’s non-dogmatic ideals. In that process an Inspector of Police Shrii Sukeshvara Prasad got initiated. He was attracted to His magnetic personality and became a regular visitor to Jamalpur. As time passed he developed a devotional relation with Him. On one such visit to Jamalpur, he cajoled Ba’ba’ to visit Patna and conduct DMC there. Ba’ba’ smiled at his devotional gesture.

During his next visit he got to go with Ba’ba’ on field walk. As they were walking close to the Jamalpur field, moved by divine attraction and he stood in front of Him and requested for holding DMC in Patna. Ba’ba’ replied that the programme of DMC is decided by GS dada. After dropping Ba’ba’ to His residence he came to the Ja’grti and met the then GS da’da’. He requested to him to fix a date for Patna DMC as he would take full responsibility of the function. He will organize stay of Ba’ba’ and arrange lodging & boarding for all. GS dada told him that he will come to Patna and will fix a date and place for the DMC function. He should organize a margii meeting on that day when he comes there.

GS dada on his arrival to Patna met with the ma’rgiis and finalized the date and venue of DMC. Sukeshvara Prasad proposed that his house would be available for stay of Ba’ba’ and also for DMC. However ma’rgiis did not agree to his idea. They said since he has built his property with black money earned through bribery, Ba’ba’ can not stay in such place. At this Sukeshvarjii was disturbed. He, however, was happy that in any case now Ba’ba’ will be coming to Patna for DMC. It was true that he had earned money through immoral means. But after his initiation he had stopped taking any bribe.

The programme was finalized and as scheduled Ba’ba’ came to Patna. He came to the venue where DMC was to take place. Margiis were seated in the pandal. Sukeshvarjii was also sitting there in the crowd. He was overwhelmed with emotions watching Ba’ba’ in a sweet mood. He was also making mental request to him to visit his residence. He had made all the arrangement for Him and the margiis.

As devotional bhajan was being sung, suddenly margiis found that Ba’ba’ was visibly annoyed. He pointed to the GS & said that it was a unsuitable place for DMC. Then turning towards Sukeshvar, He asked Sukeshvar to make arrangement for His stay and DMC at Sukeshvar’s residence. Sukeshvar jumped out of sheer joy and instantly agreed and said that His will be done. Immediately Ba’ba’ proceeded to his house.

Since he had made all the preparations beforehand, there was no difficulty. DMC took place in a very devotionally charged environment. All those who attended enjoyed the programme and felt the surging waves of devotion. Ba’ba’ is the lord of devotees.

Do you need to lift the curtain to see Him?

Saint Miira Bai has said:

> O Krshna! I have built a house in my eyes,
>
> I have spread a bed of my eyeball,
>
> a curtain of my eyelids,
>
> and I have attracted my lover to visit me.

The Lord of our lives is seated in the lotus of our heart. When the mind is cleared of dirt and turns soft by His love then He appears.

Following touching incidence was once told by Dada Ramananda. It was a holiday & Ma'rgiis had assembled in Madhu ma'lainca, Kolkata. The hall where they had assembled was reverberating with the sweet melody of Prabha samgiita and Kiirtan. Ma'rgiis were lost in the bha'va. Dada Ramananda informed Ba'ba' that ma'rgiis were waiting for Him in the hall downstairs. Ba'ba' was annoyed for some reason with the ma'rgiis of Kolkata due to the negligence of their organizational responsibility. He responded that he would not give then His darshana. Despite his gentle persistence Ba'ba' did not concede to go for darshana. He asked Ramananda dada to close the door while He rested on the bed.

All ma'rgiis were disappointed and returned back home. But a devoted margii sister was adamant to have His darshana. She was surging with strong waves of devotion for her Lord. She persisted with her request to dada Ramananda to oblige her somehow. She stationed herself in the drawing space on 1st floor of His house. She refused to move out unless she gets His darshana.

Dada realizing the intensity of her urge, told her that although He was annoyed, I will get into His room on some pretext and then lift the curtain for a while for you to have His darshana.

She felt blessed with the prospect of having to watch Him from outside. Dada entered His room and did as planned the ma’rgii sister had His darshan. She was immensely overwhelmed & felt contented.

On Da’da’s entry in His room Ba’ba’ inquired. Dada responded saying that he was Ramananda. I have some work in the toilet. Ba’ba turned to the otherside and faked as if sleeping.

Next day before field walk, He asked his PA that He would visit Riina’ Didi’s (same ma’rgii sister) home today, but donot let her know of my plan. Thus after His field walk he proceeded to her house. She was living in a flat on the third floor. There was no lift. Ba’ba’ did not care and quickly reached to her door stepping up the three floors. He asked His PA dada to knock the door as He himself stood behind. Riina didi could not believe her eyes. She was unprepared for such an unannounced visit of the lord. With affectionate smile Ba’ba’ entered her drawing room which was a bit messy as she did not expect any visitor to her house. Ba’ba’ sat on the couch in the drawing hall. He did not even allow her to spread a new bed sheet saying that He was visiting His daughter’s place hence there was no need of any formality. The family was dumb but silently cried out at this most unusual visit of their Lord. Ba’ba’ asked Riina’ didi, do you really need to lift the curtain to watch me?

Saint Kabir has said

A devotee keeps repeating His name and loses consciousness unto Him,

His name indeed is so powerful and sweet that one keeps seeing Him in everything

The all knowing too inquires into the welfare of the Ma’rgiis?

A’c. Akhaorii Himanchal Prasad happened to visit Ranchi MG quarter. After the collective DC we requested him to tell us some incidence with Ba’ba’. He told us many illuminating incidences. One that touched me most is being reproduced here in my own words.

Akhaorijii was then a very senior police officer in Bihar. He came in Ba’ba’s contact and a great devotee in him was born. He would quite often visit Jamalpur as and when he would find time from his busy official work. Seeing him in Jamalpur Jagrti, Ba’ba’ would

inquire into his welfare. He once thought that why should Ba’ba’ ask about our welfare even though He knows everything about us. What is behind this drama?

He had already become a family A’ca’rya by then. After a few days he happened to visit Jamalpur with his family and other sa’dhakas. While in Jamalpur he enjoyed the darshana & pravacana of Ba’ba’. After the darshana Ba’ba’ started to leave, Akhaorijii went with his family and did namaskar to Him. Ba’ba’ not only did not respond but He turned His face to the other side. He, however, duly acknowledged the namaskar of Akhaoriijii’s family members and other sa’dhakas & inquired about their welfare. Akhaorijii did make repeated attempts to attract Ba’ba’s attention but failed do so. To him Ba’ba’s conduct appeared somewhat unusual. He felt hurt at Ba’ba’s intentional neglect. He was losing self control but restrained himself and cried out only mentally. After Ba’ba’ left the A’shram without even looking at Akhaorijii, he cried out loudly in pain. By then Ba’ba’ had moved away from the A’shram gate. But all of a sudden he turned back & came close to Akhaorijii. Then very affectionately Ba’ba’ slapped Akhaorijii’s cheeks and inquired about him.

Later Ba’ba’ addressed him & said that there is no doubt that your Ba’ba’ is all-knowing, but so long as he is in physical form, it is quite natural for people coming in His contact to expect his verbal expression of concern for their welfare. That is their greatest asset which they cherish all their life. This duty He must perform for the sake of His devotees. Akhaorijii had an instant realization of his grace and and the swelling emotions brought tears of bliss.

### Serving all with the ideation of Na’ra’yana

This incidence occurred in 1967. Whole of Bihar, eastern UP and south-western MP (chattisgarh) was in state of severe draught. A’nanda Ma’rga WTs and family ma’rgiis were busy in relief activities. A’nanda Ma’rga was operating free food camps, free cloth distribution and medical camps. A sanyasin who was sincerely involved in relief activities, thought that when he goes back to Ranchi Ba’ba’ will call him near and appreciate the good work done by him.

After a few months he returned to Ranchi for the darshana of Ba’ba’. When he reached the a’sharam, Ba’ba’ was just leaving for the field walk. He went near Him and did pranama. But instead of acknowledging him Ba’ba’ turned His face. After the field walk Ba’ba’ returned to the Jagrti, but did not call this sanyasin. When he was not called he started thinking, if he has committed a mistake. As he was trying to recollect, he found himself overcome with emotions to meet Him. In a state of deep anguish he began

crying loudly. After a while Ba’ba’ called him in his room. Looking in eyes He said that you did lot of service of the draught stricken people but failed to remember that Ba’ba’ was always watching you. That is why I am unhappy with you. Service without the ideation of Na’ra’yana is a waste of time.

Appendix - 1

### 01. Late Dada Para’shivananda Avadhu'ta

Beginning with my first meeting with Dada Para’shiva’nandajii, he left a indelible mark of His simple but grave personality. Apart from being a great sa'dhaka, he was a silent worker, soft spoken and humble. He was bereft of any arrogance. His child-like simplicity was just adorable. I had heard about his past life as demonstrated by Ba'ba' in Ranchi using the medium of A'c. Dashratha. Ba'ba' touched the Trikuti (between the eyebrows lies the seat of A’jina’ cakra & Pituitary Gland) of Dasharath dada and asked him to look into Para’shiva’nanda's past life. Dasharath started narrating what he had started seeing internally. He saw a coconut tree in place of Dadajii. Then Ba'ba' took upon Himself to describe Dada's past life. There was a A'shrama of a Guru in Sirohi a town in Ra'jastha'n. This person in his past life this person was a disciple of that Guru. Once, before proceeding on a pilgrimage, his Gurudeva instructed him not accept any form of gift from those visiting the A'shrama during the Guru's absence. After the Guru had left, the King of Sirohi with his retinue came for hunting in the forest adjoining the A'shrama. As was king's regular wont, he also carried sufficient eatables – grains & fruits and other items with lots of money intended for donation to the A'shrama. This boy did not accept any gift from the king as instructed by the Guru. But the king pleaded with all humility and the boy conceded to accept a coconut. Later as scheduled the Guru returned after his long journey. The Guru was displeased when he learned about the gift his disciple had accepted. When his body in that life died this man was reborn as a coconut plant.

I had heard this incidence and could not quite make out why should it happen that way. Anyway this is destiny and we have no control over the laws that govern us in the universe. I developed closeness with him during my posting as the DS, Bangalore. He was then posted in Kolar, a district in the state of Karnataka. Immediately after my posting he happened to visit my school. He started narrating the strange experience he

was having in Kolar. He said that Ba'ba' had posted him to open a kindergarten school in Kolar. I tried and finally succeeded, but I don't know why, a thief steals my belongings quite regularly. This has happened on a number of occasions. I have been badly disappointed and wanted to kill myself and once I threw myself into the sea. But Lo! He appeared from nowhere and saved me there. He will neither let me die nor He would allow me to successfully accomplish the assigned task. I wondered at Ba'ba's curious ways of handling His own disciples. But in the process we came closer and slowly our acquaintance turned into friendship though he was elder to me. I noticed that he was indeed a great sa'dhaka. I loved to discuss with him matters related to our spiritual lives. We continued to meet each other quite regularly.

Once he visited my school in Bangalore and asked me to give him company to dine Dosa in a nearby hotel. He liked dosa and enjoyed the delicacy. After he was done with it, he told me that he came to me out of my greed for Dosa. It is not proper for sa'dhaka to be the slave of once desires. Then and there he took the oath not to partake salt in any form from that onwards. I tried to persued him to give up, but he was unrelenting. He honoured his pledge and never ever eat salt thereafter.

Another interesting incidence took place, when I had fallen ill. He came to Bangalore and took me with him to Kolar for treatment. He served me and when I recovered, he prepared Kshiira. Both of us took kshiira to our heart's content. Once again he took the pledge that from now on he would not take any sweet. This was his firm resove. He devoted himself totally for sa'dhana'. He would go to cremation grounds almost daily for ka'pa'lika sa'dhana'.

After the emergency he was posted in Gorakhpur. I was then serving as RS Allahabad. He opened the school there as commanded by Ba'ba'. He also started the children home there. He was very methodical and maintained his institutions very well.

This great sadhu among the mortals was born to a aristocratic family of Va'ra'nasi in the year 1939. After his MA in sociology, he joined Usha Martin company and there he rose to become its personal manager. In 1964, he was initiated by A'c. Kedarjii and within a month's time pleaded for his blessings to become a sanyasii. A'c. Kedar wondered, how could a person like him posted on an executive position resolve to be a monk after having been initiated just a month ago.

Parashiva'nandajii was a perfect a'ca'rya. He would comment only when required. He led a simple and pure life without any ostentation. Among sanyasii's he was known as an ardent ka'pa'lika sa'dhaka. He left his body on 29^th June 1984.

On his death Ba'ba' said that, He had honoured His word that he gave to Parashiva'nandajii. He attained the Supreme Goal of his life. His life will remain a beacon of light to all devotees.

## Appendix – 2

### 02. A'carya Narendra Deva

My earnest desire for spiritual sa'dhana' got consummated on meeting my A'carya. It was in the high school that I met him and in the year 1961 took initiation from him. He was an inspiring person with a towering personality devoted to a life of sa'dhana'. He always remained my inspiration on the path of sa'dhana'. Whatever I could attain in my spiritual life was the result of his inspiration that he gave me in those early years of my spiritual life. I offer my heartfelt homage & the petals of my love at the feet of my revered a'ca'ryajii.

A'caryajii was born in Jabalpur on Ganesh Caturthi on the 14^th September 1923. Born to a well-to-do devout family, a'ca'ryajii's acquired sterling qualities of service and sacrifice. He was an accomplished sportsman, freedom fighter, writer, journalist, teacher, social activist and a devotee par excellence. He was an embodiment of sa'dhana', seva and sacrifice. Even before he got initiated he was a revolutionary and a devotee of Durga.

It was in 1959, he was initiated by A'carya Haridutta Pant. Soon he came in the contact of Ba'ba' and his life received the desired impetus for a revolutionary change for better. He became a'ca'rya in 1960. Since then he must have initiated over 10,000 persons on the path of sa'dhana' in just 20years. Out of which more than 50 of his initiates devoted themselves to the service of suffering humanity and became sanyasii of A'nanda Ma'rga. Thus he was became one of the most respected of the a'ca'ryas. He passed the test of the Guru. In 1967 inspired by Ba'ba' he left his only source of meager earning for supporting his family and devoted himself for the establishment of Chattisgarhi Sama'ja. In 1968, he lead the satya'grah in A'nanda Nagar until it was freed from the unholy occupation by the communists. With virtually no physical resources, but the strength of indomitable courage inspiration and a war like strategy and unflinching devotion at the lotus feet of his Guru, he lead the victorious march of A'nanda ma'rga defeating the then communist government. In the great famine of Chattisgarh in 1967 he lead the margiis to open free kitchens and maintained them for months together in four blocks of Sarguja District. Simultaneously he organized distribution of wheat and other essential items in that area. I witnessed him successfully complete the Himalayan task as the then relief organiser of the mission.

He was the inspiration and the leader who led the Chattisgarhi sama'ja movement and had remained devoted to its mission since then. He edited the Chattisgarhi sevaka published in Chattisgarhi language from 1964 until 1984. This mouthpiece of Chattisgarhi Sama'ja has spearheaded a movement for creating a exploitation free society. Incidentally it must be remembered that chattisgarhi sama'ja is one among the most downtrodden societies of the world. He led the movement starting from 16^th May 1965. He sent out a clarion call to one & all from the playground of RD Tiwari School. The revolutionary fervour has since then continued to awaken the spirit of Chattisgarhi society. His long struggle of 36 years culminated his long march to the Indian Parliament on 24^th July 2000. Chattisgarh - a new state in the federal structure of Indian constitution was created. Needless to mention it was his hard work which has finally borne fruit. In all the district headquarters of Chattisgarh in their 90 blocks, he led hundreds of agitations, mass fasts and protests successfully, highlighting local problems.

On 19^th December 2009, this giant among men left this world to unite with His Supreme goal. Such rare persons are difficult to be replaced. He was a pillar of A'nanda Ma'rga. He was at once a great sa'dhaka, an astute ideologue, an unparalleled devotee and a great man all at the same time. Ba'ba' also used to love him. In 1977 after emergency was lifted, Ba'ba' chose him to be Ma'rga Guru Representative. He addressed the A'nanda Pu'rnima' DMS in Varanasi the same year as MGR.

### 03. Late Dada Shraddha'nanda – Purodha' Pramukha

Dada Shraddha'nanda was one among the many senior disciples. He was initiated by Him in the year 1954. Before dedicating his life in the year 1964, Dadajii led a family life and worked for Customs department of Government of India. He took diiksha' of sanya'sa in 1966 and became avadhu'ta.

Dadajii was a devoted worker and served His mission wholeheartedly. He was connected to many of marga's activities. Until October 1990 he served as the editor of A'nanda Yuga.

During Ba'ba's time he was appointed the secretary of Purodha Board. After the physical departure of Ba'ba' he was chosen to lead the organisation as its Purodha Pramukha. He was a simple and a gentle person. He had a subtle sense of humour. He always conducted himself with a smiling face and was liked by the margiis the world over.

He was a great devotee of Ba'ba' and did not accede to the pressure of CBI during emergency to desert A'nanda Ma'rga.

He had a passion for literature. He was both a poet and a writer. There are a number of books to his credit Viz. Maha'vishva, Solah Vidhi, Shraddha Suman, Man'ima'la', Bhojapuri Vya'karana.

He left his mortal frame on 15th October 2008 in Ranchi.

### 04. A'ca'rya Chandranath – Gaddopur Vaisha'lii

As a young man interested in spiritual enlightenment, he was once told by an elevated saint of his village that he would be initiated at the right time. When the opportune moment in his life arrived he was chosen by A'nandamu'rtijii and was initiated in the year 1953. A'ca'rya Chandrana'tha was a strict disciplinarian. He led a life of dedication to his mission as a stalwart. His strong sense of determination was discernible in all small or big incidences of his personal and organizational life.

He always attracted the margiis from the world over for inspiration and guidance. He followed the instructions of the Guru like His command and discharged his responsibilities with best of his ability and capacity. He was an ideal disciple, ideal a'ca'rya and an ideal family man.

Once Ba'ba' took a pledge from him to perform sa'dhana' for one and a half hour daily both in the morning and evening. He literally followed it throughout his life. He continued as the member of a'nanda ma'rga central committee all his life and gave valuable suggestions and guidance. I first came to know him in the year 1966 and more intimately in the year 1989 when I was posted in ASMU.

He left his mortal frame in the morning hours of 24th March 2007 in Patna.

### 05. A'ca'rya Nagina'jii – Muzaffarpur

A'c. Chandrana'th Dada once opined that A'c. Nagina was perhaps the greatest among the devotees of modern times. A'c. Naginajii from the very beginning was a man of firm resolve and a strict adherent of morality. After initiation he remained steadfast as a sa'dhaka and devoted to Guru's commands. He took the liberty to unhesitatingly pose questions to Ba'ba'. Ba'ba' on his part always responded to his questions lovingly.

A'nanda Katha' written by him is an immortal creation of his devotion. This book will inspire devotees on the path of sa'dhana' for all time to come.

A'c. Nagiinajii was born on 15th Feb. 1918 on the day of Vasant Paincamii. He received his initiation on 3rd November 1953 at the residence of Ba'ba'. He left his mortal frame on A'nanda Purnima' on the 11th May 1998.

A'c. Naginajii had all the desirable qualities of a brave sa'dhaka. These qualities showed up on the demand of the moment. He was an ideal person. He was obedient and disciplined. He always remained devoted to the Guru.

A central government officer, he retired as the additional collector customs. He is remembered in his department for his good conduct and helping nature. He was a likeable person. Ba'ba' once called him a large hearted man and a true lover of justice.

I came in his contact in 1966, when I was working as a praca'raka in Bihar.

### 06. A'carya' Rampari Devi – Gaddopur

A'carya' Rampari Devi was born in village Bela Mohanpur of the then Gaya district in September 1918. She got married to A'carya Chandranath and left for his permanent home on 6th January 2002.

She was initiated by Ba'ba' in 1954 in Jamalpur. She was perhaps the first lady disciple of Ba'ba' and also the first a'ca'rya' of A'nanda ma'rga. When she became a'ca'rya', she asked Ba'ba' that how will she initiate, since she was not literate. Ba'ba' replied that it was no disqualification and that many a learned women will come for initiation to her.

When she first met Ba'ba' with Chandranathajii, she also did sa's'taunga pran'a'ma to Ba'ba' like her husband. It was then that Ba'ba' taught her the process of Guru Pran'a'ma. She was the first woman to have learned the process.

She led a life of sa'dhana' & seva'. Ba'ba' started to pay His visit to their Nathnagar residence from Feb. 1955 onwards. In those days she used to serve breakfast and meals to Ba'ba'.

She was a lady of simple and straight forward nature. She started having her spiritual realisation right from the time of her initiation. She left an imitable stamp of her simplicity, devotion to Guru and the mission on the ma'rgiis and others who came in her contact. Her children have grown into good margiis. I came in her contact from 1989 onwards.

### 07. A'ca'rya Dashrath – Jamalpur

A'ca'rya Dashrath was a man of few words. He is one such person who is revered throughout A'nanda Ma'rga society. He can be rightly compared to Sainjaya of Mahabharata. Ba'ba' made him His medium for demonstrating the occult phenomenon.

Dashrath Da'da' was initiated by A'carya Shishir Datta in 1955. He was initiated into Vishesha Yoga by Ba'ba' Himself. Dashrath Da'da' was my Ta'ttvika examiner in 1965. I maintained contact with him since then. He continued to be the examiner of Ta'ttvikas and A'ca'ryas until he lived. He actively participated as a member of Central Committee. He left this world in Ba'ba' quarter at Va'ra'nasi on 9^th January 2002.

Divyacakshu is the collection of his spiritual experiences. It is an immortal creation.

In the book he describes his mystic experiences with Ba'ba'. It gives glimpses of the Liila played by Ba'ba'.

### 08. A'ca'rya Shacinandan Mandal – Birbhum

A'ca'ryajii was a person of a happy temperament. He maintained a pleasing disposition at all times. He was an embodiment of service and sacrifice. That may perhaps be the reason why he became Ba'ba's childhood friend. He lived close to Ba'ba's house in Jamalpur railway colony. He studied in the same school and in the same class. He was initiated in 1952 and later learned Ka'pa'lika sa'dhana'. Ba'ba' blessed his village home by visiting there on two occasions. He traveled there by a bullock cart. A DMC was also held in his village. Once Ba'ba' sent him to address the DMS in Kolkata's Tegraj Hall as MGR. He served the Abha' Seva' Sadan in A'nanda Nagar from 1966 to 1980.

His devotion to Ba'ba' was unparalleled. His dedication for the service of the needy has made him immortal. He left for his divine abode in the year 2000.

### 09. A'carya Vishvanath – Muzaffarpur

A'caryajii was an ardent devotee, an active person who spent his ma'rgii life with utmost sincerity. He was initiated by Ba'ba' in 1954. Ma'rgiis recall his inspiring work of Dharma Praca'ra in Narkatiya'ganj.

As the Bhukti Pradha'na of Muzaffarpur the construction of Jagrti and specifically its boundary is credited to him. Even in his old age he led an ideological life despite constant family opposition. He retired as Deputy Director of Veterinary Department.

### 10. A'ca'rya Kishunjii-Bhojpur

A'ca'ryajii is one of the brightest stars of the spiritual universe. He will remain resplendent for all time to come. A direct disciple of Ba'ba', he continued to have spiritual experiences almost spontaneously immediately after his initiation. He was a very elevated sa'dhaka.

In 1955 when Naginajii asked Ba'ba' to bless this planet for 50 more years by His physical presence, A'ca'rya Kishunjii was one of the five witnesses present there along with Naginajii. Before Ba'ba' agreed to accept their request, He had told that Kishunjii's life's mission was over. Kishunjii was a liberated sa'dhaka and this was his last sojourn in this mortal world.

Ba'ba' had a special liking for him. Once the a'ca'rya board had on some unconfirmed reports decided to relinquish Kishunjii's a'ca'ryaship. When the matter was brought before Ba'ba', He was visibly annoyed at this proposal. He had declared then that Kishunjii would remain a'ca'rya for life. He is an adorable person of our cosmic family and the one who is Pratah Smarniiya (should be remembered every morning).

### 11. A'ca'rya Sakaldeva -Muzaffarpur

Sakaldevajii was initiated in the year 1957 and became a'ca'rya the same year.

He constantly struggled for upholding the ideals of ma'rga. He actively pursued margii ideology and sincerely worked for its propagation, establishing a strong unit in his own village. He was an ideal family man and a Sthitaprajna sa'dhaka. He led a very simple life. His dedication for the Guru, his readiness to sacrifice everything for the sake of ideology continues to inspire all who came in his contact.

He was adored by the ka'pa'lika's. I also happened to get a chance to go for ka'pa'lika sa'dhana' with him in Muzaffarpur in 1966. He served the legal cell of the Ma'rga and attended many important cases. He was one of Ba'ba's affectionate sons. He left his mortal frame on the 14^th^ April 2004.

### 12. A'ca'rya Devichand – Bhagalpur

Devichanjii was born in Badgaon on the bank of river Sukkardharh in the District Hamirpur, in Himachal Pradesh. Before being initiated in the marga, he was a sa'dhaka of avidya' Tantra. He had defeated many Guru's using the power earned through Avidya' Tantra. When he met Ba'ba', he was asked by Ba'ba' to apply all his power on Him. When he failed to win over, Devichandjii eventually took shelter unto Ba'ba' as his Guru. His mind having opened up, in the state of complete self surrender Devichandjii witnessed His blissful Divine Form.

A'c. Chandranatha became His medium to inspire Devichandjii to take to Margii way of life. In 1957 he became A'ca'rya. Devicandajii evolved into an excellent Dharma Praca'raka as he became a devout disciple. During his posting in Ranchi, Ba'ba' very kindly agreed to bless him by accepting to stay in his house on four different occasions. Similarly in Sabaur also Ba'ba' visited him on three occasions.

Devicandajii wrote down his spiritual experiences with Ba'ba'. Then he brought up this matter to Ba'ba'. Ba'ba' told him – do you want a tender sapling of our sweet and affectionate relationship to be exposed to the crude glare of the world?

In his last days, Devicandajii once made a trip to Kolkata. Ba'ba' blessed him by touching his stomach with His Dukh Haran (stealer of once troubles without one's knowledge) stick and then asked him to keep visiting Him regularly like this once every month. Due to some preoccupation he had to to miss a trip, it was that month that he left his mortal frame.

In his last visit, he had accompanied A'c. Kuldiipjii. In his meeting with Ba'ba' he submitted that his children were still young and unsettled, and requested Ba'ba' to accept them in His lap. Ba'ba' quipped and sweetly touched his cheeks and assured him that they were His responsibility and that He would take care of of them. He left this world on the 3^rd^ December 1986. He still lives in the hearts and minds of devotees.

### 13. A'carya Dhruvanarayan – Patna

Dhruvanarayanjii was initiated in 1957. His deep longing for spiritual life and social service made him an ardent sa'dhaka. He soon learned Kapalika sa'dhana' ad became a'ca'rya. He was a scholar and an active social activist. He taught in the Balurghat

college as a professor. While leading responsible family life he promoted margii ideals where ever he stayed.

He left this ephemeral world on 8^th^ September 2008.

### 14. A'ca'rya Raghunath – Gorakhpur

A'ca'rya Raghunath was initiated into A'nanda Ma'rga Sa'dhana' in 1959 by A'ca'rya Dva'rka' Prasa'd. Before being attracted to A'nanda ma'rga Raghunathjii was an active volunteer of Rashtriya Svayam Sevak Samgh. He fought an election for the post of president of the student union and won it hands down. After obtaining post graduate degree in two subjects he started his career as a teacher in a college in Gorakhpur.

His spiritual bent of mind drew him near A'nanda Ma'rga. Within six months of his initiation, he became a family a'ca'rya. He was a humble and cool person. He would usually approach a problem systematically. He was an impressive orator and a giant intellectual.

Attracted by the magnetic personality of Ba'ba' and His rational world view, he left his job in 1962 and worked as a whole timer of the Ma'rga since then. The persecution in Jail during emergency did not dissuade him from the great ideology. He remained committed to Ba'ba' and His ideals and the organisation. He served as the general secretary of Sarva Sama'ja Samiiti until he became the President of A'nanda ma'rga Pracaraka Samgha. Ragunathjii spoke both Hindi and English with equal ease. When the Futures Organization invited Shrii PR Sarkar to address the conference in Peking, Ba'ba' deputed A'c. Ragunath as His representative.

During the North Indian tour of Ba'ba' in 1984, A'c. Raghunath collected His deliberations related to the historical and cultural aspects of different historical places that Ba'ba' visited during His tour. He presented Ba'ba's views on history in a new light.

### 15. Pranaya Kumar Chattopa'dhyaya – the first General Secretary of AMPS

Pranaya da' was the first general secretary. He was working in the accounts department of same workshop where Ba'ba' spent His 25 years in employment of Indian Railways.

Pranayada' was initiated by Ba'ba' in 1948. He is the first known A'nanda ma'rgii sa'dhaka. In 1955 when Ba'ba' founded the A'nanda Ma'rga Praca'raka Samgha, Pranayada' became its first General Secretary. He was blessed to have been His medium to receive instructions in many systems of Yoga Sa'dhana'. This also included all lessons of ka'pa'lika sa'dhana'. On the insistence of Ba'ba', Pranayada' got married to Pramilajii. Since she was not a sa'dhika', two days after their marriage she was also taught all lessons of sahaja yoga by Ba'ba' Himself, including all lessons of Ka'pa'lika sa'dhana'.

After the registry of the land in A'nanda Nagar, when it was made the global headquarter of A'nanda Ma'rga, Pranayada resigned from his job and moved to A'nanda Nagar.

Ba'ba' had a very special relationship with Pranayada. He once sent flowers and fruits to Pranayada who was then staying in Kolkata. This incidence took place just a few weeks before the abrupt end of His sojourn on our planet. When he realised its significant later he felt very sorry for not having met Him physically.

### 14. A'ca'rya Anant Thakur - Bhagalpur

A'ca'rya Ananta Thakur was initiated by A'ca'rya Chandranath in 1958. He became the District Secretary (Now Bhukti Pradha'na) of Bha'galpur the same year. He was employed in the central excise department.

He was responsible for the establishment of the first Prabhat Grantha'ga'ra in Bha'galpur and maintained it all his life. He also established the medical unit in didi's Ja'grti and served the needy people throughout his life. He was a good Praca'raka. He was a scholar of A'nanda Ma'rga philosophy. In those early days Da'da' Shraddhanandajii used to organise tattva sabha' and public meetings for him.

He left his body on 4^th^ September 2005.

### 15. A'ca'rya Samanvaya'nanda Avadhu'ta

Da'da' Samanvaya'nandajii was initiated in 1957 by A'c. Amulya Ratan Sarangi. Dadajii was then a family man serving in a government job in the office of A'c. AR Sarangi. Dadajii was deeply influenced by the strict moral conduct of his a'ca'rya.

He became a whole timer in the year 1960 and then became avadhu'ta in 1962. He was the 2^nd^ most senior avadhu'ta of the mission.

During emergency for a while he dissociated from the ma'rga and soon realized the folly of his decision and returned back to the fold of ma'rga. Da'da'jii was a thinker in his own right. He wrote A'nanda Shatakam in Samskrta. He wrote the following anecdote in the preface of the book. In 1972 he went to Bankipur Jail for the darshan of Ba'ba'. On entering His cell he prostrated and then stood by His side. Ba'ba' pointing to dadajii said that I know that you have composed a Rhythm in Samskrta language. No one knows it except me & you. His other compositions were Tantra Rahasyam (two parts), Guru-tattvam and Co-operative movement.

He worked hard all his life for the growth of the mission.

He was sent as MGR on two occasions to address the DMS. Dadajii was a good pracaraka, a sweet person and a great devotee. He had a large margii fan following. He had an aura of spirituality and he used that for the propagation of margii ideals.

We travelled throughout India for Dharma praca'ra and organizational work from 1993 until his death. He left us in Bokaro hospital on 3^rd^ November 2006.

### 16. A'c. Sambuddha'nanda Avadhu'ta

Dada sambuddha'nanda was the third avadhu'ta in the A'nanda ma'rga avadhu'ta tradition. He had the honour of driving Ba'ba' out of A'nanda nagar to Ranchi on the momentous day of 5^th^ march 1967. His dedication to Ba'ba' was absolute. He was in search of a Guru. By His grace, dada was initiated by a'c. Kedarjii in 1961.

In 1965, he was appointed superintendent of the first children home in Patna. In 1966, he became the central ERAWS secretary. He spent his last days in Brazil. In Petropolis near Rio-de-Janeiro he bought a sprawling Jagrti and did intensive propagation of the missionary ideals. He had a knack for collecting funds for the treasury of the Samgha. Before he became a sanyasi he was punished by Ba'ba' in jamalpur for having taken Jalebi and curd

without doing his morning sa'dhana'.

He left us on July 2002 in Petropolis, Brazil.

### 17. A'c. Pran'ava'nanda Avadhu'ta

Dadajii was the fourth among the sanyasin of a'nanda marga order of monks. He always played a leading role whether it was organizational activity or devotion to the Guru. He had to struggle a lot to fight against the immoral forces to protect the sanctity

of Is't'a and A'darsha. He was jailed during emergency and also suffered at the hands of CBI and police.

He was initiated by A'c. Chandra Dev Verma in Allahabad in 1961. He took to sanya'sa in 1962. Since 1967 he retained the post of Central Office Secretary for 18 years. He worked hard to please Ba'ba' through his efficiency and obedience. Ba'ba' once remarked "Pranava'nanda has tremendous capacity and capability".

He was responsible for the construction of Tiljala central office, Didi's building and Jagrti and also Madhu Ma'lainca. Rotunda, Power House, and central office in A'nanda nagar also bear the stamp of his hard work. He was behind the electrification of A'nanda Nagar. He had thorough knowledge of the land matters of A'nanda nagar.

In his last days he was busy building up the Kullu unit.

I have known him from close quarters since 1967. He left us on 7^th^ February 2010 in PGI, Chandigarh.

### 18. A'c. Vijayananda Avadhu'ta

Dadajii was a very learned person. The publication department of the ma'rga will always bear his stamp for the dedicated work that he carried out in a very tight schedule during Ba'ba's time and thereafter. He was a great devotee and a great teacher.

He was initiated by A'c. Shacinandan mandal in 1961 and became a avadhu'ta in 1962.

He remained in the centre throughout his margii life. By dint of his sharp intellect and exceptional memory he was chosen by Ba'ba' for taking notes and reproducing them flawlessly. Ba'ba' also taught him the science of judging a persons character and his total personality by seeing his face and body. During Dharma Samiiksa' Ba'ba' taught him to judge the personality of a person by noting one's physical features. He was asked by Ba'ba' to note down the details of each margii and also the A'sanas prescribed to them by Ba'ba'. Ba'ba' had chosen dadajii to represent Him once to address the 1^st^ January DMS as MGR at A'nanda nagar.

He left us in July 2002.

He will always be remembered for his outstanding qualities, dedicated life, his erudition and his invaluable contribution to the publication department.

### 19. A'c. Cida'nanda Avadhu'ta

Dadajii was initiated by A'c. Vishvamohanjii in 1958. He was then working as Mines manager. Later he dedicated his life for the mission & became avadhu'ta in 1965. Since then he had worked hard for 45 long years spreading Ba'ba's love in India & overseas countries. He travelled all over the world for consolidating His mission.

He was given the responsibility of Shramana (trainer of monks) of Davao city on Mindano island of southern Philippines. This Prashiksana Math was specially established to train overseas whole timers of the ma'rga. He trained over 600 whole timers during his posting in Davao. He established ‘Ba'ba' foundation’ a NGO for helping the undeveloped islands of Philippines. This foundation is managing many service projects for the poor and deprived people of Philippines. This foundation is serving over 6000 families in many islands. He wrote his memoires entitled “Unforgetable Memories”.

Da'da' Cidanandajii' made many contributions. He had a tall spiritual stature, deep human sensivity, neo-humanistic love and unparalleled sacrifice.

In his last days he suffered with cancer for four years. He received treatment from India’s leading cancer specialist Dr Nair of Appolo Delhi. He was recuperating in malur Polytechnic and was improving. All of a sudden he developed acute pain. He was admitted to the Manipal Hospital, Bangalore and left us on the 19th April 2008.

### 20. A'c. Cidghana'nanda Avadhu'ta

Cidghana'nandajii was initiated by A'c. Lalanjii. He dedicated his life for the Samgha in 1961. He spent most of his life in the service of the people of Rarh and taught English language in the High School and College at A'nanda Nagar.

Dadajii suffered a lot and was victimized by communists. He was jailed many a times. During emergency he was arrested by Interpol with A'c. Samanvaya'nanda avadhu'ta.

He was a great devotee and a scholar of A'nanda Ma'rga philosophy.

### 21. A'c. Amita'nanda Avadhuta

He was initiated while he was still studying in BIT, Mesra. He passed out as an electrical engineer and served Hindustan Motors-a motor car company of India based in Kolkata. He was initiated in 1960 by A'c. Amulya Ratan Sarangi in 1960. He became a wholetimer in 1963. He hailed from a Jamidar family of Aurangabad. His father was the

Sales Tax commissioner posted in Bihar. When Amitanandajii became a whole timer, his father made all out efforts to get his son back. But it did not succeed.

His first posting was in AMIT, A’nanda Nagar. He started the publication of Bodhikalp and remained its editor all his life. Once during annual DMC at Patna he was declared the best ta’ttvika’ in the organisation.

He was a tireless but systematic and intelligent worker. Being a strict disciplinarian, he was adored in and around A’nanda nagar as the Tiger of A’nanda nagar. Later he became Rector Master of A’nanda nagar. Much of the development of A’nanda nagar took place under his leadership despite complete destruction during emergency.

He was a loving son of Ba’ba’. He left us in 1982 after a protracted illness. Immediately after his demise when Ba’ba’ visited A’nanda Nagar, He remarked that missing Amitananda.

He once represented Ba’ba’ on the occasion of A’nanda Pu’rnima’ DMS in Kanpur in the year 1975. He had a very powerful personality. He was very sharp and had a special relationship with Ba’ba’.

### 22. A'c. Asiima'nanda Avadhu'ta

Asiimanandajii was initiated in 1963, while he was a studying in Agriculture University, Kanpur. He became a whoeltimer the same year. His first posting was in Hyderabad. He inspired Shrii Ram Chandra Reddy and Bhavesha’nandajii and initiated them.

He served many portfolios in AMPS center viz. Education, Agriculture and Finance. He was the man behind Ba’ba’s gardens. When he left us on 2nd april 1990, Ba’ba’ remarked that ‘Asiima’nanda was a great Agriculture scientist. It is a great loss to the world. He is irreplaceable’.

Asiimananda was a gem of a sanya’sin. He was both soft and sweet and a silent worker. The world needs many such persons if it has to rebuild the society in the image of Ba’ba’s ideology.

### 23. A'c. Tadbhava'nanda Avadhu'ta

Tadbhavanandajii started his margii life after being initiated by A’c. Parasnath in 1965. He had a highly sensitive mind for the suffering humanity. This prick of the pain of the humanity inspired him to dedicate his life for Ba’ba’s mission. I met him in 1967 and developed an initimacy with him.

Da’da’ Tadbhava’nanda left an ineffaceable imprint on the margii society. He was very hard working yet simple at heart. He travelled to many countries during his assignments and made significant contribution for the development of the marga. He established the Global Headquarters of Proutist Universal in Copenhagen. He built a huge Ja’grti and a master unit there. The Prout Bahvan at Malviya Nagar New Delhi, Patliputra Patna and A’nanda Nagar were built by him.

The CBI arrested him on false charges and sent him to Jail in Kolkata. The unsalutory conditions in the jail had its toll on his otherwise sound health. He suffered from cancer. By the untiring efforts of Human rights activists he was brought to Malviya Nagar for treatment. In Delhi he was served well by Dada Cittashiva’nandajii and Dada Visvotgata’nandajii and Vipin Kumar. Shrii Prem Ra’n’a’ devotedly took care of his needs.

Every one has to leave this world, but there are only a few like Tadbhava’nanda who leave a permanent imprint on the social edifice.

### 24. A’c. Kiirtya’nanda Avadhu’ta

Dada Kiirtya’nandajii was initiated in 1964 by Dada Shiveshvaranandajii. He dedicated his life in 1966.

He worked for the people of Rarh all his life and dedicated his efforts for the discovery of old relics and historical evidences of ancient Rarh. Ba’ba’ encouraged him to work for discovering the ancient relics related to Rarh. He had a natural inclination for archaeology and ancient history of Rarh. That is why ba’ba’ gave him the responsibility of establishing Rarhalogy. He discovered many lost relics of Jain religion. Ba’ba’ took him along whenever He toured the areas around A’nanda Nagar for discovering ancient archaeological and historical findings. Ba’ba’ taught him the methods to be adopted in discovering historical facts. He remarked that historical facts can not be discovered by sitting on armchair in an air conditioned bunglow of Baliganj. One will have to toil hard to find facts. Kiirtyananda adopted the spirit of a research worker of archaeology and history. He tried with all his available resources and was successful in finding many relics of ancient Rarh. Ba’ba’ used to be very pleased with him whenever he collected some new finds and showed them to Him.

Inspired by Ba’ba’ he organized the Kapil Mela in Jhalida the birth place of Maharshi Kapil. Since then it has been celebrated regularly in the Kapil cave near Jhalida.

Dadajii was the editor of A’nanda Rekha’ and served its cause all his life. He left his body in 2006 after a protracted debilating illness.

He has left behind a wealth of information for research on Rarh. All his research work has been published in his books entitled “Raktmrttika’ra Rarh”. This is a series which has been published in many volumes. He has also published his memoires with Ba’ba’. His work carry the stamp of his hard work and his sensitive mind.

### 25. A’c. Artesha’nanda Avadhu’ta

Dadajii became a wholetimer in 1963. His first posting was in Rewa, where he did commendable job. His was struggle torn life. He was arrested on false charges by CBI and was subjected to extreme torture. He was released after CBI failed to prove any thing substantial against him. On release he dedicated himself to develop service projects in New Delhi. He was responsible for the construction of Vinod Nagar Ja’grti & the Gadaipur MG quarter.

He left us in 2004. He is remembered for his dedicated life and service of the mission.

### 26. A’c. Devashrddha’nanda Avadhu’ta

Dadajii became a wholetimer in 1966. He was a person of great inner strength. He had a pure and a quite mind and was an inspiring person. He worked in NY sector as SES and SS. He made significant contributions for the mission & took special care of WTs in the difficult times of the marga.

He left us on 24th November 2008. In his last days he was looked after well by Dr. AK Mohanti and Dr. Sa’garika Mishra. Sister Sa’garika who runs the most amazing Adruta children home in Bhubaneswar took special care of dada and made him feel comfortable in his last days. Those were difficult and painful times for the Dada.

### 27. A’c. Abhiprema’nanda Avadhu’ta

I happened to meet him in Purulia Jail in 1967, where he was lodged for participating in the satya’grah for liberating A’nanda Nagar from the communist land mafia. He was then only 16 years old. He became a wholetimer in 1971. His first posting was as DS Akola. He served the Samgha as Central worker for many years and also did commendable work during his overseas postings.

He left us on 18th September 2003. He was a great devotee. He maintained a smiling disposition at all times.

### 28. A’c. Rudreshvara’nanda Avadhu’ta

Rudreshvarananda was initiated by dada Savita’nandajii in 1975. He was attracted by the practices of the ma’rga and soon became LFT and in 1976. Later he dedicated his life for the service of humanity as a wholetimer.

He spent all his life in the service of the African Nations. He toured 40 African nations and propagated the ideals of the ma’rga. He inspired many persons and trained LFTs. Many of his initiates dedicated their lives for the mission.

He worked hard to uplift the standard of many village communities, built roads, potable water supply systems, co-operatives, health centers, Jagrtis, safe maternity homes, trained nurses from among the village women. He was a tireless worker and rest was unknown to him. He worked not caring for day or night until the task is at hand was finished. His selfless contributions still inspire the margii society.

### 29. A’c. Rameshananda Avadhu’ta, Manila

Dadajii was a great devotee, a great neo-humanist, nature lover and a painter. A visitor to A’nanda Ma’rga centres in Manila sector can not miss no matter where one goes. His sweet nature and selflessness left everybody impressed.

He initiated PCAP activities and held many educative exhibition. His imagination caught the Neo-Humanistic thoughts waves. As a keen observer he would paint the images that touched his mind and heart. His classes mostly focused on Neo-humanist ideas.

He started many schools for the poor. He was suffering from diabetes and left us on 20th April 2010 in Manila.

His dedicated work keeps us inspired.

### 30. A’c. Vratadhiira’nanda Avadhu’ta, Philippines

Vratdhiira’nandajii was a great human being, a poet, an artist and a selfless worker. He was a loving person and a great devotee. He was trained as a wholetimer in Davao center in 1970. His first posting was in Brazil town of Brazil of South America in GT Sector. He remained there until 1998.

His next posting was in Kangemi of Nairobi sector. He established AMPS and A’nanda Ma’rga High School there. He got both the institutions registered by the education minister. He represented Proutist ideas very effectively in the international conference organized in Kenya Kericho town during 21st to 24th April 2005.

He left us on 2nd July 2008. The news of his sudden death was received with great shock and surprise by the margii society. His contributions are too many to be described. His was a great life of dedication and missionary zeal.

### 31. Ac. Avadhu’tika’ Ananda Bharati A’ca’rya’

The life and persona of Didi A’nanda Bharati deserves timeless praise. Her real life was far superior to her apparent person. Born and brought up in an aristocratic but highly cultured family of Allahabad, Didi’s early years at home were spent in the midst of an environment of music and art.

She had a natural inclination for spirituality from her childhood. She had regular contact with many elevated spiritual sa’dhakas and luminaries. Most notable among them were Maharshi Raman (Tamil Nadu) & Swami Ramdas (Kerala).

She lived in the A’shrama of Swami Ramdas as his disciple. However, her search for her spiritual guide continued. In that process she came across A’nanda Ma’rga. She was inspired to learn the sa’dhana of A’nanda Ma’rga. But she was confused. In that state of mind she had a dream in which a young sanyasin in Dhoti Kurta appeared in her dream. Swami Ramdas was standing by his side. Swami Ramdas indicated to her to touch the feet of the sanyasin. As she touched his feet there was the pronouncement of a divine message which proclaimed that you have received the grace of the Cosmic Consciousness.

Next day she was called to attend a spiritual meeting of A’nanda Ma’rga in Jaipur. She went to attend that programme. As she entered the meeting hall, Lo! She was beholding the same sanyasin who had appeared in her dream. He was in the very same attire. She took initiation from that Ananda Ma’rga sanyasin.

Since then the only desire that remained in her mind was get every known person initiated into A’nanda Ma’rga. Because of her efforts many from the upper strata of society in Mumbai were attracted to A’nanda Ma’rga and got initiated.

She expressed her desire to become a sanyasin to Ba’ba’. Ba’ba’ accepted her to be a nun. It was in 1966 that she was initiated into the sanyas order of the ma’rga. Thus she became the first lady avadhu’tika’ of the ma’rga.

Her intense spiritual craving always looked for any opportunity that may come her way. Needless to say she had constant realization of Ba’ba’s grace. That is why she was always successful in accomplishing any task given by Ba’ba’. Every position that she held in the WWD carried a stamp of her meticulous nature. When she became the head of WWD she always kept Ba’ba’ pleased with her good work.

For giving any instructions she was called by Ba’ba’ in person and she was always offered a chair. This was an expression Ba’ba’s affection for her.

When she became almost invalid to walk, Ba’ba’ went to her and said the only thing you should do now is to wipe the tears from the face of the suffering and distressed humanity which comes in your contact. Any one who went to meet her, returned back inspired with hope. She had tremendous grit.

Ba’ba’ had once remarked that she had enjoyed all the worldly pleasures and remained absorbed into the luxuries of life, yet she renounced everything happily to become a sanyasin. Her sacrifice is commendable.

### 35. A’c. Hari Shankar, Ranchi

A’carya Harishankarji was a devotee par excellence. He always made himself available for ma’rgii activity like a whole timer. He took pains to propagate the ma’rgiya ideals throughout the villages surrounding Ranchi. His margii life started after his initiation by A’c. Kapileshvarajii. He inspired all his children to follow the ma’rgii way of life and later all of them got married according to its revolutionary marriage system which rejects caste biases and giving or taking dowry. He worked as the secretary Marriage Bureau of A’nanda Ma’rga all his life. Following the incidence of 5th march 1967, when Ba’ba’ arrived in Ranchi, He stayed in A’c. Hari Shankarjii’s house in NCDC colony in Ranchi before moving to a hired place there.

A’caryajii met with accidents on three occasions since he used to close his eyes in a state of cosmic ideation while driving his bike. Needless to say Ba’ba’ always took care of him. Ba’ba’ even visited him in the hospital and enquired about his welfare on all the three occasions.

A’ca’ryajii left his mortal frame in August 1999. I came in his close contact since 1969. He would be remembered for his simplicity and dedication.

### 36. A’c. Devakinandan, Basti

My own contact with A’ca’ryajii took place in 1978 in A’zamgarh. Since then we have developed close association and affectionate feelings for each other.

Just before His great departure Ba’ba’ had called the margiis the world over, A’ca’ryajii also responded & reached Kolkata and had His dharshan. Ba’ba’ had once mentioned that Devaki was a great devotee.

Devakinandanji was initiated by A’ca’rya Raghunath in 1960. He became a family a’ca’rya in 1962. The same year he was selected by UP state Public Service Commission. He initiated hundreds of people in Gorakhpur, Devaria and Azamgarh. He was also functioned as the Bhukti Pradha’na of Gorakhpur and Azamgarh.

He left for his cosmic abode on the 2nd July 2009.

### 37. A’c. Devakinandan, Muzaffarpur

A’ca’ryajii was one of the leading a’caryas of the Ma’rga. In a very strange circumstance, he wrote a letter in the form of a poetry to Ba’ba’ in Jamalpur. He followed Ba’ba’s reply as a command and took initiation with all his family members. He was then serving Bihar Police Department.

From 1961 for 15 years, until he left his mortal frame in 1976 to return to his original abode, a’ca’ryajii conducted himself as an ideal devotee in terms of sacrifice, complete self-surrender, and strict adherence to the A’nanda Ma’rga ideology. He had a soft mental disposition and composed Sha’yari taking a nick name of Mushtar. His devotion for ba’ba’ was sakha’vata (like a close friend). Once on a field walk in A’nanda Nagar Ba’ba’ said that “Devaki has become Devata”. A’ca’ryajii devoted his everything at the lotus feet of Ba’ba’.

His special contribution was in regard to the construction of the first Children’s home in Patna, building Ja’grti in Arah and the degree college in A’nanda Nagar.

He lived his life in the image of Margii ideals. His wife A’ca’rya’ Janak Kishori continues to lead an ideal life even now at the age 94 years.

A’caryajii left a large family of active ma’rgiis including his sons and daughters & grandchildren numbering over 80 persons apart from his thousands of initiates. Sixteen revolutionary marriages have been conducted in his direct family. His family of five generations is a unique example of is’t’anis’tha’ and a’dharshanis’tha’.

### 38. A’ca’rya Keshava Prasad, Gazipur

A’ca’ryajii was initiated by Raghunathjii in 1961. He was a great devotee. Ba’ba’ blessed his family by staying with them five times. Once he conducted the DMC inside his courtyard of a’ca’ryajii’s house.

He left this mortal body on 7^th February 1992. He is remembered for his devotion and service of the mission.

### 39. A’carya Trptinarayan, Deogarh

A’ca’ryajii was a great sadhaka. He was an active social worker and a Neo-humanist. He is remembered even today with reverence in Deogarh, where he was responsible for the construction of Jagrti and later its maintenance & MG Quarter. Ba’ba’ would often address him lovingly as Trpti (satisfied).

He left his body on 11^th April 2005 in village Rampur (near Lakhisaray, Bihar).

### 40. A’c. Manohar Lal, Delhi

A’ca’ryajii started his margii journey after being initiated by A’c. Lalan in Jaipur.

Before being initiated he was devoted to Kali puja. When A’c. Lalan inspired him to learn Brahma sa’dhana’, Manoharlaljii responded saying he must take mother kali’s permission. On his inquiry Kali according to manoharlalji replied that if you have the desire of your supreme welfare, you should better get initiated by the A’ca’rya.

He became A’ca’rya by dint of his devotion to the Guru and His grace in 1963. Once while passing through a local area in Jaipur, looking at the college which came on the way, Ba’ba’ remarked pointing towards the college that it was Manohar’s college. A few days later Manohar Laljii became the Principal of that college.

He left his body on 23^rd January 1996 after Guru Puja’. He would be always remembered for his good deeds.

### 41. A’carya Bhis’madeva, Balaghata

Bhisma Devajii was a poet and an inspiring person. He wrote many poems which have been published as collections in many titles viz. Parivartan, Tarannum, Bal-Batika, Jagav Chattisgarhia, Kusum Kalyan.

He was born in Village Dhansa’ of Balaghata. After initiation, he taught in Jagadalpur A’nanda Ma’rga Primary school. He managed the school as a LFT and devoted himself for the praca’ra of the mission. Many people who came in his contact became sa’dhaka.

During his last days he suffered from cancer and died on 30^th June 2005 in Raipur.

### 42. A’c. Sakaldiip, Varanasi

Born in a village environment of Varanasi in 1935, he was devoted to Shiva. In 1958 he was posted as assistant engineer in Chaibasa (Bihar). It was here that he got initiated. Sakaldiipjii was a dignified person. His child-like simplicity attracted those who came in his contact. He maintained a perfect balance in his personal family life and that of the organization. Always conscious of his responsibility he devoted himself to the pracara of

the ma'rga and must have initiated a few thousand persons. In 1996 he was adjudged as the best Bhukti Pradha'na.

He was initiated by a'ca'rya Chandra Dev and became family aca'rya in 1964.

He left his mortal frame on 13^th July 2008. His selfless dedication is the asset of the collective humanity.

### 43. Shrii Aniruddha Singh, Trimuhan, Bhagalpur

Aniruddhaji is well-known in the ma'rgii community. His devotion to Ba'ba' was incomparable. He remained active throughout his life. He was initiated by A'c. Chandra Nath on A'nanda Purnima' of 1956. I came in his contact in 1972.

He continued as the chairperson of Jamalpur DMS committee until his demise. He was very enthusiastic in collecting funds for the DMS. The historic speech of Ba'ba' at the time of inauguration of Renaissance Universal was given in Trimuhan, Anirudhaji's home town. He wrote a book of his own experiences entitled "Ba'ba' Na'ma Kevalam". All his family members are active ma'rgiis. His brother Harendrajii is one of the old Ka'pa'likas.

He left this world on 10^th September 2009. His love for Ba'ba' keeps us inspired.

### 44. Dr. Ramesh, Ranchi

Dr Ramesh was initiated by A'c. Akhaorii Hima'cala Prasad in the year 1957, when Rameshji was posted in the Government Hospital, Deogarh. In A'nanda Ma'rga Dr' Ramesh has been a known person. In 1963 when Ba'ba' established ERAWS, Dr. Ramesh was given the responsibility of Relief Committee. Later he became the Secretary of Bihar Relief Committee. After successful handling of Bihar relief Ba'ba' founded A'nanda ma'rga Relief Committee (AMRC). In 1966-67, AMRC rendered yeoman service to thousands of the draught affected people of Bihar. AMRC was the precursor of AMURT & AMURTEL.

Ba'ba' chose Dr. Ramesh to head the AMURT as its Chairperson. He held the post all his life. Once Ba'ba' had remarked that Ramesh is sadhu who thinks, speaks what he does.

Dr. Ramesh was actively associated with development of A'nanda Nagar. He was an active member of the ACB of Medical Board of A'nanda Ma'rga.

Dr. Ramesh inspired his family and all of them are sa'dhakas of the Ma'rga.

Dr. Ramesh was not only a good pediatrician but also a family guardian of margiis.

Dr. Rameshwar Prasad who was better known to all as Dr. Ramesh left us on 29^th January 2010. In him we have lost a sincere friend of the Ma'rgii society.

### 45. Shrii Arvind Nath Shahdeo, Gumala'

Arvindjii was initiated by A'c. Harishankarjii. He was then a leading Zamindar of Chota'na'gpur. He made significant contributions for the development of our master Unit in Baghima' of Gumala'.

Arvindjii was an affectionate devotee of Ba'ba'. He was jailed many a times for the cause of Ma'rga and was also arrested once in Misa (Maintenance of Internal Security Act.

He always took up one or the other responsibility for the service of the Ma'rga. He was the Sama'ja Secretary of Chotanagpur Sama'ja all his life. For some time he was also the Bhukti Pradha'na of Ranchi Bhukti. Then he also took up the responsibility of Ba'ba's security guard. In a near fatal accident in Netrahat, he was miraculously saved by Ba'ba', and he regained his health in a short time.

His wife Dr. Karun'a is a family a'ca'rya. Like him she is always ready to lend a helping hand whenever there is any need in the ma'rga. His children are also active ma'rgiis. I came close to him when I was posted in ASMU.

He left us on 25^th September 2009.

### 46. Shrii Baldeo Rathi, Jaipur

Rathijii came in the ma'rga in 1965. He was a great devotee and a responsible ma'rgii always ready for His service. Rathijii travelled with Ba'ba' accompanied by his wife during His tour of Europe and South America and Jamaica. During the tour both of them got lots of opportunity to serve Him. Ba'ba' was always pleased with his service. His demeanour would make Ba'ba' very happy. Ba'ba' would be all smile whenever Rathijii performed Ta'n'd'ava Dance before Him.

Ba'ba' once remarked about him "he is a fat man and very dependable".

He remained an active member of AMURT. His wife Radha didi is also a strong devotee of Ba'ba'. At his last moment he told his wife that Ba'ba' has made enough arrangement for her. Radha Didi asked him to remember Him only. He responded by saying "who else is there for me except Him". He left us on the 16^th January 2010.

### 47. Shrii Shashi Rainjan, Muzaffarpur

Born on 30^th June 1918 at Muzaffarpur, Shashi Rainjanjii was attracted towards Ma'rga by the inspiration of his younger brother Sudha' Rainjan in the year 1957. He was initiated by A'c. Nagendra. He joined active politics in 1966 and was elected Member of Parliament for two terms on Congress ticket. When Ba'ba' wanted him to organize PBI (Proutist Bloc, India), he resigned from the membership of Parliament (MP). He fought election from the platform of PBI in 1970. He was the chairperson of PBI. Prime Minister Indira Gandhi approached her to dissuade him from resigning the parliamentary seat but he decided to sacrifice at the command of his Guru. On His way back from the Sitamarhi DMC Ba'ba' stayed in his Muzaffarpur residence. Ba'ba' also stayed at his MP residence in South Avenue, New Delhi. His wife Smt. Pratibha Devi was also a great devotee and she used to sing Bhajan for Him. She got many opportunities to serve Him.

Shashi babu was a devotee par excellence. He was an active margii and an ideal person. He was ever ready to do whatever was required to please Ba'ba'. He gave two rooms of his Patna residence on exhibition road for ma'rgii activities. One was used for PBI movement and the other for Bihar Relief. Once Ba'ba' gave him a target to open schools in all the blocks of Patna within a specified period, which he did accomplish. Ba'ba' mentioned this incidence during the course of His discourse on Guru vandana' in Sabda Cayanika' and described it as the highest stage of devotion.

He left us on 12^th April 1992.

48. Shrii Raghuvir Prasad, Bareilly.

Shrii Pandeya Raghuvir Prasad – was better known in the Margii community as R Prasad. He was one of the greatest devotees of Ba’ba’. My first opportunity to know him came when I was RS Allahabad. It was then that A’nanda Ma’rga school was started in Bareilly that I got to know this great person.

Shrii R Prasad came from an aristocratic family of Bareilly but was simple man. He was a strict moralist and an active social worker. He was initiated by Ba’ba’.

In 1969, the then, Central Government banned participation of government employees to in A’nanda Ma’rga. He challenged the order in Supreme Court of India and got a stay. He was then posted as the Custom collector in New Delhi. But he dared the government by raising an issue of constitutional rights of the citizens and demonstrated a revolutionary and adamant ideological commitment. Outraised by his action the government used its roller coaster to suppress him and his promotion was withheld.

In 1975 after the promulgation of emergency in India, A’nanda Ma’rga was banned. By then Shrii R Prasad had retired. He immediately rushed to meet the Prime Minister to request her to allow practice of A’nanda Ma’rga system of worship (sa’dhana’) even when A’nanda Ma’rga activities are banned. The then Prime minister did not meet him, instead ordered him to be arrested and he was sent to Tiha’r Jail in New Delhi. The family approached the government and it was conceded that if Shrii R Prasad gives in writing that he is not a A’nanda Ma’rgii, then he would be released from jail. When he was asked to give in writing, he replied that he was a ma’rgii and how could he write that he was not one.

Thus he had to spent the jail term until emergency was lifted in March 1977.

The speciality of Shrii R Prasad was that he would not miss any opportunity to spread the ideals of the Ma’rga. He would always present convincing arguments about A’nanda Ma’rga philosophy and way of life.

His devotion to guru was deep rooted. In 1968, during Coochbehar DMC Ba’ba’ was arrested at the instance of the then Communist Chief Minister of West Bengal. As the news of His arrest reached New Delhi, Shrii R Prasad immediately rushed to Coochbehar carrying sufficient cash with him, to meet any legal requirements to get Him freed from Jail.

Ba’ba’ also had a special liking for him. After his demise when Ba’ba’ came to visit New Delhi He fondly remembered R Prasad’s absence there.

49. Shrii Rajendra Kumar (IAS), Lucknow

Rajendrajii was born and brought up in Rajasthan. He was a brilliant person and passed the Indian Administrative Service of Government of India. He was later appointed in the Administrative Service in UP cadre. He was posted as District magistrate in Farukhabad, Faizabad, Gonda, Rampur and Saharanpur. Later on promotion he became Commissioner of Gorakhpur. Finally he retired as the Secretary Public Works department, Government of UP.

He was the younger brother of a’c. Virendra Kumar As’tha’na’, who initiated him into A’nanda Ma’rga. Ba’ba’ once stayed in his house, in Lucknow before he got initiated. He never missed the opportunity to meet his loving Guru whenever he was able to relieve himself from his administrative duty. He would always bring in his family in DMCs.

He used to write in Prout journal as Kan’a’da, a name given to him by Ba’ba’. He was trained for 15 days as a ta’ttvika in Jamalpur.

Ba’ba’ blessed his Fatehpur residence by staying there for a week. He organized the Fatehpur DMC in 1962. Wherever he was posted he made sure margiiya establishments viz. Children Home, Kindergarten school, Master Units develop properly.

He left this body and proceeded on his great journey to unite with his Supreme goal.

50. Shrii Anant Ram, Ranchi

Born in 1922 on Anant Chaturdashi, Anantjii served the Punjab National Bank as an accountant. After his initiation by A’c. Satyananda Avadhu’ta in 1958, he became a great devotee, an active Dharma Praca’raka and a dedicated social worker.

During his personal contact Ba’ba’ blessed him by saying “you are a good & brave boy and you will bring good name to the ma’rga.

I got lots of opportunity to work with him for praca’ra in Patna in the year 1965.

He donated land for the High school in A’nanda Shila’ Master Unit.

All his children developed as good margiis and got married according to the revolutionary marriage system of the Ma’rga. One of his daughter is a sanyasin of the Ma’rga. his wife Smt. Chandramani Devi was also a dedicated sadhika’ and always took good care of the wholetimers.

Anantjii left his mortal farme on 18th January 1989. His younger brother Shrii Ramlakhanjii is also a gem of ma’rgii. His entire family consisting of his four sons and their wives are all dedicated for the mission.

### 51. Shrii Omkar Nath Kaul, Kashmir

Omkarjii was a great devotee of the Guru and lived up to His expectations. He was a one man army who held the flag of Dharma flying high in Kashmir.

I salute him for his brave stance.

His entire family including his wife and his sons and daughters are all dedicated for the mission. All his children were married according to A’nanda marga revolutionary marriage system.

### 52. Shrii Faguaram Sahu, Raipur

Born in 20th August 1944 Shrii Faguaram had spiritual inclination. He was initiated in 1967 by A’c. Kailshaji. He lived according to the A’nanda margii way of life. In Jabalpur he worked for many years as the Bhukti Pradha’na and helped in exapanding the mission.
