He received Ba’ba’s blessings through Dharma Samiiksha’, Gardening programme and on many occasions accompanying Him on field walk. All his three children are highly educated and and married according to A’nanda ma’rga system (no caste bias and no dowry and tilak).

He retired as Deputy GM Telephone and departed from this world on 27th September 2007.

### 53. Shrii Vrijalal, Durg

Born in Jamshedpur, he moved to Chattisgarh and got initiated by A’c. Ramlal Dani. He remained a sincere devotee all his life.

For him Kiirtan was his life. He would never leave any opportunity to attend a Kiirtan programme and always participated with his family.

On the Golden jubilee celebration, when 50 days Akhand Kiirtan was organized in Jamalpur, he stayed there for 40 days. Before that he participated in 9 days Kiirtan in Jamshedpur. He must have organized umpteen number of Kiirtan programme in Bhilai.

He was blessed by a supporting wife who co-operated with him in all spiritual endeavours and always inspired him.

### 54. Shrii Kunjabihari, Raipur

Kunju Ustad as he was affectionately called by his near and dear ones was initiated by A’c. Narendra Deo in 1963. A wrestler in his younger days, he lived upto the ideals of the Ma’rga. He was a dedicated worker of Chattisgarhi samaja and vahinii. He attended Ba’ba’ as his security guard during many DMCs. On one such occasion during Bhopal DMC pleased by his dedication Ba’ba’ gave him his garland.

He was particular in performing sa’dhana’ together with his family members. He had an adept hand in treating people suffering with dislocation of shoulder and sprain in hands and legs.

He is affectionately recalled in the margii society for his simplicity and love for Ba’ba’.

### 55. Shrii Sunil Bhagi

Born in village Jarao of Ludhiana on 24th August 1957, Shrii Bhagi was initiated by Dada Shankara’nanda Avadhu’ta. His early education took place in A’nanda Nagar High School. Later he completed his post graduation in Commerce. He was a dedicated margii. Most of his time was spent in creative pursuits. He devoted himself for propagating Dharma to please his Guru.

Bhagi’s left us for his permanent home on 25th December 2008. He was suffering from Kidney problem. At the time of his death he was lost in His thought.

### 56. Shrii Sharat Chandra Pradha’na – Talcher (Orissa)

Sharat Babu was a accomplished poet, literatuere and a thinker. He was devoted to the ideals of marga.he was initiated by A’c. Nirmohananda Avadhu’ta. He wrote over 600 devotional songs. He presented the A’nanda marga philosophy in Oriya for pracara in the name of Adarsh Saral Bhagavata. He also wrote Guru Giita depicting the sweet relation between the Guru and the disciple.

He wrote many songs for propagating margii ideals and produced a cassette in the voice of famous singers Kumar Sajan and Saroj Nanda. His devotional songs were compiled in the name of Bhakti Sarita in the voice of Laksmi Kant Palit.

He left us on 15th February 2010. He is missed by the margii society.

### 57. Shrii Ramchandra Reddy, Hyderabad

Shrii Ram Chandra Reddy was a Stalwart of Hyderabad and a devotee par excellence. He was a leading a’nanda margii and a selfless social worker. Initiated by A’c. Asiima’nanda Avadhu’ta in 1965, he was a disciplined person. He retired as a Chief Engineer Government of Andhra Pradesh. He himself worked hard to spread the ideals of the Ma’rga and also inspired those who came in his contact.

He established the A’nanda Ma’rga School, Ja’grti, Yoga Center, Children Home and other institutions of the marga. Ba’ba’ stayed with him on two occasions and blessed him. Ba’ba’ once remarked about him “Ram Chandra is a brave person. He is the tiger of Telangana.

He had a heavy body, despite that he used to perform tan’d’ava. Once he demonstrated ta’nd’ava before Ba’ba’ smiled and appreciated his effort.

During emergency he was kept under house arrest. He left us in 1981.

His memory is still fresh in the minds of margiis and WTs.

### 58. Shrii NRK Raju, Vizag

Rajujii was a great devotee of Ba’ba’ ever ready to do anything for the marga. He was initiated in 1964, since then he sincerely devoted himself for advancing the cause of the ma’rga.

He was very particular about taking care of the WTs posted in Vizag. That is why every time a new worker was posted there, Ba’ba’ used to advise the WT to meet him. Rajujii would take care of the new workers as his own children. During the two visits of Ba’ba’ in Vizag, Rajujii and his wife took care of His requirements. Raju Didi cooked Andhra delicacies and served Ba’ba’ much to His delight during DMCs in Vizag. All his children got married according to A’nanda Ma’rga System.

Once a WT misbehaved with him, Ba’ba’ immediately sent a message and advised him to forget the hurt caused by the worker.

He left us on 18th April 2009, but his legacy of dedicated service would be remembered.

### 59. Shrii Amir Chand Nirankar

Nirankarjii was attracted to the lofty ideals of the ma’rga when he was still a student. He was born in a village Manihari of Katihar. He graduated in law from Bihar University. He was practicing in Patna High Court.

His was a revolutionary marriage to the Smt. Vidya, daughter of Shrii Rajmangaljii of Chakia. Both of his two daughters and a son are advocates. I came in his close contact in 1965 and was impressed with his dedication for the ma’rga.

This sincere devotee of Patna left us on the 7th march 2010. He was a famous advocate. When he breathed last he was with his clients. The last word he uttered was Ba’ba’. He was only 67 years old.

### 60. Smt Sumitra Devi, Deoghar

Sumitra didi was initiated by A’ca’. Cinta Devi in 1961. She is one of the senior lady ta’ttvikas of the ma’rga. She was married to Himanshu Prasad. Sudhanshujii of Ranchi is her eldest son.

She was blessed to have got the opportunity to garland Ba’ba’ and to cook for Him whenever He visited Deoghar. She was also the witness when Ba’ba’ gave the first Prabhat samgiita in Deoghar. She learned by heart over 1200 songs of Prabhat Samgiita.

On many occasions, elsewhere, Ba’ba’ would inquire if Sumitra likes this song. Ba’ba’ once remarked about her, “she is very active and hardworking. She loves all. She also loves me. She is very particular in attending to the needs of the WTs.”

She did commendable work as the secretary of lady’s wing of Samaja. She was blessed person. She donated the land for MG quarter in Deoghar.

She left us on 6th January 2005. She will be remembered for her service of humanity.

### 61. Smt. Shashiprabha, Gazipur

From the beginning after her initiation Shashiprabha didi devoted herself to lead her life as an ideal disciple. She transformed her life in accordance with the triple ideals of sa’dhana’, seva and sacrifice. She was a liberated woman and a scholar. She retired as the vice principal of Government Girls School, Gazipur.

She donated the land and supported the construction of the A’nanda Ma’rga Girls school in Gazipur. Her husband Shrii Ranjiit Prasad was also a sincere devotee of the ma’rga.

She has remained devoted to the ma’rga all her life. Before she departed she spent all her time day and night in singing kiirtan. On her physical death she had a special glow on her face. She left us on the 30th June, 2007.

### 62. Smt. Kranti Devi, Jamalpur

Kranti didi is one of foremost among lady devotees of Ba’ba’. She was a fine woman, full of love and always in devotional mood. She would instantly lose consciousness no

sooner she would behold Him and danced in a state of bha’va. She could be seen dancing in that elevated state of devotion during DMCs.

She was initiated by A’c. Shishir jii in 1964 in Jamalpur. She always played an active role in the margii activities. She would take a leading role in the organization of Jamalpur DMC. She had special affection for the WTs and considered them as His representatives.

She retired as the Principal of Mahila college, Jamalpur and had a benign presence in the society.

She suffered a lot in her last days due cancer of her intestine. She left us on 17^th October 2000 in Gangaram Hospital. Her memory inspires devotion.

### 63. Smt. Vidya Nirankar, Patna

Vidya didi was a great sa’dhika’ and a sincere disciple. She got married according to the revolutionary ideal of marriage with Shrii Amiir Chand. Her love for ba’ba’s ideals was contagious. She remained steadfast all her life. She was the mother of three able children.

She had a very fine family tradition. Her elder brother was the legendary Abhedananda Avadhu’ta. She had a natural inclination and deep association with service activities. She was the general secretary of ‘Peoples Council Of India and the chairperson of Jharkhand Vikas foundation. She was also the member Bihar State Welfare Advisory Board. She was well known for her honesty and integrity. She was a liberated woman and a journalist. She was the editor of the ‘Consumer Times’ published from Patna.

When the last moment of her life came, she was in a blissful mood and face had the glow of divine love.

### 64. Smt. Snehlata Goenka, Mumbai

She got initiated in 1965 by dada Sha’nta’nanda in Mumbai. She was the wife Shrii Shyam Sundar Goenka. She was also a good sa’dhika’ and an affectionate mother.

Ba’ba’ visited her house in Mumbai five times and gave left many the blissful moments to cherish.

She left her physical body in 2002.

Her devotion, her sweet behavior and seva bha’va will live forever.

### 65. Smt. Shanti Devi and Shrii Abhilash Singh, Chaprai (Etah)

Shanti didi was a devoted margii and very service minded. She was initiated by a’c. Satisha in 1963. In the year 1979, Ba’ba’ visited Etah for DMC, that was the fulfiment of her desire to serve Him. She was blessed to have cooked His food and also served Him. Her husband Shrii Abhilash Singh donated 125 acres of land for master unit project in Etah.

Like her, he was also a devoted margii. He left us on 2^nd August 1994 & Shanti Didi on 11^th Feb. 1996.

She would always be remembered for her sweet nature and sacrifice.

### 66. Shrii Brajbihari, Bhagalpur

Brajbiharijii was one among the greatest of His devotees. He was fortunate to have been blessed to serve Him variously. He was a very senior officer of the government of Bihar. He had a very close personal relation with Ba’ba’. In Patna Brajbiharijii used to be His guardian.

Brajbiharijii was initiated in 1960, by a’c. Prem Baha’dur Mathur. He left no stone unturned in helping A’c. Ramtanukjii to ensure Ba’ba’s release from jail.

His contribution for the propagation of the ma’rga is very significant. His elder brother Kunjbiharijii was also an active margii.

He hailed from Bhagalpur. He left us to be with his beloved on 21^st March 2002.

He will always be remembered for the yeoman service of the mission.

### 67. Laksmi Chand A’nand- Mumbai

LC Anand, as he was fondly called, was initiated in 1964 by A’c. Chandra Dev Verma. He was a staunch follower of the ma’rga, a great devotee and a very active & spiritually conscious person. Ba’ba’ blessed him by staying with him on four occasions.

For him Ba’ba’ was everything. No decision was taken in his family without consulting Ba’ba’. Once one of his son wanted division of his property. He told him to go and ask Ba’ba’.

Ba’ba’ had a special affection for him. Ba’ba’ invited him to attend a’c. Kinshuk Rainjan Sarkar’s marriage. He started an institution dedicated to the memory of his Guru for the education & welfare of children of special need. The institution runs in apart of his house in Bandra, Mumbai even now. It is managed by the family trust.

He left his physical body on 24^th August 1995. He has left an indelible mark of his dedication to the Guru and his highly sensitive approach for the welfare of the children with special needs.

### 68. Shrii Chamanlal Munshi, Mumbai

Munshijii had such a unique emotional bent of mind that he always remained overwhelmed in the thoughts of Ba’ba’ and His mission. He almost always enjoyed being close to WT dadas. Initiated by a’c. Chandra Nath in 1965 he instantly became a devotee. He saw Ba’ba’ as Lord Krsna’ intuitively and fell in His love with Him.

After the demise of Shashi Ranjanjii, he took over as the Chairperson of PBI. He was jailed during emergency and had to suffer for holding on to Dharma.

He contributed for the purchase of MG Residence in Andheri, Mumbai. Ba’ba’ blessed him by staying in his house at Ultamount Road.

He left his mortal frame on 9^th September, 1978 and attained the goal of his life. He will be always remembered for his simplicity, devotion to the ma’rga and Gurunis’tha’.

### 69. Shrii Chandraprakash Atreya, Meerut

Prof. CP Atreya was a teacher of physics in DN College, Meerut. He was initiated in 1964 by a’c. Shuddha’nanda Avadhu’ta. He dedicatedly spread the ideals of the Ma’rga among the students and teachers.

He edited a weekly journal ‘Mahasambhuti’. He went to Australia and Philippines for propagating the ideals of ma’rga. Prout Prabha’ti entitled essays written by him was widely accepted. Later he got the audio of the same done and popularized Prout among the common people. The songs composed by him are still popular among the margiis of North India.

He left us on 31ˢᵗ August 2002. His devotional songs still reverberate the minds of northern India for their social content and inspirational value.

### 70. Shrii Gopal Krishnan, kocchi

Gopal Krishnanjii was initiated by Dada Pranava’nandajii in 1964. He was then District Sessions Judge. He was a dedicated sa’dhaka and a great devotee of Ba’ba’. He was a silent worker and used his resources for propagating margii ideals. Ba’ba’ stayed in his house in Kocchi during DMCs. He and his equally devoted wife were blessed to get an opportunity to serve him.

Ba’ba’ used to call him “a silent worker and a devoted sa’dhaka.

Although he is no more his sincerity and devotion still inspires the margiis.

### 71. Shrii Rajgopalan, Coimbatore

Rajgopalanjii’s margii life started in 1966. He was the man behind A’nanda Ma’rga activities in Coimbatore. He ensured that the WTs posted in his area are well taken care off. Through his dedication and devotion to the Ma’rga Guru, A’nanda ma’rga attracted many sincere young men and women to the ma’rga.

He looked at Ba’ba’ as the guardian of his family. He had unconditional love for Him. He suffered a lot for following the path of Dharma. He was arrested during emergency and had incarcerated in jail.

He is remembered for his good conduct and sacrifice for Dharma.

### 72. Shrii Gopalma’na Shres’tha, Janakpur, Nepal

Gopaljii was a strict moralist and service minded devotee. He was initiated in 1966 by a’c. Giridhar Narayan. He is credited for having established a’nanda Ma’rga in whole of Nepal. He translated many books in Nepali. He himself wrote two books viz. Yoga Tantra & Kalki Avatar, describing A’nanda Ma’rga way of life.

His three sons and four daughters are all good ma’rgiis. He left us on 27^th September 2007 in Kathmandu. He is remembered for his service of the ma’rga.

### 73. Shrii Kanaiyalal, Thakurganj

Kanaiyajii was initiated by dada Shraddha’nandajii in 1962. He bought the land and constructed the school in Thakurganj started A’nanda ma’rga Primary School there. This institution developed as an ideal School. He remained its secretary all his life. He was also responsible for starting a Master Unit after managing 5 acres of land.

He brought his entire family in the fold of a’nanda ma’rga. His sons and daughters are all sincere ma’rgiis. He helped in the editing of Bhojpuri Grammer written by Da’da’ Shraddha’nandajii.

He left us on 19^th January 2001.

### 74. Shrii Umakant, Purnia

Umakantjii was initiated in 1966 by A’c. Amulya Ratan Sarangi. Later inspired by the ideals of A’nanda Ma’rga he would regularly accompany A’c. Sarangi for intensive pracar in the whole of Purnia Bhukti. He was an active volunteer of Aungika’ Sama’ja and PFI. Later he became the secretary of Aungika’ sama’ja and continued as its secretary for ten years.

He was arrested during emergency and had to suffer a lot. One of his son is also a Sanyasin of the ma'rga.

He had his Dharma Samiiksha' in 1981.

He left us on 16^th July 2005. He was devoted to the ma'rga all his life. He was a great devotee, a good praca'raka and an ideologically committed person.

### 75. A'c. Indradeo of Katihar

Indradeojii was initiated in the year 1956. Soon he became an a'ca'rya. He was then teaching in the Hindi department of DS College, Katihar. He later became the head of department. He had earned the reputation of a scholar in the town. He had a fertile mind. He was sincere Sa'dhaka'. The grace of Guru added charm in his personality. He was held in high esteem in the circle of the learned people. He used all his reputation as a learned person for propagating A'nanda marga in the public and among the students community. Many of his initiates later became a'caryas of A'nanda marga. They were A'ca'rya Vishveshvar from Araria, A'ca'rya Suryanarayanan from semapur & A'ca'rya Jagadish.

He was a pioneer among the a'nanda ma'rgiis of his time. Ma'rgiis approached him and sought his advice in matters of philosophy and practice of Sa'dhana. He did intensive pracar covering six districts Bihar in the vicinity of Katihar. He personally initiated more than a thousand margiis from all walks of life. He tirelessly worked for the mission until 1962 like a whole timer.

He used to be one among those who would take notes of Ba'ba's speech during DMCs and in Jamalpur in those days when there were no tape recorders. He associated himself the publication department of A'nanda ma'rga and helped in editing many books. Inspired by Ba'ba' he wrote a commentary on A'nanda su'tram in Samskrta. He was selected by Ba'ba' to address the challenge from Swami Bhayankara'carya during Ara'ha (Saharsa') DMC, During the Sha'stra'rtha (intellectual debate) about A'nanda ma'rga Philosophy he defeated Bhayankara'ca'rya. Bhayankaraca'rya later admitted the superiority of A'nanda Ma'rga philosophy and admitted that he was asked by a conservative Hindu outfit to malign A'nanda ma'rga. Many of his shiks'a'bhra'ta's became wholetimers of the marga.

He was initiated in Vishes'a yoga by Ba'ba' and he remained a steadfast devotee of ba'ba' all his life. He encouraged ma'rgiis willing to get married to follow AM revolutionary marriage system.

Due to some misunderstanding he dissociated himself with the organization after 1962, His contributions to A'nanda Ma'rga are incomparable. He is remembered by the society.

### 76. A'c Surya Narayan from Semapur, Katihar

A'c Surya Narayan was initiated by A'c Indardeo when he was his student. Later after completing his academic studies he took up the job of a teacher in Govt. High School. He became an a'ca'rya in 1962. A'c. Suryanarayan was a devoted person and always worked as a dedicated worker initiating hundreds of people. He was an active social worker and always ready to help during natural calamities.

He inspired his family to follow the ideals of the ma'rga and all his children (sons and daughters) got married according to A'nanda Ma'rga system. He left us to be united with his eternal love in 2004 leaving behind an inspiring legacy.

### 77. Shrii Kumar Guru from Ipoly, Malaysia

Bro. Kumar had his roots in South India but his forefathers had settled in Malaysia. He was a high school teacher when he was initiated by dada Pranakrsna'nanda in the year 1980. Both he & his Chinese wife Amrta' became ardent devotees of A'nanda Ma'rga since then. His house used to be a veritable living place for WTs. His hard work and the material and the emotional support helped in building the Ma'rga Guru residence in Ipoly. The same place has been further developed by dada Citkrsna'nanda into a model children's home project.

He was a very dependable and a affectionate person. His demise is great loss for His mission in Ipoly.

### 78. Sister Sa'vitrii, Metro Manila

Sister Sa'vitrii was inspired by Dada Advaisha'nanda and got initiated in the year 1980. She became a LFT while she was still young and was a source of inspiration to all those who worked with her. Later she got married to another LFT brother Surendra and led an ideal family life.

For a long time they were responsible for running the sectorial commerce project called the Morning Dew, in Manila sector. This way they supported the sectorial office and took care of the Dadas and Didis as their family.

She left us in 2010 to unite with her beloved for eternity.

### 78. Bro. Chandrabu's'an, Manila city

Bro. Chandra Bhu's'an is one of the oldest sa'dhakas of Manila. He was initiated by Dada Sumita'nanda in early 1970. He later became the President of AMPS, Philippines. He worked like a whole timer and sincerely propagated His mission and established many a social service projects for the benefit of the poor in Manila sector. He managed his small family by running a printing press. He was ever ready to help the sectorial

office and its workers. He is remembered for his sincere & hard work and for his affectionate & caring nature.

He left us in 1990.

### 79. Bro. Parmeshvara from Germany

Initiated in 1979 by Dada Yukteshvarananda bro. Parmeshvara worked tirelessly as a LFT. He was qualified medical professional and was married to an Indian sister Hema from Gujarat.

His was an ideal family man, a source of love and inspiration for the sectorial workers. He left us after a brief illness in 2010.

### 80. Sister Mahesvari's life - The Lily and the Moon!! (written by her daughter sis. Carol)

When I think of my mother Mahesvari, the first thing that comes to my mind is her love for BABA and dedication to HIS mission. Early morning she sat in front of her meditation table for about 45 minutes. Every day she sang Baba nam Kevalam kiirtan even while taking a shower. Every day she would ask me if I have done Kaoshikii? I remember it so well even after so many years. The warm energy radiating from her face got absorbed into HIM. I am thankful to her for all this. It is thanks to her constant love, example and guidance that I feel peace and harmony. I am thankful to BABA for letting me grow up with her love and guidance. But most of all for using her love to guide me to HIM.

My mother and I were best friends and we shared many experiences and moments, both happy and sad. Among the happy moments we shared, the happiest of all was travelling together to India to meet BABA! I will never forget our first visit to India together. It was amazingly full of both excitement and struggle as we went through all kind of challenges adjusting to the hard environment of India sleeping on the floor sharing a room with mouse, cockroaches, without any basic facilities, yet we were so happy always laughing. We had a fantastic time. BABA was really loving and generous towards us. HE blessed us with HIS presence, smiling to us. He asked us different questions. It was such a magical and a powerful experience. Mahesvari and I were crying of joy, trying to keep our balance.

While walking to the market to get some flower garlands to give to BABA Mahesvari decided to buy coconuts to serve Him coconut water. It was the craziest things she had done. She bought 5 coconuts, they were so heavy!!! but she carried them a long way. I

was frustrated as I did not want to carry the heavy load of coconuts but at the end I had to help her and while she was happily carrying her coconuts I thought this was madness.

So we came to BABA's house and she gave the coconuts to PA dada and asked him to please serve Him the coconut water. What was surprising that just one hour later while all of us waited outside for Him to come out for HIS walk, a dada started screaming for Prasad, called out all of us to come and then my mother and I ran to get Prasad. And then she came back full of joy because BABA had send us coconut water. Then I understood how much BABA was aware of what HIS devotees feel and do for HIM. This is only one example among several others where Mahesvari taught me how BABA is always with us, how HE is always present in our mind and that he knows everything we think or do. HE is always aware of everything happening in our lives.

Something my mother used to say very often stayed in my mind. Carol, remember we are not normal beings, we are tantrics, therefore we never give up! She kept joking about it and I thank her so much for this as now that she has left her physical body, her words stayed with me and continue inspiring me every day. Everytime things become difficult in my life I remember her jokes and this helps me to continue walking forward, overcoming any kind of obstacles and keeping a positive mind full of optimism. She was very positive, humble and patient. Her favorite flower was "sunflower" because she said this flower was always smiling to the sun.

She had a very challenging life (she was like a Lily flower floating in the muddy waters). She endured and survived many turmoils during her family life married to a very unaware husband who made her life very difficult through physical and mental abuse. Despite the hardship she had to put up for many years, she never stopped her dedication and commitment to serve humanity and please BABA.

Mahesvari lived a very meaningful but sacrificing life travelling and living in different places. She played the role of a family acarya as she was always taking care of both her little family and her universal family.

She started a children home in Guatemala together with her best friend within AM didi Tapatii who she dearly loved her. She loved children and took care of many Indian children in Guatemala for several years.

She was also actively involved with AMURT/AMURTEL work in the time of the earthquake in 1976. We all were, but she did most of the running around taking care of so many. Her energy was amazing. Wherever there was crisis, she will be there trying to help and eventhough there was not much she could do, still her positive and loving presence there radiating love, warmed and supported many to feel comfort.

Then she moved to Venezuela where she started the first AM jagrtii and worked as the manager of the Jagrtii for some years. She and her son David were with BABA while HE was touring in South America. BABA touched the head of her son David while she was there and this fulfilled her heart. She was so thankful and happy for this.

Later on she moved to Los Altos Hills, in USA to take care of her oldest son who became mentally sick. She struggled and worked very hard facing a lots of difficulties trying to help him. This was a very painful and hard situation to face and while struggling to find some alternative treatment, she continued to remain active teaching yoga classes and supporting acaryas at all levels as she always felt love and responsibility for their well being as they were serving BABA. She had a dream to start an alternative mental hospital where her son and other mentally sick people could get some help. She dreamed with a hospital where people could be treated and healed while living in a sentient, spiritual and loving environment, but unfortunately she never found the right help, had the money or time to realize it. She died in India trying to do this. I always admired her faith, and spirit of enthusiasm and hope. She continue working towards this dream until the last moment of her life trying to find a solution to her sons desease.

Then she moved to Norway to continue doing His mission.

During her last years living in Norway before she died she continued to serve BABA always praising and sharing HIS love with others. Whether through her Yoga classes for the parents and different woman organisations she always shared HIS teachings with all. She helped to establish Neo-humanistisk kindergarten in Oslo. She wrote, filmed and produced a video for AM “Stress Reduction with Meditation and Yoga.”

Mahesvari was really a busy hard working tantric bee, flying around from one thing to the other, never giving up before she finished it. From working in the kindergarten all day long, to the school to learn Norwegian, to her yoga courses, to her meetings to help refugees foreign women, to her video editing, to her role as mother, grandmother and univesal mother taking care of everyone around her who needed love and care. Such a dynamic soul. She is missed by all of us her daugheters and sons. She is also missed by the children and parents in the kindergarten, acaryas, friends, family, she is missed and loved by everyone.

What is most inspiring to my heart is that everyone who remembers her or talks about her has always something positive to say. She is loved and remembered by many hearts inspired through her existence to come to the path of bliss. She is Mahesvari the daughter of Shrii Shrii Ananda Murt. Till the end of her physical existence, I was present with her to witness and feel HIS bliss around us!

Thank you BABA!
